<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 1 -->

As-salamu 'alaykum. Wa 'alaykum as-salam. As-salamu 'alaykum. How are you? Are you well? As-salamu 'alaykum wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuh.

A'udhu billahi min al-shaytan al-rajim (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). My dear ones, Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). Wa bihi nasta'in wa salla-llahu 'ala rasulihi sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala alihi wa sallam (And in Him we seek help, and may the blessings and peace of Allah be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon his family and his progeny).

Previously, we read from the Zakhira. Uh... Kunuz al-Hikmah. We read Kunuz al-Hikmah. And the second book we read was al-Mula'iyya, which was a risala (treatise), a small one. Now, the third book is Kitab al-Mabda' wa 'l-Ma'ad (The Book of the Origin and the Return) by Sayyidna Mu'ayyad, qaddasa-llahu ruhahu (may Allah sanctify his soul). This is related to the haqa'iq-i Tayyibi (Tayyibi esoteric truths), that... and this is the first book that was written on mabda' wa 'l-ma'ad. After this, many books were written on mabda' wa 'l-ma'ad, but this is the first book by Sayyidna Mu'ayyad, Hibatullah bin Musa Shirazi, who is one of our famous bab al-abwab (gate of gates). And... whose famous couplet is: law kuntu 'asartu fi 'asri muhammadan ma qassartu rutbata 's-salmani (If I had lived in the time of Muhammad, my rank would not have been less than that of Salman). If I were in the time of the Prophet, my rank would not have been less than Salman's. So, he is the one who said this.

Sayyidna Mu'ayyad. And he has 800 majalis (sermons), Sayyidna Mu'ayyad's, which you have written, and you have encompassed all 'ulum (sciences) in them.

He was a bab-i zahir (manifest gate).

Ji, ji (Yes, yes). He was bab-i batin (the esoteric gate), I believe.

His rank is very high. In the da'wah, that is, among our bab al-abwab who have passed, his rank is very high. The rank of Sayyidna Mu'ayyad.

Meaning, to the extent that you are bab al-du'at... The... who were abu 'd-du'at (the father of the du'at)... meaning, when the du'at... the a'immah (Imams) went into concealment, and their representation... the du'at took over the representation of the a'immah, then you... you were given the rank of abu 'd-du'at by Imam Mustansir. That you will lead the prayers of the du'at. So he was called abu 'd-du'at. And umm al-du'at, the mother of the du'at, is Arwa bint Ahmad. Meaning, Hurrat al-Malika.

Yes.

In your time, the a'immah were not in concealment, were they?

No, it was after that. The era of the manifest Imams ended very quickly. After that, your... you remained, and Sayyidna Zu'ayb bin Musa was appointed by you and Hurrat al-Malika as the first da'i.

And today, the du'a (prayer) we read, of the second, of the second... the prayer of the second day, it has a very special quality. The prayers of Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq that we read in the morning, they are short prayers. So in them, Mawlana Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq, in the 30 days of prayers you have, in none of them has he used a muqatta' (disjointed letter), except in the prayer of the second day. That, yes, the wasila (means of intercession) that has been taken is from a muqatta' which is ghayr-i mukarrar (non-repeating). It has come only once in the Quran Majid. And what is that? Kaf Ha Ya 'Ayn Sad. Bi-kafin kafayta (With Kaf, You sufficed). Meaning, with Kaf, You are sufficient. Meaning, the being of Allah. As He says, kafa bi-llahi 'alima (Allah suffices as All-Knowing). Allah knows everything, and He is sufficient. Wa bi-ha'in hadayta (and with Ha, You guided), meaning ihdina 's-sirat al-mustaqim (guide us to the straight path). Yes. And bi-ya'in yassarta (and with Ya, You made easy). And with Ya, you created ease. As it is, fa-inna ma'a 'l-'usri yusra, inna ma'a 'l-'usri yusra (For indeed, with hardship [will be] ease. Indeed, with hardship [will be] ease.). And after that, Ya... Kaf Ha Ya 'Ayn. With 'Ayn, 'afawta (You forgave). You forgave. So Allah Ta'ala completely says that inna-llaha yaghfiru 'dh-dhunuba jami'a (Indeed, Allah forgives all sins). Allah Ta'ala has the power to forgive all sins. And then what is after that? Sad. Bi-sadin sadaqta (with Sad, You spoke the truth). That you spoke the truth in the Quran Majid, and your word... you are asdaqu 'l-qa'ilin (the most truthful of speakers). The most truthful of speakers. And your Messenger is also the most truthful. That just now you... before the risalat (mission), those people who later became your great enemies, would call you al-Sadiq al-Amin (the truthful, the trustworthy). That you are truthful and you are absolutely trustworthy. And to the extent that you... when you gathered... when you were commanded that you should call your near relatives, and when the verse wa-andhir 'ashirataka 'l-aqrabin (And warn your nearest kindred) was revealed, you called all your relatives and said, "If I tell you that a large army is coming from behind this Faran mountain that will attack you, will you accept my word?" They said, "We will believe your word."

Although they have not seen the army. But they said that you are truthful and you have never, you have never told a lie. So at that time, you presented the proof, meaning you presented the hujjat (argument), and then after presenting the hujjat, you announced your nubuwwat (prophethood) and risalat (mission).

And after that, it is said... it is said in history that there are two important days in the history of Islam.

The first day is the yawm-i 'ashirat al-aqrabin (day of the nearest kindred), when you gathered your relatives and made the declaration of your mission. This is the first day. And what did you say at that time? That, is there anyone who will support me and aid me? So Janab Ali was ten years old at that time. You stood up and said, "Ya... Ya... Ya Rasulullah." You said, "Ya Rasulullah." So the first person to say "Ya Rasulullah" was Janab Ali. He said, "I will support you, at your every opportunity." So you made him sit down. In this way, you asked the gathering three times, but all of them were sitting as if there were birds on their heads. But Janab Ali stood up and said this. You even said that whoever supports me today will be my khalifa (successor), my wasi (legatee), and my janishin (heir).

When this matter was settled, then it is said that the younger brother, that is, Ali 'alayhi 's-salam, fulfilled his promise. The entire history shows how much he supported the Prophet at every moment. So it was also the responsibility of the elder brother to fulfill his promise. So you, on the Day of Ghadir, you declared that al-yawma akmaltu lakum dinakum wa-atmamtu 'alaykum ni'mati (This day I have perfected for you your religion and completed My favor upon you). You conveyed this message. And you declared, man kuntu mawlahu fa-hadha 'aliyyun mawlahu (Of whomsoever I am the master, Ali is his master).

So this book, al-Mabda' wa 'l-Ma'ad, is by Sayyidna Mu'ayyad. Al-Mu'ayyad was a laqab (title) given to you by Mawlana Mustansir, who is our 18th Imam. And his original name was Hibatullah. Hibatullah bin Musa. And your father was also a da'i. Hibatullah bin Musa bin Sulayman bin Adam al-Shirazi. Yes.

So he was from Shiraz, a resident of Iran.

Where did your residence used to be, Hazrat? Your residence was in Iran or in Makkah/Madinah?

No, later he came here. He had come to Misr (Egypt).

Where did the a'immah live at that time?

In Misr. They were in Misr. The da'wah was in Misr.

Kitab al-Mabda' wa 'l-Ma'ad by Sayyidna Mu'ayyad, qaddasa-llahu ruhahu. Bismillah. Now there is a small mention about Sayyidna Mu'ayyad, nubdhatun min sirati sayyidina 'l-mu'ayyad (A glimpse from the life of Sayyidna Mu'ayyad). Qala sayyiduna 'aliyyu bnu sayyidina sulayman 'alaqahu fi kitabihi mufarrij al-abwab (Sayyidna Ali bin Sayyidna Sulayman mentioned it in his book, Mufarrij al-Abwab). His famous book is Mufarrij al-Abwab (The Opener of the Gates), in which you have compiled the du'at (prayers) of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd al-`Aqus`, the collection of prayers, it is a collection of prayers, collected in a very magnificent way. That they... the du'at of the Ahl al-Bayt, Rasulullah, Janab Ali, which are mustanad (authentic) du'at, you have compiled them in this Mufarrij al-Abwab. So... so in it you say that, wa-qala fa-kana sayyiduna mu'ayyadu 'd-din min waladi 'n-nabiyyi sulaymana bni dawuda 'alayhi 's-salam (He said: Sayyidna Mu'ayyad fi 'l-Din was from the progeny of the Prophet Sulayman son of Dawud, peace be upon him). That Sayyidna Mu'ayyad fi 'l-Din, he was from the progeny of the Prophet Sulayman bin Dawud 'alayhi 's-salam. And Sulayman bin Dawud has great eminence, whose mention is in the Quran Majid. And there are only two such prophets who went from mustawda' (temporary abode) to mustaqarr (permanent abode). Who are they? The first is Idris 'alayhi 's-salam. Idris 'alayhi 's-salam, about whom it is said that wa-rafa'nahu makanan 'aliyya (and We raised him to a high station). That we elevated his place and rank. The second is this Sulayman, the prophet.

Rasulullah was also one, right?

Yes. Rasulullah...

Rasulullah was also...

Yes, Rasulullah's status is different. Yes.

Wa-kana abahu wa-ajdaduhu du'ata a'immatin atharin min azman al-a'immat al-masturin al-thalatha (And his fathers and forefathers were du'at of the pure Imams from the time of the three concealed Imams). And your fathers and forefathers were du'at of the A'immah Athar (Pure Imams), from the time of the A'immat al-Masturin al-Thalatha (Three Concealed Imams). And your fathers... your parents were, and forefathers were, who are your grandfathers, they are from the du'at of the A'immah Athar, who were those Imams who were... the three masturin (concealed) Imams. The Imams of the three masturin... meaning after Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il, when the Imams went into concealment, then three Imams were called masturin. Because after that, Mawlana Mahdi's appearance happened. So the appearance of Mahdi was completely public. So these three Imams with their muhtajibin (veiled ones) are the masturin. And who are those Imams? Ya'la, Ali, and Abu Talib Ali. Yes, these are the three. Who are muhtajibin. And their hujub (veils) are 'Abdullah, Ahmad, and Husayn. And in the time of Mawlana Ahmad, there was Ikhwan al-Safa... the Rasa'il of Ikhwan al-Safa.

Wa-kana huwa al-hujjata 'l-a'immat al-'uzma laday al-imam al-mustansir bi-llahi 'alayhi 's-salam (And he was the Hujjah of the great Imams for Imam al-Mustansir bi-llah, peace be upon him). And you were the hujjat al-'uzma. Before Imam Mustansir 'alayhi 's-salam. Wa-lahu insha'atun jammah fi 'z-zahir wa 'l-batin wa 'l-haqa'iq (And he has many compositions, in the exoteric, the esoteric, and the truths). And you have insha'at, meaning your compositions, which are many, which are in the zahir, in the batin, and in the haqa'iq. Wa-ntaqala fi 'l-'ashr al-awakhir min shahri shawwal al-karim sanata arba'a mi'ah wa sab'in hijriyyah (He passed away in the last ten days of the month of Shawwal in the year 470 Hijri). On the year 470 Hijri, when it was the month of Shawwal, he passed away in its last decade. Wa-qubira fi 'd-dari 'llati 'ntaqala fiha bi-dar al-'ilm bi 'l-qahirat al-mu'izziyyah bi-misr (And he was buried in the house where he passed away, in the Dar al-'Ilm in al-Qahira al-Mu'izziyya in Egypt). And you were buried, in that place, which is the Dar al-'Ilm. Meaning, the Qahira (Cairo) that is there, al-Mu'izziyya, in Misr (Egypt).

The ziyarat (visit) has not been made. Hasn't been done.

The ziyarat... Yes.

Of Sayyidna Mu'ayyad.

His ziyarat... ziyarat... inside it... uh... we went there, but perhaps we didn't go inside, to the Al-Azhar University. To its... to one of its sections, we went to the library section. Yes. Allah knows where his grave is.

Sayyidna Mu'ayyad had a maternal uncle, who... uh... wanted... that Sayyidna Mu'ayyad should not go into the presence of the Imam, because that uncle knew that he was very intelligent and was fond of knowledge. So he kept stopping him. And to the extent that for five years, he did not get a chance to go to the Imam. Yes. Then when he went to the Imam, then the Imam imparted his ma'rifat (gnosis) to him, and then he reached high ranks. To the extent that it is narrated that once you... once a... a... a letter from the Imam reached you, and it was said that you have to go to this place and perform jihad. So you wrote on the back of his letter that if Mu'ayyad's hands are cut off, you will not find another Mu'ayyad.

He wrote this and signed it. So actually, it was his uncle's machination. Yes. Yes. So this was his status, Sayyidna Mu'ayyad's.

And his du'as too, you see in the Sahifah, his continuous du'as are present. He has a large Sahifah, Sayyidna Mu'ayyad's, of du'as.

Was it him about whom... the ones who were around your Imam, the enemies who were hostile to you, they sent him to war? Was that the incident?

Yes, yes.

And... and this... Sayyidna Mu'ayyad's du'as, haqa'iq have been written on them by Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il ibn Muhammad. Isma'il ibn Muhammad, who is the Da'i Shumukh. And he is a very high-ranking da'i, and his books are the highest books of the da'wah. Like Mansak, Mansak al-Hajj is his book, Sayyidna Isma'il Muhammad's. And Kitab al-Subuh. So in Kitab al-Subuh are the haqa'iq of his books. Yes. So I remember that Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan used to come and Sayyidna 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad would be in i'tikaf. He would be sitting in the mosque. Because he used to live in the mosque. So Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan would come to the mosque and sit in a place. And then he would go and sit with Sayyidna 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad. And Sayyidna sahib's... Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan's uncle was there, who was the son of Sayyidna 'Abdullah. His uncle, Sayyidna 'Abdullah who was the da'i ghayr al-munfarid (non-solitary da'i). His son... anyway, so he would go and sit with Sayyidna sahib in the lesson. So only Sayyidna 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad would be there and his uncle would be there, Sayyidna sahib's. He would not stop his uncle. So once I had asked, Sayyidna sahib, Sayyidna 'Abdullah Muhammad, "Which book do you read with Sayyidna sahib?" So he looked at me with some thought. "What kind of question are you asking?" Meaning I am asking beyond my capacity. So then he finally gave the answer, that "I am reading Kitab al-Subuh." The haqa'iq of Sayyidna Mu'ayyad. Yes.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi rabbi 'l-'alamin, wa 's-salatu 'ala rasulihi sayyidina muhammadin khatami 'n-nabiyyin, wa 'ala wasiyyihi 'aliyyi bni abi talibin ashrafi 'l-wasiyyin, wa 'ala al-a'immati min dhurriyyatihim at-tayyibin at-tahirin (All praise is for Allah, Lord of the worlds, and blessings be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, the seal of the prophets, and upon his legatee, Ali ibn Abi Talib, the noblest of legatees, and upon the Imams from their progeny, the pure and the purified). All praise is for Allah, who is the Lord of the worlds. And may blessings be upon His Messenger, who is Sayyidna Muhammad, the seal of the prophets, and upon his legatee, who is Ali ibn Abi Talib, who is the noblest among legatees, and upon the Imams from their progeny, the pure and the purified. Now look, al-hamdu lillahi rabbi 'l-'alamin. Allah, what does He say? All praises are for Allah, rabbu 'l-'alamin (Lord of the worlds). Who is the Lord of the worlds. Now, the thing upon which Allah has prided Himself and which He has mentioned in the first verse is that He has said rabbu 'l-'alamin. Whereas Allah Ta'ala could have said that He is khaliqu 'l-'alamin (creator of the worlds). That He is the creator of the worlds. So what is the thing that is higher than the rank of creation? That is the nurturing, the tarbiyat (upbringing). Tarbiyat. Tarbiyat is higher than the rank of creation. Therefore, Allah Ta'ala has described Himself with the highest rank, that He is rabbu 'l-'alamin. That He is indeed the creator of the worlds, but He also nurtures them, He is their Lord. Therefore, in our society, the importance of parents is not because they gave birth to us, but because they are responsible for our upbringing. Yes.

And then there is also this, that for whichever world... Allah Ta'ala has said for the Messenger that he is rahmatan li 'l-'alamin (a mercy to the worlds). For whichever world Allah Ta'ala is the Lord, you will have to believe that for that world, the Prophet 'alayhi 's-salat wa 's-salam, he is... nurturing? ...no, for that world, you are a mercy. For whichever world Allah Ta'ala is the Lord, for that world you have become a mercy. So how great is this eminence?

And then, what is the status of the Messenger? That he has said that sayara-llahu a'malakum wa-rasuluhu (Allah will see your deeds, and His Messenger). That soon, Allah will see your deeds, and His Messenger. Meaning, Allah who is wahdahu la sharika lahu (One, with no partner), He has given such eminence to the Messenger that He has made him a partner with Himself. That the deeds of the Muslim ummah and of the disbelievers and polytheists, all their deeds, Allah Ta'ala will see and will decide, and His Messenger. Yes.

And then, Allah says that He is the knower of the unseen. 'Alim al-ghaybi wa 'sh-shahadah (Knower of the unseen and the witnessed). Allah has called Himself this. But what did He say? In Surah... Surah Jinn, that... la yuzhiru... 'ala ghaybihi ahada illa man irtada min rasulin (He does not disclose His unseen to anyone, except to a messenger whom He has chosen). That He does not make His unseen manifest to anyone. Meaning He does not make anyone a knower of the unseen, except that messenger with whom He is pleased. And the mention of being pleased is in the Quran Majid, in Surah al-Duha. That... what did He say? Yes? fa-tarda (and you will be satisfied). Yes, fa-tarda. Allah will give you so much that you will be happy and pleased.

So you will see that the Quran Majid is like a qasidah (ode) in praise of the Messenger. From beginning to end, it is the mention of the Messenger.

A moment ago, it was mentioned that... about Kaf Ha Ya 'Ayn Sad... Janab Zaynab was teaching its haqa'iq to women. There is no mention of Janab Zaynab's rank, whereas Imam Hasan and Imam Husayn's ranks are mentioned. Is her rank... her rank is also high, right?

Yes, I mean... I haven't read about it, but there is a mention, that Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, Sayyidna Wawaam, in the next raj'at (return), the rank he will get will be that he will manifest as Mawlatuna Zaynab. Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan.

aba'u mawlana al-imam ibni 'l-imam at-tayyib ab al-qasim amir al-mu'minin. i'lam ayyuha 'l-akh as-sa'id al-muwaffaq ar-rashid, anara-llahu basiratak wa-akhlisa li-wilayati mawalik sariratak. innahu wasala kitabuka ittasalta bi 'l-fawzi fi 'l-ma'ad, asbabuka tas'alu... O brother, who is pious, who has been granted success, and who is rightly guided, may Allah illuminate your insight and make your heart sincere for the wilayat (guardianship) of your masters. That your book, meaning the question you asked, has reached me, in which you have connected with success in the hereafter... the reasons... you ask... an khilafa'ida bima tanalu bihi... wa-hiya munqalibuka 'l-ajfa... that I should benefit you so that you may reach your place of return, meaning your hereafter, with success and felicity. Thus, I have fully answered your question, about what I know of your worthiness, that you are completely attached to the rope of wilayat for the rightly guided Imams, and your attachment and your fulfillment of the obligations upon you, from the religious duties, and your being organized in the ranks of those who act upon the religious commands, the mujtahidin (those who strive). And what I will explain to you in this book of mine is from the secret of the spiritual malakut (kingdom), which is connected through revelation with every rank, the spiritual ranks. And from there to the hudud (ranks) of the sincere ones, who are entrusted with the treasury of secrets, who are responsible for... the responsibility is to take the creatures out of the storm of atoms. The sincere ones... the question... no, no, the question hasn't started yet... The sincere ones, and he is the secret of the souls and the elixir for them. Meaning it will illuminate the souls and make them like gold. Such that the color of Allah will be cast upon it, the good color. As it is in the Quran Majid, Sibghata-llah wa-man ahsanu mina-llahi sibghah (The religion of Allah. And who is better than Allah in [ordaining] religion?). The color of Allah, and whose color is better than Allah's? Yes. The good... and it will make it... the pure... pure... and it will make it pure, after the sensual state has ended. And this is that your... the state you were in, the sensual soul, which was animalistic, then these sciences of the da'wah, which come from the hudud of the da'wah and from the Imam's gaze to the hudud of the da'wah, these things will make your sensual state, which is animalistic, into a speaking, angelic one (`natiqah-yi malakiyyah`). Fa-sunhu 'an kulli wahid... illa man dhakartu laka... yasunuka-llah. So you must protect it from everyone, except those I have mentioned to you. And Allah will protect you. Wa-hfazhu yahfazuka bihi bi-llah (And protect it, and Allah will protect you through it). And you protect it, and Allah will protect you through it. Wa-ana akhidhun 'alayka 'ahda-llahi 'l-mas'ul. And I am taking from you a covenant of Allah, about which you will be questioned. Wa 'l-mu'akkad. And which is confirmed. Wa-'ala man adhintu laka... 'alayhi 'an-llahi 'l-mas'ul al-mu'akkad. Such a covenant that you will be questioned about and which is confirmed. Wa-illa fa-anta wa-man yuqifu 'alayhi bari'un minhum ajma'in (Otherwise, you and whoever is informed of it will be free from them all). Otherwise, you and whoever is informed of it will be free from them all. Meaning from the Imams and the hudud of the da'wah, you will be absolved. Yes. Wa 'l-mu'akkad wa-mithaquhu mughalladh. This covenant of Allah is confirmed, and its pact is very strict. Wa-mushaddad, and severe. Alladhi akhadhahu 'ala al-mala'ikat al-muqarrabin. Which Allah Ta'ala took from His close angels. Wa-anbiya'ihi 'l-mursalin. And His sent prophets. Wa-a'immat ad-dinin al-hadin. And the guiding Imams of the religion. Wa-hududihim ar-rashidin. And their rightly guided hudud. Salawatu-llahi 'alayhim ajma'in (May the blessings of Allah be upon them all). Yes. And this 'ahd sharif (noble covenant) which is being talked about, about taking it from the angels, its mention is in the Quran Majid. That Allah took a bay'at (oath of allegiance) and a covenant, and in it, He first took the name of the Messenger. That we take your covenant. Then he took Adam's, then Nuh's, then Ibrahim's, Musa's, and 'Isa's. Yes.

Allah nasakhta... aminan harfan, wa-la aqalla wa-la akthara (You shall not copy it... not a single letter, neither less nor more). And it is binding on you that you do not naskh (copy) it, meaning my discourse, you should not take notes of it. Do not put it in writing. Not a single letter, neither less nor more. Wa-la waqafta 'alayhi illa anta. And no one should be informed of it except you. Meaning only you are permitted to know it. Au man adhintu lahu... bi 'l-wuquf... adhintu lahu bi 'l-wuqufi 'alayha (or the one whom I have permitted... to be informed of it). Or the one whom I have commanded and given permission to be informed of it. Wa-innaka tu'idu ilayya hadhihi 'n-nuskha ba'da an tafrugha min qira'atiha (And you will return this manuscript to me after you have finished reading it). And you will return this manuscript to me after you have completely finished reading it. Wa-llahu 'ala ma naqulu wakil (And Allah is a witness over what we say). And Allah Ta'ala is a witness over what we say. So you will do this. I'lam anna ghayb al-ghuyub alladhi la tajassaru nahwahu 'l-khawatir (Know that the Unseen of the Unseen, towards which thoughts dare not venture). That the Unseen of the Unseen, towards which our thoughts dare not venture, la yudraku bi-'add az-zamir (cannot be comprehended by the effort of the mind), and it cannot be comprehended by the effort of the mind, and it cannot be confined within the mind, that Unseen of the Unseen. Wa-la bi-iha'ati 't-tafkir (And neither by encompassing it through thought). And neither by encompassing it through thought and reflection can its reality be known. Wa-la yaqa'u 'alayhi ismun wa-la sifah wa-la sabil ila idrakihi bi 'l-iqrar (And neither can a name fall upon it, nor an attribute. And there is no way to comprehend it through confession). And neither can a name fall upon it, nor an attribute. And there is no way to comprehend it through confession. Bi-anna li-jami' al-'awalim mubdi'an abda'aha (That for all the worlds, there is an Originator who originated them). That for all the worlds, there is an Originator who created it. Ta'juzu 'l-'uqul 'an idrakihi (All intellects are powerless to comprehend Him). All intellects are powerless to comprehend Him. Wa-'lam anna 't-tawhid huwa ma'rifat al-hudud al-'aliyah wa 'd-daniyah (And know that tawhid is the gnosis of the higher and lower hudud). And know that tawhid (monotheism) is the ma'rifat (gnosis) of the higher hudud and the lower hudud. Meaning, the ma'rifat of the Exalted Originator is not possible. The ma'rifat of the Exalted Originator is not possible. So, as Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman says, that... at-tawhid la-hum... no... at-tawhid laysa li 'l-ma'bud wa-lakin at-tawhid ma'rifat al-hudud (Tawhid is not for the Worshipped One, but tawhid is the gnosis of the hudud). That the ma'rifat of the Worshipped One is not tawhid, but the ma'rifat of the hudud is tawhid. Yes. The higher hudud and the lower hudud. And who has become the asman (heaven) of whom, and whose zamin (earth) has one become? Who is 'ali (higher) and who is dani (lower)? Yes. So its ma'rifat... as it is said, ma'rifat na kuni, zindiqi (If you do not have gnosis, it is heresy). If there is no ma'rifat, then that is kufr (disbelief). Yes.

Wa-'lam anna 't-tawhid huwa ma'rifat al-hudud al-'aliyah wa 'd-daniyah (Know that tawhid is the gnosis of the higher and lower hudud). Wa 'l-i'tiraf (And confession). And this is why Sani's rank fell from Munba'ith. He acknowledged Allah, meaning he confessed the Exalted Originator, that the Exalted Originator created everything. He did not do anything wrong there. And then he even had ma'rifat of 'Aql al-Awwal (the First Intellect). He had ma'rifat of the rank of 'Aql al-Awwal, and his... but where he lacked ma'rifat was of Munba'ith al-Awwal. And he... he said this: "I and Munba'ith al-Awwal are sawa sawa (equal). And in rank, we are one and the same." So that... due to not having that ma'rifat, his rank fell. Yes.

Hadrat Ibrahim sahib was also there...

Yes.

Wa 'l-i'tiraf anna kulla haddin minhum wahidun fi rutbatihi (And confess that every hadd among them is unique in his rank). And know this, that every hadd among them is unique in his rank. La yusharikuhu fiha siwahu (And in that rank, no one else is his partner). And in that rank, no one else is his partner. This is tawhid. Meaning, every hadd is unique. Yes. La ilaha illa-llah (There is no god but Allah). There is no object of worship, meaning no ilah, no object of worship, except Allah. There is no ilah except Allah. Meaning, among the intellects of the spiritual world, none is worthy of the word "Allah" being applied to them, except 'Aql al-Awwal. They are all at the rank of ilah. The hudud, the intellects of the spiritual world, are at the rank of ilah. And at the rank of Allah is only 'Aql al-Awwal. As in our time, the lower hudud and the higher hudud, whether they are mansus 'alayh (designated) or ghayr-i mansus 'alayh (non-designated), all of them are at the rank of ilah. And at the rank of Allah is the Imam of the time. La ilaha illa-llah. Yes.

And this kalimah, which is `la ilaha illa huwa`, is higher than `la ilaha illa-llah`. Yes. Because the Mubda' al-Awwal, meaning 'Aql al-Awwal, the confession he made of the Mubdi', what was it? Shahida-llahu annahu la ilaha illa huwa (Allah witnesses that there is no deity except Him). That Allah confessed this, that there is no object of worship except Him. Yes. So there, there, He used the word `huwa` (He). Al-huwiyyat al-muta'aliyah (The Transcendent Ipseity). He... his... meaning, he confessed. Gnosis is not possible, but he confessed. And then Rasulullah did not make that his kalimah. He did not make `la ilaha illa huwa` his kalimah, but rather he propagated `la ilaha illa-llah` and made it his kalimah.

And the numerical value of `la ilaha illa huwa` is 110. If its value is calculated from the science of jumal, the value of `la ilaha illa huwa` is 110. So... as it is in Surah Muzzammil: `rabbu 'l-mashriqi wa 'l-maghribi la ilaha illa huwa fa-ttakhidhhu wakila` (Lord of the East and the West, there is no god but Him, so take Him as a representative). That He is the Lord of the East and the West. Meaning, from where it begins to rise. If we look at this dawr (cycle), then from where it begins to rise, meaning Hasan. The Imams rose from Hasan. And where does it set? With Abu 'l-Qasim. So, `rabbu 'l-mashriqi wa 'l-maghribi`. Meaning, all of them are going to be organized in the being of Ali. So, `rabbu 'l-mashriqi wa 'l-maghribi la ilaha illa huwa`, meaning Ali. Fa-ttakhidhhu wakila (so take Him as a representative). The Messenger is commanded to take him as a wakil (representative). Make him your wasi (legatee). Yes.

That is also `rabbu 'l-mashriqayni wa-rabbu 'l-maghribayni` (Lord of the two Easts and Lord of the two Wests).

Yes, from there, emerging from Mawlana Ali and ending with him.

Yes.

So when a single one came, then a dual came, then `mashariq wa magharib` (easts and wests) also came. So that is...

The Amir al-Mu'minin himself explained this, in the zahir (exoteric) sense. Yes. In the exoteric sense, how did he explain it? That in a year, there comes a time when the sun rises from the same point it rose from. And it sets at the same point it sets at.

And the famous book, which is... which is... what is its name? `Saluni qabla an tafqiduni` (Ask me before you lose me), the answer is given in it. Mawla-i Ka'inat was asked this question. That Allah Ta'ala has said `rabbu 'l-mashriqi wa 'l-maghribi` in one place, `rabbu 'l-mashriqayni wa-rabbu 'l-maghribayni` in another, and `rabbu 'l-mashariqi wa 'l-magharib` in a third. What does this mean? So he said, "Look, when it rises and sets, it happens from a single point. Such a time also comes in a year." The second is that its beginning and end have been taken. Meaning, from where it is rising, and from where it is setting. The name of that state is `rabbu 'l-mashriqayni wa-rabbu 'l-maghribayni`. And `rabbu 'l-mashariqi wa 'l-magharib` means it rises from different places and sets. Meaning, its rising point is not one, nor is its setting point. Yes. Yes. And its haqa'iq have also been explained by one of our elders, what is meant by `rabbu 'l-mashriqayn` and `maghribayn`.

And `tajrid` (abstraction) is what? That `huwa salb al-ilahiyyah 'an jami' al-mubdi'at wa 'l-makhluqat wa-ithbatuha li-mubdi'ihim` (it is the stripping of divinity from all originated and created things, and affirming it for their Originator). That the attribute of divinity (`ilahiyyat`), meaning bringing forth from `nis` (non-existence) to `hast` (existence), from `na the` (were not) to creating existence from there... this attribute, to `salb` (strip) it, to snatch it from all created things, from all creatures. That this power is not in anyone, to bring forth from non-existence to existence, to bring into being where there was nothing. This power and strength is not in anyone. So, and to affirm it, that this attribute of divinity, if it exists, it is in their Originator.

And `tanzih` (transcendence). And `tanzih` means `huwa nafy jami' as-sifat 'an ghayb al-ghuyub` (it is the negation of all attributes from the Unseen of the Unseen). To negate all attributes from the Unseen of the Unseen. That He is knowing, manifest, hidden, creator... all these attributes, the beautiful names of Allah, to negate them from Him. This reality is not in the Originator. The Exalted Originator created the bearers of these realities. Yes. So that... to deny all attributes from the Unseen of the Unseen, meaning the Exalted Originator. To deny it. `i'tirafan lahu bi-anna jami' ash-sharaf wa 'l-jalalah` (confessing for Him that all nobility and majesty). To confess for Him that all nobility and majesty... and attributes, whatever attributes of nobility and majesty there are, and what is expressed in all languages through signs, with the definitions of divinity, `fa-innaha waqi'atun 'ala al-'aql al-awwal` (they all fall upon the First Intellect). All these realities and nobility and majesty apply to the First Intellect. As I will explain to you later... later on, its reality will be explained. `fa-hadhihi haqiqat at-tawhid wa 't-tajrid wa 't-tanzih` (So this is the reality of tawhid, tajrid, and tanzih). So this is the reality of `tawhid`, `tajrid`, and `tanzih`. `wa-zuddatu ma ja'a 'an mawalik salawatu-llahi 'alayhim fi 't-tawhid` (And this is the essence of what has come from our masters, blessings of Allah be upon them, regarding tawhid). And this is the essence of what has come from our masters, 'ala dhikrihi 's-salam (upon his mention be peace), regarding `tawhid`.

`faslun fi ibda' ar-ruhaniyyin` (A chapter on the origination of the spiritual beings). A chapter on the origination of the spiritual beings. Meaning... uh... the mention of the origination of the spiritual beings is happening here. That... meaning, what I mean is... uh, how the Unseen of the Unseen created the intellects, about that. `i'lam anna ghayb al-ghuyub jalla wa-'ala... abda'a 'alam al-ibda' daf'atan wahidah` (Know that the Unseen of the Unseen, glorified and exalted is He... originated the world of origination in a single instance). That He created it in a single instance. `bi-la zamanin wa-la makanin` (without time or space). Not time, nor space. Time and space are later things. Meaning, 'Ashir created time and space. Meaning, what is time? The rising and setting of the sun, from that time is created. And space? First 'Ashir created space, and then he created time. Meaning, he created space, i.e., the world and the universe, and then he arranged the movement of the sun. So that is a much later thing, time and space. So this is a thing beyond and above time and space. `suwaran nuraniyyah kathirah la yusiha al-'adad` (luminous forms, numerous, which number cannot encompass). He created such forms that were very luminous, numerous, and number cannot encompass them. `mutasawin fi 'l-kamal al-awwal wa 'l-wujud al-awwal` (equal in the first perfection and the first existence). And they were equal in the first perfection and the first existence. The reality in which they were created, they were all created in the same reality. They were adorned with the same attributes. And `alladhi huwa al-hayat wa 'l-qudrah wa 'l-'ilm` (which is life, power, and knowledge). And He gave them life, power, and knowledge. Life, meaning in the most excellent state. He created them in the most excellent state. That they came into being in the most excellent state. And He gave them power, that they can do whatever they wish. Yes. And He gave them knowledge. Meaning the knowledge of what was and what will be. `wa-kana dhalika bi-mujibi 'adlihi ta'ala` (And that was by the requirement of His justice, the Exalted). And all this that Allah Ta'ala did, meaning the Unseen of the Unseen, was by the requirement of His very justice. `an ja'alahum siyan` (that He made them equal). That He made them all equal. `la fadla li-ahadin minhum 'ala akhar` (no one among them had any superiority over another). That no one among them had any superiority over another. `wa-kanu fi 'l-kathrah 'ala halin la tu'addu 'adadan` (And they were so numerous that their number could not be counted). And they were so numerous that their number could not be counted. `dalilun 'ala anna al-'uqul la tuhitu bi-mablaghi fadlihi wa-judihi ta'ala` (a proof that intellects cannot encompass the extent of His grace and His generosity, the Exalted). And... that... this is a proof that intellects... his grace and his generosity and bounty, which Allah has, meaning the Exalted Originator, it cannot be encompassed. `wa-hum yusammawna 'alam al-ibda'`. And they are called the World of Origination. Meaning, this... this... this is a very fine point. That the World of Origination is what? The spiritual world. `al-'alam ar-ruhani` (the spiritual world), they themselves are the intellects. It is not that a world was created, and Allah Ta'ala placed intellects in it, it didn't happen like that. `al-'uqul hum al-'alam ar-ruhani`. The intellects, they themselves are the spiritual world. And the World of Origination. `li-kawnihim arwahan nuraniyyah la kathafata fiha wa-la juthum wa-la yahwiha makan` (Because they are luminous spirits in which there is no density, nor any body, and space cannot contain them). And `wa-la ihtaja da'iha ila zaman` (and their invoker did not need time). And their originator did not need time for them. `wa-dalilu dhalika inna najidu nufusana wa-hiya min tilka al-'alam ka 'l-juz' al-haqir min kullin 'azimin al-khatir` (And the proof of that is that we find our souls, which are from that world, like a tiny part of a great and mighty whole). That is, we see, we find that our souls, which are a part of that world... a tiny part... which are from the world of intellects that have been created, so they are a tiny part of that world, we... `yaqta'u masafat ash-sharqi wa 'l-gharbi bi-jawalan al-khawatir fi aqalli min lamhi 'l-basar` (it traverses the distance of the east and the west through the wandering of thoughts in less than the blink of an eye). And our reality is that we can traverse from east to west. Our heart or our mind goes from one place to another instantly. Our mind instantly goes from one place to another, in the blink of an eye. Today it's here, and today it's miles away at that place. So this is our reality. `wa-taqdiru 'ala tasawwuri ma ghaba 'anha` (And it has power over conceiving what is absent from it). And what is absent from us, in that regard also, from an intellectual perspective, we can encompass it. `min ghayri an yahwiyaha min dhalika makanun aw yahtajju fihi ila zaman` (without it being contained by that place, or needing time for it). `bal ta'thuru fi fikratiha al-jihat al-ba'idah bal as-samawati wa 'l-ard wa-ma baynahuma fi aqalli min lahzah` (Rather, it affects in its thought the distant directions, even the heavens and the earth and what is between them, in less than a moment). We bring various directions into our thoughts, which are very far away, even the heavens and the earth, and what is between them, in less than a moment, they come into our minds. `fa-idha kana dhalika minna ma'a 'ajzina wa-qusurina` (When this is from us, despite our powerlessness and our deficiencies). When this is our reality, that despite our powerlessness and our deficiencies, this is our reality. `wa-kawn nufusina murtabitatan bi-ajsamina` (and our souls being connected to our bodies). And our... being in the physical world, despite that, this power and reality is present in our souls. `fa-kayfa bi-man huwa kamilun mutajarridun 'an al-ittihad bi 'l-ajsam` (So how would it be for one who is complete and abstract from being connected to bodies?). So what would be the reality of that which is complete and abstract? From being connected to bodies, from physical reality, from which there is no reality upon them. `fa-hiya idhan ma'ani suwari 'alam al-ibda' 'ala ma hiya min al-jalali wa 'sh-sharafi wa 'l-fadli wa 'l-kamal` (So then it is the meaning of the forms of the World of Origination, in their majesty, nobility, grace, and perfection). So what I mean is, the forms of the World of Origination, regarding them, that they were established in majesty, nobility, grace, and perfection, in such a high rank. `ya'juzu 'uqul al-bashari 'an idraki aqalli sifatin min sifatihim` (that the intellects of humankind are powerless to comprehend the least of their attributes). That our human intellects cannot comprehend their realities, the least of their attributes. `fa-kayfa yakunu min 'uqulin sabilun ila idraki mubdi'ihim` (So how can there be a way for the intellects to comprehend their Originator?). So how can a way be found to... to comprehend their Originator? `ma'a 'l-'ajzi 'an idraki mubda'atihi` (while being powerless to comprehend His origination). That we cannot comprehend our own intellects, and the intellects cannot comprehend their Originator, so how can we confess about their Originator? `bal huwa muta'alin 'an dhalika 'uluwwan kabira` (Rather, He is transcendent and higher than all that, a great transcendence). Rather, he is high and sublime from all those things, a great transcendence. Yes.

`thumma inna suwar...` No, this is another discussion.

And see how clearly he is explaining, one thing at a time. And among us, this Hindi and `Ajami` (non-Arab) `du'at`, this is their specialty, that their compositions, like those of Ali bin Sulayman, and Hasan Khan, and other `du'at`, the statements of the `hudud` of the da'wah, Sayyidna Akram al-Safi, although they are very high-level statements, they are so clear that a person understands them, understands them literally. But the Arab `du'at`, they have that high status, but they make the matter so ambiguous that it is very difficult to understand. Now, the reality of Sayyidna Hasan Khan is that the 70 books he wrote on `haqa'iq`, you don't need to look at a dictionary even once. He explained it in such an easy way. And what high-level topics he has clarified. Yes.

This is also very clear.

Yes, this is very clear. There is no complication.

And this is the same reality, that to say something easily is a very difficult task, whereas to say it in a difficult way is easier. Like Sayyidna Hamiduddin al-Kirmani, who was the `da'i` of Iraqain (the two Iraqs), meaning the `da'i` of Iran and Iraq, and who has a great rank, he became a `bab al-abwab`. So you see, a high-level book like `Rahat al-'Aql` (The Repose of the Intellect), which is a very voluminous book, you will see that you don't need a dictionary even once. But there are such subtleties in it, in the book `Rahat al-'Aql`, that once, Hadrat Fathullah Sahib Muqaddas was teaching the `hudud` of the da'wah. In those days, there used to be meetings with various shaykhs, they would come and go. So by chance, the `da'wah`... he was teaching in a room. So a learned person, a shaykh, came by chance. He said, "Mawlana, which book are you teaching?" He took the book in his hand. From Hadrat Fathullah Sahib Muqaddas's hand. It was `Rahat al-'Aql`. So, Hadrat... he saw it, he was reading it, he was a scholar of Arabic anyway, but he looked at it, turned the pages here and there. Then Hadrat Fathullah Sahib, who was very witty and quick with a retort, he said, "Mawlana, this is `rahat al-'aql` (repose of the intellect) for us, but `kulfat al-'aql` (a burden for the intellect) for you." This is `rahat al-'aql` for us, but a burden for your intellect.

As Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad, Sayyidna Sharfi, Sayyidna Ali bin Husayn, the father of Wawaam, who stayed here for two years in Hindustan. When you were in Hyderabad, you were teaching. So our method is that the beneficiaries... they determine everything from grammar, that this is written in the grammar. So one gentleman said, "This is the `khabar` (predicate) and this is the `mubtada` (subject), this is the `khabar`." So you said, he said, "O Allah, read! The `mubtada` is here, and the `khabar` is with Allah." The `mubtada` is here, and the `khabar` is with Allah. And you had said another very good sentence, that your example, O Indians, is like this: you caught the cow, held its horns, and we milked it. To control the cow, you held its horns, but in the meantime, we milked it. Meaning you were caught up in the entanglements of grammar, while we obtained the essence.

So I have also reflected on this, that too much grammar is not needed. To read the books of the da'wah, you will see that if a dictionary of the books of the da'wah were made, very easily, because the words and the accents and their noble names repeat quite a lot. So, if a person becomes familiar with a little bit of grammar, it is enough to read the books of the da'wah. Yes. It's not that one should get into the narrow alleys of it, spend one's life learning grammar, and not come to the actual book and the actual topic. Yes. So I mentioned once that until a person learns to read without understanding, he can never read Arabic. Without understanding? Without understanding. Until he learns to read without understanding, he can never read Arabic. Meaning what? That you take a book, buy a book from the market, for example, my... my experience is that I used to take a book from the market and I would start reading it. So when I started reading, it's my habit to mark, in my own books. So the things... like `Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim`, or `hamd`, or some such things... those sentences which I understood, completely, 100%, I would mark them. In this way, I would read the whole book. And I would put it aside in it. Then I took another book. Now, the book I had read, I only understood 25% of it. I didn't understand 75%. So I put it aside. After that, I took another book, in that I understood 30%. Then I put it aside. In this way, I read 10-15 books. Then, then I would again start with that first book, after a year had passed. A year. And after a year passed, I would start with the first book again, and I would see that what I had understood 25% of, now I understood 50% of it. Whereas I wasn't studying grammar. But reading, and reading, and reading... such a reality comes to a person that he starts to understand. The main thing is to understand. The Urdu we read, the Urdu speeches we give, the Urdu sermons we deliver, we write letters and we write `majalis`, have we ever studied the grammar of Urdu? No, we haven't. So by reading and reading, we learn all these things. In the same way, Arabic, you learn it by reading. It starts to make sense.

But the `zer` and `zabar` (vowel markings) can make a big difference, the meaning changes. If we don't understand this...

Yes, yes. Basic grammar is necessary for Arabic. But to get into its intricacies, its alleys, to do it completely, that's not necessary. Yes, if someone wants to become a master, if someone wants to do research, if someone wants to write books, his situation is different. But for us, we have to read the books of the da'wah, understand them, and mend our own path to salvation. For that, this much is enough.

[Prayer in Arabic]

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 2 -->

As-salamu alaykum. As-salamu alaykum. Wa alaikum as-salam. Wa alaikum as-salam, wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuh. Wa alaikum as-salam. As-salamu alaykum, brothers. Wa alaikum as-salam. Shall I read the lesson? Yes.

Alright, from the 27th section, from its end, we will read.

A'udhu billahi minash shaitanir rajim. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim, wa bihi nasta'in, wa sallallahu 'ala rasulihi sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala ali Muhammadin, wa barik wa sallim.

Thumma inna kulla da'in (Then every caller) amara ahla jaziratihi (commanded the people of his island) an yatazawwaja kulla wahidin minhum al-untha (that each one of them should marry the female). And then, every caller commanded the people of his island that they should marry, each one of them, with a female, with that woman, allati takawwanat fi magharatin (who was formed in a cave) other than his own. That she who has been born, who has taken form, besides his own, besides his own clod of earth. Wa yatazawwaja min ghayrihi (and he should marry from other than his own) unthahu (his female). And he should marry from other than his, with that woman, allati zaharat min magharatihi (who appeared from his cave). And she who, from his clod of earth, who has been born, min fudlati ma'ihi (from the leftover of his water), from his own leftover water, that is, from his remaining water, wa hiya lahu ka-'l-ukhti (and she is like a sister to him), she is his sister. La yahillu lahu nikahuha (marrying her is not lawful for him). And to marry her is not permissible. Hadhihi tariq al-haqq (This is the path of truth) al-tahira (the pure one). Alright, this has been done for us, al-munazzah (the exalted one). Yes.

Alhamdulillah. Alhamdulillah. Alhamdulillah. Alhamdulillah. Alhamdulillah. From al-hamdu lillah.

Yes. Fa-'l-hamdu lillahi 'l-ladhi hadana li-dhalika (So praise be to Allah who guided us to this). So for Allah is all praise, who guided us to this matter. Wa ma kunna li-nahtadiya lawla an hadana Allah (And we would not have been guided if Allah had not guided us). If His guidance were not there, we could never have found guidance. Wa kana hadhihi 'l-wilada min al-ardi tilka 'l-daf'ah al-wahidah al-uwwala la ghayr (And this birth from the earth was that one single first instance, and no other). And the matter of guidance, Allah Ta'ala Himself told the Rasulullah, who is the greatest guide, the most exalted, that you cannot guide anyone. You cannot guide anyone. Whatever is related to guidance is from Us. Meaning, the Rasulullah wanted people to become Muslims from among them in his own lifetime. He tried very hard for that, and Allah Ta'ala also praised this in the Qur'an-i Majeed. But Allah said this: that you cannot guide anyone. The Being who grants guidance is Our Being. Yes. So guidance is a great thing. And you see that inside it, what subtlety is there? That just as the beginning verses of the Qur'an-i Majeed, that Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Alif. Lam. Mim. Dhalika 'l-kitabu la rayba fih (That is the Book, in which there is no doubt). That this is the book in which there is no doubt. Huda li-'l-muttaqin (a guidance for the God-fearing). It is guidance for the God-fearing. So the word huda is used. And the letters of ha, dal, ya—what is their numerical value? The letters that it has are 19. Ha has five. Ha has five. Dal has four. Five and four is nine. And ya has ten. So in this way, it becomes 19. And the Ahl al-Bayt al-Kiram, who they are, their letters are also 19. Muhammad has four. 'Ali has three, that's seven. Then Fatima has five, twelve. Then Hasan has three, twelve, thirteen, fourteen, fifteen. And Husayn has four. In this way, it becomes 19. Which is in correspondence with Bismillah. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim also has 19 letters. So the blessings that are in Bismillah, meaning, it is said that the blessing that has come into the Qur'an-i Majeed, it has come due to the beginning with Bismillah. In one sense. Meaning, Allah started this word, this speech, His own speech, with Bismillah. Therefore, inside it, this is… it has been called blessed, this book. In the same way, the Rasulullah said that you should start every act with Bismillah, so that your every act will be blessed. And this… and look, in this Surah al-Fatiha also, the virtues it has, in it, the one which is Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in (You alone we worship, and You alone we ask for help), right? And this is a phrase that is non-repeated. It has come only once in the Qur'an-i Majeed. And those that are non-repeated, great virtue is attached to them over the repeated ones. Just as in the muqatta'at. In the muqatta'at, there are many muqatta'at that are non-repeated. For example, Ya-Sin. It has come only once. Like Kaf-Ha-Ya-'Ayn-Sad. This muqatta' has come only once. In the same way, the muqatta'at that have been repeated, for example, Alif-Lam-Mim has come many times. Ha-Mim has come seven times. So in this way, the ones that are repeated are many. And the non-repeated ones are specific. And… so that non-repeated one has virtue over the repeated one. So Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in, this is a non-repeated phrase. And if you count its letters too, they will be 19. Yes. In the same way, the most beloved prayer, which is in Surah al-Fatiha, is ihdina 's-sirat al-mustaqim (Guide us to the straight path). That we, we recite this verse 51 times daily: ihdina 's-sirat al-mustaqim. That, "Guide us to the straight path." Yes. So its letters are also 19. Yes. And this, ihdina 's-sirat al-mustaqim, so which path is the invitation towards the straight path? What is the straight path? Its mention has been made in Surah al-An'am. Sirat al-mustaqim. Alladhina an'amta 'alayhim (Those upon whom You have bestowed favor). Min al-nabiyyina wa 's-siddiqina wa 'sh-shuhada'i wa 's-salihin, wa hasuna ula'ika rafiqa (Of the prophets, the truthful, the martyrs, and the righteous, and excellent are those as companions). Dhalika 'l-fadlu min Allah, wa kafa bi-'llahi 'alima (That is the grace from Allah, and sufficient is Allah as Knower). So this, which path is the straight path? For which we are seeking guidance. It is the path of the prophets, and the truthful, and the martyrs, and the righteous. To this path, "Guide us." And upon whom you have bestowed favor, have bestowed blessings.

And this birth was from the earth, which was one single instance. And this birth happened only once. Al-uwwala (the first one), which happened in the beginning of the cycle, the births that happened from the earth, from the clods, they happened only once in the entire cycle. Yes. Now when births happen, they will happen in the next cycle. In the beginning of the next cycle. La ghayra (no other). It did not happen besides this. Wa raja'a min al-tanasul bayna 'l-rijali wa 'l-nisa' (and it returned to procreation between men and women). And then the births after that returned to procreation. That they happen through men and women. Ilal-an (until now). Until today, the births that are happening, they are happening because of the union between women and men. Thumma jarat al-da'wa ila an istakhraja tilka 'l-shakhs al-fadil waladahu 'l-karim (Then the mission continued until that virtuous person brought forth his noble son). The birth that happened from the clods, and it has been mentioned to us that one time a man was born and one time a woman was born. Yes. Is it also possible that many men were born, many women were born from a single clod? No, from one clod, only one man. One man. Because, understand that if one man was born and one girl was born, then they became brother and sister. Understand that if two men were born, then they became brother-brother. No, from one clod, from the clod, from one person, a virtuous person was born. And after that, his sister, who was there, was born from his leftover water. From one clod, many individuals were not born. Only two were. One woman and one man. One man, yes. And the woman who was born was his sister. The clods that were made on Sarandib, was there just one clod? No, on Sarandib there was only one clod. I mean, there were many clods there. 28 clods were made, no? 28 clods were made. And among them, the most excellent one was the one of the juz'i ibda'iya. Yes.

Thumma jarat al-da'wa ila an istakhraja tilka 'l-shakhs al-fadil (Then the mission continued until that virtuous person brought forth...). Then the mission continued, until that virtuous person extracted, meaning, appointed, his noble son, from this noble mission, he appointed his noble son, alladhi yakhlufuhu fi maqamihi (who would succeed him in his station), so that he would become the successor of his station. Meaning, the successor of the station of the juz'i ibda'iya. Min hududihi wa ahli da'watihi (from his ranks and the people of his mission). From his ranks and from the people of his mission, he chose him. Yes. Kama nubayyinu ba'du an ba'd ba'da haqiqat al-ibtida' (As we will explain, one after another, after the reality of the beginning). And we will explain this in detail later. And this is the reality of the beginning. Musarrihan fi ghayri ramzin wa-la ishara (Explicitly, without symbol or indication). We have explained this explicitly, without symbol and without indication. Meaning, we have not explained it in a roundabout way, but have clearly explained how the condition of the beginning was, how the condition of the ibda' was. Yes. How much time would have passed between the hudud? They would have come out of the clods, it would have been four years... some time would have passed, then they would have gotten married, then a child would have been born, no? Yes. So the duration is not written anywhere, for how many years their life was. It is not written. And this is... Amir al-Mu'minin, one time... Amir al-Mu'minin said one time that a believer should know four things. That where... where did he come from? And where will he go? And how did he come? And how will he go? You said. Where did he come from? Meaning, from the spiritual world, his coming happened here. Through... in the episode of... he was included, and then he came to the earth. Where did he come from? And how... where will he go? We have to go to the spiritual world, by becoming an intellect. So how will our going be? This is a big process. And how did he come? Meaning, how did his coming happen here? The entire subject of mabda'. And how will he go? This is the subject of ma'ad. That how will our return be? Through the A'imma, through the Qawa'im, until the final one, who will be the Qa'im, who is called the Sultan in the Qur'an-i Majeed. So through him, from here, he will depart. Yes. So this is a very special saying of Amir al-Mu'minin. That you should know these four things.

Fa-khudh ma ataytuka wa-kun min al-shakirin (So take what I have given you and be among the grateful). So take what I have given you. And be among the grateful servants. Li-mawalina 'ala ma an'amu bihi 'alayna (To our masters for the favor they have bestowed upon us). And be among the grateful servants of our masters who have bestowed favor upon us. And you should thank them. Aqamahum Allahu bi-'l-jaza' (May Allah establish them with the reward). May Allah Ta'ala give them, meaning our mawali, their full reward. Faslun fi haqiqat al-ma'ad (A chapter on the reality of the return). Now, this is about mabda'. The mention of mabda' ends here. Now, they are speaking about ma'ad. In what way is the return happening? Faslun fi haqiqat al-ma'ad. So what is the reality of ma'ad? About that. I'lam annahu idha istajaba 'l-mustajib 'ala yadi haddin min al-hudud (Know that when the respondent responds at the hands of one of the hudud). That when the mustajib, mustajib means the one who is ready for the covenant. Now, they have taken the 'ahd-i sharif. Until now, they are Muslims in the houses of believers. Muslims are children. But we cannot say about anyone that, well, are your Muslims okay? Or if a believer's child dies, we cannot say this either, that your child was a Muslim and he passed away. These are all things that cause heartache. So we... those Muslims who are... children in the houses of believers, they are potential believers. They become actual believers at the time when they take the 'ahd-i sharif. Yes. So when a believer accepts at the hands of one of the hudud, from among any of the hudud... as is the method... ittasala bi-nafsihi 'inda tilawati 'l-'ahd al-sharif al-karim 'alayh (he connects with his soul at the time of the recitation of the noble, sacred covenant upon him). So, a point of light connects with his soul at the time of the recitation of the noble 'ahd. idha khalusat niyyatuhu (if his intention is pure) li-haddihi (for his hadd), for his hadd, with whom he is taking the 'ahd-i sharif, wa li-sahib al-zamanihi (and for the Master of his Time), and for his Sahib al-Zaman, meaning the Imam of the time, his intention is pure towards him, alladhi yad'uhu ilayhi salawatullah 'alayh (to whom he calls him, peace of Allah be upon him). Yes. So as... who... he is calling towards him, salawatullah 'alayh. Nuqtatun min nur (a point of light). A point of light... that point of light connects with him, with his soul. Yakunu mujawiran li-nafsihi (It is a neighbor to his soul). That point of light remains in proximity to his soul. Meaning, it stays in its neighborhood. The point of light that he has received due to the blessings of the 'ahd-i sharif, that point of light is in proximity to his soul. The sensual soul that he has, it is in proximity to his sensual soul. Ghayra mumazajatin (not mixing with it)... musharraqa (illuminating it), mujawhira laha (making it radiant). And it does not mix with his soul, meaning it does not merge. Until death. Yes, until death. It does not merge. It is its opposite. And that, it illuminates. And it makes it radiant. Who? That point of light illuminates and makes it radiant. Whom? His sensual soul. It wants the sensual soul to also become luminous like me. And also accept my word. As much as it accepts, it becomes a part of the natiqa soul. And that which it does not accept, it remains within the sensual. Yes. Only one part of the point of light is received? A small part is received. When he comes to every cycle, he will get... from that same point of light... it cannot be received in its entirety. No. And that too is a part of the portions of his repentance. The repentance he did in the spiritual world, a portion of that repentance is given. Yes. It will not be received in its entirety... from there it will end. No. It doesn't have that much power inside it... it doesn't have that much power that it can take it in its entirety, can bear it.

Wa 'ttisaluha bihi min qibali haddihi 'l-'ali (And its connection with him is from the side of his high-ranking hadd). And from the side of his high-ranking hadd, its connection happens. Meaning, he does not receive it directly, but the high-ranking hadd becomes his intermediary. This is the importance of the high-ranking hadd. Wa 'aslu dhalika min al-maddah wa 'l-ta'id al-sari (And the origin of that is from the substance and the flowing support). And that original substance and support which has flowed, min man la tujasiru nahwahu 'l-khawatir (from the one towards whom thoughts do not dare to venture). From his side, from whose side? That towards whom our hearts cannot dare to venture. Meaning, the Mubdi'-i Ta'ala (the Exalted Originator). From the side of the Mubdi'-i Ta'ala, he has received this point of light. How grand is the condition that is obtained by the mustajib, when he has just entered the 'ahd-i sharif. Look at the status of the Mubdi'-i Ta'ala, and the mustajib, a believer who has just entered. So how great a nobility he is receiving from the side of the Mubdi'-i Ta'ala. Yes. Then... ila 'l-'aql al-awwal al-sabiq (to the First Preceding Intellect). And to the First Intellect, that substance and support is issued. Thumma minhu ila sa'ir al-'uqul al-ibda'iya (Then from it to all the other originating intellects). And all the originating intellects, to them, that support and substance is issued. Thumma ila 'l-'ashir (Then to the Tenth). The tenth, the tenth intellect's... he is the administrator of the world of nature. Who? The 'Ashir. Thumma ila kulli maqamin (Then to every station). Then that substance and support comes to every Imam of the station. Kulli maqamin karimin fi waqtihi (Every noble station in its time), who is the Maqam al-Karim in his time. Min natiqin aw wasiyyin aw imamin (From a Natiq, or a Wasi, or an Imam). So he is either a Natiq, or a Wasi, or an Imam. Yes. So it comes to them. Thumma ila hududihi al-a'la (Then to his higher hudud). Then it comes to his higher hudud. Al-a'la thumma (The higher, then...). Then after the higher hudud, then the one who is higher than him, to him. Ila man yalihi (To the one who follows him). Meaning, up to... the hudud who are under him, to them. Ila an yattasila ila 'l-mustajib (Until it connects to the respondent). Until it reaches the mustajib. Al-sadiq al-niyyah (the one with sincere intention), who is sincere in his intention. Wa 's-sahih al-tawiyyah (and sound in his nature), who in his nature and his actions is correct. Fa-qistuhu min dhalika (His portion from that). So his portion comes from there. 'Ala qadri istihqaqihi (According to the measure of his worthiness). According to his worthiness, that portion is given to him. Because those who take the 'ahd-i sharif have different intentions. The intentions they have, they will get things accordingly. Now he came and sat down, that, "Well, my father is sitting, I should also sit." So there are such people too. So they come and sit. And they think that we too have received our portion, we too have become honored. But there is a big difference inside them. The nuqta-i nur which is received in the 'ahd-i sharif, it is received directly, isn't it? No, there is no intermediary for it? No, all these are intermediaries. The intermediary... at that time, his hadd-i 'ali has not even been made. Where? His hadd-i 'ali will be made when the 'ahd-i sharif happens, no? No, I mean, after the 'ahd-i sharif he got it, right? He is getting the nuqta-i nur after the 'ahd-i sharif. Yes. So up to here, from the Mubdi'-i Ta'ala, through all these means, he gets the nuqta-i nur through his hadd-i 'ali. Yes.

And that too according to his worthiness. As much as his worthiness is, according to that. Fa-kullama zada (And whenever it increases)... and as much as he increases... al-mustajibu min a'mal al-khayr (the respondent, from the deeds of good). The respondent who does good deeds in his life, min iqamat al-a'mal al-shar'iya (from establishing the works of the shari'ah), that the works of the shari'ah, with their establishment, meaning the seven pillars, with their adherence, wa 'l-muwadhabah 'alayha (and being constant in them), and being fully constant in them, wa 'stifadat al-'ulum (and benefiting from the sciences) min al-hudud al-malakutiya (from the celestial hudud), and benefits from the sciences, that the celestial sciences, from them he is benefiting from the sciences. Wa imad dhalika 'l-nuqta 'ala 's-suni'a (And the support of that point is on the sunnah). And the entire foundation is on his sincerity of intention. Fi muwalat al-mawali (In loyalty to the mawali). The one he has... with his mawali, with his A'imma, with his awliya' Allah, the intention he has. Sidq al-niyyah (sincerity of intention). Wa sidq al-tawiyyah (and sincerity of nature), wa 'l-i'tiraf li-kulli haddin bi-fadlihi (and acknowledging every hadd for his virtue), his good nature, and acknowledging every hadd, recognizing their virtue in his station of wahdaniyat (oneness), that their station is the station of wahdaniyat. Fa-kullama jadda fi jami'i dhalika (And whenever he strives in all of that). As much as he strives and works hard in good deeds and in the establishment of the works of shari'ah, and in benefiting from the sciences, as much as his effort is, then that... man jadda wajada (he who strives, finds). As he has strived, he has found. So this... zaliya kaburat tilka 'l-nuqtah (that point of light grew larger). So inside that point, it starts to grow. The point of light that he had received from the 'ahd-i sharif. Wa-zada 'shraquha (and its illumination increased) wa diya'uha li-'l-nafs (and its radiance for the soul). And its illumination increases. And its radiance, for his soul. Wa tasfiyah (and purification) wa sayqal (and polishing). And his purification and his polishing, polishing means to polish, to make it shiny, to polish utensils, so... 'ala qadri ma yaktasibu min al-khayrat (according to the measure of what he earns of good deeds). According to the good deeds he earns. Takunu suratuhu tilka (His form will be like that). His form keeps getting better. Li-annaha a'malun saliha (Because they are righteous deeds). Because these are all righteous deeds. Kama qala Ta'ala: man 'amila salihan fa-li-nafsihi (As the Exalted said: Whoever does righteousness, it is for his own soul). The one who does a good deed, it is for his own soul. Wa man asa'a fa-'alayha (and whoever does evil, it is against it). And the one who does evil, it is against his own soul. Yes. Meaning, look, that man 'amila salihan fa-li-nafsihi, the one who does a good deed, the person, in his life, all the things that have been told, it is for his own soul. Meaning, for the progress of his own soul. Yes. And this also... look that the lowest illumination that happens, there are several types of illumination. So the lowest illumination that happens to a believer, is the illumination of his own point of light upon his sensual soul and his body. The very least illumination. But it is an illumination. The very least illumination that happens is that of the point of light upon his sensual soul. Yes. He feels it, no? No, he doesn't feel it. Because the beauty of the natiqa soul is that it is so secretive that it does not inform its closest neighbor, the sensual soul. Yes. The one we call conscience in English, that is there, I mean... he doesn't feel it... we feel the conscience. Yes.

Wa man asa'a fa-'alayha. In my book, in Mudalaliya, it was mentioned, right? That Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawad al-Qusu, he had reached a very old age, but your eyesight was completely fine, and your jaws were completely fine, and your face was so radiant that it always remained luminous, and people used to feel the luminosity of your face. So it was asked, "What was the reason for this?" So they said that at the time of a believer's death, when only the last breath remains in him, meaning he is just about to die, at that time, he is told from the side of the divine power that if you want... his place in paradise is shown to him. At such a time. That this is your place in paradise. So he, upon seeing his place, feels extreme joy and happiness. And because of that, light spreads on his face, because of the joy. So that state, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawad had for his entire life. Which a believer gets at the time of his death, that state, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawad had it for his entire life, due to the services he rendered. And he rendered such magnificent services that no one could. The way you saved the Da'wah-i Hadiya. And for which you were called Ghadanfar. And you have great titles. And because of this, in your offspring, until the Day of Judgment, the posts of du'at have been established, illa ma sha' Allah. Who were not in your... only four du'at were such who are not from your offspring. Otherwise, all the du'at, even today's Da'i, the Caliph of the Da'i, all are from your offspring. From your lineage. So this state was obtained by Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawad. Yes. And Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman, look, how... how amazing a thing it is. Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman, at that time, when this chain of du'at was not even established, but at that very time, he had said that the du'at will be from the lineage of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawad until Qiyamah. This is what Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman had said. At that time. You can imagine how magnificent a thing it is. The latest Da'i, the current Da'i... when they had become Da'i... after the death of Sayyidna Ja'far, in 1150. So for about 35... 38 years you were the Da'i. So in that period, the actions you performed were strange and wonderful, your affairs. Yes. And the books you wrote, the books you wrote on haqa'iq, and the one which is Shu'a' al-Anwar, which is... they say this, that Shu'a' al-Anwar is not in haqa'iq, but in daqa'iq. You yourself have mentioned it. And it is a magnificent book. Which the du'at before him had not opened such subjects that you opened. Yes. And Anvar al-Shashaniya, which is your book, it is an extremely difficult and magnificent book. Yes.

And it happened once that you were holding a majlis, Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman. So while holding the majlis, you suddenly stood up. You said, "Please come. You have bestowed a great favor. You have been gracious to me. And you have been gracious to our companions that you have graced my gathering." So this became a strange and wonderful state, right? So later, people asked you, "What was this matter?" So he said, "Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan and Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawad had come." Because of which this was my state. So how many affairs happened with you. Yes. And how much certainty there was. One gentleman gave a feast, and he was... a walima was his, and in the walima, he had invited a few people. So from some other village, his in-laws were. So the father, he invited a few people from that village, his relatives were. So they came running to Sayyidna Ali. So he said, "I had invited a few people, but here the whole... the whole village has come. And I haven't cooked that much food. So I am very worried, what should I do?" So you said, "Go, lift that pot, lift the lid of the pot, and blow Sayyidna Sulayman's name into it. And say, 'Ya Sayyidna Sulayman, aghithni!' (O Sayyidna Sulayman, help me!)" So he did the same. And that... the entire village... the several hundred people who had come, they all ate their fill from that same food, and not only that, but food was left over as well. So this is the state that happens. With the kalam of the awliya' Allah, and the blessings of their kalam. Yes. It happened like this in the time of the Rasul too, no? Yes, in the time of the Rasul it happened. Their state is very high. This quality was created in the du'at, so what would be the quality of the Rasulullah?

One time, you... Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman... someone gave a rupee to him. And one rupee had a great value in those days. In one rupee, a goat would come. Which you have also mentioned, that one... one rupee... a goat is coming for one rupee. Prices have increased so much that a goat has started coming for one rupee. Yes. So anyway, someone gave you one rupee as a gift. So you took it and kept it. Then he... later you started looking for it, under the pillow, looked around. That... you didn't find it. So in your mind, that thought remained that someone gave me a rupee as a gift, and where did I put it? So he remained in this thought. Then when it was night, your habit was that you would go and close the main gate yourself. So... you were in Dar al-Taseer, which was the house of Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman. So he went down, and there were a few steps, he went down and closed the main gate and started climbing up. So you saw that there was some burden on your head. You felt a burden, felt a weight. So you put your hand on your head and saw that the rupee was present there. You can imagine that what a strange and wonderful thing happened. A sahib wrote your mojizaat, that how the mojizaat... you have... one time, it happened that of Rajab or some month, it was the first night. So you... what did you do? Before that, one... one night before that, in the afternoon or evening, some person came and he said that, "You should clothe me, I don't have any clothes." So you gave him one garment. Then you thought that tonight... at that time you were in Dar al-Taseer and you were in taqiyya. So tonight, it is the first night, people will come to meet me, so what will I wear? I don't have any proper clothes. People used to come the next morning to meet. So I don't have any proper clothes, so what will I do? In this thought, you fell asleep. While thinking this. So late at night, someone knocked on the door. And knocked. So then you went and opened the door, so your... the time is up.

The door was knocked on. You... someone knocked on the door. So you went and opened the door. And you saw a believing brother, and with him there was some luggage as well. So...

So he said, "Mawlana, you know that I am a merchant. And for the purpose of trade, I had gone across the sea. Whatever his portion was. So he had gone. And on the way, a storm came. And I was in such a state that I would have drowned. And my wealth would also have been lost. So at that time, I made a vow that when I reach the shore safely, I will present a bundle of 500 garments in your service." So he brought it. So he, the bundle, placed it in your house. And left. You thanked him. Then you saw that in it there were garments, robes and such. So then you... at that time you raised your hands. It is said that you raised your hands and said, "O Allah, be a witness that I was not miserly in giving. I, to whom I gave, I was not miserly in that. And you too did not show any lack in your grace. Meaning, I was not miserly in giving, and your grace is also matchless, that you were not miserly in giving, and your grace has increased." Yes. So these were the states that the awliya' Allah had. And of which... how many there must have been, of which the mention... some, some have been mentioned by their hudud. Otherwise, there must have been so many such states of the du'at, who are the awliya' Allah. Yes. A questioner came and he was bothering a lot, so you rebuked him and he died the next day. Yes, that one... it happened that... you reminded me. His state was this that Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman used to sleep very little. And his routine was that... the hadd who wrote the book, he has mentioned the routine in it. That he would pray Maghrib, and after the Maghrib prayer, you would rest. For an hour and a half or two. He would rest for an hour and a half or two. And then after that, he would pray 'Isha. And then eat food. Then after eating, he would eat a light meal. And the whole night, you would stay awake. And you would write, you would read, and you would contemplate. Until the time of dawn came. After Fajr, you would not sleep. Until the time of Zuhr came. And you would be busy with all the affairs of the da'wah. And after Zuhr and 'Asr, you would not sleep until Maghrib. So whatever... sleep you would get would be for an hour, two hours. This was your state. And this was your state for your entire life. So in that two hours, two and a half hours that you would get, in that, it was that you had told your hajib, who used to take care of you, you had told him that at this time, don't wake me up. Whoever comes, don't wake me up. And tell them that I am sleeping, I am resting. So from the village, two people came. And they said that we want to meet Sayyidna. So he, the hajib, said that, "Look, this is his time to sleep. And he rests very little. And at this time, I have been told that I should not let him meet anyone. No one should come to meet me." But they were insistent on their point, that, "No, we have come from very far, we will only go after meeting." So in this conversation, Sayyidna's eyes opened. So he asked, "Who is it?" So he, the hajib, said that, "Mawlana, I tried to stop them a lot, but they are insistent on meeting you. And they... want to meet you." So he said, "Send them inside." So you were in a state of jalal. Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman was in a state of jalal. So he said, "What is the matter? For what purpose have you come?" So one of them said that, "We want that we have come for du'a, that you should pray for us." So you said, "Should I pray for your death or for your life?" You said. So that one person, he said that, "I hold life dear. I do not want death." So he, after taking the du'a, left. There was another person. So you asked him, "If you... what do you want?" So he said, "Pray for my death." "If death has to come, and it comes through your du'a, then what could be better?" So you said, "Look, if you want me to pray for your death, then you will not be able to see the coming Friday." You said. "You will not be able to see the coming Friday."
