And the author of the book writes that this is exactly what happened, that he died before the coming Friday, on Wednesday. And we performed his funeral prayer. Yes. So these states were strange and wonderful states. He said, "I have invited a few people."

Allahumma salli 'ala Muhammadin wa Ali Muhammad.

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 3 -->

They are present in the mosques. Masha’Allah. Subhan Allah. [unclear] in the two islands [unclear] is present. From the *Sadaat* (descendants of the Prophet), [unclear] *ahsan lil-nas al-akharin* (better for the other people). Its places are very distant. In Munba'ith also, the places are distant. It is farther than the airport. Farther than the airport. The middle of the Da'wah is farther than the airport. [They] reside farther from the airport. It is not possible for them to come every time. Mosques are present. Except for this, the mosque is this. And the mosques... In other cities, mosques are present. And people are present there. The worshipers. *[Phone rings]* Hello. As-salamu 'alaykum wa rahmatullah. Zoom, Zoom. Zoom, Zoom. As-salamu 'alaykum. As-salamu 'alaykum. Yes. At this time, with us, guests from Makkah al-Mukarramah have also come. They are present in our gathering. Sayyidna… Hasan… Hasan… Hasan Muhammad Qurayshah. Hasan Muhammad Qurayshah. So, they are conveying their salams to all of you. They are conveying salams to you. Wa 'alaykum al-salam. Yes, yes. Wa 'alaykum al-salam. Wa 'alaykum al-salam. *A'udhu billahi min al-shaytan al-rajim* (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim* (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). *Wa bihi nasta'in, salla Allahu 'ala rasulihi Sayyidna Muhammadin wa 'ala ali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim* (And in Him we seek help, may Allah's blessings be upon His messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon the family of Muhammad, and may He bless and grant peace). *Thumma la yazalu tilka al-surah tantaqilu ma'a kulli haddin 'inda naqlatihi ila man huwa a'la minhu* (Then that *surah* continues to transfer with every *hadd* at the time of his transfer to the one who is higher than him). Then, the matter of the *surah* that was mentioned, that the *surah* of the *mustajib* (respondent) that is formed at the time of passing away, that… it keeps transferring from every *hadd* along with his passing away, to the one who is a higher *hadd* than him. For example, it went to one *hadd*. Then after that, a higher *hadd* comes. So... it... from there it is... goes there. Until, *ay yantahiya ila Bab al-Maqam 'alayhi afdal al-salat wa al-salam* (until it reaches the Bab al-Maqam, upon him be the best of prayers and peace). Until that *surah sharif* progresses and progresses and reaches the *Bab al-Maqam*. The *Bab* of the Imam of the Time, who is the greatest servant of the Da'wah. Because he has performed a great service, in that the collection of all *suwar* (plural of *surah*) happens with him. Just as in the celestial spheres, with the *falak al-buruj* (sphere of the constellations), all the stars and all the celestial bodies and all that we can see and cannot see, the unseen and the seen, the collection of all of them happens with the *falak al-buruj*. So in the same way, here in the world, it is with the *Bab*. It is not with the *Imam*. No *surah* goes to the *Imam*. Just as the *falak al-muhit* (the encompassing sphere) is *bari'* (free/exempt), and no *surah* goes to it, in the same way, no *surah* goes to the *Imam*. And whatever *surah* is going, it is going to the *Bab al-Abwab*. Just as we send *durud* and *salam* (blessings and peace) upon the *Imam*, we also send *durud* and *salam* upon them, who is the *Bab*, who is the *fatihu al-kitab* (opener of the book). *wa yasiru 'indahu... wa taridu 'alayhi suwar min jami' al-jaza'ir al-ardi kulliha* (And it proceeds to him... and the *suwar* from all the islands of the earth, all of them, come to him). And it reaches him, and all the *suwar* (forms/images) from all the *jaza'ir* (islands) of the earth come to him. Until slowly, slowly, the *jaza'ir*—all the *jaza'ir* that are there, the 12 *jaza'ir*—the collection of the *suwar* of the 12 *jaza'ir* gathers at that *Bab*. *fa-yajtami'u dhalika 'indahu haykalun nuraniyyun* (And so a luminous temple/structure is gathered at his station). Until, a *haykal nurani* (luminous structure) is formed at his station. A *haykal nurani* is prepared for him. *'ala surati insan* (in the form of a human). And the *haykal nurani* is like this, that it is in a human form. Just as it has a head, hands, feet, a body, in that form that *haykal* is prepared. Yes. *illa annaha nurun kulluha* (except that it is entirely light). That that *haykal* is entirely luminous. *law badat lil-bashar la-dhashat... lam taqdir 'ala idrakiha...* (If it were to appear to mortals, it would dazzle... they would not be able to perceive it...). If one were to want to see, a mortal, meaning human eyes, its reality, what this *haykal* is like, then they cannot perceive it. *bal yadhhabu biha* (rather it would take away [their sight]). Rather, their eyes would be lost. *wa yantafi'uha* (and it would extinguish them). And their eyes would be blinded. As, *kama anna nur al-shams la yaqduru al-basar 'ala al-nazar ilayhi fi 'ayn al-shams* (just as the light of the sun, the sight is not able to look at it in the very eye of the sun). That just as the light of the sun, we cannot see it with our eyes. Through our sight. So in the same way, this *haykal sharif*, its witnessing cannot be done with the eyes. *wa huwa nurun jismaniyyun* (and it is a physical light). And that is a physical light. *fa-kayfa bil-nur al-ruhani* (so how about the spiritual light?). Whereas the sun is a physical light, and this light is a *nur ruhani* (spiritual light), *qudsani* (holy), yes, *azali* (pre-eternal). Yes. Because pre-eternity has come into it. It is preparing for... to enter the *haykal sharif* and go to the *farzand* (child/son) of the *Imam*, and in this way, it will progress and *yawman ma* (one day) it will become *'aql* (intellect). *fa-la tazalu al-suwar al-sharifah tantaqilu* (So the noble *suwar* continue to transfer...). In this way, the *suwar sharifah* keep transferring, they keep passing from one higher rank to a second higher rank, and from the second higher rank to a third higher rank, until they reach the *Bab al-Abwab*. And after reaching the *Bab al-Abwab*, they become a *haykal*. The collection of all of them happens.

So, in the *haykal*, when the... formation is happening, in the *haykal*, until it is complete, the *Imam* does not give *imamat*...

Yes, until the *haykal*... when the formation happens in the *haykal*, when... it becomes complete. When the *suwar* become ripe, when the *suwar* become ripe, and they gather in the *haykal*, then the formation of the *haykal* happens. It is possible that besides it, there are also *suwar* that have remained behind in the *jaza'ir* (islands), but that quality has not been produced in them. So they remain with the *hudud daniya* (lower-ranking *hudud*). But from the *hudud 'aliya* (higher-ranking *hudud*), they transferred to the *Bab*, and at the *Bab*, a *haykal* was formed. It has become ready. Ready for the *Imam* to "throw" it. So this is the matter. It's possible that besides it, many *suwar* remain behind. And it also happens that the very sinful momineen, who... have done forbidden acts, and who have not repented, and have not sought forgiveness, so what happens to them? They are held back. That the next *Imam* will come and absorb them. And the next Imam... so one Imam after another Imam passes away, until their progress does not happen. This also happens. Yes. *Fa-la tazalu al-suwar al-sharifah tantaqilu wa taridu ila al-Bab al-karim* (So the noble *suwar* continue to transfer and arrive at the noble *Bab*). Until that…

*Bab al-Abwab... lazim lahu an yantaqil jismaniyyan... li-dukhul haykal al-Imam?* (The Bab al-Abwab... is it necessary for him to transfer physically... for the entry of the Imam's haykal?)

*Lazim 'alayhi an yamut...* (It is necessary for him to die...).

*La yamut awwalan... 'inda wafaati Imam al-Zaman, ya'khudhu al-haykal... haykalan min 'indahu, huwa yu'ti... al-walad... al-dar... waladuhu huwa... hadha Bab al-Abwab yakunu lil-walad al-qalb.* (He does not die first... at the passing of the Imam of the Time, he takes the haykal... a haykal from him, he gives... the son... the station... his son, he... this Bab al-Abwab becomes the heart for the son.)

Yes.

*Fi haykalihi... fi haykalin... walad. Ya'ni kan walad... kan walad... fi hayati walidihi imam... li-anna kan huwa al-Nasut. Wa lakin lamma yantaqilu al-haykal bi-idhni al-Imam 'alayhi al-salam ilayhi... hadha al-haykal al-imamah. Lamma yajtami'u al-Imam wal-imamah, yakunu walad al-Imam... bil-quwwah, bil-fi'l.* (In his haykal... in a haykal... a son. Meaning, he was a son... he was a son... in the life of his father, an Imam... because he was the Nasut. But when the haykal transfers, by the permission of the Imam, peace be upon him, to him... this haykal is the Imamah. When the Imam and the Imamah unite, the son of the Imam becomes [Imam]... from being [Imam] in potentiality, to [Imam] in actuality.)

So our question is, is it necessary for the *Bab al-Abwab* to also transfer physically?

Physically as well, yes. As... at the time of the passing of Mawlana Abu Talib ('alayhi al-salam), who was the *Bab al-Abwab* for Mawlatina Khadijah bint Khuwaylid. So she transferred at the time of Mawlana Abu Talib. After three days, Mawlana Abu Talib ('alayhi al-salam) transferred. Yes. Like this. *wa suratun fi 'alaqatin ma yara al-mudabbir* (and a form in a connection as the Director sees fit). And they are arranged as the *Mudabbir* (Director) wants. *Innama tastaqirru 'ala qadri iktisabiha fil-dunya* (It only settles according to what it earned in the world). And this *surah*... its settlement is upon its earning, which it had done in the world. For instance, the good deeds it had performed, the righteous acts it had done, according to that, its place is there. *Fa-minhum man yakunu fi mawda' al-qalb* (Among them are those who are in the position of the heart). And among those *suwar naiyirah* (luminous forms), which have gathered at the *Bab al-Abwab*, among them are those who are in the position of the heart. Meaning, their own... the righteous deeds they have done, the acts of piety they have performed, according to that, they come to the position of the heart. *wa minhum man yakunu fil-dimagh* (and some of them are in the position of the brain). And some of them are... those *suwar naiyirah* are in the position of the brain. *wa minhum fi mawda' al-'ayn* (and some of them are in the position of the eye). And some in the position of the eye. *wa al-udhun* (and the ear). And some, the ear. *wa al-yad* (and the hand). In the position of the hand. *wa al-rijl* (and the foot). Some in the position of the foot. *wa minhum fi manzilati al-sha'r* (and some of them are in the station of the hair). And some in the position of the hair. *wa al-zufur* (and the nail). The nail. They also come to the position of the nail. *wa asfal al-rijl* (and the sole of the foot). And in the soles of the feet, they also come to that position. *kullun bi-qadri 'amalihi* (each according to his deed). Everything is according to its measure, according to the measure of the deed. Now, those hair that are trimmed, nails that are cut, they come for a time, because of their good deeds, and then they are cast out. Yes. They are also such *suwar*. So... their... because of the blessings of the *haykal sharif*, they enter, but they are cast out. Some are like this as well. *kullun bi-qadri 'amalihi* (each according to his deed). And every... every person, according to the measure of his deed, attains a position in the *haykal*. *wa istiqraruhu la zulma 'alayh* (and in his settlement, there is no injustice upon him). And his settlement is such that no injustice is done to anyone. The position he is to be given, the *Mudabbir Ta'ala* (Director, the Exalted) gives him that position. *la mu'aqabatan lahu bal bi-qadri ma iktasaba* (not as a punishment for him, but according to what he earned). As his efforts are, according to that, his position is. *fa-la tazalu taridu 'alayhi wa tajtami'u 'indahu ila an yakmula 'indahu haykalun nuraniyyun* (so they continue to arrive to him and gather at his station until a luminous haykal is completed at his station). Until, those *suwar* keep arriving, and a *haykal* is formed from them, until a complete *haykal nurani*, a holy luminous one, becomes complete. *wa li-dhalika summiya al-Bab* (and for that reason he was named the Bab). For this reason, he is called the *Bab*. *li-anna dhalika al-haykal alladhi 'indahu al-Bab majmu'ah... 'uqulan...* (because that haykal which is with him, the Bab, is a collection of... intellects...). *li-anna al-'aql huwa al-lubb* (because the intellect is the essence). Because the *'aql* (intellect) is the name of the *lubb* (essence). Meaning, it is the name of the summary. Meaning, the summary of our life is our *surah sharif*. So that becomes the very essence of the essence of our life. So for this reason, this... the *Bab* is what? He is the *jami' al-albab* (gatherer of the essences). He becomes the summary of all. The summary of all summaries becomes his gatherer. *Wa-'lam anna hadha al-haykal huwa al-musamma bil-imamah* (And know that this *haykal* is what is named the Imamah). And know this matter, that this *haykal* is named *Imamah*. So when the *Imam* meets the *Imamah*, the *farzand-i Imam* (son of the Imam), who is the *walad-i Imam* (child of the Imam), becomes [Imam] *bil-fi'l* (in actuality) from *bil-quwwah* (in potentiality). And then the *Imam* passes away. *Faslun fi kayfiyyati husul al-imam alladhi yanzilu bihi al-imamah* (A chapter on the nature of the arrival of the Imam upon whom the Imamah descends). A chapter on the reality of the *Imam's* arrival, for whom the *Imamah* descends. In what way is it attained by him? *'Ilam anna kulla mu'minin... idha faraqat nafsuhu jasadahu... kama zakarna... baqiya fi jasadihi atharun min al-nafs al-namiyyah.* (Know that every believer... when his soul separates from his body... as we have mentioned... there remains in his body traces from the vegetative soul.) That every... every believer, when his soul separates from his body, his *nafs hissiyyah* (sensory soul) departs from his body, as we have mentioned, so what happens? Traces of the *nafs namiyyah* (vegetative soul) remain in his body. The three souls that there are: *namiyyah*, *hissiyyah*, and *natiqah*. So when the passing away happens, all the souls leave their place, meaning, they leave the body. In that body, that which is the *namiyyah*, its traces remain in the body. Its... *wa yusamma al-hararat al-ghariziyyah wa dhalika al-musamma al-riyah* (and it is called the innate heat, and that is what is called the riyah/winds). That is called the innate heat. And these are also called the *riyah*. And these *riyah* exit... they begin to exit immediately after passing away, but their complete exit takes three days. Three days until they continue to exit, but completely, they exit after three days. The *riyah* that exit at the time of passing away, before the burial and shrouding, they are of a higher status, they are superior. And from them, the *hudud 'ulyawiyyah* (higher-ranking *hudud*) of the *Imam* are created. And those that... after the burial and shrouding, the *riyah* that exit, they are of a lower status compared to the first ones. And they are given the light of the moon. And from them, the *jism* (body) of the *Imam* is formed, *yawman ma* (one day). After being purified. They are taken to the moon, and to Jupiter, and to the sun, where their purification happens. And after the purification happens, and then they come from there. It has been said that 360 years in going and 360 years in coming, that is, 720 years they remain there, and then 280 years for their temperament to be moderated. Until they... become moderate in their temperament. Until they... become... a thousand years after that, whether it is a believer or a kafir, whether a munafiq or a Muslim, for all of them, the rule is the same, that a thousand years after the passing away, there is *raj'at* (return). From there, this reality exists. Of course, this happens that the believer's... souls, *namiyyah*, *hissiyyah*, and *natiqah*, they become united and become the *surah sharif*. And... the one who is a kafir, a mushrik, a murtadd, his *natiqah* is not formed. So the *namiyyah* and *hissiyyah* become manifest in his body. And this is his most base form, that he... whom Allah had given such honor that he came to the station of the soul, but because of his actions, his downfall was such that from his station... and for this reason, after dying, those people feel the punishment. Their... those divine punishments that are there, they are felt by them.

Everyone, everyone comes after a thousand years?

Yes.

Everyone comes after a thousand years. So for the believer, I understand the believer's... he will come back into a human [form]. But suppose, if someone who is a kafir, a mushrik, and he has gone to the *darkat* (lower realms), so will he... from the *darkat*, he will come back after a thousand years?

Yes, in the *darkat*... he will come.

In the *darkat*. The one who has a *tasawwur khabith* (foul conception), he comes to *rah-i sutur-i janam* (the path of the covers of hell). Yes.

But those who go to the *darkat*, I mean... a human being who has committed sin, is a mushrik...

Yes.

...so he will go to the *darkah*.

Yes.

So when he comes after a thousand years, it is not necessary that he will come in a human form...

The *dawat* will be presented, the *dawat* will be presented. If he persists, then...

Yes, yes.

And the enemies of the *azdad* (opposites)... for the one whom... for example, the enemies, for them, when the *Qa'im* comes, the *Qa'im* will come. In the time of the *Qa'im*, these mushrikeen and... will come in physical form, and for them, the punishments of the *azdad* are... they will be resurrected in a physical state. And the *Imam*... they will see the *Imam*... from that era, they will see the *Qa'im* in the form of the *Imam* of their time. For example, if it is Yazid, then when he sees the *Qa'im*, Imam Husayn will appear to him. Yes. For example, if it is Mu'awiyah, and he sees the *Qa'im*, then Janab 'Ali, 'alayhi al-salam, will appear to him. That's how it is. And then Imam 'alayhi al-salam will give him punishment and...

This is about the *azdad*, the enemies. But those who are other than believers, they... their imams will also be different?

Yes.

So they will be raised with their own imams in the time of the *Qa'im*?

Yes, yes. *A'immah al-dalalah* (Imams of misguidance), the Quran has mentioned, no? *ya'ti kullu...* (every... will come). The Quran has mentioned the *A'immah al-dalalah*. So with them, their... there will be... it could happen. *...wa takhruju fil-yawm al-thalith* (...and it exits on the third day). And on the third day, its exit is completely... happens. Which one? The *nufus rihiyyah* (windy souls). And for this reason, we read *Sadaq Allah*. For three days... the *riyaah* exit for three days. Therefore, during those three days, that which... is done, prayers are offered, charity is given, alms are given, or a vow is made for good deeds, because what they will do will be for his body. Later, the Fatiha gatherings they will hold, or read the Quran, etc., it will not be for his body. It will... but within three days, this has gone away. And it went to its place. But what was done within three days, the good deeds that were done, he benefits greatly from them. Yes.

This is that in the Quran, it says that every group will be raised with their Imam. So these... the mushrikeen, their imamat is with the mushrikeen? So will they be gathered like that?

Our belief is that our Imam is the true Imam, we have gone with our Imam. Those who are other than believers...

Their imams will be different.

...their imams will be different.

So will they be raised with their imams in the time of the *Qa'im*?

Yes, yes.

No matter in what form.

The *Qa'im* will come, the *Qa'im* will come. In the time of the *Qa'im*, these mushrikeen...

*thumma yas'adu bukharan latifan la tudrikuhu al-nazar* (then it ascends as a subtle vapor which the sight cannot perceive). And then it ascends as a subtle vapor, which the eye cannot see. *wa talaqqahu ashi'at al-qamar* (and the rays of the moon receive it). And the rays of the moon meet with it. *thumma yas'adu ila al-shams* (then it ascends to the sun). Then it ascends towards the sun. *fa-yusallimuhu bi-wasitati al-'utarud wa al-zuhrah* (and it delivers it through the medium of Mercury and Venus). And then it... through the medium of Mercury and Venus, it... the sun sends it towards the moon. *wa yuqimu 'indaha ma sha' al-mudabbir* (and it resides there as long as the Director wills). And they remain there as long as the *Mudabbir* (Director) wills. *wa tusallimuhu ila al-mushtari* (and it delivers it to Jupiter). And then it sends it towards Jupiter. *wa taridu 'alayhi al-nufus al-rihiyyah kadhalika min jami' al-jaza'ir* (and the windy souls likewise arrive to it from all the islands). And in this way, from all the *jaza'ir* (islands), the *nufus rihiyyah* (windy souls), their arrival happens. Meaning, at the moon, and this... Jupiter, and sun, etc. *wa al-shamsu wa al-mushtari yuqassiran dhalika al-riyah* (and the sun and Jupiter shorten... that wind). And the sun and Jupiter, they purify it. They completely refine it. *tasfiyatan... ila an takhlus al-suwar* (a purification... until the *suwar* are purified). They purify it, until their *suwar* become complete. *allati fil-Bab* (which are at the Bab). Meaning, this is the talk of the *riyaah*, but when the *suwar* which are at the *Bab*, when they become complete, so in that period, their purification keeps happening. The *riyaah's*. The passing away of the believer has already happened. So after the passing away, his *riyaah* first left. Now, slowly, slowly, it has reached the *Bab*. So in this intervening period, its purification has happened. And here, along with the purification, with it, the completion of the *suwar* has happened at the *Bab*. The completion of the *suwar* has happened. *wa takmalu hadhihi al-nafs al-rihiyyah 'inda al-shams* (and this windy soul is completed at the sun). And at the sun, these *nufus rihiyyah* (windy souls), their completion happens. Meaning, the purification happens, all the flaws have come out. *thumma idha azina al-mudabbir ta'ala bi-zuhur al-shakhs al-imami* (Then when the Director, may He be exalted, permits the appearance of the Imamic personage). And when the *Mudabbir Ta'ala* wills for the appearance of the *shakhs imami* (Imamic personage), meaning, the son of the *Imam*. The appearance of the son of the *Imam* should happen. So... *harraka al-shamsu... fa-sallamat tilka al-nufus al-rihiyyah ila al-qamar* (The sun moves... and delivers those windy souls to the moon). Then the sun gives it to... delivers those *nufus rihiyyah* to the moon. Because in the sun, they have become completely pure. Now, that... its main station is the moon. *bi-wasitati al-zuhrah wa al-'utarud* (through the medium of Venus and Mercury). Through Zuhrah (Venus) and 'Utarud (Mercury), it sends it. *thumma anna al-qamar yursilu dhalika bil-shu'a'in imma ila ma'in tahirin aw imma ila fakihatin sharifatin* (Then the moon sends that with its rays either to pure water or to a noble fruit). Then what happens is that the moon sends it towards... with its rays, either to pure water or to a noble fruit. Meaning, those things descend. The *riyaah* that have become completely pure, they descend to the earth. In what way? They descend onto pure water or onto a noble fruit. *yasiru fi qatar al-shabnam* (it proceeds in a drop of dew). It happens that they come in drops of dew. *wa ya'khudhu al-mudabbir ta'ala dhalika... fa-la yanaluhu ahadun ila an tasila ila al-imam 'alayhi afdal al-salat wa al-salam* (And the Director, may He be exalted, takes that... and no one can attain it until it reaches the Imam, upon him be the best of prayers and peace). And no one can attain it. Meaning, no other fruit or water which... the pure water that has descended, no person can attain it. But it reaches the *Imam*, upon whom be the best of prayers and peace. *fa-ya'kulu dhalika* (so he eats that). Then the *Imam*... eats it. For example, as a noble fruit, say an apple. The apple on which it descended... So, as the Prophet said, that an apple came from Allah's side, I ate half and Khadijah ate half. And after that, the birth of Fatimah al-Zahra happened. So this is the same subject.

The *Mudabbir Ta'ala*, who is repeatedly mentioned... is it the *'Ashir*?

It's the *'Ashir*, but Hazrat 'Abdullah Sahib used to always say that the intention behind this is the *Juz'-i Idayi* (Creative Part) of this cycle. The *Juz'-i Idayi* of this cycle.

So the *Imam*... the *Imam* who... for the son, who does the... whatever he does, does it by his command?

Yes, yes, they are all connected. The stations are all connected with each other. No one transgresses.

And their own personal...

*wa huwa wa zawjatuhu al-karimah al-tahirah* (and he and his noble, pure wife). And he, the *Imam*, and his wife, who is very noble and pure, so they... eat this. *allati qad hu'iyyat* (which has been prepared). Such that it was prepared for them. *wa u'iddat* (and it was made ready). And it was prepared. *an takuna wi'a'an lahu tahiran* (that she be a pure vessel for him). That this wife is a vessel for the *Imam*. In which the *Imam* has produced the substance for his son. *lil-shakhs al-imami* (for the Imamic personage). For the *shakhs imami*. *fa-idha ittasaqa dhalika wa qad baynahuma al-mula'amah* (so when that is in harmony, and the suitability is between them). When they eat and partake of those things that have come in the form of a pure fruit, so between them there is *mula'amah*, meaning, the act of marriage happens. *wa ijtama'a dhalika 'inda al-zawjah al-tahirah* (and when that gathers with the pure wife). Then it gathers with the pure wife at the Imam's side. *fa-yusirru nutfatan* (so it becomes a nutfah/sperm-drop). And it becomes a *nutfah*. That *haykal... tastamiddu mimma baqiya min dhalika fil-aghdhiyah* (the haykal... it draws sustenance from what remains of that in the nourishment). And in this way, that... *riyaah*, in their nourishment, they are continuously... received. *wa ummahat al-a'immah salawat Allahi 'alayhim mutahharatun... min dam al-tamt* (and the mothers of the Imams, may Allah's blessings be upon them, are purified... from the blood of menstruation). And the mothers of the *Imam*, meaning, the mothers of the Imams, are pure. From the blood of menstruation, they are pure and clean. They... that which generally happens to women, the menses, they are pure and clean from it. Allah has kept them pure and clean. They are *batul*. They are called *batul*. Yes. That they are pure and clean. And all... the Imams have several wives. So not in every wife, but the one from whom the son of the *Imam* is born, she is the *batul*. Yes. And the one who becomes the *Ummahat al-A'immah* (Mothers of the Imams), for her, a story happened, that during Hajj, Hazrat Shah Sahib Muqaddas, Maulana Ghulam Husayn Sahib Muqaddas, had gone. And at that time, a large delegation had gone. It was the time of Sayyidna 'Ali bin Husayn al-Mukarrami. So you... there was a gathering, so you asked various questions. Among them, one question was that, what is the reason that... for being the Mothers of the Imams? What is the reason for becoming *Ummahat al-A'immah*? How does one become *Ummahat al-A'immah*? It's a matter of great honor, no? To become the mother of an *Imam*. So, what is the honor of being *Ummahat al-A'immah*, and what is its reason? So everyone remained silent, but Mawla Shaykh Husayn Sahib gave the answer. And Sayyidna said, "Correct, it's correct." In the presence of Sayyidna 'Ali bin Husayn. In Makkah al-Mukarramah. Yes. And he said that the reason for this is that from them, great services have been rendered in a previous existence. The great services they have rendered as a *mansub* (appointee), as a *da'i* (missionary)... so they are great services. For which they are given the reward that they become *Ummahat al-A'immah*. Even though they come in a female form. But their honor is great, that they are becoming the mothers of the Imams. *qala ta'ala...* (The Exalted said...).

Finished.

Allah, the Exalted, says this in the Quran, in praise of... the Ahl al-Bayt and... the *Ummahat al-A'immah*, that they are far from the blood of menstruation. *kama qala Allahu ta'ala... innama yuridu Allahu li-yudhhiba 'ankum al-rijsa Ahl al-Bayt wa yutahhirakum tathiran* (as Allah the Exalted said... Indeed, Allah desires to remove all impurity from you, O People of the House, and to purify you with a thorough purification). That Allah has intended this, that He keep you far from impurity, O Ahl al-Bayt, and purify you as it is the right to be purified. It ends here. *Allahumma salli 'ala Muhammadin wa Ali Muhammad.* (O Allah, send blessings upon Muhammad and the family of Muhammad). *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim.* O Allah, send this Fatihah as a gift, a conveyed offering, praiseworthy, pure, and pleasing, to the soul of Sayyidna al-Mu'ayyad al-Shirazi, may Allah sanctify his noble soul, and may his blessings be upon us, and may His benefits, support, and graces overflow upon us. And accept from us this benefit and acquisition in the best possible way. For the sake of Sayyid al-Mursalin (Master of the Messengers), and the blessings of the Imams of the believers, and the Imams of the righteous, and for the sake of the pure and holy Da'wah, O Most Merciful of the merciful. For the sake of the Prophet, peace be upon him, and the pure Imams of the Da'wah. Fatihah. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim.* In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful. Send this Fatihah with the intention of healing for Mawla Ghulam Husayn, son of Walid Muhammad Mawlana Nasrullah, son of Ferman Ali, son of Ghulam Ali, son of Zulfiqar Ali, son of Nathu Khan. May He protect him, guard him, give him long life, and keep him well, and lengthen his noble life. For the sake of Sayyid al-Mursalin, and the blessings of the holy Imams of the Ahl al-Bayt, and the pure Du'at, and the righteous Momineen. Fatihah. Thank you very much. Allah Hafiz.

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 4 -->

[unclear] Zidan Amen. Not this book. [unclear] This is yours. Give it. Not this. From here, tell me, in this, tell me. Tell me. I am telling. For this... for [unclear] to come out. This is the book. [unclear] Only the print is taken out. What? Computer print. This is not a print, this writing, this written, its photocopy should be taken out. No, computer... This is the same book. What? The second manuscript. The book of *mabda'-ma'ad*. [unclear] Okay, come. I will... [unclear] I don't know. [unclear] What is the matter? No, it's correct, correct. As-salamu alaykum. Walaykum as-salam. *Adab* is not required. Turn on the light. As-salamu alaykum. Walaykum as-salam. The third line. This verse that we were reading, right? A'udhu billahi minash shaitanir-rajim. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim, wa bihi nasta'in, wa salla Allahu 'ala Rasulihi Sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala aali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim. Kama qala Allahu Ta'ala: innama yuridu Allahu liyudhhiba 'ankumu al-rijsa Ahl al-Bayti wa yutahhirakum tathira (as Allah Ta'ala has said, "Indeed, Allah desires only to remove from you all impurity, O People of the Household, and to purify you with a thorough purification"). Yes, that Allah Ta'ala has decided on this matter, that, O Ahl al-Bayt, He will remove *rijs* (impurity) and defilement from you, and will purify you as is your right to be purified. Fala yazal yastamiddu dhalika al-ghidha' min la'ami 'izzati al-'amali kathrati al-shughli. And the matter of the *Farzand-i Imam* (son of the Imam) was being mentioned, that in what way does that *farzand*, the son of the Imam, `walad al-Imam`, he receives nourishment (*ghidha'*) to the extent that during the period of gestation (*muddat-i haml*), which is of nine months, in that nine-month period, he digests the nourishment, just like the child of ordinary human beings. Thumma zahara dhalika bi al-wiladah jisman sharifan kafuriyyan (Then that became manifest through birth, a noble, camphor-like body). Then, after that, he becomes manifest with his birth, that is, after completing nine months in the mother's womb, he becomes manifest. The body is such that his... it is very noble, and it is a camphor-like body, which is made of *ru'yah*. His body is of *ru'yah*. Tis'ah shuhur, hadha shart li walad al-Imam aydan? (Nine months, is this a condition for the son of the Imam as well?) Na'am, na'am. Wa ma huwa ya'kul? Ghuriyyah. Na'am. Wa tul al-'umr, ma ya'kul wa yashrab? Wa al-ri'yah. (Yes, yes. And what does he eat? *Ghuriyyah*. Yes. And for a long life, what does he eat and drink? And the *ri'yah*.) And the condition is also that the entire formation happens... the entire formation happens... What is the situation? But until the formation is not complete, the entire... the whole body of the Imam is not ready, until then the *Imamah* cannot be given either, right? Yes. One has to wait. What? For whom? That is, there must be a condition that the Imam... the Imam gives the *Imamah* to the child. But when the *Bab al-Abwab* does not have a complete formation, the full body's formation has not occurred, then... No, they run in parallel. That is, the *Farzand-i Imam* is born, and the *Bab al-Abwab's* *surat* (form) is completed and assembled. That runs in parallel. Sawa'an bi sawa'an (exactly the same). Yes. So his body is very noble and camphor-like. Yes, because it is made of *ru'yah*. Thumma innahu yantaqilu al-bab wa tattasilu nafsahu al-karimah (Then indeed, the *Bab* passes away, and his noble soul connects). And then the *Bab* passes away, and his noble soul, which had carried all the *suwar* (forms/souls), that soul itself... his noble soul connects with the *Farzand-i Imam*. That `haykal-i nurani` (luminous temple/body) `alladhi kana fi dhimnihi` (which was in his charge), and the `haykal-i nurani` that was in his charge, whose... The *Bab's*. The *Bab al-Abwab's*. The `haykal-i nurani` that was in his charge, which his soul was the carrier of, `alladhi kana fi dhimnihi`, which was in his charge, `bi nafs al-mawud al-Imam`, so with the Imam, the `mawud`, the `mawud`... from `walad al-Imam`, and from the soul of `walad al-Imam`, it connects. Yes. Nafs-i natiqah... al-mawsum nafs, nafs-i natiqah... nafs al-bab aydan huwa natiqah? (The rational soul... is it called a soul, a rational soul... the soul of the Bab, is it also rational?) Na'am, huwa natiqah. (Yes, it is rational.) Fi muddat li al-mawud (in the period of the *mawud*), and this happens when? That `aqrabuha arba'una yawman` (the closest of it is forty days). At a minimum, in forty days, this state occurs. That is, the *Bab al-Abwab's* connecting, passing away, with the *Farzand-i Imam*, this happens in forty days. `Arba'ina yawman` (forty days). `Wa aktharuha arba' sinin` (and the most of it is four years). Or if it is a very long period, then in four years this state is created. But, `illa Mawlana 'Abd al-Muttalib`. Mawlana 'Abd al-Muttalib, the `haykal-i sharif` that he received from Mawlana Hashim, that... your... you, Janab Salma, who was your mother, she received it during her pregnancy. `Fi waqt al-haml` (at the time of pregnancy). Yes. That... ittasalat al-haykal al-nurani min taraf... `min taraf-i walidihi Hashim 'alayhi al-salam ila Mawlana 'Abd al-Muttalib` (the luminous temple connected from the side of... from the side of his father Hashim, peace be upon him, to Mawlana 'Abd al-Muttalib). `Qabl al-wiladah` (before the birth). `Qabl al-wiladat Mawlana 'Abd al-Muttalib` (before the birth of Mawlana 'Abd al-Muttalib). Li-anna shanuhu 'azim, wa jaddu al-'ayn wa al-mim. (Because his station is great, and he is the grandfather of the 'Ayn and the Mim.) Yes. That when Mawlana Hashim passed away, because of that... Yes, because as it is here, that when the *Bab al-Abwab* passes away, his soul connects with the soul of the *Farzand-i Imam*. So he also passes away and the father also passes away. Yes. As it happened with Mawlana Khadijah. Mawlana Khadijah was the *Bab al-Abwab* of Mawlana Fatimah. When she passed away, then after that, three days later, Mawlana Abu Talib, peace be upon him, passed away. Fa yasiru dhalika al-haykal al-nurani mushriqan 'ala dhalika al-ghilaf (So that luminous temple becomes radiant over that covering). So that `haykal-i nurani` which is there, which has come to the *farzand*, it radiates upon the *ghilaf* (covering). By *ghilaf* is meant `wa al-alladhi huwa jism al-Imam` (that which is the body of the Imam). The body of the Imam. The *nasut*. `Wa al-haykal huwa al-Imamah` (and the temple is the *Imamah*). And the *haykal* is the *Imamah*. `Wa al-jism al-kafuri huwa al-Imam` (and the camphor-like body is the Imam). And the camphor-like body is the Imam. So the Imam is *adna* (lower) and the *Imamah* is *bar-tar* (higher). Yes. And with the Imam, when the *Imamah* joins, then that *farzand* goes from *bi al-quwwah* (in potentiality) to *bi al-fi'l* (in actuality). Yes. `Wa huwa yusamma al-ghilaf` (and it is called the covering). It is also called the *ghilaf*. That which is the *nasut*. It is also called the *ghilaf*. `Wa yusamma al-shibh al-kafuri` (and it is called the camphor-like form). And it is also called the camphor-like form. `Wa yusamma al-nasut` (and it is called the *nasut*). And it is also called *nasut*. `Wa yusamma al-haykal al-nurani al-Lahut` (and the luminous temple is called the *Lahut*). And the `haykal-i nurani` which is there, which is the collection of the *suwar*, it is called the *Lahut*. `Anwar wa asraru farqayn` (lights and secrets are two different things). That is, as Sayyidna Ali bin Hiba al-Makrami has stated in *Kunuz al-Hikmah*, that `ijtima'at al-asrar min awwali al-da'i, ya'ni Sayyidna Dhu'ayb bin Musa, ilayhi asrar 'inda Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan`. (`The secrets gathered from the first of the *Da'i*s, that is Sayyidna Dhu'ayb bin Musa, to him are the secrets with Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan`). That is, *asrar* are different from *anwar*. *Asrar* means *ri'yah*. *Ri'yah*. And *anwar* means *suwar*. *Suwar*. Just as different types of *nasut* have been mentioned in your presence, that there is the *nasut-i tabi'i*, the *nasut-i dhilli*, the *nasut-i khas*. In the same way, that *lawa'ih* are also different. The way there is a difference here, there is a difference there too. That is, in one *nasut*, say, a formation has been found, in the same way that *lahut*... that *ghilaf* is for *lahut*. That... this *nasut* is a covering for the *lahut*. This is the *nasut-i tabi'i*, so in opposition to this *nasut-i tabi'i* is that *lahut*. The *nasut-i dhilli* is in opposition to it... That is, the *lawa'ih* are also many. Just as the *nawasit* are many, that is *tabi'i*, *dhilli*, *khas*, in this way... Wa nafsu dhalika al-mawud hiya al-hamilah li al-haykal al-nurani (and the soul of that *mawud* is the carrier of the luminous temple). And the soul, that is, the *nafs-i hissiyah* (sensory soul) of that *mawud*, who is the *Farzand-i Imam*, his *nafs-i hissiyah* becomes the carrier of that `haykal-i nurani`. That is, what greatness and majesty there is, that in the sensory soul there is this capability, that all the *suwar*, in which there are *du'at*, in which there are *hujjatayn*, in which there are *mu'minin*, *ma'dhunin*, *hudud*, all... and the *Bab al-Abwab* himself is there. He has the power to gather all of them, that... his *nafs-i hissiyah*. The soul of the Imam, the `mawud`. The soul of the `mawud`. Yes. Nafs-i hissiyat al-mawud a'la min nafs-i natiqah li al-bab. (The sensory soul of the *mawud* is higher than the rational soul of the *Bab*.) Na'am. Bi jami'i suwarat. (Yes. With all the forms.) In this way, the *nafs-i hissiyah* of Amir al-Mu'minin was superior to all the *lawa'ih* of the pure Imams, from Mawlana Hunayd to Abu al-Qasim. `Illa thalathah sufara'` (except for the three *sufara'*). `Illa thalathah sufara'` (except for the three *sufara'*). Wa idha nassa 'alayhi abuhu (and when his father designates him). And when his father performs *nass* upon him, for the people of his time, `ittasalat bihi al-maddah al-sariyah al-madhkurah bi wasatat abihi` (the aforementioned flowing substance connects with him through the medium of his father). So this substance which was there, which flowed, towards you, it, through the medium of your father, is obtained by the *Farzand-i Imam*. `Wa man fawqahu` (and who is above him). And what is above him. From the father's presence, the substance that was transferred to the *Farzand-i Imam*, that substance is in the father's presence and what is above him (`fawq`), the ranks (`hudud`), up to the First Intellect. This substance is connected. It is not some separate thing, but it is all connected. This from the world of corporeality to the world of spirituality is connected. `Wa sara hijaban lahu` (and it became a veil for him). And it becomes his veil, `wa baban` (and a gate), it becomes his gate. `Li-annahu yusarrifu nazrahu ilayhi` (because he turns his gaze towards him). Because he turns his gaze towards him, `khasatan min hudud 'alam al-din` (specifically from the ranks of the world of religion). That is, leaving all the ranks of the world of religion, he chooses his son and performs this sacred *nass* upon him, so this state is created, that the substance flows to him, which is `azali` (pre-eternal) and `abadi` (post-eternal). Yes. `Wa yakunu nazr al-munba'ith al-awwal ila babihi` (and it is the gaze of the First Emanation to its gate). And in this way, that in the spiritual realm, the gaze of the *Munba'ith al-Awwal* was towards his *Bab*. Yes. `Wa nazara man yalihi min al-'uqul ila hujubiha` (and the gaze of the intellects that follow it is towards their veils). And in the same way, the ranks below the *Munba'ith al-Awwal*, and the intellects, their gaze was upon their veils. That is, the substance kept descending from top to bottom, the substance of guidance. `Ala dhalika nazara kullu 'aqlin min al-'uqul al-ibda'iyyah ila haddin min al-hudud al-din` (In this way, the gaze of every intellect from the creative intellects is towards a rank from the ranks of religion). And up to the point that every intellect from the creative intellects, it made that substance flow and be current, to the ranks below it. `Muttasilan dhalika 'ala marur al-ayyam` (this is connected throughout the passing of days). That is, as eras kept passing, in that way this substance kept being transferred. Yes. Thumma yaqbalu hina'idhin al-Imam al-mansus 'alayhi 'ala al-hudud bi al-maddah ila an yastakhrija huwa dawrahu (Then at that time, the designated Imam accepts from the ranks with the substance until he brings forth his own cycle). And then the designated Imam, he receives this substance from the ranks, and he prepares for his own *farzand*. That *farzand*... he is still a child, but what is his purpose? The purpose is that he should prepare his own *farzand*. Just as when a *Da'i* is made, when a person becomes a *Da'i munfarid* (sole *Da'i*), then what is the most important thing for him? That he should choose his successor. He looks in his ranks to see who can be his successor. Because there, for the *du'at*, the condition of being a son is not there. So his very first thought is what? That... the first thought is that `man yakun khalifatuhu` (who will be his successor). `Min ba'dihi` (after him). For the *Da'i*. `Awwal al-fikr lahu`. Yes, the first thought. `Wa akhir al-'amal` (and the last action). `Awwal al-fikr wa akhir al-'amal` (the first thought and the last action). That his thought is that I... who will be my successor? And within that, he puts him through tests. He administers various tests for his successor, whom he wants to establish. And if we look at the entire history of the *da'wah*, it is full of tests. In what ways... Sayyidna Yusuf bin Sulayman had to go through what kind of test? Sayyidna Muhammad bin Husayn Aqil gave what kind of test? And for many *du'at*... Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il, in his time, the ranks of the *da'wah* were severely tested. And them, Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il established them in high ranks. Yes. He performed *nusus* (designations) upon them. So this... this is the method, that one doesn't get ranks without a test. Yes. Amir al-Mu'minin himself says that Allah Ta'ala tested me fourteen times. He tested me fourteen times, and he listed those tests in a gathering. And people shouted *takbir* and said, "Yes, we bear witness that we... you have passed through these tests." In this way, he mentioned fourteen tests. That... `Imtahan Allah li Mawlana Ali` (Allah's test for Mawlana Ali). `Arba'ata 'ashar marrah` (fourteen times). And he said that six... seven times the test... my test was taken by the Messenger of Allah. Seven times. And seven times Allah took it. And he mentioned each and every test. Yes. Wa yastakhriju huwa dawrahu wa waladan thaniyan yahlifuhu (and he brings forth his cycle and a second son who succeeds him). That his successor should be his own son, who will be his successor. `Kama istakhrajahu abuhu` (just as his father brought him forth). Just as his father, who was the Imam, had made him the successor. `Sawa'an bi sawa'an` (exactly the same). That as it happened before, it will happen after him as well. `La farqa fi dhalika` (there is no difference in that). `Wa ya'sadu al-Imam al-awwal wa yasiru fi ufuq al-'ashir` (and the first Imam ascends and proceeds into the horizon of the Tenth). And the first Imam, who had performed the sacred *nass*, so the first Imam, having done his work, then he... up to the point that he ascends, the Imam, and becomes... enters the horizon of the Tenth. In the horizon of the Tenth, which is the *barzakh-i 'ashiriyyah*, he goes there. Yes. Fa-amma al-shakhs al-fadil sahib al-jussah al-ibda'iyyah alladhi taqaddama al-qawlu fihi (As for the excellent person, the possessor of the creative body, about whom the statement has already been made). And the excellent person who was the `jussah ibda'iyyah`, about whom the statement has passed, `fa-innahu ya'sadu ila ufuq al-'ashir` (then indeed, he ascends to the horizon of the Tenth). And he too, the `jussah ibda'iyyah`, he also ascends to the horizon of the Tenth. But all of them have their own ranks. Yes. `Khalifatihi fi rutbatihi` (of his successor, in his rank). In his own rank. `Fi al-hal` (at that moment). `Fa sara mudabbir al-'alam` (so he became the governor of the world). So he becomes the governor of the world. `Wa sa'ada al-'ashir ila rutbah a'la min rutbatihi` (and the Tenth ascended to a rank higher than its rank). And the *'ashir* himself ascends to a rank higher than that. Yes. That is, this becomes the *'ashir*, and the *'ashir*... This is the `mudabbir`. The *'ashir* goes to a higher rank, and this *'ashir* takes his successorship. But these successorships are `juzwi khilafatein` (partial successorships), not `kulli khilafatein` (total successorships). Because the total successorships happen in one *mawr*. The `kulli khilafah` (total successorship) of the *'ashir*, who was the *'ashir*, happens in one *mawr*. So now the one who was the first *'ashir*, so one *mawr* has passed. So the form of that one *mawr*, that *'ashir* took its `kulli khilafah`. And the *'ashir* became the Ninth Intellect. In this way, it kept moving forward. To the point that the intellect that progresses, it goes to the *Munba'ith al-Awwal*. It is not necessary that after the first *mawr*, the one who reached the Ninth Intellect as the *'ashir*... that he doesn't reach the eighth or seventh. Meaning... No, no. He will reach, but the formation is such that... there is a large crowd in this, in the Ninth Intellect... below it, below... Not, what has been understood is that it is not an overlapping. Rather, he progresses slowly. For this, there are ten *mawrs*, so that all of them... one by one... When ten *mawrs* are completed, then the whole realm will be gathered up and will go, becoming intellects, and it will become a congregation of the *Munba'ith al-Awwal*. Then there will be only two... two states. One, the `da'irah` (sphere) of the First Intellect, which is the `da'irah wahdat` (sphere of unity), within which there is no intellect. Yes. That's one. And the second sphere will be of the *Munba'ith al-Awwal*, in which there are all intellects. Just as in the physical world, the `falak-i muhit` (encompassing sphere) is `khali 'an al-aflak wa al-kawakib` (empty of the spheres and the planets). There is no star, nothing there. And all the stars, all the planets that are there, they are all in the `falak-i buruj` (sphere of constellations). Yes. حضرت, when the *'ashir*, progressing, becomes the Ninth Intellect... Yes. ...so, in his rank, what is the difference? No, no. Now the governance is with his successor. He was the `mudabbir al-'alam` (governor of the world), but now his successor will be. The *'ashir* has done his job. A *Da'i*... like a *Da'i*, right? When a *Da'i* makes his successor, then whatever work they do, the successor will do it. Meaning, he will implement someone, he will appoint ranks, he will establish some ranks, then he... the *Da'i* will not do it. `La yunazzilu al-da'i ayya shakhs fi wujud khalifatihi`. (The *Da'i* does not send down any person in the presence of his successor.) If the successor is present, if his successor is present, then `kullu kullu al-ahkam yajri min taraf khalifatihi` (all of all the rulings flow from the side of his successor). `Bi wasitatihi` (through his medium). Yes. The substance is flowing, that is, in this way. When the *Da'i* at that time... the *Da'i* is fine, then he will implement his own... the rulings he can issue. But... No, no, they don't do that. The successor himself will do it. There are several examples of this. For example, Sayyidna Ali bin Mawsin al-Shibam... Sayyidna Ali bin Mawsin al-Shibam performed *nass* on Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn Sahib, for Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad. In his lifetime. So the one who established the offices, Sayyidna Ali bin Mawsin al-Shibam, he... Abu Sayyidna Ahmad and Sayyidna Muhammad Jamal, whom he appointed as the *Da'i* of Najran, he... `ma nassa Sayyidna 'Ali bin Mawsin al-Shibam 'alayhim, nassa Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn 'alayh`. (`Sayyidna Ali bin Mawsin al-Shibam did not designate them; Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn designated him.`) He did it when he was alone, or before that? Yes. He hadn't become a *Da'i* then. `Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn ma kana da'iyan waqt...` (`Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn was not a *Da'i* at the time...`) `...nassihim 'ala Sayyidi 'Ali bin Sayyidi 'Ali bin 'Abdillah al-Baqimi`. (`...of their designation upon Sayyidi Ali bin Sayyidi Ali bin Abdullah al-Baqimi.`) And even now, Mawlana Muhammad Husayn Sahib was also appointed at that time, that is... `Nassa 'ala Sayyidna Muhammad Husayn... nasb al-mansubiyyah waqt Sayyidna 'Ali bin Mawsin al-Shibam`. (`He designated Sayyidna Muhammad Husayn... the appointment of the office at the time of Sayyidna Ali bin Mawsin al-Shibam.`) He must have done it at the time of being alone. This state, does it also occur when the *Da'i* is not *munfarid* (sole)? Yes, it does. No, when the *Da'i* is *ghayr-munfarid*... the matter is known later, right? After the passing away. After passing away, when the *Da'i*... the *Da'i* passes away, then it is not known whether he will be a *Da'i ghayr-munfarid* or *munfarid*. So `da'i ghayr-munfarid` is what we say at the time when he has passed away. `Ba'd intiqalihi` (after his passing). Otherwise, he is the `khalifah al-da'i` (successor of the *Da'i*), at first. He was at first `khalifah al-da'i`. Yes. Just as Sayyidna Ali bin Sulayman... he performed *nass* on Sayyidna Ibrahim. `Nassa 'alayhi faqat` (he designated only him). So he... `fi hayatihi, fi hayat Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman, ma nassa Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman ayya wahid`. (`In his lifetime, in the lifetime of Sayyidna Ali bin Sulayman, Sayyidna Ali bin Sulayman did not designate a single one.`) `Kullu hukm jarin min taraf Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad`. (`Every ruling flows from the side of Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad.`) `Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad al-Fahd nassa 'ala waladihi Sayyidna Isma'il`. (`Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad al-Fahd designated his son, Sayyidna Isma'il.`) `Wa lakin Sayyidna Isma'il bin Ibrahim intaqala fi waqtihi`. (`But Sayyidna Isma'il bin Ibrahim passed away in his lifetime.`) `Sayyidna Isma'il intaqala fi waqt Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad al-Fahd`. (`Sayyidna Isma'il passed away in the time of Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad al-Fahd.`) `Ba'dayn nassa Sayyidna Ibrahim 'ala walad waladihi`. (`Afterwards, Sayyidna Ibrahim designated his grandson.`) His nephew. His nephew. `Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il`. (`Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il.`) `Wa Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il nassa 'ala Sayyidna Hasan Khan bin 'Abd al-Malik`. (`And Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il designated Sayyidna Hasan Khan bin 'Abd al-Malik.`) `Wa lakin Sayyidna Hasan Khan bin 'Abd al-Malik intaqala fi waqtihi`. (`But Sayyidna Hasan Khan bin 'Abd al-Malik passed away in his lifetime.`) `Li hadha al-sabab nassa Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il 'ala Sayyidna Hibatullah`. (`For this reason, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il designated Sayyidna Hibatullah.`) His uncle. Meaning, in the time of Sayyidna Ali Sahib, Sayyidna Ibrahim had performed *nass*. He had performed *nass* on his son. Yes. In the time of Sayyidna Ali. Sayyidna Ali was present. But the *nass* was performed by Sayyidna Ibrahim. Sayyidna Ibrahim performed the *nass* on Sayyidna Isma'il. Yes. Because when the successor comes, becomes the successor, then `kullu ahkam jarin min taraf al-khalifah` (all rulings flow from the side of the successor). And when the successor is *ghayr-munfarid*, then... No, that is known later. That is only known later. `Hatta ja'at al-kutub`. Yes. `Hatta ja'at al-kutub fi hayat Sayyidna Hibatullah, waladuhu Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hiba nassa 'ala Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba`. (`Until the books came in the lifetime of Sayyidna Hibatullah, his son Sayyidna Ali bin Hiba designated Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba.`) Yes, yes. That is true. `Wa kana Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hibatullah al-Makrami kana afdal al-surah fi majma' Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba`. (`And Sayyidna Ali bin Hibatullah al-Makrami was the best of the forms in the congregation of Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba.`) Yes, yes. `Li hadha al-sabab Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba da'iman yatawassalu bi Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hiba fi kutubihi`. (`For this reason, Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba always seeks intercession through Sayyidna Ali bin Hiba in his books.`) `Wa al-kawakib al-durriyyah allafa Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba fi hayat walidihi`. (`And *al-Kawakib al-Durriyah*, Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba authored it in the lifetime of his father.`) Yes, yes. `Wa kana Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah al-Makrami kana fi al-tartib, fi waqt walidihi, al-thamin`. (`And Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah al-Makrami was, in the ranking, in the time of his father, the eighth.`) But he was designated as his successor. `Wa kana Da'ud bin 'Ajab Shah...` `Da'ud bin Fi...` `Alladhi qatala Da'ud bin 'Ajab Shah`. `Li hadha al-sabab, li hadha al-sabab, kataba Sayyidna Hibatullah tartib, tartib hudud abuhu`. (`For this reason, for this reason, Sayyidna Hibatullah wrote a ranking, the ranking of his father's ranks.`) `Awwal al-hudud min tartibihi kana Isma'il, 'Ali bin Hibatullah`. (`The first of the ranks from his ranking was Isma'il, Ali bin Hibatullah.`) `Wa al-thamin Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba`. (`And the eighth was Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba.`) `Wa al-thamin wa al-'ishrin, hadha Sayyidi al-Mansur, Karamullah, Karamullah`. (`And the twenty-eighth, this is Sayyidi al-Mansur, Karamullah, Karamullah.`) That is, the master of the two islands, India and Sindh, was `min al-hudud al-thamin wa al-'ishrin` (from the twenty-eighth of the ranks). That is, twenty-seven ranks were present above him. And on the ninth page. It's astonishing. Wa alladhi fawqahu ila rutbah min fawqihi (and the one who is above him to a rank from above him). That is, they keep ascending, to their respective ranks. Fala yazal tasawwud rutbat al-'uqul ila an yahsala al-thalith minha fi awwal al-'uqul al-sab' fi da'irat al-munba'ith al-awwal (So the ascension of the ranks of the intellects does not cease until the third of them is obtained in the first of the seven intellects in the sphere of the First Emanation). To the point that, ascending and ascending, the third intellect, `fi awwal al-'uqul al-sab'`, in its sphere is called `hadirat al-quds` (the sacred presence). And that sphere is called `hadirat al-quds`. Yes. Wa ilayha muntaha al-adwar wa al-akwar, wa intaha al-ta'ali min 'uqul 'alam al-ibda' (and to it is the final end of the cycles and the eras, and the end of the ascension from the intellects of the world of creation). And the final end of the cycles and eras is there. The ascenders from the intellects of the world of creation ascend. That is, the intellects from the world of creation are ascending, and the intellects from the world of nature are also ascending. That is, from here, the Imam, *qawa'im*, and *jussah* become and ascend here. So they are called the intellects of the world of nature. And there, they are the intellects of the spiritual world. Fa-amma martabat al-sabiq al-awwal fala wusula li ahadin ilayha (As for the rank of the First Precedent, there is no access for anyone to it). And as for the rank of the *Sabiq al-Awwal*, that is, the First Intellect, no one can reach there. Li-annaha rutbat al-wahdah (because it is the rank of unity). Because it is the rank of unity. Fa-'lam dhalika, fa-idha sa'ada sahib al-jussah al-ibda'iyyah ila rutbat al-'ashir (So know that, and when the possessor of the creative body ascends to the rank of the Tenth). So know this, that when the `jussah ibda'iyyah` reaches the rank of the *'ashir*, `ba'd istikhraji waladihi` (after bringing forth his son). That is, after he has given birth to his son and has performed *nass* upon him, and the *haykal* that was there, he has given it to him. `Alladhi yahlifuhu`. That is, he has made him the successor. So, `kama dhakarna`, `kana waladuhu idha istakhraja waladahu 'aydan wa khalafahu` (as we have mentioned, his son, when he also brings forth his son and succeeds him). As we have mentioned, that it is also incumbent upon his son that he prepare his son, `fi maqamihi` (in his place). In his place. `Sa'ada ila ufuq abihi` (he ascended to the horizon of his father). So he also ascends to the horizon of his father. That is, after preparing his son and giving him the place. `Alladhi qad sara fi rutbat al-'ashir` (who had already proceeded into the rank of the Tenth). Such that his father had come to the rank of the *'ashir* in a partial capacity. `Wa kullu Imam ba'dahu yas'adu ilayhi` (and every Imam after him ascends to it). And every Imam who ascends, in this way, he ascends towards it. That is, towards the rank of the *'ashir*, in a partial capacity. `Wa al-kull fi ufuq al-'ashir alladhi qad sara 'ashiran mawkufuna ila an yaquma al-Qa'im` (and all are in the horizon of the Tenth, who have become the Tenth, waiting until the *Qa'im* rises). In this way, the ascenders ascend, and they... in the successorship of the *'ashir*, and they remain there in the `barzakh-i 'ashiriyyah`, until their *Qa'im* rises. That is, now this is the Imam. One Imam is, another Imam is, another Imam is, to the point that their *Qa'im* comes. Thumma ittasala bihi tilka al-maqamat al-muntaqilah (Then those departed stations connected with him). To the point that all the stations, that is, the pure Imams, they pass away, and `ijtima' 'adaduhu ka-ijtima' suwarihi 'ind al-bab` (the gathering of their number is like the gathering of their forms at the *Bab*). And the assembly of all of them takes place at their *Bab*. That is, the *Qa'im's* *Bab* is who? Mawlana Ali. `Qa'im Al-i Zikr al-Salam's* *Bab*... `Bab al-Abwab`, who is it? Mawlana Ali. Yes, Mawlana Ali. `Huwa ijtima'at 'indahu al-hayakil al-nuraniyyah min waqt...` (`He is the one at whom the luminous temples gathered from the time of...`) `Mawlana Hunayd ila Abu al-Qasim`. (`Mawlana Hunayd to Abu al-Qasim.`) `Illa thalathah sufara'` (except for the three *sufara'*). Yes. And he also... `sab' kashf wa sab' sitr` (seven of unveiling and seven of concealment). Amir al-Mu'minin is the accumulator of the *hayakil* of the Imams of every cycle. Who is the accumulator? `Nafs al-hissiyah li Mawlana Amir al-Mu'minin` (the sensory soul of Mawlana Amir al-Mu'minin). He is the accumulator. Just like the Imam. Just like the Imam, yes. `Nafs-i hissiyah` of Mawlana Amir al-Mu'minin... can accumulate... Their physical life is not there now. So how are they accumulating? Physical... physical life is not necessary in this, is it? It's the forms of those who have passed away that are being gathered, right? The special thing about Amir al-Mu'minin is what? That you... it never happens that an Imam, when an Imam passes away, he gathers the previous Imams. They are gathered, as the seventh one has the previous six Imams gathered. But no Imam has ever gathered the future Imams until now. But the majesty of Amir al-Mu'minin is that not only did you gather the previous Imams, but you also gathered the future Imams. `Al-awwal wa al-akhir`. Yes. So that majesty was created in you. That... `jama'a al-awwalin wa al-akhirin`. `Awwalin min qablihi wa akhirin min ba'dihi ila yawm al-qiyamah`. (`He gathered the first and the last. The first from before him and the last from after him until the Day of Resurrection.`) Fa-ijtima'at 'adaduhu ka-ijtima' suwarihi 'ind al-bab, fa sara 'indahu haykalan 'aziman (So their number gathered like the gathering of their forms at the *Bab*, and so there came to be with him a great temple). And for him... for the *Bab al-Abwab*, a great temple is prepared. `Kullu Imam 'udw min dhalika al-haykal` (every Imam is a limb of that temple). And every Imam is what? He becomes a limb of that temple which has been gathered at the *Bab al-Abwab* for the *Qa'im*. `Haykalan 'aziman al-a'zam, fa yakunu 'ala yadayhi al-hisab wa al-thawab` (A great, the greatest temple, so at his hands will be the reckoning and the reward). And his state is that at the *Qa'im's* hands will be the reckoning and the reward. That is, all the Imams who have passed, what did they do? They kept deferring. That the reckoning and accounting, the *Qa'im* will do. The *Qa'im* will do. And then when the *Qa'im* comes, he will do the reckoning of each and every thing. Yes. `Wa yastakhriju waladan min fi da'watihi` (and he brings forth a son from his *da'wah*). And you, the *Qa'im*, peace be upon him, you... just as it happened with the Imams, you too will prepare your successor. `Wa yas'adu huwa bi jami'i man fi dhimnihi min al-maqamat` (and he will ascend with all those in his charge from the stations). And then you will ascend, and all the stations that were in your charge, `wa qad saru suratan wahidah` (and they have become a single form). And they become a single form. That is, the *haykal* that is with the *Qa'im* will be gathered, so it will become a single thing. `Suratan wahidah`. The son who the *Qa'im* will have, will he be an Imam? Yes, he will be the first Imam of the *dawr al-kashf*. And that will be a second cycle. This cycle is now finished. So he will be the first Imam of the second cycle. First Imam. But here, `walad al-Qa'im ma huwa fi maqam...` (`the son of the *Qa'im* is not in the station...`) `al-Qa'im yahlifu bi rutbat al-qiyamah`. (`The *Qa'im* succeeds with the rank of the Resurrection.`) Yes. The *Qa'im* takes the place of his son. The *Qa'im* `yahlifu bi rutbat al-qiyamah`. Yes. `Mawlana 'Ali waladuhu Hasan wa Husayn`. (`Mawlana Ali, his sons were Hasan and Husayn.`) He was the Imam. `Wa waladuhu bi al-nisbah bi rutbat al-qiyamah alladhi yaqumu al-Qa'im`. (`And his son, in relation to the rank of the Resurrection, which the *Qa'im* will establish.`) Yes, yes. That is, with him is a `haykal 'azimus-shan` (a temple of great majesty), with the *Qa'im*. But `fi al-haqiqah waladuhu laysa huwa khalifatuhu`. (`In reality, his son is not his successor.`) `Khalifatuhu fi al-Imamah faqat`. (`His successor is only in the *Imamah*.`) `Laysa fi rutbat al-qiyamah`. (`Not in the rank of the Resurrection.`) He will not be the *Qa'im*. No. `Wa Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah yaqulu wahid walad`. (`And Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah says one son.`) `Ma dhakara ka-mithli Imam Hasan`. (`He did not mention like Imam Hasan.`) `Waladuhu yaqulu Imam Husayn`. (`His son, he says Imam Husayn.`) `Yaqulu waladuhu yakun Husayn ibn 'Ali`. (`He says his son will be Husayn ibn Ali.`) In the beginning of the *kashf*. The beginning of the *kashf*. That is, `walad al-Imam yakun khalifah al-Qa'im wa lakin fi al-Imamah faqat`. (`The son of the Imam will be the successor of the *Qa'im*, but only in the *Imamah*.`) `Fa al-Imamah faqat`. (`Only in the *Imamah*.`) His successor will be, but that will be the last Imam of the concealed cycle. The one who will be the *Qa'im*... as it was Mawlana Ali, Mawlana... at the time of Mawlana Ali, peace be upon him, `walad`, this Hasan, 'alayhi al-salam, wa Husayn, 'alayhi al-salam, `ma kana khalifatuhu fi al-qiyam, fi rutbat al-qiyamiyyah`. (`The son, this Hasan, peace be upon him, and Husayn, peace be upon him, was not his successor in the rising, in the rank of the *Qiyamah*.`) `Ma kana`. The rank of the *Qiyamah*... his successor is `al-'alam al-akhirah` (the next world). The *Qa'im*, 'alayhi al-salam. He is the successor. But `khalifatuhu fi al-Imamah huwa Hasan wa Husayn`. (`His successor in the *Imamah* is Hasan and Husayn.`) In that way. The *Qa'im* who is coming now, he was the successor of the *Qa'im* of the previous cycle. He will be the successor. They were gathered with Mawlana Ali, right? They were gathered with Ali, 'alayhi al-salam. All of them. Therefore, Ali, 'alayhi al-salam... his successors were the *Qa'im*. So the *Qa'im*, 'alayhi al-salam... `dar asal` (in fact), the successor was of Ali, 'alayhi al-salam. And Ali, 'alayhi al-salam, became the `wasi` (executor of the will) of the *Qa'im*. He became the `wasi` of the *Qa'im*. As-salamu alaykum, bhai hazrat. Walaykum as-salam. Go ahead. As-salamu alaykum. Walaykum as-salam. Hadhiz khadim. There is no mention of the reward. Yes, go ahead. The phone is disconnected. Yes, it's connected. Speak, speak. In the cycle... as Mawlana Ali had two sons in his time, Imam Hasan and Imam Husayn, yes, and in the same way, at the beginning of the *dawr al-kashf*... yes, will there be a personality of the like of Imam Hasan? No, no, no, that is what I said. Sayyidna Isma'il bin says that `kana yakun li al-Qa'im walad faqat, na fi waladayn`. (`The *Qa'im* will have only one son, not two sons.`) So there will be no rank for Imam Hasan. No, no. He will not be `mustawda'` (temporary). He will be `mustaqarr` (permanent). In the *dawr al-kashf*, they will be `mustaqarr`, not `mustawda'`. So Imam Husayn, I mean, as it happened before, that there were two sons of Mawlana Ali. So now there will be only one son of the *Qa'im*. Yes, yes. And from him, the *Imamah* will continue. Just as there, too, in the *dawr al-sitr*, when the *Imamah* continued, it did not continue from Hasan, 'alayhi al-salam. Hasan, 'alayhi al-salam, did not perform *nass* on his son. He did not perform *nass* on Mawlana Qasim. On Qasim bin Hasan. He did not perform *nass* on him. Because `Husayn ibn 'Ali kana mawlud ma'sum ka-mithlihi`. (`Husayn ibn 'Ali was an infallible born one like him.`) He was `mansus 'alayhi min al-nabi salawatullah 'alayhi wa al-salam`. (`He was designated by the Prophet, the blessings of God be upon him and peace.`) And he was the second Imam after him. Yes. But the *mawlud* was like him. In the rank of `ismah`. In the rank of `ismah`, he was present. Therefore, he performed *nass* on Mawlana Husayn. But Mawlana Hasan, didn't Mawlana Husayn become his subordinate? Yes, he became his subordinate. So there was no need for him to perform *nass*, because the Messenger had already performed *nass*. No, no, he did. He did it. He did it for sure. It is necessary to perform *nass*. So the *nass* that the Messenger did was in his own capacity. But Imam Hasan also had to perform *nass* on Imam Husayn. Mawlana Ali himself performed *nass* on Hasan and Husayn. It is necessary to perform *nass*. `Nass wa tawqif`... that is necessary. Mawlana Ali must have performed *nass* only on Imam Hasan. Because he passed away after that. No, we don't know that. That Janab Hasan himself was designated or... In our books, it is mentioned that you had said that you... after you, Husayn should be obeyed. Yes. That is, you reiterated the Messenger of Allah's will. `Akkada wasiyyat Rasulillah fi shan Imam Hasan wa Imam Husayn, Mawlana 'Ali fi akhir hayatihi`. (`He confirmed the will of the Messenger of Allah concerning Imam Hasan and Imam Husayn, Mawlana Ali, at the end of his life.`) So for the *Qa'im*, 'alayhi al-salam, Imam Hasan... I mean, the son... will not be a *tabi'i* (natural) one. No, he will not be a *tabi'i*. So Imam Hasan is `dhamm` (subsumed) in Imam Husayn. `Al-Hasanu fi al-Husayn` (Hasan is in Husayn). Even in the apparent words of Husayn, Hasan is present, isn't he? In the same way, `al-Hasanu mawjud fi al-Husayn` (Hasan is present in Husayn). We'll stop here. Yes. Let us recite the *Fatihah*. [unclear] `Tawassulna bi Mawlana wa al-'alam, sharif al-Fatihah, ila ruh Sayyidna al-mu'ayyad Hibatullah bin Musa al-Shirazi, qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara darayhahu wa a'ada 'alayna min barakatihi wa darajatihi wa fayyudatihi wa ta'idatihi wa taqabbal minna ya Allah, bi hurmat al-qur'an al-'azim wa al-sab' al-mathani wa man huwa fihi, wa 'ala hadhrat sayyid al-mursalin, wa 'ala ma'minin wa mu'minat wa 'ulama' wa 'amilin wa ikhwanina al-muwahhidin ahl al-bayt al-tayyibin al-tahirin, bi al-salawat wa al-taslimat wa al-an'am, al-Fatihah`. Bismillah. [unclear] `Bi hurmatihi salli 'alayhi wa sallim`. `Allahumma salli 'ala Muhammad wa 'ala aali Muhammad`. `Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim, al-balagh ma sharif al-Fatihah min hadhrat Sayyidi wa Mawlaya walidi al-mansub al-awwab Sayyidi Mawlaya Ghulam Husayn ibn wal-Muslim al-mansub Sulayman bin Karamullah al-Haddad bin Mahfuz bin Futah bin Ladha al-Adhri, yahfazuhu wa yahmihi wa yahuduhu wa yansuruhu wa yaqiyhi wa yashfihi min sharr jami' al-amrad kamilan wa jaliyan wa al-a'lam wa yazidu fi sihatihim da'ima, wa 'alayna ulfatun da'imatan 'alayh, 'alayna min ulfatihi wa mahabbatihi wa la yatna' ilayhi da'iman bi haqqi sayyidna Muhammadin wa aalihi tahirin salawatullah 'alayhim ajma'in. Al-Fatihah`. Bismillah. [unclear]

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 5 -->

As-salamu alaykum. Wa alaykumu as-salam wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuh. Wa alaykumu as-salam. As-salamu alaykum, Raja... Wa alaykumu as-salam. As-salamu alaykum. As-salamu alaykum. As-salamu alaykum, bhai. Wa alaykumu as-salam. Wa alaykumu as-salam. As-salamu alaykum, Mawlana. Wa alaykumu as-salam.

It is from page 39. It's page 39 of our book. "Fa yakunu 'ala..." (And it will be upon...). A'udhu billahi minash-shaytanir-rajim (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). Bismillahir-rahmanir-rahim (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). Wa bihi nasta'in (And in Him we seek help). Wa sallallahu 'ala rasulihi Sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala aali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim (And may Allah's blessings and peace be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon the family of Muhammad, and may He bless and grant peace).

"Fa yakunu 'ala yadayhi al-hisab was-sawab wal-'iqab" (And upon his hands will be the reckoning, the reward, and the punishment). And upon his hands... Meaning, whose? Upon the hands of the Qaim 'ala dhikrihi as-salam there will be the reckoning (`hisab`), the reward (`sawab`), and the punishment (`'iqab`). Major decisions will be upon his hands. "Wa li-yastakhrija waladan fi da'watihi yakhlufuhu... wa yas'adu huwa bi-jami'i man fi zimmihi min al-maqamat" (And that he may bring forth a son in his `da'wah` who will succeed him... and he will ascend with all those who are under his charge from among the stations). And that he will surely bring forth his son from his `da'wah` so that he may make him the `khalifa` (caliph). And he himself will ascend with all the `maqamat` (stations) that are under his charge, meaning the stations that have been coming from ancient times, which are under his charge, taking them, he will ascend. "Wa qad saru suratan wahida, wa huwam-maqamat wa huwa khud..." (And they have become a single form, they being the stations and he himself...). ...together they will become a single form.

In three days, you... it will be over for you... so you... your station, `qaim-maqam` (deputy)... within three days, meaning your son... Your `hijab` (veil/occultation) will not last more than three days. What is the reason for it? That they are `riyahat` (winds/spirits). And the `riyahat` cannot remain for more than three days. And they are the highest `riyahat` of the Qaim 'ala as-salam. So in this world, the duration of their station will not be more than three days. Its reason is this. The `riyah`... to his son... he will appoint his son.

And what is it? "Kullu yawmin huwa fi sha'n" (Every day He is in [some] affair). Every day he has a [new] glory. Meaning, today it is one thing, tomorrow another. His affairs are wondrous and strange. They are the essence... the essence of the `adwar` (cycles) and `akwar` (spheres). You can imagine, all the stations that belong to the Pure Imams, their consolidation is happening with him. So what will be his glory?

"Mathaluhu ka-'aql min 'uqul al-'alam al-ibda'" (His likeness is like an intellect from the intellects of the world of origination). And what will his example be like? Like the intellect of the intellects of the `'alam al-ibda'` (world of origination), he will become like that. "Wa yakhlufu dhalik al-qaim al-aashir fi rutbatihi" (And that `qaim` will succeed the Tenth in his rank). And he will become the caliph of the Tenth in his rank. But this is a partial caliphate (`khilafat juzwi`), not a total caliphate (`khilafat kulli`). The total caliphate is taken by the `qaim` of the `mawr` (cycle). But the total... the partial caliphate continues to be established. Like they are stationed in the `barzakh` of the Tenth... They stop in the `barzakh` of the Tenth. Like this, many are stopped. Many are stopped and many will be stopped in the future too. And then when the final `qaim` comes, the final `qaim` of the `mawr`, he will gather all of them and become `qindis 'ala 'ala` (holy of holies/most high).

"Wa ya'tifu bil-maddah wat-ta'id wat-tadbir lil-'alamin" (And he will manage with substance and support for the worlds). And then, through `ta'id` (support/inspiration) and `maddah` (matter/substance), he will manage the affairs of the world. Who? Because they have become the caliph of the Tenth, so this `qaim` will do that management. But this is a partial management. "Wal-maqamat tantaqilu ila ufuqihi imaman ba'da imam" (And the stations transfer to his horizon, one imam after another). And the `maqamat` in your horizon will keep transferring, one after another, "ila an yaquma qaim al-qiyama as-sani" (until the second `qaim` of the resurrection rises). Here, until the second `qaim` of the `qiyamah` comes. Meaning, after the son, just as the Pure Imams came, in that way other `qaims` will come. So, "mathal al-awwal" (like the first one), just as the first `qaims` were... "Fa-yas'adu ilayhi wa yakhlufuhu fi rutbatihi kama khalafa huw-al-awwal" (So he ascends to him and succeeds him in his rank, just as the first one succeeded). ...just as the first `qaim` had established the caliphate, and they had become the `jame'` (gatherer), in the same way, the future coming `qaims` will also become their `jame'`. Now what is this `qiyamah`... what is being discussed? This is a discussion of the `dawr-e-kashf` (cycle of unveiling). In the `dawr-e-kashf`, every thousand years... "`'ala kulli alf sanah yaqum al-qaim`" (a `qaim` will rise every thousand years). In this way, for 47,000 years... there will be 47,000 `qaims`. And actually, the `dawr-e-kashf` is of 47,000 years. The `dawr-e-kashf` is "saba' wa arba'in alf sanah" (47,000 years). "Ba'dain thalatha alf sanah huwa dawr-e-fatrah" (After that, 3,000 years is the `dawr-e-fatrah` (period of interval)).

This talk of `qaimin` (pl. of `qaim`) you are giving, this is... of today's era... of the `qaim` of that era... No, this is about the various `qaims` who will come every thousand years. Then after that, their seventh (`sab'e`) will come. The seventh will come and gather... he will gather the previous six. In this way... 7, 14, 21, and after that 28, and 35, and 42. After 42, the `qaim` who comes will come at 47,000 years. And the `qaim` of 47,000 years, his glory will be very magnificent. And he is, in fact, the `qaim` of the `dawr-e-kashf`. What did the `qaim` of the `dawr-e-kashf` do? He gathered 47,000, not 50,000 years. Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman has mentioned this in a book. He gathered 47,000 years. And the remaining 3,000 years, Mawlana Hunaid gathered them. "Thalatha alf sanah" (Three thousand years). With Janab Hunaid, those 3,000 years are gathered, which are of the `dawr-e-kashf`... of the `dawr-e-fatrat`.

The known history we have is only from Adam 'alayh as-salam... the history before that... Because it [the `da'wah`] wants that it should all be erased from us, removed from our minds. So we do not remember what things happened before this. But the artifacts and history that science describes in today's date... we don't have any mention of that... but science describes it. Yes, science describes it in its own place. And that too it describes partially (`juzwi`), not completely (`kulli`).

So this knowledge that we have, has it come only through `ta'id`? Yes, it has come through `ta'id`. All these things are present in the Quran Majeed. Although this `kashf`, `sitr`, `adwar`, `akwar`, these are not mentioned in the Quran, but they are implicitly present. For example, once when Sayyidna Ghulam Husain Sahab was in Hyderabad, at that time he had a connection with the Dawoodis. So, their, the Dawoodis', elder came to us to meet, for a meeting. So Sayyidna Ghulam Husain Sahab said that... okay, it's done, fine. Sayyidna Ghulam Husain Sahab said, "Look, brother, your sciences and our sciences up to Sayyidna Burhanuddin are one and the same... your `adwar` are also ours." So, he said, "Yes." So then, the sciences of the `da'wah`, where is their source and repository? So this, which has been described, the matters of the `qawaim`, `dawr-e-kashf`, `dawr-e-sitr`, and this and that, where is it in the Quran Majeed? Whatever there is, is in the Quran Majeed. The real thing is the Quran Majeed. So where is it? So, they said that I will ask my... so-and-so and come back. So he said that I know that you will not return. You do not have the answer to this.
