It does not have an answer. Sayyidna Ghulam Husain Sahab said this.

"Mathal al-awwal" (Like the first), just as the `qaim` of the first one was... "Wa la yazalu dhalika muddat ad-dawr al-kashf, wa huwa khamsuna alf sanah" (And this will not cease for the duration of the `dawr al-kashf`, and it is 50,000 years). And this keeps happening, that a `qaim` will come every thousand years... then after that a seventh (`sab'e`) will come, and in that way their `jame'` (gatherer) will be... here... until the entire `dawr-e-kashf`... is of 50,000 years. In which, a pure 47,000 years are of the `dawr-e-kashf`, and the remaining 3,000 years are of the `dawr-e-fatrat`. "Thumma anna akhirahu yaksuru fil-'alam fi'l ash-sharr wa yaqillu fil-khayr" (Then indeed, at its end, the act of evil will increase in the world and the act of good will decrease). And the final 'alam (world/era) of that `dawr-e-kashf`, meaning the last 3,000 years, in it the act of evil will be more... and the acts of good will be less. "Fa-yastaqirru an yuqta'a 'anhum al-haqa'iq" (So they will resolve that the realities be cut off from them). Therefore, they will confirm that the `haqa'iq` (realities/truths) should be concealed from them. Until now they were pure... for 47,000 years they were pure. But now... they became involved in evil deeds, so... music came, the science of astrology (`'ilm-e-nujum`) came, all these things increased at that time, in the 3,000 years. So therefore, the `haqa'iq` will be concealed from them. "Ba'da an kanat lahum zahira fi dawr al-kashf" (After they were manifest to them in the `dawr al-kashf`). Before this, in the `dawr-e-kashf`, all those things were manifest, meaning the sciences of `haqa'iq`. "tuqra' 'alal-minbar" (it was recited upon the pulpit). They were recited from the `minbar` (pulpit)... just as we give a sermon (`wa'z`), in the form of a sermon, in the form of a teacher, they were explained. And "fil-mahafil" (in gatherings) and assemblies, the `haqa'iq` were explained. "Fa-sutirat 'anhum" (So it was concealed from them). But now they have been concealed. "wa lam yasilu ilayha illa man talabaha min abwabiha bil-'uhud wal-mawathiq" (and none reached it except one who sought it from its doors with covenants and pledges). And those things will not reach them, except for that person, it will only reach him, who seeks these `haqa'iq` from their doors (`abwab`), through covenants (`'uhud`), a religious covenant (`'ahd-e-shar'i`), and through pledges (`mawathiq`)... a very strict religious covenant will be taken from him, that this should not be made public, it should be kept concealed, etcetera. "Wa kana dawr as-sitr... awwaluhu Adam" (And the `dawr as-sitr` was... its first was Adam). And in the beginning of the `dawr-e-sitr` (cycle of concealment), Adam came. "Wa huwa awwal an-nuqata'" (And he is the first of the `nuqata'`). And among the `nuqata'` (speakers/prophets), he is the first. "Thumma qama ba'dahu Nuh 'alayh as-salam. Fa-nasakha shari'atahu" (Then Nuh, peace be upon him, rose after him. And he abrogated his law). And after him, Nuh 'alayh as-salam came. And he, Nuh 'alayh as-salam, [abrogated] his `shari'ah`... well, he didn't have a `shari'ah`, but the principles of ethics etc. that he had, he established (`nasb`) them. Therefore, since Adam 'alayh as-salam did not establish (`nasb`) any `shari'ah`, he is not called `Ulul-'Azm` (Possessor of Resolve). And the first of the `Ulul-'Azm` is Nuh 'alayh as-salam. He is not included in the rank of `nuqata'`, but Adam is. But he is not in the rank of `Ulul-'Azm`. "`Lam najid lahu 'azma`" (We found no firm resolve in him). We did not find firm resolve in Adam. Meaning, he is not of the `Ulul-'Azm`. "Wa ja'a bi-shari'atin jadida" (And he came with a new law). And he (Nuh) brought a new `shari'ah`. "Thumma kadhalika fa'ala Ibrahim 'alayh as-salam" (Then similarly did Ibrahim, peace be upon him). Then similarly did Ibrahim 'alayh as-salam, that he brought a new `shari'ah`, he abrogated the `shari'ah` of Nuh. And Musa also came in the same way, and Janab 'Isa also came. And then the Prophet, `salawatullah 'alayh`. May blessings be upon all of them. "Fa-kana Adam wa a'immat dawrihi wa hududuhum mathal an-nutfah... wal-qaim al-muntazar mathal az-zahar" (So Adam and the Imams of his cycle and their `hudud` were like the drop of fluid... and the awaited `Qaim` is like the manifest form). So the `Qaim al-muntazar` (the awaited `Qaim`), if he is a human person or the final rank, then this Adam 'alayh as-salam and the Imams of his time and the `hudud` of his time, all of them are, in comparison to the `Qaim al-muntazar`, like the `zill` (shadow). Which is the first rank in human creation. The first rank. "Wa Nuh wa a'immat dawrihi wa hududuhum mathal an-nutfah" (And Nuh and the Imams of his cycle and their `hudud` are like the drop of fluid). And Nuh 'alayh as-salam and the Imams who are in his cycle, they are in the example of `nutfah` (a drop of fluid). "Wa Ibrahim wa a'immat dawrihi wa hududuhum mathal al-'alaqah" (And Ibrahim and the Imams of his cycle and their `hudud` are like the clot). And the Ibrahim and his Imams of his cycle and their `hudud` are the example of `'alaqah` (clot). "Wa Musa wa a'immat dawrihi wa hududuhum mathal al-mudghah" (And Musa and the Imams of his cycle and their `hudud` are like the lump of flesh). And Musa and the Imams of the cycle and their `hudud`, they are the example of `mudghah` (lump of flesh). "Wa 'Isa wa a'immat dawrihi wa hududuhum mathal al-'izam" (And 'Isa and the Imams of his cycle and their `hudud` are like the bones). And 'Isa and his Imams of the cycle and their `hudud` are in the comparison and example of `'izam` (bones). "Wa Muhammad salawatullah 'alayhi wa a'immat dawrihi wa hududuhum salawatullah 'alayhim wa sallam mathal al-lahm" (And Muhammad, blessings of Allah be upon him, and the Imams of his cycle and their `hudud`, blessings and peace of Allah be upon them, are like the flesh). And you, the Prophet, and your pure Imams and your `hudud`, they are in the comparison of `lahm` (flesh). Therefore, Janab Rasool loved meat very much. This is also one interpretation, that you loved meat and you yourself are in the station of meat. "Wa kullu natiq..." (And every Natiq...). Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman, in the book he wrote... which book is it... no, no, the big book, in which... in which... it's there, no... `Mafatih al-Jinan`... `Jinan al-Jinan`, `Jinan al-Jinan min fawaqih al-azman`, the voluminous book he wrote, in it, the magnificent research he has done is that you have... he has stated this, that in our era, the three `ulama` there are, meaning Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd, and Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman, these three are the essence of the `du'at` (preachers). And the proof he has presented for this is that their names, the letters of their names, the letters of Sulayman's name, the letters of Muhammad's name, and... so these, Adam... the Imams of the time of Adam 'alayh as-salam and their `hudud`, the names of their `hudud` and their letters, and the letters of these names, if they are seen, they all coincide. A comparison. Yes, they all coincide. Meaning, in Ja'far, the `jim` and `'ayn` and `fa'` are not common words, they don't appear everywhere, but you have shown that they all coincide. Meaning, all the letters of their... of Sulayman, Ja'far, and Muhammad, are matching with their `hudud`. They all... and there is no one like that... meaning he took from Janab Adam. Adam, Nuh, Ibrahim, Musa... up to the Imam of his own time. The Imams of his own time... this is a wondrous and strange research you have done.

I was in Hijaz, so I thought that I... When I was in Hijaz... When I read this statement from Jinan al-Jinan, I thought, I should see if this... is from the `hudud` of Sayyidna Husain bin Hasan. And I wrote on a paper... the `hudud` of his cycle, Sayyidna Husain bin Hasan. And I saw that this... it matches with the names of these three: Sulayman, Muhammad bin Fahd, and Ja'far... from... it matches with the `hudud` of Sayyidna Husain bin Hasan. I wrote it down. So I presented it to him. Sayyidna says, "Masha'Allah, Ghulam, Masha'Allah, Ghulam." He said it many times like this. And Sayyidna says... "Masha'Allah, Ghulam, Masha'Allah, Ghulam," like this. But he didn't say anything. For about ten days, they passed, but he didn't say anything. I thought... I went once more to Sayyidna, and I said, "May I be your ransom, did you see the paper I presented to you?" He said, "How did you make... your uncle (`ammukum`) from among my `hudud`?" Your uncle, meaning Sayyidi 'Abdullah... from the `hudud`. "How did you make him their `mansub` (appointee)?" My `mansub` (`haggi`). I said, "May I be your ransom, I did not say... I did not write `mansub`. Just from the `hudud`... from your `hudud`... and I wrote his name." He said, "But he is the `mansub`," Sayyidna says. "But he is the `mansub`." He said it like that. Then he said, "How did you make this Muhsin bin Shayba... Muhsin Shayba from the `hudud`? He is not from my `hudud`." A believer sees with the light of Allah. These days... he has turned back from the `da'wah`. On that very day, he says... on that day he was benefitting people in Khashiba, along with Sayyidna Muhammad bin Jabali. The room is there. On that day he says, "How did you make Muhsin Shayba from the `hudud`?" So I fell silent. And he says, "He is not from my `hudud`." I wrote the names of Sayyidi Ziyali, the `mansub` of Hind, and Sayyidi 'Abdullah, Mawli Amiruddin, his grandfather, and Mawlawi 'Abbas, Mulla 'Adnan, his father, and so on from the `hudud` of Hind. And this one... but when he saw Muhsin Shayba, he said, "He is not from my `hudud`." Like that. And he says... Sayyidi 'Abdullah, "How did you make him the `mansub`?" I said, "May I be your ransom, I did not write `mansub`." "But he is the `mansub`." He wasn't the `mansub` then. No, he wasn't the `mansub`. Sayyidna Husain bin Isma'il, when he used to... I mean, when he was helping Sayyidna Husain bin Isma'il, Sayyidi 'Abdullah... but whenever any person came, he would say, "This is the `mansub` of Hind." The `mansub` of Hind was present there in Hind... Sayyidi Ziyali. But he would say, "This is the `mansub` of Hind."

Once we were invited to someone's house, and Muhsin bin 'Ali asked Sayyidna Husain bin Hasan, he said, "We will not return by the time of Maghrib (evening prayer). Who will lead the people in prayer?" They were sitting in the mosque. He said, "He (`huwa`)." Meaning... no one understood... who... Sayyidna was pointing towards... Sayyidi 'Abdullah understood that "he" meant me, Sayyidna is saying it about me. And Muhsin bin 'Ali understood that "he" means he himself is saying it... about himself. And they were both puzzled. So Muhsin bin 'Ali sent his... this... Muhammad, to clarify. And Sayyidna Husain bin Hasan said, "I mean 'Abdullah bin Hibatullah ibn Mansub Muqaddas 'Abdullah." He said this. Meaning, he says, "I mean... what I was saying, that who will lead the people in prayer at the time of Maghrib or 'Isha... 'he' (`huwa`), 'he' means 'Abdullah bin Hibatullah." Like that. At that time, Muhsin bin 'Ali was not a `mansub`. No, no, he was not a `mansub`. Muhsin bin 'Ali, he appointed Sayyidna Mansub after he left Hijaz... after he left Hijaz, then he announced in Fil-Hina... He announced it in Fil-Hina. In the year 1406 Hijri. They used to sit for his `tijarat` (business). No, no, afterwards. After Sayyidna Muhammad Jabali... yes. But Sayyidna Muhammad Jabali would only come on Fridays. And he, every day, at the time of Zuhr and 'Asr... At other times, he wouldn't come. Sayyidi Muhammad Jabali... At that time... other `manasib` were not there? Sayyidna 'Abdullah was there. He used to sit on the right side. On the right side. After Sayyidna Husain bin Isma'il... Sayyidna Husain bin Hasan... Behind him sat, on the left, Sayyidna Husain bin Isma'il. And on the right, the `manasib` of Yemen used to sit. Sayyidi 'Abdullah, and... Yes. Along with Sayyidna Husain bin Isma'il sat Sayyidi 'Abdullah bin Muhammad. And after that, Sayyidi 'Ali bin 'Abdullah al-Wajih. And after that, 'Ali bin 'Ali. And after that, I and Sadullah. Like that. And 'Ali Salih was at the head. 'Ali Salih was at the head. This incident you are narrating, at that time the `manasib` were not there? Or they had gone for some reason? He was a `mazun`. He was a `mazun`. But every `mazun` was called `mansub` by Sayyidna Sahab. No one was called. Hindis were called... The `muqaddam` of 'Arasi, 'Ali 'Abdullah al-'Arasi, he was the `muqaddam` (head), but he used to sit with humility. With humility. Very humble.

And every `natiq`, `wasi`, and `imam` — this is called a `maqam` (station) — from Adam until the last of the Imams of the cycle of Muhammad, `salawatullah 'alayhim`, when they transfer, they become in the horizon of the Tenth. You understand? From Adam, the Imams who transferred, up to the Prophet of Allah, they, after transferring, settled in the horizon of the Tenth. Managing the world of nature. Until the last Imam from the Imams of the cycle of Muhammad, `salawatullah 'alayhim ajma'in`, is born. Ya'ni Abu al-Qaim. Yes, Abu al-Qaim. Until Abu al-Qaim, the Imams who will be, they all will settle in the horizon of the Tenth. "Wa huwa al-qaim al-qiyamah" (And he is the `qaim` of the resurrection). And he who... who are those Imams? They are the `qaim al-qiyamah`, who will come at the end. The last Imam, who is of the cycle of Muhammad, he is the `qaim al-qiyamah`. And these, all of them, from Adam up to you, the Prophet, and from the Prophet up to the last Imam, all of these will settle in the horizon of the Tenth. "Fa-yattasilu bihi jami' al-muntaqilin min Adam ila waqtihi min al-a'immah... wa tasiru nafsuhu al-karima hamila lahum" (So all those who have transferred from Adam until his time from among the Imams will connect with him... and his noble soul will become a carrier for them). And... all the Imams who are transferring, from the time of Adam, the Pure Imams, they... his noble soul which will become their carrier, the noble soul that will become the carrier... "wa yasirun... wa huwa..." (and they will become... and he...). They all, and he himself, all will become a single, luminous form. `Qaim` (standing). "Thumma yazhar al-qaim 'alayh as-salam" (Then the `qaim`, peace be upon him, will appear). Meaning, the... who... 'Ali ibn Abi Talib, `salawatullah 'alayh`, who will gather the Imams and he himself will merge with them, with the `qaim`, so the `qaim`... with him, all of them, the Pure Imams, he, and the `qaim` himself, will become one form. "Yakunu suratan wahida nuraniyya" (He will be a single, luminous form). Then the `qaim 'alayh as-salam` will appear. "wa qad taqaddamathu hujjatuhu bid-da'wah" (and his `hujjah` will have preceded him with the `da'wah`). And before the light of the `qaim`, his `hujjah` (proof/argument) will come. The `hujjah` is who? The Mahdi 'alayh as-salam. The Mahdi of the End of Time. "Yakunu qabl al-qaim" (He will be before the `qaim`).

The present Imam, if he is Abu al-Qaim, then among the possibilities is that the birth of the `qaim`... it can happen. The mention we had that the birth of the `qaim` will also be in the Ka'bah... No, from the Ka'bah, it hasn't come to my reading. Just as Mawla 'Ali's birth was... Yes, it is for Mawla 'Ali, but for the `qaim`... He is the `muhayyir al-rutbah` (the one who confounds the ranks), that it came for Janab 'Ali that he... but it did not come for the `qaim` that just as he (`'awwal`) was born from the House of Allah (`baytullah`), this one (`akhir`) will be born from the `baytullah`. This has not come anywhere. So the birth of the `qaim` is also from among the possibilities... Yes, this is the thing, that among the possibilities is that the `qaim` has already been born.

Sayyidna Idris bin Hasan said in Subur as-Sabi', its last words are that "inna idha yaqum al-qaim, yakunu 'umruhu saba' wa thalathin" (Indeed, when the `qaim` rises, his age will be 37). 37. Sayyidna Idris bin Hasan... in his book Subur as-Sabi'. And this is the number of the phrase `La ilaha illa Allah` (There is no god but Allah) in the Quran. The number of the phrase `La ilaha illa Allah` in the Quran... and his age when he appears... in the world, his age will be 37 years. 37. And what is 37 years? The number 37 is that the phrases `La ilaha illa Allah` that have come in the Quran Majeed are 37. In 36 verses, 37 times it has come. In 36 verses, it has come 37 times. In five different accents/forms. In five different accents, it has come 37 times. `La ilaha illa Allah`, `La ilaha illa Hu` (There is no god but He), `La ilaha illa Ana` (There is no god but I), `La ilaha illa alladhi` (There is no god but the One who), and `La ilaha illa Anta` (There is no god but You). And `La ilaha illa Anta` came once in the Quran, once. The rest of the phrases, `La ilaha illa Allah`, `La ilaha illa Hu`, `La ilaha illa alladhi`, all of them are repeated. This is repeated, but `La ilaha illa Anta` is not repeated. Therefore, with `La ilaha illa Anta`, Mawlana Yunus, the prophet 'alayh as-salam, supplicated with the phrase `La ilaha illa Anta`. He said, "Subhanaka la ilaha illa anta inni kuntu min az-zalimin" (Glory be to You; there is no deity except You. Indeed, I have been of the wrongdoers). "Fa-istajabna lahu" (So We responded to him). Meaning, the response to the prayer came instantly. "Fa-istajabna lahu wa najjaynahu min al-ghamm" (So We responded to him and saved him from the distress). "Wa kadhalika nunji al-mu'minin" (And thus do We save the believers). And in this way, when the believers supplicate, they too are answered through this prayer.

Our last... the one who has passed... the next one who will come... He comes in 1470. In 1470. So we can probably think that... 1470. So the `qiyamah`... in the world of Barzakh. No, we cannot say because... at once. At once. At once. At the appearance of the `qaim`, at once.

With the principles... 1470. With the principles. When? Tomorrow, possibly. The century, the end of the century... and this 1470. It will end.

And it is that the `hujjah` of the `qaim` will be before you. But what happened in the case of the Prophet? The `hujjah` came after you. Meaning, the Prophet came first, and then his `hujjah` came later. Mawla-e-Ka'inat came later. In reverse. In reverse. Most matters are in reverse. Regarding the `qiyamah` of the `qaim`.

His `hujjah` will be through the `da'wah` to him. "Wa dhalika anna Allaha ta'ala yumalliku hujjatuhu jami' al-ard... fa-yajbur an-nasa 'ala ta'atihi" (And that is because Allah, the Exalted, will give his `hujjah` possession of the entire earth... and he will compel the people to his obedience). Then, your `hujjah`, he will be given possession over the entire earth. And you will compel the people to his obedience. To the obedience of the `qaim`. And you will achieve victory. As it was said that in the first battle, you will lose the battle. Janab Mahdi. The Romans will be victorious. Yes. The second battle that will be, you will win that battle. And the seven continents (`saba' aqalim`) will come under your control. This will be the situation. So when this situation occurs, you will say that I am a servant of the `qaim`. The `qaim` is my master. And I am a servant of the `qaim`. At that time, people will turn away from you. They will become alienated. And after that, these events will happen. The great gathering (`hashr`) will happen. "Fa-yajbur an-nasa 'ala ta'atihi" (And he will compel the people to his obedience). So you will tell people that they should obey him, meaning the `qaim`. "Thumma yandibu wa yunadi fi jami' al-afaq... man akhtara li-nafsihi dinan... fal-yadun bihi" (Then he will call out and proclaim in all the horizons... whoever chooses a religion for himself... let him adhere to it). Then you will make a proclamation in all the horizons that whoever wants to choose a religion for his own self, let him come and choose a religion. "wa huwa amin" (and he is safe). So in such a state, he will be in safety. "Fa-yazharu kullu wahidin 'ala i'tiqadihi" (So everyone will manifest according to his belief). So with this proclamation, everyone will reveal his belief. "Wa yashtaddu al-amr 'ala ahl al-haqq" (And the matter will become severe for the people of truth). And that will be a time of great hardship for the people of truth. Meaning, for us, the people of truth, that time will be a time of intense hardship. "bi-zuhur al-batil" (with the appearance of falsehood). Because falsehood will have appeared.

Sayyidna Husain said, when he appeared in Ahmedabad, the `sutara` (veiled ones)... of the `qiyamah`. He told us that he is a `sutrah` of the `qiyamah`. People asked about the nature of the `qiyamah`. He said, "With knowledge and the sword, both." "With knowledge and the sword." "He will appear with knowledge and the sword, both." Sayyidna Husain said this. We sat how many times, Sayyidna Husain, when he came here... we sat, we sat with many brothers. He used to arrive at `Tasiswala`. Yes, yes. Once we were in Ahmedabad, in a gathering of the believers, one of the believers asked, "May I be your ransom, when will the `qiyamah` come?" A question. So Sayyidna looked... he looked at Sayyidna and said, "Stand up." He made him stand up. He said, "Recite Surah al-Fatiha." He recited Surah al-Fatiha. This is your answer. Meaning, an indication that in Surah al-Fatiha, everything is present. Everything is present.

Because Surah al-Fatiha has seven verses, but how many words? 25 words. Like the 25 prophets mentioned in the Quran. And 25 indicates to the... system of the Imam 'alayh as-salam, that for the Imam, in the `da'wah`, there are 12 daytime `hudud` and 12 nighttime `hudud`. And the nighttime `hudud` are in his presence. And the daytime `hudud` are in the islands (`jaza'ir`). And they are 12. And 12 plus 12 becomes 24. And the Imam himself is the 25th. And so on. And this word indicates to the words of Surah al-Fatiha.

Sayyidna Hasan bin Hamid bin Malik says that Rabb Subhanahu mentioned how many Imams there will be in this `dawr` (cycle) in Surah al-Fatiha. How so? He says Surah al-Fatiha begins with "Al-hamdu lillahi rabbil-'alamin" (All praise is due to Allah, Lord of the worlds). Meaning, `hamd` (praise). The `alif-lam` is for definition. The original word is `hamd`. `Hamd`. How many does it count for? `Ha` is 8, `mim` is 40, and `dal` is 4. Meaning, the total is 52. 52. Meaning, if we count from Hasan 'alayh as-salam to... to Abu al-Qaim, it becomes 49. And then... and then... the Prophet, Mawlana 'Ali, and Mawladatuna Fatima, three. And 49 Imams. So this becomes... 52. And after that, the `qiyamah` will occur. This, this is 52. The Prophet, Mawlana 'Ali, and Fatima. And 49 Imams. This is... 52. Which is `hamd`.

"...with the appearance of falsehood (`batil`), because falsehood will have appeared. Then the `qaim 'ala dhikrihi as-salam` will rise. Then the appearance of the `qaim al-qiyamah` will happen. `Baghtatan` (suddenly). Meaning, it will happen suddenly. It will not be known when the `qaim` appeared. "Fa-yashtami'u ilayhi jami' al-khalq min jami' al-afaq" (So the entire creation from all horizons will gather to him). Then all of creation from all parts of the earth will be gathered. To him. "Wa yahduru bayna yadayhi..." (And they will be present before him...). And they will be present before him, "jami' man intaqala min waqt Adam" (all who have transferred from the time of Adam). ...who have transferred from the time of Adam. "kama qala ta'ala: innal-awwalina wal-akhirina la-majmu'una ila miqati yawmin ma'lum" (as Allah, the Exalted, has said: "Indeed, the former and the later peoples will surely be gathered for the appointment of a known day"). As Allah the Exalted has said, the first and the last will be gathered at a `miqat` (appointed time) on a known day. So the known day is the `qaim 'ala dhikrihi as-salam`, and the `miqat` is the place of their meeting, meaning with the `qaim`. "Thumma yatashakkalu kullu imam wa kullu hadd wa kullu mu'min li-hadhi zamanihi wa azdadihi... fa-yuwaqqifunahu 'ala ma kana minhum" (Then every imam, every `hadd`, and every believer will take form for this time and his opposites... and he will make them stand upon what came from them). And every imam and every `hadd` and every believer, of their time, and their opposites (`azdad`) too, he will make them aware of what their deeds were in life. He will make them aware of their deeds. Then the `qaim 'ala dhikrihi as-salam` will order all the believers... He will command all the believers to slaughter all the opposites (`azdad`). Just as happens in the field of Mina on the day of Eid, in the field of Mina, just as the pilgrim performs sacrifices (`zabihah`), in the same way, the `qaim 'ala dhikrihi as-salam` will command them to slaughter them. The opposites. "Thumma tanzilu 'alayhim min as-sama'i nar" (Then a fire will descend upon them from the sky). Then after that, a fire will descend upon them from the sky. "tahriquhum" (it will burn them). Their... remains... it will burn them. "Thumma yanhadiruna ilal-'adhab al-akbar" (Then they will descend to the greater punishment). Then it will take them down to the greater punishment (`'adhab al-akbar`). To Sijjin. To Sijjin. "wa huwa Saqar" (and it is Saqar). And that is Saqar (hellfire). "fi asfal al-ard" (in the lowest part of the earth). And Saqar is in the lowest part of the earth. So punishment is of three types. As has been said. In the Quran Majeed, what is there? Two punishments are mentioned. "Min al-'adhab al-adna ila al-'adhab al-akbar" (From the nearer punishment to the greater punishment). But punishment is of three types. The nearer punishment (`'adhab adna`), that is `rah khunnar`. Meaning, `rah khunnar` is... `adhab adna` is of seven types. `Darajat` (levels). `Rah khunnar`. It is called `rah khunnar`. `Rajs`, `fakhs`, `'aks`, and `khars`, `khunnar`, `naks`, `najas`, `rajas`. So in this way... its details are many. And after that is the intermediate punishment (`'adhab awsat`). In the `adhab awsat`, they will be placed in the North and South Poles, in the ice, inside the mountains. This is... and for whom the greater punishment (`'adhab akbar`) is intended, they will be put in the `adhab awsat`. Otherwise, from the `adhab adna`... to take them out, they will not be put in the `adhab awsat`. Okay. And the third is the `adhab`, the punishment of Saqar, which is the most severe. In which the opposites (`azdad`) of the `da'wah` will come. And for one great cycle (`kawr-e-a'zam`), for one great cycle, they will be kept there. And they will be punished. During the day, with the light of the night, with the light of the moon. And in the day, "`fi an-nahar... bi-shu'a' al-qamar... wa fi al-layl yu'azzabu bi-shu'a' ash-shams`" (in the day... with the rays of the moon... and in the night, they are punished with the rays of the sun), like that. "La yamutuna fiha wa la yahya" (They will neither die therein nor live). In every night, a thousand killings. And a thousand times they will be made alive. A thousand times they will be killed, and a thousand times they will be brought back to life. Neither will they die, nor will they live. They will remain in such a painful state.

The first and the last people will all be gathered. So the population we see in the world today, we think that these many people... In the `da'wah`. Only in the `da'wah`. Those outside the `da'wah`... they will go into punishment. The punishment has to be given to them. No, that `yawm ma'lum` (known day) is for those in the `da'wah`. But the opposites... as Adam's opposites were, they will come in human form. Nuh's opposites will come in human form, and Allah will punish them. Through the believers, the `qaim` will give punishment. And there are many who are not opposites. These are opposites. This is about opposites, that they... and those who see the `qaim`, for example, Janab Yazid, when Yazid sees the `qaim`, he will see Imam Husain, because Imam Husain is present within the `qaim`. In this way. Similarly, the opposite of Janab 'Ali, Mu'awiyah, when Mu'awiyah sees the `qaim 'ala as-salam`, he will see 'Ali ibn Abi Talib in his being. Like that.

Then the `qaim` will appoint his son as caliph, whom he brought forth from the `da'wah`. And he will ascend with all that is under his charge from among the `maqamat`. So he will become the caliph of the Tenth. And the Tenth will ascend to the rank which is higher than him. As the `qa'id` (leader) has stated before. "Wa yasiru dun al-qaim ma'a waladihi muddat khamsina alf sanah, dawr al-kashf, wa la yazalu 'ala ad-dwar" (And the `qaim` will proceed with his son for a period of 50,000 years, the `dawr al-kashf`, and it will not cease upon the cycles). And the `qaim`... he... with his son, for 50,000 years, which is the period of the `dawr-e-kashf`, he... "`yata'aqabu bi-kashfin wa sitrin`" (it will alternate with unveiling and concealment). And this `dawr-e-kashf` and `dawr-e-sitr` will continue in this way, and... the `dawr-e-sitr` is 7,000 years. And the `dawr-e-kashf` is... and it has been preceded by the end of the `dawr-e-kashf`. And these are the last 3,000 years of the `dawr-e-kashf`, "thalatha alf sanah" (3,000 years). "Kanat fatrah" (It was a period of interval). And this is also called `fatrah` because this is a period of sluggishness. `Fatrah` means sluggishness. "Wa hiya tunzafu ila dawr as-sitr" (And it is added to the `dawr as-sitr`). And this is counted within the `dawr-e-sitr`, even though it is at the end of the `dawr-e-kashf`. But it is counted in the `dawr-e-sitr`. So it means there are three cycles. Pure `dawr-e-kashf`, then `dawr-e-fatrat`, then `dawr-e-sitr`. The `dawr-e-kashf` is upon `haqa'iq` (realities). It manifests `haqa'iq` more. The `dawr-e-fatrat` is upon `ta'wil` (interpretation). And the `dawr-e-sitr` is upon `shari'ah` (religious law). In this way.

And it is added to the `dawr as-sitr`. "Wa man intaqala fi hadhi ath-thalath alf sanah, kana fi ufuq al-'ashir" (And whoever transferred in these 3,000 years was in the horizon of the Tenth). And these 3,000 years in which there is a transfer, they will be in the horizon of the Tenth. "`A'ni min al-a'immah ila riyasat imam a'immat dawr as-sitr`" (I mean, from the Imams to the leadership of the Imam of the Imams of the `dawr as-sitr`). I mean those Imams for whom the Imam of the Imams of the `dawr as-sitr` will attain leadership. "`fi zimn qaim al-qiyamah alladhi yakhtimu dawr as-sitr`" (under the charge of the `qaim al-qiyamah` who will conclude the `dawr as-sitr`). ...in the charge of the `qaim al-qiyamah`, who will conclude the `dawr as-sitr`. Meaning, after the `dawr-e-kashf` comes the `dawr-e-fatrat`, and the `dawr-e-sitr`. These 3,000 years plus 7,000 years. So... as Mawlana 'Ali 'alayh as-salam says, that every 10,000 years a form will appear, and that form is me. Meaning, he means these 3,000 years and 7,000 years, he combines them.

"...who will conclude the `dawr al-kashf`. So know this, O my brother, that every `maqam` from a prophet, or a `wasi`, or an `imam` is connected to it, as we mentioned, the substance from the first intellect, "da'iman la tanqati'u tarfat 'ayn" (perpetually, without interruption for the blink of an eye). And whatever station there may be, whether it is a prophet, or a `wasi`, or an `imam`, from them, as we mentioned before, the substance, meaning the `ta'id` (support), it is continuously flowing from the first intellect, and it is such a `ta'id` that its interruption cannot happen even for the blink of an eye. As the Quran Majeed says, "La ta'khudhuhu sinatun wa la nawm" (Neither drowsiness nor sleep overtakes Him). Meaning, in sleep, what happens? There is complete inattention. And `sinah`, in `sinah`, what happens? There is partial inattention. Partial inattention. So the first intellect is neither in a state of complete inattention nor partial inattention. Meaning, by complete inattention, the meaning is... that he, his `hudud`... the intellects... to convey the substance and `ta'idat` that he has received from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala` (the Originating Exalted One), he is never heedless. There is no complete inattention. And the substance he is receiving from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`, in receiving it, there is no partial inattention. Therefore, it is said that "La ta'khudhuhu sinatun wa la nawm."

Bismillahir-rahmanir-rahim. Allahumma, this Fatiha, as a gift and a `wasilah` (means of approach), pure and blessed, to the soul of Sayyidna Hibatullah Musa bin Shirazi, may Allah sanctify his soul and illuminate his resting place, and may He cause his blessings to flow upon us, and his `azahat`, and his `ta'idat`. And accept from us, O Lord of the Worlds, this recitation, with benefit and profit in the best way possible, by the grace of the master of messengers, Sayyidna Muhammad al-Mustafa, and the `ahl al-bayt`, the pure and purified. Blessings and peace of Allah be upon them all. Al-Fatiha.

Bismillahir-rahmanir-rahim. O Allah, accept this Fatiha with the intention for my master and my guide, my benefactor, Mawlana, my revered father, Sayyid al-Mansur Ghulam Husain ibn Wali al-Mufassal Mawlana Nasrullah bin Mukarramullah, `rahmatullah 'alayhim`. May he be in health and well-being. And may he be in blessing and well-being. From the Fatiha. Bismillahir-rahmanir-rahim.

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 6 -->

*As-salamu alaykum. Wa alaykum as-salam. Wa alaykum as-salam wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuh. Wa alaykum as-salam.* We are on page 43. *Wa alaykum as-salam. Alhamdulillah. Ji.* We were on page 43. Ah, *I'lam. A'udhu billahi minash shaitanir rajim. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Wa bihi nasta'in wa sallallahu 'ala rasulihi Sayyidna Muhammadin wa 'ala ali Muhammad wa barik wa sallim.*

Recording in progress.

We will do it from the top. Should I read from the top? This is the last, fourth line. From the bottom. From the bottom? *Ji.*

*Bismillah. I'lam... I'lam anna kulla maqamin... anna kulla maqamin min nabiyyin aw wasiyyin aw imamin...* that know this fact, that every station, meaning that which is from a prophet, and from a *wasi* (legatee), and from an Imam—the word *maqam* (station) applies to all of them. *Yattasilu bihi kama dhakarna al-maddah min al-'aql al-awwal.* That connected to all of them is... as we have mentioned, the *maddah* (substance/matter) from the *'aql al-awwal* (First Intellect). We have mentioned that... *da'imatan...* always... the *maddah* was issued from the First Intellect. That which the First Intellect has been called in the Quran Majid is *al-Hayy al-Qayyum* (The Ever-Living, The Self-Sustaining). *Al-Hayy al-Qayyum* is its attribute. And it has been said that *al-Hayy al-Qayyum*, which in different... different verses and different words is where the *Ism al-A'zam* (Greatest Name) of Allah is present, among them is also *al-Hayy al-Qayyum*. Just as there is *Ya Dhal-Jalali wal-Ikram* (O Possessor of Majesty and Honor), in the same way, the *Ism al-A'zam* is present within this as well. *Ji haan.*

And *al-Hayy al-Qayyum*... is also done as a *tasbih* (glorification). The *tasbih* for Sunday is *al-Hayy al-Qayyum*. *Ji.*

This... the *Dua al-Hayat al-Dakhad*, there is... it is said that in it is the *Ism al-A'zam*, but it is not pinpointed. No. No, no. But it is of great stature. It was not revealed in the same way as the Quran Majid. It was revealed separately from Allah's side. *Ji.*

So this *Dua Nasuma* is not the *du'a* of Maulana 'Ali? It is his *du'a*. They are both his *du'as*. And he always gave it so much importance that he would keep it in his... turban. *Ji haan.*

In *Ism al-A'zam*, who do we... whom do we refer to? The First Intellect or... No, not the First Intellect. Their... no name can be applied to the *mubda' ta'ala* (Transcendent Originator). He is beyond and above all names, indications, and qualities. Whatever dealings there will be, they will be through the First Intellect. The *Ism al-A'zam*... for the First Intellect. *Ji haan.* So, *al-Hayy al-Qayyum* is a name for it. Meaning, *Hayy*, *al-Hayy al-Qayyum* means the Living and the Giver of life. The *maddah* that it is receiving, because of the *mubda' ta'ala*, it is living. And it is the giver of life, meaning the *maddah* that it is receiving, it does not keep it to itself. Rather, it gives it to the intellects that are below it. So this is the quality within it, that it is both giving and receiving. But what it is receiving, it is not giving all of it. Rather, according to their capacity of reception (*istiqhaq*), it gives it to the lower ranks. *Ji.*

Is there a difference in their understanding as well? In what? The intellects that are after the First Intellect... There is a difference in their precedence (*sabaqiyat*). There is a difference in their acuity of vision (*hiddat-e-nazar*). But the understanding of all of them is the same. Whatever the First Intellect can understand, that too... No, they can understand, but there is a difference in the acuity of vision, isn't there? In the acuity of vision, in precedence, so because of that, there is some deficiency or excess in their thinking.

The knowledge, power, and life are... No, for everyone, it is the first level of knowledge. *Ji.* After that, how they increased that knowledge, power, and life, how they used it, that was... in their اختیار... that was in their choice. But when the Originator originated them... created them, the *mubda' ta'ala* did, at that time He created them with these three powers: knowledge, life, and power. But after that, after receiving them, how each one used them, on that very basis their... ranks were determined. *Ji.*

So, that always remained جاری flowing from the First Intellect. And *la yanqati'u turfata 'ayn* (it is not cut off for the blink of an eye). And this chain of *maddah wa ta'yid* (substance and support), for even a single moment, for the blink of an eye, it cannot be interrupted. *Fa idha azhara al-qudrah wal-mu'jizah wal-af'al*... (And when he manifests power, miracles, and actions...) And when he... that... manifests, they do, meaning who? The Prophet, the *wasi*, and the Imam. They manifest power, and the miracle, and actions, *tabhara 'uqul al-khala'iq* (the intellects of the creatures are bewildered). Due to which, the intellects of the creatures, meaning the creation, are astonished and bewildered. What is this state that has come upon them? *Fa inna dhalika fi'lun mansubun ila al-naziri ilayh* (For that is an action attributed to the one who looks upon him). So this action, this state that occurs, the manifestation of miracles, the state that occurs, it is attributed... towards whom? The one who is looking upon them. Meaning that... from the higher circle... Yes, the one who is looking towards the Prophet, the *wasi*, and the Imam... *al-muhtajib bihi* (the one veiled by him)... and who is the *muhtajib* (veiled one) for them? And they have become their veils. *Al-mumiddu lahu* (the one who aids him)... whereas he is their helper, their aid. *Wa idha azhara al-'ajz... asabahu al-alam* (And when he manifests weakness... pain befalls him)... And when they manifest weakness, just as the Amir al-Mu'minin had shackles put on him, and he was dragged, and the weakness that you manifested, so... and... *wa asabahu al-alam* (and pain befell him), and the suffering that is reaching them, *wa i'tarahu al-marad* (and illness afflicted him), and the illness that occurs, they fall ill. *Wa jara 'alayhim majari al-ahwal al-bashariyyah* (And the courses of human states run upon them). And the human states that occur, they apply to them. That one has a headache, a stomach ache, is falling ill, is on the bed... all these things are happening, *fa inna dhalika mansubun ila al-ghilaf* (for that is attributed to the covering). So this is attributed to the *ghilaf* (covering). *Ghilaf* meaning... *hijab* (veil). That which is the *nasut* (human form). The *nasut*... it is attributed to that. It is not towards the essential self. The miracles that happen will be... so they are... from the *muhtajib*... the support is received... Yes, from the *muhtajib*'s side, the help they are receiving, so the *muhtajib* is its... that... is... the current they are receiving is being received by the *muhtajib*. And here, the state that is happening is due to the veil and the *nasut*. Because what is the *nasut*? *Mustakhrajan min 'alam al-tabi'ah* (extracted from the world of nature), which has been taken out from the world of nature. *Al-ka'in min nufus al-rihiyyah* (existing from the airy souls)... that the *nufus rihiyyah* (airy/subtle souls)... it is made from that. That which... the body of the Imam is... so that has found its form due to the *nufus rihiyyah*. *Al-hasil bil-wiladat al-jismaniyyah* (obtained through physical birth). Due to which, due to the *nufus rihiyyah*, there was a physical birth for him. *Ji haan. Fa'lam dhalika wa-iyyak... thumma iyyaka* (So know that, and beware... then beware)... so know this matter, you... and... this... [phone rings]

Hello. Hello. Have you children arrived? You've arrived, child? Okay, I'll give you the key. Okay, I... I am in the mosque now. Take the key from me. Huh? The lesson is until 7 o'clock. Take the key. Okay.

Ali Bhai, you are muted, Ali Bhai. Oh, I have... okay, I'll open it. I'll open it, just a moment. The phone came.

*Fa'lamu dhalika* (So know that), and I am warning you about this matter. Further, he says that I am warning you about this matter, *an taghlu fi ahadin min al-a'immah* (that you should commit *ghulu* regarding any one of the Imams). That you... with any of the Imams... commit *ghulu*. Meaning *ghulu* means to raise them above their station. *Ghulu* means to diminish them from their station. So *ghulu* and *qulu*, both are not permissible. Meaning, to raise them above their station and to diminish them from their station. From the Imam's station, what more can they raise them to? Imam is Imam. No, this, that he created, he is our creator, sustainer... things of this nature. *Ji.* Without understanding. *Ji haan, ji haan.* Just as it happened with Maulana 'Ali, and... that someone elevated Maulana 'Ali to a great extent. So 'Ali said, "Brother..." *Ji haan, ji haan.* You, rather, warned them. And when they did not listen, then he gave the order to burn them. After him too, many... *du'at*... his... what they were... did this. Mughira and... *Ji haan, ji haan.* Maulana Ja'far al-Sadiq. *Ji.*

*Wa ta'taqid fi ahadin minhum annahu ilahun mubdi'un* (And that you should believe regarding any one of them that he is an originating god), *la ilaha illa huwa wa rabbun liman dunahu* (there is no god but he, and a lord for those below him), *bi-ma'na anna man dunahu*... and this fact that people also believe this, among them, that he is God, and he is the Originator, that there is no deity besides him, and he is the Lord of those who are below him. So, these states that are there, so for them he is saying that *annahu... ...fi 'azamatihi wa mutahayyirun*... and that he who is astonished and bewildered in his greatness, and is perplexed... that he is the nurturer of those below him. That he nurtures those who are of a lower rank than him. *Bi-ma yumidduhu bihi min al-hikmah* (With the wisdom with which He aids him). And with that state by which... that the help they are receiving from the higher ones, with wisdom. *Wa jami' al-maqamat wal-hudud... 'an ilahiyyatihi wa rububiyyatihi* (And all the stations and the *hudud*... from His divinity and His Lordship). All the stations... meaning, the Prophets, the *wasis*, and the Imams, all of them, and their *hudud* (ranks), all of them negate divinity from their own selves. Meaning, that he... the quality of creating from nothingness, of bringing into existence, is not in any of them. They acknowledge this fact. *Wa jami'an... wa yuqirruna bi-dhalika... li-ilahi al-alihah wa rabbi al-arbab* (And all of them... and they affirm that... for the God of gods and the Lord of lords). And they acknowledge this fact that this quality belongs to the God of gods and the Lord of lords. Meaning, it belongs to the *mubda' ta'ala*. *Mubdi' al-kull* (Originator of all). Who has made everyone the originator. *Wa mujiduhum* (And their finder/creator). Who is the one to bring them into existence. *Jallat qudratuhu* (Exalted is His power). That His power is very lofty and great. *Kama qala Allahu ta'ala* (As Allah the Exalted has said), just as Allah Ta'ala says, *wa man yaqul minhum inni ilahun min dunihi fa-dhalika najzihi jahannam, kadhalika najzi al-zalimin* (And whoever of them should say, "Indeed, I am a god besides Him" - then that one We will recompense with Hell. Thus do We recompense the wrongdoers). And Allah Ta'ala says that he who will say from among them that "I am God," leaving them aside... then we will give him the recompense of Hell. Meaning, anyone who claims to be God. Leaving us aside... without acknowledging us, he says, "I am God." So him... the state of being God is not in anyone, rather it is within the *mubda' ta'ala*. That's why Nimrod's... punishment is more severe than Yazid's. Than Yazid's too. Because he... Yazid did not claim to be God. But Nimrod did.

So we will give him the recompense of Hell. Just as we give recompense to the wrongdoers, meaning we give punishment. The word *jaza'* (recompense) comes in two ways. *Jaza'* comes for punishment, and *jaza'* also comes for reward. *Wa-'lam anna qawman kathiran lamma nazaru ila ma azharahu Allahu ta'ala 'ala yaday 'Ali ibn Abi Talib salawatullahi 'alayh min al-mu'jizat al-bahirat wal-af'al al-ha'ilat* (And know that many people, when they looked at what Allah the Exalted had manifested at the hands of 'Ali ibn Abi Talib, may Allah's blessings be upon him, of dazzling miracles and astounding actions). And this, that many communities, when they saw towards that which Allah had manifested through the hands of 'Ali ibn Abi Talib, meaning the miracles that 'Ali ibn Talib had, which were miracles such that they were astonishing... astonishing. And such actions that would confound the intellect, the actions that he had, of Amir al-Mu'minin, *fa-ghalaw fih* (so they exaggerated concerning him). So they committed *ghulu* (exaggeration). They raised him above his status. *Wa-wadduhu annahu ilah* (And they loved that he was a god). And they claimed that you are God. *Alladhi yakhluquhum wa yarzuquhum* (The one who creates them and provides for them). And such a god who creates and gives sustenance. *Wa-annahu lam yalid wa-lam yulad* (And that he was neither born nor does he give birth). And this, that Amir al-Mu'minin, that no one created him, nor does he create anyone. *Wa-lam yakun lahu kufuwan ahad* (And there is no equivalent to him). And that he has no equal, no partner. *Wa-dhalika minhum... la'anahum Allahu... dalalan wa-kufran* (And that from them... may Allah curse them... is misguidance and disbelief). So Allah Ta'ala has cursed them for this... as misguidance and for their disbelief. *Wa-bu'dan* (And as a distance). And He has distanced them from His mercy. *Wa al-sahih alladhi tajibu i'tiqaduhu* (And the correct [view] which it is obligatory to believe)... And the correct thing is, what is it? That the correct belief in this matter is this, that *anna Muhammadan al-Nabi salawatullahi 'alayhi wa-'alihi afdalu 'uqul al-'alam al-tabi'ah* (that Muhammad the Prophet, may Allah's blessings be upon him and his progeny, is the most excellent of the intellects of the world of nature). The correct conception is that Rasulullah, *salawatullahi 'alayh*, that you, in the world of nature, meaning in this world, the intellects of the world of nature that are there, you are superior to all of them. *Ji. Wa-ashrafu 'alam al-hudud al-din* (And the noblest of the world of the *hudud* of religion). And... what does *'alam al-tabi'ah* mean? Will *muhtajib* not come? No, not *muhtajib*. Intellects of the world of nature means those who are in the world of nature with this quality that they become intellects, one day. Right now they are in potentiality (*bil-quwwah*). Are you talking about the veil? Yes, the veil's matter. One day they will become intellects. So their state is that right now they are in potentiality, but one day they will become intellects. So they are the intellects of the world of nature. Just as there are intellects of the spiritual world, in the same way, this is their state. That the intellects of the world of nature... *ji haan*. That you are the most excellent among them. *Wa-ashrafuhum* (And the noblest of them) in the world of the *hudud* of religion. Meaning, in the *hudud* of the world of religion, the most noble and honored is Rasulullah *salawatullahi 'alayh*. *Wa-anna al-mu'jizat allati kana yuzhiruha Amir al-Mu'minin salawatullahi 'alayh min ta'yid al-'aql al-awwal* (And that the miracles which the Commander of the Faithful, may Allah's blessings be upon him, used to manifest were from the support of the First Intellect). And the miracles which Amir al-Mu'minin *salawatullahi 'alayh* used to manifest were from the supports of the First Intellect. *Wa-annahu lam yasil ilayhi illa bi-wasitat al-Nabi* (And that it did not reach him except through the mediation of the Prophet). That those supports which were received by Amir al-Mu'minin from the First Intellect, they could not be received or were not received except by the mediation of the Prophet. The Prophet became the mediator. For everything. Even after the Prophet's passing away? Yes. After the Prophet's passing away too, the Prophet remained the mediator. He will always remain the mediator.

One time Jibra'il came to you, to Rasulullah, and said, "O Rasulullah, convey my *salam* to Amir al-Mu'minin." Convey my *salam* to Amir al-Mu'minin. So the Rasul asked in surprise, "*Ma ana Amir al-Mu'minin?*" (Am I not the Commander of the Faithful?). Am I not the leader of the faithful? "*Ma ana Amir al-Mu'minin?*" (Am I not the Commander of the Faithful?). Am I not the leader of the faithful? So Jibra'il said, "O Rasulullah, *anta sayyid al-anbiya'i wal-mursalin*" (you are the master of the prophets and the messengers). You are the master of the prophets and the master of the messengers. *Wa khatam al-nabiyyin* (and the seal of the prophets). And you are the seal of the prophets. *Wa 'Ali ibn Abi Talib huwa Amir al-Mu'minin* (And 'Ali ibn Abi Talib, he is the Commander of the Faithful). So 'Ali ibn Abi Talib, he is Amir al-Mu'minin. So saying this, he left. Then جناب 'Ali came to you. So you... Rasulullah said, "*Wa alaykum as-salam ya Amir al-Mu'minin*." (And peace be upon you, O Commander of the Faithful). So he said, "O Rasulullah, *anta Amir al-Mu'minin wa ana khadimukum*" (you are the Commander of the Faithful and I am your servant). You are the Amir al-Mu'minin, and I am your servant. So he said, "No, Jibra'il just came, and he said such and such. I also asked him a question, and he gave this answer from Allah's side, that *anta Amir al-Mu'minin wa ana sayyid al-anbiya'i wal-mursalin wa khatam al-nabiyyin*." (you are the Commander of the Faithful and I am the master of the prophets and messengers and the seal of the prophets). So, the title of Amir al-Mu'minin came to you from Allah's side through Jibra'il. *Ji haan.* And look, every Imam is Amir al-Mu'minin. Every Imam of the time is Amir al-Mu'minin. But when "Amir al-Mu'minin" is said without a name, we understand it to mean whom? Hazrat 'Ali ibn Abi Talib. *Ji haan.*

*Wa-madatuhu lahu li-annahu... ...wa mu'allimuhu wa murabbihi wa muraqqihi ila tilka al-rutbah* (And his substance to him because he is... and his teacher and his nurturer and the one who elevates him to that rank). And the *maddah* (substance) that you received, Amir al-Mu'minin, from the First Intellect, that *maddah* was received by you... by جناب 'Ali... through the Prophet. Because the Prophet, peace be upon him, is your *hadd* (limit/rank). He is your *hadd*. And he is your teacher. He taught and educated you. And he is your nurturer, the one who nurtured you. And the one who elevated you to that rank, to the rank at which you are established, the one who brought you to that rank is who? Janab 'Ali... Janab Nabi. And *mustakhlifuhu ba'dahu* (and his successor after him). And he is the one who made you the caliph after himself. *Fi ummatihi* (in his community). In his community. *Wa-huwa hujjatun... ...fi hayatihi* (And he is a proof... ...in his lifetime). And you are his *hujjat* (proof) in his lifetime. *Ji haan. Wa-kanat martabatuhu... al-'aql al-awwal al-sabiq fi... ...waqtihi* (And his rank was... the First Intellect, the preceding one in... his time). And... and the First Intellect, the First Intellect, just as it is preceding in its time, who is that? That is Janab Nabi. Meaning, the rank of Janab Nabi, just as in the spiritual world it is of the First Intellect, so in the physical world, the Beloved of the Universe, Nabi, peace be upon him, has it. *Wa-martabatu Amir al-Mu'minin fi al-din* (And the rank of the Commander of the Faithful in religion). And the rank of Amir al-Mu'minin in religion, *ma'ahu... li-martabat al-munba'ath al-awwal fi 'alamihi* (with him... is to the rank of the first emanation in his world). Your rank, Amir al-Mu'minin's, is the rank of the *munba'ath al-awwal* (first emanation) in the world of religion. *Wa-al-Nabi mithlu al-dhakar fi al-din* (And the Prophet is like the male in religion). And the example of the Prophet is that of a man in religion. *Wa-Amir al-Mu'minin ma'ahu mithlu al-untha al-qabilah minhu* (And the Commander of the Faithful with him is like the female who accepts from him). And Amir al-Mu'minin, with you, is the example of a woman, that the woman accepts the man's affairs. *Wa-al-Nabi mithlu al-sama'* (And the Prophet is like the sky). And the Prophet is the example of the sky. *Wa-Amir al-Mu'minin mithlu al-ard* (And the Commander of the Faithful is like the earth). And Amir al-Mu'minin... he is the example of the earth. Meaning, Rasul, peace be upon him, who is the sky, from the sky the water that descended, pure and clear, Amir al-Mu'minin absorbed it. And became the bearer of all qualities. So, he became the container of those things. As the Quran Majid says, *wa-ta'iyaha udhunun wa'iyah* (that a retaining ear might retain it). That an ear that listens attentively and an ear that retains, it retains those things. And Amir al-Mu'minin said, *tilka al-udhun ana* (that ear is me). The ear which has been mentioned, that is me. And... here... it is in the Quran that people started calling you *udhun* (ear). Meaning, Rasulullah would tell you the secrets so much that you had become, as it were, the ear of Rasulullah. So its mention has been made in the Quran Majid. So Allah Ta'ala says, *udhun khayr* (an ear of goodness). He is an ear of goodness. And whatever things Rasulullah said, Janab 'Ali memorized them. After Rasulullah's passing, Janab 'Umar ibn al-Khattab said that I wish Rasulullah were alive, because some questions came to my mind which I forgot to ask, and those things remained. So Amir al-Mu'minin said, "What were the questions?" So he said that the dreams we see, why are those dreams true and false? So Amir al-Mu'minin said that when a person sleeps, when his sensory soul separates from his body, so in that state when it is separating, and it tours the heavens, in the middle, when it is touring the heavens, the dreams that come to him at that time, they are true. And when it returns towards his body, then those dreams are false. He said this. So in this way, there were also three or four other questions. So he asked in surprise. So you said, "O 'Umar ibn al-Khattab, do you remember this question from that gathering in which we were present, where we were invited? And Rasulullah was present. And the Sheikh of that place had asked this question. But you forgot, and I remembered." There were three or four such occasions of yours where you mentioned that in this way, you did not remember, but I remembered.

The substance that Maulana 'Ali is getting from the First Intellect is direct, so isn't 'ayn becoming a medium in the middle? No, no. All... all are... it's not being received directly. Nothing is being received directly. It is mentioned that Nabi is the intermediary. Nabi is the intermediary. The famous sermon of 'Ali, *ana al-awwal, ana al-akhir* (I am the first, I am the last), that also relates to 'ayn. It relates to 'ayn in its own place. But in the present life, the one closest to Nabi... the last intermediary... is Nabi, peace be upon him. Because all the intellects became intermediaries. But the last intermediary was Rasul, *salawatullahi 'alayh*. *'Allamani Rasulullahi... min makhnun 'ilmihi alfa bab* (The Messenger of Allah taught me... from his hidden knowledge, a thousand chapters). That Rasulullah taught me from the hidden knowledge, from the treasured knowledge, a thousand chapters. *Wa-inftaha li min kulli babin alfa bab* (And from each chapter, a thousand chapters opened for me). And from every chapter, a thousand chapters opened for me. Janab 'Ali said this. And at the time when you were born, Rasulullah... Janab 'Ali, so when Bibi Fatima bint Asad, *'alayhas salam*, took you and from the Kaaba... from the place where the crack had appeared, and she came out from there, Rasulullah met you. And he... he rushed towards Janab Rasul. Janab 'Ali. So Janab Bibi Fatima said that, "O Muhammad," whatever name she would call him by, because he was not the Rasul then, "O son, this child, for three days, has neither drunk my milk nor opened his eyes." So the very first thing he did was, he looked towards you... his gaze was towards you. So at that time, this was the first meeting of Janab Nabi and Janab Wasi. At that time, Rasulullah said that *khassastani bin-nazr... wa-khassastuka bil-'ilm* (You have singled me out with your gaze... and I have singled you out with knowledge). You have singled me out, that you did not even look towards your mother. And you fixed your gaze on me. So *khassastani bin-nazr, wa-khassastuka bil-'ilm* (You have singled me out with your gaze, and I have singled you out with knowledge). So I have also singled you out with knowledge. *Ana madinat al-'ilm wa 'Aliyyun babuha* (I am the city of knowledge and 'Ali is its gate). So the word 'Ali, you said at that very moment, within three days. That "I am the city of knowledge, and its gate is 'Ali ibn Abi Talib." Even though his mother had named him Haidar. Yes.

*Fa-lamma intaqala al-Nabi salawatullahi 'alayh sara Amir al-Mu'minin salawatullahi 'alayh ba'dahu qa'iman fi 'alam al-din* (So when the Prophet, may Allah's blessings be upon him, passed away, the Commander of the Faithful, may Allah's blessings be upon him, after him, became the one standing in the world of religion). So when Rasulullah passed away, then Amir al-Mu'minin, after you, became the one established... the established authority in the world of religion. *Maqama al-'aql al-awwal* (the station of the First Intellect). So your station became the station of the First Intellect. Just as the Prophet, peace be upon him, had the station of the First Intellect in the physical world. *Wa-hujjatuhu... maqama al-munba'ath al-awwal* (And his proof... the station of the first emanation). And your *hujjat* (proof)... so her station became that of the *munba'ath al-awwal*. And who is your *hujjat*? Bibi Fatima Zahra. As he says in the *du'a*, that *hujjatu ba'liha wa khalifatu abiha* (the proof of her husband and the successor of her father). You are the successor of your father. Meaning, in whichever stations the footsteps of the Rasul will fall, in those very stations, the footsteps of Bibi Fatima Zahra will fall. The very first whose footsteps will fall in those stations will be Bibi Fatima Zahra. She became the successor of the Rasul. And the successor of the Rasul is not Janab 'Ali. Rather, the successor of the Rasul is Bibi Fatima Zahra. And you are the *hujjat* of your husband. And the greatest *hujjat*. *Ji haan.*

And your station became the station of the *munba'ath al-awwal*. After the Rasul. *Fa-alladhi yajibu an ya'taqidahu* (So that which is obligatory to believe)... and this, that the matter which one must believe is *anna... sara al-Nabi wa-Amir al-Mu'minin salawatullahi 'alayh fi manzilah wahidah wa martabah wahidah la fadla li-wahidin minhuma 'ala al-akhar* (that... the Prophet and the Commander of the Faithful, may Allah's blessings be upon them, came to be in a single station and a single rank, with no superiority of one of them over the other). And this, that when the Prophet, peace be upon him, and Amir al-Mu'minin, *salawatullahi 'alayh*, are in one station. *Manzilah wahidah*. *Martabah wahidah*, in one rank. *La fadla li-wahidin...*, there is no superiority of one over the other. *Minhuma 'ala al-akhar. Bal qad tasawaya* (of them over the other. Rather, they are equal). Rather, both are equal. Except, what is the difference? The difference is of precedence (*sabaqiyat*). There is no other difference besides the difference of precedence. *Ji haan. Kama qala al-Nabi* (As the Prophet said)... just as the Prophet says, that *ana wa-anta ya 'Ali ka-hatayn* (I and you, O 'Ali, are like these two). So he... *min yadi al-yumna wal-yusra* (from the right hand and the left)... you... that... your two... the fingers of the *kalimah* (declaration of faith), from both hands, he joined them and said, like this. Like this, he said. That... like this, he said, that just as this is the state, so I and you are like this. And... from the fingers of both hands, he said this. *Wa-qala: wa-la aqulu ka-hatayn* (And he said: and I do not say like these two). And he said, "I don't say it like this." Meaning, his little finger, the *kalimah* finger, and the big finger... he said, "Not like this, but like that," as I just showed you. Like that. *Ji haan. Wa-ja'ala al-musabbihah wal-wusta* (And he made the forefinger and the middle finger)... you... your index finger and the middle finger, he joined them. In which what happened? *Sabaqa ahaduhuma 'ala al-akhar* (one of them preceded the other). That one goes ahead, and one remains behind. So what is the purpose of that? That... Janab Nabi is saying that the finger that is ahead, that is Abu Talib, peace be upon him. And the small finger is you. So he is saying that in any world, in any stage, I was never ahead of Janab Abu Talib. He, Abu Talib, peace be upon him, always remained ahead of me. That is the indication. And I remained behind him. *Ji.*

*Fa-qad ghala* (So he has exaggerated). If someone has said this, that this... if this conception is not there, then people have committed *ghulu*. And *qassara fi al-thaniyah* (and he has fallen short regarding the second). And... one was raised, one was lowered. *Fa-la ta'taqid illa hadha* (So do not believe except this). And do not hold any belief except as we have told you. *Wa-la'natullahi 'ala man ya'taqidu khilafahu* (And the curse of Allah be upon the one who believes contrary to it). So the curse of Allah is upon him who acts contrary to it, or holds that conception.

The lesson ends here. Should we take it up to here?

Should I get the book? Yes, get the book, let's read a little. You get it. I was thinking that... Tanvir and I had typed it in Baroda. It's in the *shari'ah* of Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, but it's a very beautiful book, *Kitab al-Zahirah*. So I'll see, I've requested the PDF. It must be the first book. And there are two or three other books that they have sent the list for, which we had typed. So... Insha'Allah, we'll see which... This will finish in seven days. Yes, it will finish in one session. Yes, it will finish in one session. *Bismillah. As-salamu alaykum.*

This is a balance, sometimes we'll read *muqaddamat* (introductions), sometimes *ta'wil* (esoteric interpretation). And *shari'ah* too... *Kitab al-Zahirah* has that special quality of *shari'ah*. This book should be read again. Yes, this is a book to be read again. Insha'Allah.

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Allahumma'shrah bi'l-Fatihah, hadiyyatan wasilatan saniyyatan zakiyyatan marziyyatan, ila ruh Sayyidna Mu'ayyad... Musa Shiraazi qaddas Allahu ruhahu wa-'alla darajatahu, wa-adafa 'alayna min barakatihi wa-baraka ta'yidatihi wa-ijazatihi. Wa-taqawwam minna 'an ifadatihi wa-istifadatihi bi-ahsan ma yakun. Bi-jah Sayyid al-Mursalin wa-Sayyidina Amir al-Mu'minin wa al-Quran al-'Azim wa Ahl al-Bayt al-Tayyibin al-Tahirin, salawatullahi wa-salamuhu 'alayhim ajma'in. Al-Fatihah.* *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim...*

*Wa bi-niyyat Sayyidi Maulana Mufaddal Saifuddin, Da'i al-Mutlaq fi al-Jaziratayn al-Hindiyyah wa al-Siniyyah. Li-sharaf al-Hadrati al-Fakhri al-Nabiyyil al-'Alim al-'Alim al-Khaliq al-Sharafi. Usama al-Din Maulana Ghulam Husayn bin Sayyidi Mansur al-Muqaddas walid Mansurullah bin Mulla Rahmatullah bin Mansur al-Muqaddas, ratab Allahu ta'ala maqamahu al-ma'ali wa-ja'ala yakfuzuhu wa-yahfizuhu wa-yahmihi wa-yumittuhu wa-yubqihi wa-yufshihi min sharri jami'i al-amrad wa al-asqam wa-sharri al-awja' bi'l-abdan wa-sharri ghayri al-ilman, wa-min sharri nahw sa'at al-kusufat wa al-tathlithat wa al-tarbi'at wa al-muqabalat, wa-yudim al-nasr 'ala a'da'ihi wa-yahfuzuhu min waqti al-'umr. Wa-yadum al-wafd wa al-iqbal wa-yahmihi fi himayat al-abdan wa-tash'sha'niyyatihi wa-muqam al-ard wa al-hayati wa-fi 'yani muqam al-darajat. Wa-yushkuruhi ya'qub fi ziyad al-da'wah wa-yudumuhi fi khidmat al-awdat. Bi-jah Sayyid al-Mursalin wa-Sayyid Maulana Amir al-Mu'minin wa-Ahl al-Bayt al-Tayyibin al-Tahirin, salawatullahi wa-salamuhu 'alayhim ajma'in. Al-Fatihah.* *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim...*

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 7 -->

*Wa 'alaykum al-salam*. *Wa 'alaykum al-salam*. *Assalamu 'alaykum*. *Wa 'alaykum al-salam*. *Assalamu 'alaykum, Mawlana*. *Wa 'alaykum al-salam*.

*A'udhu Billahi min al-Shaytan al-Rajim* (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim wa bihi nasta'in, salla Allahu 'ala rasulihi Sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala aali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim* (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful, and in Him we seek help. May Allah's blessings and peace be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon the family of Muhammad). *Wa hadha haqiqatu al-ibtida'i wa al-ma'ad* (And this is the reality of the beginning and the return). Page 46. Page 46. It is in the middle. *Wa hadha haqiqatu al-ibtida'i wa al-ma'ad* (And this is the reality of the beginning and the return).

The last, fifth line. *Wa hadha haqiqatu al-ibtida'i wa al-ma'ad* (And this is the reality of the beginning and the return). So this is the reality of the beginning and the return. That is, the reality was explained of what the beginning was, that the beginning occurred, and in what way it happened. And the return was also mentioned, that... the return was also mentioned, that... the return was also mentioned, that in what way the praiseworthy return occurred. That in what way the return of the believers happened.

Then now, what is being done is, in the end, the unpraiseworthy return. How did the evil return happen? *Fa qad kashaftuha laka ayyuha al-akh musarrihatan mujarradatan min ghayri ramzin wa la isharat* (So I have unveiled it for you, O brother, explicitly and plainly, without any symbol or indication). So, O my brother, I have unveiled to you, meaning, I have made apparent, every matter with clarity and by explaining it fully, without any secret, without any indication. Meaning, I have clarified every matter. There is no hidden matter said in symbol or indication, but rather it has been clarified. *Wa hadha al-kalam la yakshifuhu wa la illa al-du'at* (And this speech is not unveiled except by the Du'at). And this... And this... And this is a speech that is not unveiled, not explained, except by those who are the great *Du'at*. The great *Du'at* are the ones who unveil this matter which we have mentioned to you. *Kama a'lamuna* (As they informed us). And those people... those great *Du'at* have informed us of these matters. *Hududuna* (Our Hudud). And they are... they are our *Hudud*. *Fa jazahum Allahu 'anna* (So may Allah reward them on our behalf). So may Allah grant them the best reward. *Afdhal al-jaza'* (The best of rewards). The best of rewards. *Kama kafahum Allahu bi-l-husna* (As Allah has sufficed them with the best). And may Allah the Exalted suffice them completely with the best of rewards.

*Fa-fham dhalika wa inna nashrahu al-qawl fi ma'ad al-mukhalifin* (So understand that, and indeed we shall explain the statement regarding the return of the opponents). And know this matter, that we will now explain to you that statement which is about the return of the opponents. Meaning, the opponents who opposed the *Awliya Allah*, the *A'immah*, and the *Anbiya'*. *Al-dallin* (The misguided ones), who went astray. So how will their return be? *Li-taktamila suratuka* (So that your form may be completed). So that in your form, the concept may be fully established. *Wa ta'lu darajatuka* (And your rank may be elevated). And your ranks may be elevated. *Bi-ma'rifati dhalika* (Through the knowledge of that). Through the knowledge of that matter. Because knowledge is of two types. One is, as it is mentioned within *La ilaha illa Allah*, that one is the knowledge of Allah, and the knowledge of *la ilaha*. The knowledge of *illa Allah* is there, but with *illa Allah*, the knowledge of *la ilaha* is necessary. Meaning, along with the knowledge of the *Awliya Allah*, the knowledge of those who are other than the *Awliya Allah*, who are the opponents, is also necessary—what did they do, and how will their return be? That too is necessary. So, he says that so that your ranks may be elevated. *Dhalika, fa-tastahida fi a'mal al-khayr* (That, so strive in the deeds of goodness). And you, along with this knowledge, should strive in good deeds. The deeds of the people of good. *Li-tulhaqa bi-jumlatihim* (So that you may be joined with their company). So that you may be joined with the company of the people of good. *Al-sharifah* (The noble one). Who are very noble people. *Fi hadhirat al-quds* (In the sacred enclosure). And whose abode was in the sacred enclosure. The sacred enclosure means, what is in *Bab al-Abwab*. If we look in our physical world, the sacred enclosure is that great enclosure, that is, the well, in which all of them will go and gather. That is *Bab al-Abwab*. That the forms of all the islands, the gathering of the forms of the 12 islands, happens near *Bab al-Abwab*. So that has also been called the sacred enclosure. And *fi jawari Rabb al-'alamin* (in the vicinity of the Lord of the worlds). Those who are in the vicinity of *Rabb al-'alamin*. *Rabb al-'alamin* is the Imam of the Time, *'alayhi al-salam*. They are in the vicinity of the Imam of the Time, *'alayhi al-salam*. *Tastanibu a'mal ahl al-sharr* (You avoid the deeds of the people of evil). So that you may remain safe and... the deeds of those who are the people of evil, from their deeds. *Fa-taslamu min al-wuqu' fihi* (So you may be safe from falling into it). And you will be safe from falling into their evils. Falling, that you may fall completely into it and become like them. *Ajaran Allahu wa iyyaka min al-wuqu' fihi bi-mannihi wa karamihi 'azza wa jalla* (May Allah protect us and you from falling into it, by His grace and generosity, the Mighty and Majestic). And we seek Allah's protection, and for you too, this is a protection, that you may fall into those punishments, by Allah's grace, meaning His forgiveness, and by Allah's generosity, *'azza wa jalla*.

*Thumma inna al-mukhalif li-l-haqq al-mu'adi* (Then indeed, the one who opposes the truth and is hostile). So, the one who is an opponent of the truth, the one who is an opponent of the truth, and who is hostile to the truth, *yahsunu 'indahu min 'adawat al-haqq* (it becomes good in his view from his hostility to the truth). To him, because of his hostility to the truth, what happens? That he acquires the deeds of the people of evil. And the deeds of the people of evil which he is doing... the evil people's deeds he is doing, *suratun zulmaniyyah* (a dark form). So a dark form of his is created. Just as a believer's luminous form is created, his dark form is created. *Fa-idha kana 'inda mawtihi tajarradat lahu tilka al-zulmah* (So when he is at the point of his death, that darkness is stripped away for him). And that dark form separates from him at the time of his death. Now, he is actually calling the dark form... that form is not like a form. It is his vile concept. And what is his sensory soul, it spreads within his body. His sensory soul spreads within his body. And what is his concept, his vile concept, that he has enmity for the people of truth, from that, a concept was formed for him. And that vile concept, you call a dark form. So, it is stripped away, meaning it separates from his body at the time of his death. *Fa-afza'athu wa ar'abathu* (So it terrified him and frightened him). *Wa-stahashaha minha wa-rta'a* (And he felt alienated by it and was terrified). And it frightens him and it distresses him, and it makes him feel alienated, and he... and he becomes terrified. *Wa dhalika awwalu 'adhabiha* (And that is the first of its punishments). And this is the first punishment that is given to that dark form. Yes.

*Wa asluha min akhbathi tilka al-zulmah al-habita* (And its origin is from the foulest of that descending darkness). And what is its origin? Of this dark form? It is that darkness which had fallen at the time of the sin. At the time of the sin, the darkness he had created for himself there, in the world of beginning, by not listening to the *'ashir* and not repenting, the darkness, his state he had created, that is a part of it which he is receiving here. *Min 'alam al-ibtida'* (From the world of beginning). Which he had done in the world of beginning. *Wa hiya zulmatun 'atiyyah, al-ra's wa al-dhanab* (And it is a powerful darkness, the head and the tail). And this darkness which he has acquired in the world is also its *ra's wa dhanab*. Meaning, it is a part of it which... what is in the darkness of *ra's* and goes into *dhanab*. *Ya'mal magnatis li-hadhihi al-suwar* (It acts as a magnet for these forms). Meaning, from these very dark forms, from these vile concepts, a part is formed which is called *ra's wa dhanab*. Yes. And this becomes a magnet for those dark, vile, satanic forms. Yes. *Li-ma baynahum min al-munasabah* (Because of the affinity between them). Because within them, this *ra's wa dhanab*, within it, and this, its satanic, vile forms, there is an affinity. As the person is, so are his angels. Similarly, this is also a matter of the same kind. *Wa hiya markazun laha* (And it is a center for it). And this becomes a center for them. Who? This *ra's wa dhanab* and Rahu and Ketu, which are beyond the orbit of the moon. Meaning, they do not enter the orbit of the moon, but are... rather, they are under it. Meaning, the sphere of the moon, they are under it, not above the sphere of the moon. *Wa hiya markazun laha* (And it is a center for them). And this becomes a center for them. *Fa-tuqimu hunalika* (So it resides there). And these vile, evil forms, they stay there. That becomes their residence. *Wa takunu minha al-ahzan al-mudirrah bi-l-'alam* (And from it come the harmful sorrows to the world). And remaining there... because in life they have done all sorts of mischief, they have troubled the *Awliya Allah*, they have adopted stubbornness, so there too, coming there, they try to trouble especially the believers in the world and to perplex them. Yes. *Wa yasiru ba'da dhalika ila...*

Is *ra's wa dhanab* also a sphere, or is it separate? Is there a sphere of *ra's wa dhanab* formed or is it separate? No, sphere means there is a place. A non-physical place which is not visible. The two... the place of *ra's wa dhanab*, the two places, they are not visible. They are conceptual, but they exist. No, but does its sphere form or not? No, it's not a sphere. It's not like the spheres of the planets.

The world is so old. Those who are in the lower ranks... those who are in the ranks of the lower ones, maybe they are ascending from being *musirr* (insistent upon sin) gradually. Yes, absolutely. Because *qudrat* (Divine Power) also wants that there should be progress, that the *musirr* and the wretched should enter the fold of faith. This is what *qudrat* wants, and it keeps happening. In the *adwar* (cycles), in the *aqwar* (eras), this matter keeps happening that those who are from below... but they have to be raised with great services. He will mention that those who ascend, they are entrusted with very great services. And after those services, when they ascend, they reach a very high station. Because the services they have are encompassing entire *adwar*. They are not for a year or two, but they have to perform services for entire *adwar*. Due to which, a great deal of luminosity and transparency develops within them. Therefore, when their return happens in the human form, they reach a very high station. Some of our *du'at* have also mentioned that those who are *du'at* now, were once *musirrin* and *mustakbirin* (arrogant). Yes. And then later, when their return happened after many years, they were established in the rank of a *da'i*.

*Thumma yasiru ba'da dhalika ila barazikh 'adhab al-adna* (Then after that he proceeds to the isthmuses of the lowest punishment). Then they are taken to the isthmuses of the lowest punishment. As you know, there are three punishments. There is *'adhab al-adna* (the lowest punishment), *'adhab al-awsat* (the middle punishment), and *'adhab al-akbar* (the greatest punishment). Yes. So, *'adhab al-adna* is the punishment of the physical world itself. That in the physical world, they are taken into *ra'h wa khundar*. *Ra'h wa khundar* means, the very first punishment is in the human, physical state. The Habshi, etc. Then, the second punishment is the bear, the monkey. After that, there are wild animals, then birds. In this way, they are taken into punishments. He has mentioned which are those levels.

The final level is that of the mineral. Mineral. Yes, the final level is mineral. First, there is the human level, then the animal level, then birds, then... those that are predatory animals. And then after that is the mineral level. And at the end, there is tar, in which the *da'wah* is not presented. Because every time a form changes, meaning one level... from one level to another, meaning they are descending in punishment, at that time, the *da'wah* of the Imam of the Time is presented to them. And if they accept, and this happens very rarely, the matter of acceptance. They don't accept much. But if they accept, then they ascend. From there, their ascent happens. And if they don't accept, then they descend. And descending, they go into another punishment. To the extent that at the time of every form, the *da'wah* is presented to them. Yes. This is a mercy of *qudrat* upon them. But when they once come into the punishment of tar, at that time no argument is presented to them and there is no *da'wah*. Then they go from the lowest punishment to the middle punishment and the greatest punishment. Yes.

When the lava comes out, it is in a liquid state. Yes. The souls that are... being returned... to human form. That is, it is a very harmful state for them.
