No, for them it would be a good thing, wouldn't it? For them, it is good because they can be released from the punishment of rock. And go out from the vicinity of the fiery mountain. And then that fiery mountain, when it erupts in different places, from it, *namaz* (minerals) are formed. Then, when the *namaz* are eaten, they ascend from that.

They say that when lava comes out, afterwards, the land becomes fertile. Yes, yes, yes. It means they are ascending, they are ascending.

This eclipse, the lunar eclipse and the solar eclipse, it is also because of the vile forms. Yes, it is because of the vile forms. What happens is that when the lunar or solar eclipse occurs, these vile forms gain mastery over the plan that the Imam is carrying out. They become aware of the plans. So, because of becoming aware, their mischief increases a great deal. Whatever plans the Imam is carrying out, those plans are happening in a hidden way, however they are happening. Now, they become aware of those plans. And *qudrat* also wants that the reward for the good deeds they had done should be given. So, *qudrat* wants that their account of rewards should be settled. So, they become aware of the plans of the Imam, and because of being aware of the plans, they cause trouble. They trouble human beings, especially the believers, in this matter. And that's how this matter happens. Therefore, it has been said that during the eclipse, a person should be engaged in prayers and all the... prayers are also offered, supplications are also made, etc. But, that lunar eclipse, as it happened once that Hadrat Ibrahim, who was the son of the Messenger of Allah, he passed away, and at that time there was a solar eclipse. So people started speculating that because of... the sun... because of Janab Ibrahim, this solar eclipse has happened. This... so when Janab al-Nabi heard this, he said, "It is not so, that the solar eclipse has not happened because of my son's death. The solar eclipse is one of the signs of Allah, and it has no connection with my son's death." Yes.

At that time it happened that you yourself wanted to enter the grave, but Mawla al-Ka'inat stopped you. That "O Messenger of Allah, you do not descend, I will descend." So Janab Mawla al-Ka'inat descended into that grave, of Janab Ibrahim. And Janab Ibrahim became a ransom for Imam Husayn. Yes. That is, Janab al-Nabi... Janab Jibril had come to him and said that Allah the Exalted says that what matter do you prefer, that the trial be placed upon Janab Ibrahim or upon Husayn? Because one of the two has to be sacrificed. So you said that I make Ibrahim a ransom for Janab Husayn. Yes. So this happened. And it happened that you... you said that at that time your tears were flowing. Janab Ibrahim... because he was four years old, Janab Ibrahim. And... and he was born from Janab Mariyah al-Qibtiyyah. So you loved your son very much. So when... when... so you started hiccuping. Tenderness arose. So the hypocrites were also there. Because the number of hypocrites in Madinah had increased a lot. As long as he was in Makkah, there were no hypocrites. There were disbelievers and polytheists, or Muslims. The abundance of hypocrites arose in Madinah. So the hypocrites said that "O Messenger of Allah, you had forbidden us from weeping over the deceased, so you said, 'Look, my weeping and my state is a natural state. I have not performed mourning. I have not torn my clothes. I have not created a commotion. This is a natural thing, that in grief for my child, my tears have flowed and I have started hiccuping.'" So you said this. Yes.

And a large gathering assembled for you, for Janab Ibrahim, which was the gathering of the young children of prophets. The young children who passed away at a young age among the past prophets, their gathering assembled with you, with Janab Ibrahim. It came to Ibrahim bin Rasulullah. And then you took care of them. Then later, that gathering came to Bibi Fatimah.

And this is what is called *khulasatu al-jawhar al-islami* (the essence of the Islamic jewel). That it came to Janab Fatimah, in your sermons there is that you are the *khulasatu al-jawhar al-islami*. That you are the essence of the Islamic jewel. And it happened in this way that this gathering came to you, and those people who passed away in the presence of the Messenger of Allah, before the *wilayah* of Janab 'Ali. Meaning, before the *wilayah* of Janab 'Ali was presented by the Messenger of Allah. So those who passed away before that, their gathering also came to Janab Bibi Fatimah. Yes. And that is why she is called *khulasatu al-jawhar al-usmani*... and in that gathering, who is the highest form? That which is the *khulasatu al-jawhar al-islami* gathering, in it, the highest form is that of Mawlana Hamzah.

*Thumma ila al-'adhab al-akbar* (Then to the greatest punishment). Then they will be taken towards the greatest punishment. And the greatest punishment is an extremely severe punishment, the punishment of rock. Yes. *Wa rubbama anna minhum man takunu dhunubuhu qalilah* (And perhaps among them are those whose sins are few). And among them, those whose sins are few, *wa tujibu da'wata ba'd al-anbiya'* (and they answer the call of some of the prophets). And who have accepted the call of some prophets, *fa-yuslimu* (so they submit). And they have accepted Islam. *Wa yuqimu wa yukhdamu kama huwa mujarradun fi ma'unat al-awliya'* (And they are established and served, just as it is stripped in the assistance of the awliya). And... they remain in this way and perform service, just as they are stripped in the assistance of the *awliya* Allah. *Wa takhlisuhu min ma waqa'a fi shiddah minhum thumma awfa* (And his salvation from what he fell into of hardship from them, then he fulfilled). First, they were in a great deal of hardship. In enmity towards the *awliya* Allah. Then later, after repenting, they were saved, they were liberated. And then when they... when they are given various services, in those services, they spend their time. And then, when their services are completed, *wa-stahaqqa al-majazat alladhi yaliq bi-l-sahik* (And he became deserving of the rewards that are befitting of the grinding). And then they... to raise them, what does *qudrat* do? It grinds them. *Sahik* means to crush. It crushes them. *Thumma ja'a fi barzakh al-su'ud* (Then he came into the isthmus of ascent). And it brings them into the isthmus of ascent. *Al-mahmudah fi al-ma'adin wa al-nabat wa al-hayawan* (The praiseworthy one, in the minerals, plants, and animals). And how does that happen? That it brings them into the state of minerals, plants, and animals. *Ila an yahsul fi surat al-bashariyyah* (Until he obtains the human form). Until he comes into the human form. Meaning, into the human body. Those who have to be raised. *Thumma yastajibu* (Then he responds). Then he... coming into that human body, he accepts. He accepts the call. *Wa yas'adu fi fada'* (And he ascends in the space). And then he ascends. He accepts the call of the truth and ascends. And in that way, their ascent happens. Meaning, the ascent, unless a person comes into the noble *da'wah*, through the people of the noble *da'wah* only does it happen. There is no other way. There is no other way for ascent, except that one accepts the call of the Imam of the Time. Other than that, there is no way. Meaning, prayers, Hajj, zakat, etc., are of no use. They are not accepted. Yes, it's another matter that these things bring one closer to the *da'wah*. But until a person accepts the *da'wah*, becomes honored by the people of the noble *da'wah*, he will never progress further. So, in our lives, the deeds that we do, among those deeds, which is the noblest deed? It is our noble *da'wah*. That which we consciously accept as the noble *da'wah*, is our most high and blessed deed. Yes.

But Hadrat, when Janab Ibrahim received the gathering in his childhood, at the time of his passing, he wouldn't have had the noble *da'wah*, nor would he have had consciousness. No, but... that is another matter. But those people, although they passed away in childhood, their ranks are very high. Their ranks are very high. Although they passed away at a young age. Just like Janab Asghar. Yes, Janab Asghar bin Imam Husayn. Their ranks are very high. They have the ranks of great *du'at*.

*Wa hadhihi jumlatu al-qawl fi al-tasawwur* (And this is the sum of the statement regarding the conception). This is the sum of the statement. *Thumma narji'u ila al-qawl fi ma'ad jism al-dhidd wa nafsuhu* (Then we return to the statement regarding the return of the body of the opponent and his soul). Then we return to how the return happened, that of the body of the opponent, the enemy of the Ahl al-Bayt, and his soul, how does its return happen? *Wa dhalika annahu idha faraqahu al-tasawwur* (And that is, when the conception departs from him). Now, when his conception departs from him... *qabla jismihi* (before his body). That is, one is his body, and his *riyah* (spirit) does not come out. Because his matter is not that. And his... his body is whatever it is. And his sensory soul has spread within his body. Therefore, he is feeling the punishment. And whatever has come out is his vile conception. So, when his vile conception departs from him, *wa qubira jismuhu* (and his body is buried). And his body is buried, *ba'da an tashayya nafsuhu* (after his soul has spread). After his soul spreads within it, *fihi digradation hai* (it is a degradation). That the soul, which is a high thing, for it to spread within his body is extremely reprehensible.

But sir, this is about burial. What about those who are cremated? Yes, cremation, that state is the same. That is, they are in even greater punishment. That *qudrat* wanted to put them through a natural process, but instead of that, they have hastened their own punishments. Meaning, that punishment... they have put themselves into punishment. Into the punishment of fire.

*Fihi, fa-inna nafsuhu tashi' fi jismihi* (In it, for his soul spreads in his body). Because his soul spreads in his body. *La yufariquhu ghayru al-tasawwur* (Nothing departs from it except the conception). And nothing comes out except his conception departs from it. *Wa baqi nasib min...* (And a remaining portion of...). And its remaining portion, meaning, what is in the form of vapors from his body, the air that is coming out, *alladhi kana yastanshiquhu* (which he used to inhale). Which he used to breathe. *Wa yusamma nafs hawaiyyah* (And it is called an airy soul). And it is called an airy soul. Just as for the believer, they are called spiritual souls (*nufus ri'iyyah*), for them, they are called airy souls (*nufus hawaiyyah*). *Tufariqu ajsam al-hayawan* (It departs from the bodies of animals). And this also comes out from the bodies of animals. *Kulluha 'inda al-mawt* (All of them at the time of death). That at the time of death, the vapors that come out from their bodies, they are airy souls. Whether it is a human being who is a disbeliever, a polytheist, or an opponent, or an animal. At the time of death. *Fa-idha dufina* (So when he is buried). And when he is buried, *intanna* (it rots). So a stench arises from it. *Wa tamargha fi al-turab* (And he writhes in the dust). And in the dust, he writhes, meaning, he tumbles over and over. *Fa-lahiqa kullu qismin minhu min al-amhat* (So every part of it from the mothers joined). Then every part... is joined to its mothers. *Bi-bukhar al-safra' min al-nar* (With the vapor of yellow bile from the fire). And what is in their body, the part of yellow bile, it goes to fire. *Wa al-dam bi-l-hawa'* (And the blood to the air). And the blood that is in them goes to the air. *Wa al-balgham bi-l-ma'* (And the phlegm to the water). *Wa al-sawda' bi-l-turab* (And the black bile to the dust). Meaning, the four elements, in them, the parts of their body go. And that too, gradually, not quickly.

*Wa dhalika awwalu 'adhabihi kama anna...* (And that is the first of his punishments, just as...). This is... it's not written here.

*Wa kullu dhalika mahfuzan min qudrat al-mudabbir* (And all of that is preserved by the power of the Planner). *Thumma yajtami'u ma yas'adu minhu fa-yakunu mataran fi jumlah ghayra* (Then what ascends from him gathers and becomes rain in a different form). And then all these things, He preserves. *Qudrat*. And then He gathers them, to the extent that they ascend and, becoming water, they ascend. The rain's. *Fi jumlah ghayrihi wa yalhaqu ma yanhillu minhu ila al-turab* (In a different form, and what dissolves from him into the dust joins). Then they descend onto the earth. *Alladhi baqiya minhu fa-yakunu minhu nabatun* (What remained of him, so from it becomes a plant). And it is eaten.

I don't know where this came from. Anyway. *Ma ashkaliha min al-suwar al-najisah fi al-barari wa al-qifar wa al-mawadi' al-wasikhah wa al-bawali' wa al-sawaqi wa al-shi'ab wa tharaya al-nas fi manamiha, manawatihim. Wa yahdurruhum 'ala af'al al-khina'. Fi 'adawat ahl al-haqq. Wa hadhihi al-suwar hum al-jinn al-madhmumun wa al-shayatin wa al-'afarit al-mutamarridun al-darrun li-l-bashar* (What forms of impure figures in the wildernesses and wastelands, and the filthy places, and the sewers and streams, and the ravines, and the dreams of people in their sleep... their provocations. And it incites them to acts of lewdness in enmity to the people of truth. And these figures are the blameworthy jinn and the devils and the rebellious demons who are harmful to mankind).

*Wa dhalika awwalu 'adhabihi* (And that is the first of his punishments). And this is the first punishment that is given to them. *Kama anna surur al-mu'min yataraya suratuhu* (Just as the joy of the believer, his form appears). In contrast to this, the reward that is given to a believer, how is it? That his form is shown to him. At the time of his passing away, it is shown to him. It is shown. *Wa ishqraquha 'inda mawtihi* (And its radiance at the time of his death). And the radiance of the form occurs upon his body at the time of his death. *Awwalu thawabihi* (The first of his rewards). This is his first reward. That is, the believer's first reward. And as it has been explained, what did Sayyidna Muhammad Burhanuddin say in *Al-Anwar al-Latifah*? That when a believer passes away, there is a light that gives him comfort. So, he asks... the dying believer asks, "Who are you? When my loved ones, my family, my dear ones have all left me in this darkness, who are you who is giving me comfort?" Because of which, I am receiving comfort from you. So, it says that "In your life, the way you treated the believing brethren and the happiness that entered their hearts because of your actions, Allah the Exalted gathered all that happiness and gave it the form of a light. And I am that light, which is giving you comfort today." Yes. This... this is what that light says. And this is the first reward that is given to a believer.

*Thumma inna tilka al-surah al-zulmaniyyah tufariqu nafs al-mayyit* (Then indeed, that dark form departs from the soul of the deceased). And then that evil form which was his vile concept, it departs from the soul of the deceased. *Wa tahulu fi al-ufq* (And it roams in the horizon). And then in the horizon, it roams. *Yatlubu al-su'ud* (Seeking to ascend). And it wants to ascend as well. *Fa-la yumkinuha* (But it is not possible for it). But that is not possible for it. *Wa tatlubu al-ruju'a ila dhalika al-jism* (And it seeks to return to that body). And then it wants that if it cannot ascend, then it should enter its body again. Enter the body. Because its pleasure is from the body. That is, pleasures are from the body. So it wants to enter the body. Who? The vile concept. *Fa-la yumkinuha* (But it is not possible for it). This is also not possible for it. *Fa-tajulu fi al-hawa'* (So it roams in the air). Therefore, it roams in the air. *Wa hiya allati tusammaha al-'ammah al-munaf* (And it is what the common people call the munaf). And the common people call it *munaf*. *Ila an yuwafiq man yastahiqquhu* (Until it finds one who is deserving of it). And those voices that are heard... for 40 days, they are given a respite. These vile concepts, which... which want to ascend but cannot ascend, and want to enter their body but cannot enter, so finally, they roam between the sky and the earth. They roam and do whatever they please. So they...

The *hudud* have passed.

Then it happens that *man yastahiqquhu an yahulla fihi min imra'ah jahilah* (one who is deserving that it should descend into him from an ignorant woman). Then this, that they do such mischief that there are women who are ignorant, and who do not remain pure and clean, upon them they descend. They possess them. *Aw sabiyyin* (Or a boy). Or on children they possess. *Aw ma ashakalahu* (Or what is similar to it). Or people of this kind. So... *fa-yadhulu fihim* (so it enters them). And it enters them. *Wa yusra'u* (And he is thrown down). And it throws them down, meaning it possesses them. *Wa yasiru yantiqu 'ala alsinihim* (And it begins to speak through their tongues). To the extent that they begin to speak through their tongues. And in reality, that child or those women are not speaking themselves. This... this possesses them and makes them speak. *Wa tuwaswisu* (And it whispers). And it puts whispers into them. *Wa ya'muruhu bi-l-ma'asi* (And it commands him to sin). And it commands them to sin. *Wa rubbama zala 'anhu* (And perhaps it departs from him). And sometimes it happens that it separates from them as well. *Wa rubbama lazimathu ila an yamut* (And perhaps it clings to him until he dies). And sometimes it does not leave. To the extent that until their death, it remains in possession of them. *Dhalika al-masru', wa faraqahu dhalika al-tasawwur* (That possessed one, and that conception departed from him). *Wa sara ya'wi ma'a ba'di man yastahiqquhu* (And it began to take shelter with some of those who are deserving of it). And to the extent that it takes shelter. They become a shelter. In such women's homes, in such children's homes. And especially those children, it has been said, that those children who urinate and do not take ablution, and their feet are dirty, and in their homes they keep jumping around, so from their walking, impurity spreads in the house, due to which these things enter them. Therefore, it is said that children's hands and feet should be washed, after going to the washroom, and at night, while sleeping, their hands should also be washed. Because when they eat something, children, and they fall asleep just like that, the devils lick their hands. Of the children. Due to which, that... those children scream and shout and cry. So the reason for their crying, waking up at night, is that the devils are licking their hands. Due to which, this... this crying of children happens. Therefore, a pious elder... a woman from his congregation came to him and said that "My child cries a lot at night and troubles me a lot. Our sleep is also disturbed, and... in this way." So he said, "Look, wash his hands and feet and then put him to sleep." And that's what happened, that by washing his hands and feet and putting him to sleep, that state went away. His meaning was this, that the devils lick his hands and feet. Therefore, you should take care of this.

But Mawlana, there is a *du'a*, *Allahumma khalaqtani*, in it, it says that the devil runs in our blood, we don't see him, he puts whispers, he urges us to evil deeds, so then... He is already present within us. He is present, but *qudrat* also saves us, doesn't it? From all their evil and corruption, it saves us too. Our prayers, our deeds... From the prayers, it saves us. Our good deeds, our intentions, all these things keep us safe too. Otherwise, the devil is always in this pursuit, of how we can make mistakes and how we can fall into his trap and net. Yes. But this is the grace of Allah and His Messenger, that for us, such small prayers... look, the prayers of the Messenger of Allah are often not very big. His prayers are short, which a person can also memorize. So such prayers... how many prayers has he explained? That if you do this, you will be safe. If you do this, you will get so much reward. So much... you will be saved from so many punishments. So his great sermons, prayers, and graces are upon us. Yes.

Once, you were worried and concerned, so Janab Jibril came to you. And he said to you that "O Messenger of Allah, seeing your worry, Allah has sent me. Allah has sent me. What is the reason that you are worried?" So you mentioned some issues that there were. So he was told that "O Messenger of Allah, whenever you are worried like this, you should recite this prayer." So... look at the grace of the Messenger of Allah, that he did not reserve that prayer for himself. Rather, he reserved it for the Muslim ummah. That *Allahumma inni as'aluka al-'afw wa al-'afiyah wa al-mu'afah fi al-dunya wa al-akhirah, ya arham al-rahimin* (O Allah, I ask You for pardon, well-being, and safety in this world and the hereafter, O Most Merciful of the merciful). This is his prayer. So... when you were ever sick, you would recite this prayer. This short prayer. Yes. And then Janab Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman, the short prayers that he has compiled, in reality Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd had taught you. That *Amanun min Allah wa Rasulihi, ya sayyidi, ya karim* (A protection from Allah and His Messenger, O my master, O generous one). And then he explained its realities, which are in almost half the book... the realities are in it. That which is your... this... on this *tasbih*. On this *tasbih*, how great... he has written almost 40 pages on this *tasbih*, in its realities. Yes. So...

*Wa sara ya'wi...* from the lower ones... the isthmuses of punishment. And the first of them is the form of the Zanj. And this is that these things, then they go into punishments. And in the punishments, he has given the full detail of the lowest punishment, in which punishments they go. That's it for here.

I think the next lesson will be the last one.

*Allahumma balligh thawab al-fatihah hadiyyatan wasilatan saniyyatan zakiyyatan mardiyyatan ila hadarat jami' al-anbiya' wa 'ala hadrat... hadrat Sayyidina Hibatullah bin Musa Shirazi qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara zarihahu wa-llah 'ad 'alayna min barakatihi wa anatihi wa ta'idatihi wa... wa taqwalahu Allahumma... al-fath wa al-istifadah bi-ahsani ma yakun bi-jah Sayyid al-mursalin Mawlana... wa ahl baytihi al-tayyibin al-tahirin al-ma'sumin. Al-Fatihah.* *Bismillah...*

*Wa bi-niyyat Sayyidina 'Umar bin al-Sultan Mutlaq Badr al-Din bin Hindush al-Hindi, akhdh sharr dhu al-faqr al-nabiyy, wa 'alam al-millah al-Haji Sharaf 'Ali Usamuddin, Mawla Ghulam Husayn, Sayyid al-hudud al-muqaddas, al-razi wa al-sundari, rizwana Allah 'alayh. Wa ruhi al-quds ruhullah ta'ala, wa quwwat din Allah ta'ala, Yahudu, Yahudu, Yahudu, miway, khuduyat, pi, pishi, munkar, mumin, yaz, farans, allah wa da'i al-'alam. Wa sharr kull dharr... wa ayyam, wa sharr al-naffathat wa al-qusurat, wa ta'til al-tasbihat, wa ta'til al-tahmidat, wa al-muwazirat, wa al-wajib, wa a'dh wa al-mukhas wa yahfazuna wa yahfuzu 'umrihi wa ayyida Allah wa uqtana yahmi fi himayat al-rabbaniyyah al-sha'sha'aniyyah wa ta'la kulli hayatihi wa fi a'la maqami wa darajatihi wa-shrikna iyyakum fi sa'ir da'watihi wa yarzuqna wa iyyakum mahabbat wa ta'at wa khidmat Sayyidina Mawlana... wa ahl baytihi al-tayyibin al-tahirin al-salawatullah alayhim ajma'in. Al-Fatihah.* *Bismillah...*

These *awliya* who are there, they are very eager to receive help. Meaning, the help that comes from Allah's side, and the confirmations, they are very eager for them. There is an incident that Sayyidna Hibatullah Sahib, whose book we are reading, after the Fajr prayer, he was sitting and reading or writing something. Until the light of dawn... in the courtyard he was writing. Until the light of the sun was spreading and it was falling on his face and body. So, after some time had passed, his wife sent a message through a servant that "Tell Mawlana to go and sit in the shade. Where he is sitting, a lot of sun is coming. Go and sit in the shade." So, you said to the servant that "I am afraid that if I get up from here and go to the shade, the connection I have established, of the materials of confirmation from the Imam's side, it will be broken." That is, it will be broken. Therefore, he was so eager for it that the connection of confirmations that is going on, if I move my location from here, it will break. So, in this way, these people... because those confirmations and materials are not an ordinary thing. Due to which, they attain *'ismah*.

*Insha'Allah*. Thank you.

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 8 -->

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. *Astaghfirullah*. *Sallallahu 'ala rasulihi Sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala aali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallam* (May Allah's blessings and peace be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon the family of Muhammad).

Up to this point, it was mentioned that the evil imaginations/conceptions (*tasawwurat-e-khabisa*) cause distress in various ways, and they wander (*jawlani*) in the air, and they are called *munaf* because of their loud cries and wailing, and it is said that they are called *munaf*. To the point that *an tahillu fihi min imra'atin jahilah* (that they enter into an ignorant woman). So they *hulul* (enter into), meaning they enter such an ignorant woman.

The *munaf*... is *munaf* the plural of *munif*?

The *munaf*... is *munaf* the plural of *munif*?

*Munaf*... here *munaf*... no, *munaf* is something else. *Munaf* means... uh... uh... the sound of screaming and shouting that comes out... in a state of panic, or in a state of fear, or in a state of distress, that is called *munaf*.

Yes, in this same context, the word *munaf*, is it the plural of *munif*?

*Munaf*... No, it's... with *'ayn*.

It's *nun-'ayn-fa'*.

That one has an *alif*. The *alif* comes first. Munaf has an *alif*. Munaf, right, Alim Shah Bhai's son, Munaf. That has an *alif*. That has an *alif*.

This one is *munaf*. *Nun-'ayn-fa'*. *Munaf*.

If you understand `munaf` to be from *alif*, it is from *anf*, meaning 'one with a high nose'. Meaning, honorable. From *anf*. From *anf*. Honorable. One with a high nose, as they say. And here, *munaf* means that screaming and shouting which is born of dread (*wahshat*). That is called *munaf*.

To the point that they *hulul* (enter) into such an ignorant woman, or inside a boy who remains dirty, or whatever else resembles it. And they enter into him and throw him down. And he begins to speak on his tongue. And it whispers to him... it puts whispers into him, and it commands him to sins. And sometimes it leaves him, and sometimes it clings to him until he dies. They remain mounted upon him. Just as the condition is. *Dhalika al-masruh* (That is the one who is seized/possessed). This is the one they throw down. *wa faraqahu dhalik al-tasawwur* (and that conception separates from him). Then that conception which is there, it separates from him, *wa saara ya'wi* (and it goes to take shelter)... *ma'* (with) ... and on the side. That conception then takes shelter, with its forms, *ma'a ashkalihi min al-suwar al-najisah* (with its forms from the impure images). The impure images that exist, with them, it takes shelter *ila al-marari wal-qifar* (in the deserts and wastelands), in the great plains, the deserts, there, and in the wastelands, and in such places that are dirty. Their shelters are there. Meaning these evil imaginations/conceptions (*tasawwurat-e-khabisa*) take shelter here.

They are left in confinement for 40 days, then they get loose. Then they enter people's bodies, they possess them.

Yes, in these 40 days, they are doing all these antics.

In this, what the author of the book has written is that they remain with a person until they die.

Yes, this also happens, this is also true. No, it means that for 40 days they do these antics, but those that *hulul* (enter), it can also be that they remain inside them until death.

Those that enter someone's body, they corrupt their faith, they... I mean, they incline them towards sin. So in that, then how will their reckoning be done? For whom this has occurred...

Then *minhum fihim* (from them, in them). Why did it go inside them? Because they had inclinations. The one they entered was not pure and clean. They had inclinations.

But being impure is one thing, and entering them and corrupting their faith is another...

No, it is for a time, but the one who is possessed (`masruh`), the one they have entered, they have inclinations towards them. Without inclination, nothing can happen. As the Holy Qur'an says: *al-khabithuna lil-khabithat wal-khabithat lil-khabithin* (Evil men are for evil women, and evil women are for evil men). That the good are for the good, and the bad are for the bad. If there were no inclinations, then it would not happen. It is because of the inclinations that all this has happened. And even the friends of Allah (`awliya' Allah`) could not escape these inclinations.

They mount the two knots (*ughdatayn*) then?

Yes. They mount the two knots after that. We will mention this as well. They mount the two knots.

And... and... and towards the bathhouses, and where wine is drunk, their shelters are there. *wa al-shi'ab wa tara'a lil-nas fi manamatihim* (and the ravines, and it appears to people in their dreams). And they also appear to people in their dreams. *wa yarizuhum 'ala al-af'al al-khana'* (and it incites them to wicked deeds). And it incites them to do evil things. `wa 'adawat ahl al-haqq` (and enmity towards the people of truth). And these images are blameworthy jinn. And they are devils (*shayatin*), and they are `'afarit` (powerful demons). `al-mutamarridun al-darun` (the rebellious, the harmful ones) who cause harm to humans. `al-muhdithuna fihim al-ahdath` (the ones who innovate events within them), and they make them commit many sins.

Some of them deserve to remain `min ajnas` (from the kinds) in the `barazikh al-'adhab` (isthmuses of punishment). And this is how they remain in various isthmuses of punishment. `fa awwaluha` (and the first of them)... and then what happens to them is that they endure the seven plunges, after death. These... whose death is a bad one, they face the seven plunges of punishment, which is also called `al-nahas`. So the first is being cast in the form of a `Zanj`. First of all, it is being cast in the form of a `Zanj`. Meaning, they come in a human form, those who are Abyssinian, etc. `wa man yushakiluhu` (and whoever resembles him), people like that. `fa idha ikhtaza bi-dhalika al-dhakaru wal-untha` (so when the male and female are nourished by that), and when any man or woman eats these things in which they have `hulul`, *zahar shay'un minhu* (something from it appears), *zahara dhalika khabithan* (that appears as something evil). *mathalu walidayhi* (like his parents). *wa yu'inu muddah ma yasta'iqqu thumma yamut* (and he is helped for a period that he deserves, then he dies). They remain with them for a period. *wa tashi'u nafsuhu fi jismihi* (and his soul spreads in his body). And then their soul becomes widespread in them. This is a regression. *wa hiyya al-'aqasur, awwalan tasiru fi-mithl al-'awdah, nafsuhu fi jismihi*. And then they walk a reverse path, until their soul becomes widespread in their body. *thumma takaththafat bil-ma'asi* (then it became dense with sins). And from sins, they become completely dense. *yasiru jisman lahu, thumma tamut* (then it dies). Then their death occurs. `wa tanhallu... marratan ula, wa tasiru nabatan` (and it dissolves... the first time, and becomes a plant), `wa taqtadihi man yastahiqqu al-liqa'a... 'inda... mathal al-nasnas` (and he is led by one who deserves the meeting... like the nasnas/ape-like creature). And until it becomes a plant. Until then they come in the form of monkeys. And the `langur` and bears, they come in their forms. `thumma tamut ba'da dhalika` (then it dies after that). And then their death occurs. `wa... tajzilu... wa al-sar'u alladhina yatlubun al-ruqa` (and the epilepsy for which they seek incantations). And the things that distance them are the *ruqa* (incantations), meaning amulets. And `aza'im`, which is a type of amulet. `wa qira'at al-Qur'an` (and the recitation of the Quran). `fa yahrubuna` (so they flee). So they run away from there. `wa yansarifuna` (and they turn away), and they turn their face away. `wa la tazalu kullu suratin minhum ta'wi fi al-amakin al-wahsha` (and every form from them continues to take shelter in dreadful places). But it continues to happen that every dreadful form enters dreadful places. Meaning their place is these filthy places. `ma sha'a al-mudabbir` (as long as the Director wills). `thumma yas'adu ba'da dhalika` (then he ascends after that). Then they ascend after that. `ila dhanab al-shay'` (to the tail of the thing). And they are towards the tail, meaning the two knots (`ughdatayn`). *wa hiyya zulmah* (and it is a darkness). *tusamma al-dhanab wal-ra's* (it is called the tail and the head). `al-dhanab wal-ra's`. `kharijan 'an nitaq al-falak` (outside the sphere of the celestial orb). They are outside the sphere of the celestial orb. And... [unclear] `wa yatadhi` (and it is nourished)... *muthl al-qurud* (like monkeys), *wa al-kilab* (and dogs), *wa al-khanazir* (and pigs). `wa jumlatun... wa yasir fi sa'ir al-suwar al-madhmumah` (and he goes into all other blameworthy forms). Until they also enter into various animals. Becoming an animal. `thumma idha istawfa dhalik al-ajal kana fi barazikh al-najasat` (then when that term is fulfilled, he is in the isthmuses of impurity). Until when they complete the stages of their punishment, `thumma fi al-nabat`. Then they come in the form of a plant. *wa yasiru nabatan* (and it becomes a plant). And they become such a plant that is *samman qatilan* (a killing poison), which is poisonous and kills. `murran` (bitter), `muhlikan` (destructive). `wa kullama istahala wa tahayyama sara ila asfal min dhalika` (and whenever it transforms and wanders, it goes to something lower than that). And in this way, they also come into things lower than that, until they complete the parts of the plant's `barzakh`. `thumma yarji'u ila al-ma'dan`. Then they enter into minerals, `ajmal al-madhmumah` (the most blameworthy minerals). `mathal al-zaj` (like glass), `kibrit` (sulfur), `al-hadid` (iron), and `al-usas` (lead), brass. They come in these forms. `wa ma shakal al-wurud... thumma idha istawfa ma yastahiqqu min al-ma'dan` (and what forms the flowers... then when it fulfills what it deserves from the mineral realm). Until they also complete all the stages of the mineral realm. `wa qada bi-isti'adatih marrah ba'da marrah` (and it is decreed by its repetition time after time). And one state after another. `wa husulu hululihi fi surat al-haywan` (and the occurrence of its entry in the form of an animal). And after that their coming is in the form of an animal. `ay yakunu bi-istihalah wa al-wiladah kama la yara ahl al-tanasukh` (that it be through transformation and birth, not as the people of transmigration see). In this way, not in the way that the people of reincarnation (*tanasukh*) have this belief (`aqidah`), that *inna al-nafsa tantaqilu min jism al-mayyit 'inda mawtihi* (that the soul transfers from the body of the deceased at his death), that the soul of the deceased leaves his body and enters the body of another. So this belief of the people of *tanasukh* is not our belief. We have a different belief. `wa anna dhalika dalal` (and that is misguidance). And that belief is a misguiding belief, and it is ignorance. `la yajibu i'tiqaduhu bal halik` (it is not obligatory to believe it, rather one is destroyed). That it is not obligatory to believe in it, rather the one who believes in it is destroyed, and its believer is cursed. `thumma idha istawfa min 'adhabi fi hadha al-barazikh` (then when he is fulfilled from the punishment of this barzakh). When their punishments are completed. `wa hiyya al-'adhab al-adna` (and it is the lesser punishment). This is the lesser punishment. These are seven stages of punishment, which are the lesser punishments. So, `inhalla min akhirha` (it dissolves from its end). Until they reach the stage of the final punishment. `'inda qurb qiyam al-qa'im` (when the rising of the Qa'im is near). When the rising of the `Qa'im` is near. At that time, they will have endured all the stages of punishment. `thumma yulhaqu bil-sa'ir` (then it is joined with the fire). Then they are ground. `wa al-nabat` (and the plant). They come into the state of a plant. `wa yughtadha bihi min jinsihim` (and people of their kind are nourished by it). And then people of their kind eat those plants. From among humans. `wa kharaja shakhsan bashariyyan` (and a human person came out). And from them that human person is born. From eating that plant, that person is born. Then, `wa yuhsharu 'inda qiyam al-qa'im` (and he is gathered at the rising of the Qa'im). And then that person is made apparent at the time of the rising of the `Qa'im`. `wa yuwaffaqu 'ala ma salafa minhu` (and he is confronted with what proceeded from him). And the `Qa'im` tells him what things have proceeded from him. `wa yubakkituhu 'ala af'alihi` (and He rebukes him for his actions). And He rebukes him for his actions. *thumma yudhbah* (then he is slaughtered). Then he is slaughtered. `thumma tahriquhu al-nar` (then the fire burns him). And the fire burns him. `al-nazilah min al-athir` (that which descends from the ether). And that fire descends from the pure ether. `thumma yuslaqu bihi ba'da dhalika ila al-sijjin` (then he is led by it after that to Sijjin). And when this happens, they are brought towards `Sijjin`. Meaning, towards `Sakhar` (the rock). `wa yakhludu fi al-'adhab al-akbar` (and he abides in the greatest punishment). And then the greatest punishment, which is the punishment of `Sakhar`, which is a severe punishment, they are kept there. For the duration of the Great Cycle. `wa huwa thalathah alf mi'at alf sanah wa sittin wa alf sanah` (and that is three hundred thousand, one hundred thousand years and sixty and one thousand years). And that is 360,000 years.

At that time, no one will express remorse?

No, at that time there is no remorse. Those who are to go to the greatest punishment, there is no remorse then. Remorse is during the time of the lesser punishment. There is remorse in every *karr* (cycle). At every *ghark* (plunge), there is remorse. Here, *jaffa al-qalam* (the pen has dried), the matter is settled.

360,000 years multiplied by 360,000 years.

`A'adhana Allahu min dhalika` (May Allah protect us from that). May Allah protect us from all these things, `bi-hawlihi wa quwwatihi` (by His might and His power). `'ahd sayyidina Muhammad wa aalihi al-sifat al-'alin`. `hadha la yakunu illa 'ala al-azdad al-kibar` (this only happens to the great opposites). And these things that are happening, that they are being taken to the greatest punishment, towards `Sakhar`, and will be kept for one Great Cycle, this is only for the `azdad` (opposites) who are the great ones. Those who opposed the friends of Allah (`awliya' Allah`) and those who killed them, it is for them. `wa 'ayn al-ma'asi wa al-khubatha' wa al-ashrar` (and the essence of sins, and the wicked, and the evil). But the other people who are sinners, who are wicked, and who are evil. `wa amma kafat al-hamaj al-ri'a'` (and as for the generality of the rabble). And the general rabble. Meaning the ignorant people, who joined the evil ones like sheep. `fa-la yasiluna ila tilka bi-'inayat Allahi ta'ala` (so they do not reach that, by the care of Allah Ta'ala). So they do not receive this punishment. They do not get the greatest punishment. `li-annahu la yujaza kullu 'abdin bi-qadr fi'lihi` (because every servant is not recompensed except by the measure of his deed). Because every servant is given recompense according to the measure of his deed. `bal inna al-mayyit min al-hamaj al-ri'a' idha mata` (but indeed the deceased from the rabble, when he dies). But the deceased from the rabble, the ignorant people, when he dies, his soul spreads in his body. `wa lam yufariqhu illa al-hawaiyyah` (and nothing leaves him except the airy self). And nothing leaves him except the airy self. Just as from a believer, the spiritual souls (`nufus-e-rihiyya`) depart. But from this one, the airy souls (`nufus-e-hawaiyya`) depart. `allati dhakartuha` (which I have mentioned). `thumma yatahallalu wa yasiru min al-barazikh al-madhkurah` (then it dissolves and proceeds from the mentioned isthmuses). Then until he reaches the mentioned isthmuses, meaning towards the lesser punishment. `fi ma yastahiqqu minha 'ala qadr 'amalihi` (in what it deserves from it according to the measure of its deed). As his deed is. `ay yastahiqqu ba'daha aw kullaha, thumma yarji'u sa'iran ila al-istihalah wa al-wiladah ila surat al-bashariyyah` (that it deserves some of it or all of it, then it returns, proceeding to the transformation and birth into a human form). Then he is also made to ascend, into a human form. `wa yu'radu 'alayhi al-da'wah` (and the mission is presented to him). And the noble mission is presented to him. Meaning, the noble covenant (`'ahd-e-sharif`) is presented. From the Imam. `fa in istajaba` (so if he responds). If he accepts, then he becomes pure. `wa illa radda ila ma yastahiqqu bi-iktisabihi` (and if not, he is returned to what he deserves because of his earnings). If not, then he is returned to what he deserves. `marratan thaniyah` (a second time). A second time also he is returned. After receiving punishment once, if he doesn't accept, he is again put into punishment. `li-anna Allah ta'ala 'adil` (because Allah Ta'ala is Just). Because Allah Ta'ala is Just. `la yazlimu al-'ibad` (He does not oppress the servants). He does not oppress His servants. `wa la yukhlifu al-mi'ad` (and He does not break the promise). And He does not go against His promise. `wa al-gharadu kulluhu fi nash'at al-khilqah` (and the entire purpose is in the genesis of creation). And the whole purpose is to give genesis to creation. Meaning to be born again. `wa istikhlasuha` (and its purification). And to give them salvation. Slowly, by presenting the mission to them, they should be given salvation. `mimma waqa'at fihi min al-khati'ah` (from the sin into which it has fallen). From the sins they have committed, they should be taken out of it. `wa inkar... wa man khalusa fa-sa'ida` (and denial... and whoever is purified, he is saved). And whoever becomes pure, he ascends. `wa man aba wa 'anada al-hudud ittukisa` (and whoever refuses and shows enmity to the Hudud, he is inverted). And whoever refuses and shows enmity to the *hudud*, he falls. `wa habata` (and he fell). And he falls from his rank. `haris ayyuha al-akh` (be eager, O brother). O my brother, become greedy for action. `wa al-takhallus` (and purification). And in purifying yourself. `wa al-takhaffuf min al-dhunub` (and lightening of sins). And in lessening the sins. `wa la takhtar bi-al-dunya` (and do not be deceived by the world). And do not be deceived by the world. `wa hiyya za'ilah` (for it is transient). For it is a transient world. `wa 'mal kama qala amir al-mu'minin salawatullahi 'alayh` (and act as the Commander of the Faithful, peace of Allah be upon him, has said). And act as the Commander of the Faithful, peace of Allah be upon him, has said. `takhaffafu talhaqu` (lighten yourselves and you will catch up). Lighten yourselves, and you will catch up. Meaning, lessen the weight of your sins, so that you may become worthy, for your high rank. `fa-innama yantaziru bi-awwalikum qudum akhirikum` (for the first of you are but awaiting the arrival of the last of you). For your first ones are waiting for the arrival of your last ones. Meaning your former self, that which has already passed, is waiting for you, that you may join it. `fa'lam dhalika wa hadha sirr al-malakut` (so know that, and this is the secret of the celestial realm). So know this, that this is the secret of the celestial realm. `i'tamadtuka 'alayh` (I have entrusted you with it). I have entrusted you with it. `fa-sunhu yasunka Allah` (so protect it, may Allah protect you). So protect it, may Allah protect you. `wa-hfazhu min kulli ahad` (and protect it from everyone). And hide it from everyone. `yahfazka Allah` (may Allah protect you). May Allah protect you. `illa ma dhakartu` (except what I have mentioned). ...what I have mentioned to you. `wa Allahu 'alayka shahid` (and Allah is a witness over you). And Allah is a witness over you. `wa al-wafa' bi-al-amanah` (and the fulfillment of the trust). So you fulfill the trust. `matalib, fa-hazar kull al-hazar min al-ta'addi fi shay'` (demands, so be extremely wary of transgressing in anything). And... fear... be afraid completely of transgressing in anything. `wa Allahu 'ala ma naqulu wakil` (and Allah is a guardian over what we say). And what we are saying, Allah is a guardian over it. Meaning, He is a witness. `waffaqana Allahu wa iyyakum li-mardatihi` (may Allah grant us and you success for His pleasure). May Allah grant us success, and you, that we may do what pleases Him. `wa khatama lana wa laka wa li-kaffat ikhwanina bi-khayr al-khatimah` (and may He seal for us, and for you, and for all of our brothers, with the best of endings). And may He seal our end, and yours, and all our brothers', with the best of endings. `wa al-hamdu lillahi rabb al-'alamin wa salatuhu 'ala Muhammad wa aalihi khatam al-nabiyyin wa 'ala 'ali ibn abi talib ashraf al-wasiyyin wa al-a'immah min dhurriyyatihim al-tahirin al-tayyibin al-salawatullahi 'alayhim ajma'in`. (And praise be to Allah, the Lord of the worlds, and His blessings be upon Muhammad and his family, the seal of the prophets, and upon 'Ali ibn Abi Talib, the noblest of successors, and the Imams from their progeny, the pure, the good, may the blessings of Allah be upon them all).

Have you mentioned that there are such people who then ascend and become `du'at`? Have you pinpointed any, who the `du'at` were?

Yes, yes. It's mentioned in a book. If you become... then I'll pinpoint it. Right now it's not in my mind. But it is mentioned that they reached the rank of `bab al-abwab`. And it's mentioned how much service they performed. For one... for thousands of years they performed service. After that, such purity was created in them that they reached that rank. And this is also there, isn't it, that the `du'at`, `ma'adhib`, and `hudud al-da'wah` who serve with sincerity, they are given the recompense that in their future cycles (`karrat`), they will attain high ranks. So, what is the criterion for becoming `ummahat al-a'immah` (mothers of Imams)? The mothers who are of the Imams, it's a matter of great honor, isn't it, to become the mother of an Imam. And she is infallible, and she is `batul`, meaning she does not have menstrual blood. She is pure and clean. So, the cause for them to become so, who are these women who become like this? It is said that `qad sabaqathunna al-khidam` (service has preceded them). That they are people who have performed much service in the times of the Imams. Those who performed extreme service are given the recompense that they become mothers of this kind. For example, Sayyidna Muhammad bin al-Walid, or Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawad, who performed such services. So this... I'm giving an example, that such individuals who perform service are given great recompense.

In the next cycle (`wahdah`), like Salman, was he... will Salman's story be the same, that he will come from Fars, will be a non-Muslim, and in this way...

No, the template will be the same, but their succession (`khilafat`) is established. The one with whom the matter happened, he had veils (`hijabat`). The higher veil ascends. And that higher veil is called `muhtajib` (the veiled one). Meaning he becomes so high that he becomes a veil for other veils.

In the next cycle (`wahdah`), their excesses (`fuzalat`) are there, which... those who...

The `fuzalat` that will remain... that's how they will ascend.

Yes, their `fuzalat` that remain, the purified `fuzalat` ascend. The `fuzalat` that remain, they become their veils (`hijabat`) and appear.

He was born a Parsi in Fars. So what about his parents? Where was he brought from, and then from there...

Yes, yes. He came out in search of the truth. He... he came in search of the truth, and then somehow he reached...

He was hidden since childhood.

Yes, yes. In the apparent, this is the story, that he came in search of the truth, and somehow... and then you see, in the entire Makkan period of Rasulullah's life, Salman al-Farsi is not mentioned. When did he come? He came to Madinah al-Munawwarah. He came around 2 Hijri. Two Hijri. And after coming, the progress he made was such that `Salman minna Ahl al-Bayt` (Salman is from us, the Ahl al-Bayt) was said.

He was very intelligent.

`Salman minna Ahl al-Bayt` was said. And he was brought as a slave. And Rasulullah... he was brought in a caravan as a slave. And Rasulullah freed him from slavery. That is, the Jewish man he was with, he made a deal with him, and 300 date palms... he planted 300 date palms, and then the Muslims planted 300, and then a lot of gold was also given. Gold was also given. And he saw the signs in Rasulullah, that he accepts a gift but does not eat charity. And on your back, there is the seal of prophethood. These three signs were there. So when you came, someone gave you a gift of dates. So you asked that person, "What is this?" He said, "This is a gift in your service." So you ate it yourself and also gave it to the companions. Then, two or three days later, it happened that a person came, he had brought a bundle of dates. And you asked, "What is this?" He said, "This is my `zakat`." So you recited the du'a, because the Qur'an commands: `khudh min amwalihim sadaqatan tutahhiruhum wa tuzakkihim biha wa salli 'alayhim inna salataka sakanun lahum` (Take, [O, Muhammad], from their wealth a charity by which you purify them and cause them increase, and invoke [Allah's blessings] upon them. Indeed, your invocations are a source of reassurance for them). The term `salli 'alayhim` means "pray for them," not "send salutations upon them." That your praying for him is a source of tranquility for him. For whom is it a source of tranquility? For that person, for his family, for his form. It is a source of tranquility for all. So, he recited the du'a and asked, "What is this?" The man said, "This is `zakat`." So you distributed that `zakat`. So Salman was watching all this. Because the Ahl al-Bayt do not eat `zakat`. Zakat is filth (`mayl`). It is filth, and the Ahl al-Bayt are pure and clean, they do not eat filth. But, I was asked whether we can eat the meat of a `sadaqah` goat. I said, "Are you Ahl al-Bayt?" You can eat the meat of a `sadaqah` goat. Even if it's your own `sadaqah` goat, or a goat for a vow (`nadhr`), a person can eat it himself and distribute it to others. There is no problem with that. However, for the goat of `'aqiqah`, one should be careful that its bones are not broken. And the second thing to be careful about is that if she is the mother, the mother is breastfeeding the child, and it is not permissible for the child, the one for whom the `'aqiqah` is, it is not permissible for him. Now, that meat, the child will not eat because he is only a few days old. So if the mother eats it, it will reach him through the mother's milk. His share of the `'aqiqah` will reach him. Therefore, the mother should not eat it either. From this perspective. If the mother is not breastfeeding him, she can eat it. But mothers mostly breastfeed. So she should not eat it. Because it is not permissible for the child. Just as during Hajj, the meat of our obligatory sacrifice, we cannot eat it. One cannot eat it oneself. But if it's a voluntary sacrifice, a voluntary Hajj, then one can eat one's own meat as well. In a voluntary one. But for the obligatory one, it is necessary that... one has to be very careful during Hajj. That the meat of the obligatory sacrifice should be kept separate. One has to act according to that procedure.

Al-hamdulillah, our third book is finished. And, Insha'Allah, tomorrow, I am thinking that since we have so far studied `Mabda' wa Ma'ad` and Tayyibi `haqa'iq`, which we have just read. So now, we will study a book of `shari'at`, Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan's. So Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan has written a marvelous book, `Kitab al-Zahrah`. Although `Da'a'im` is available for us for `shari'at`, and other books are also available, and Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn sahib has also mentioned many points of `shari'at` in `Sharh al-Masa'il`. But Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan's style is completely different. So you will see that although it is a book of `shari'at`, he has revealed great subtleties (`rumuz`) in a very fine manner within it. So, Insha'Allah, that... the photocopy of that book has arrived. It has arrived, Insha'Allah. Okay, then we'll have to make copies, otherwise, it will be a problem. Or we can... photocopy it. The one that's there, we can do about 10-15 pages of it. Yes. Insha'Allah.

I had two questions. Yes. You just mentioned that because of their good deeds (`hasanat`) and services (`khidmat`), they will become `Umm al-A'immah`. So, is it... for them or anyone else, is it possible for a man to become a woman, or a woman to become a man, in the cycles (`karrat`)? In one cycle he is a man, and in another cycle he is...

Yes. In one cycle a person came as a man. As you mentioned, through service, she becomes `Umm al-A'immah`. So is it... does this happen for anyone, that a man becomes a woman or a woman becomes a man?

No, it is a rare case. This... `istisna'` (exceptional) case... an exceptional case, it's a very rare case. But this... what happens to their case then... for cycles upon cycles... they will remain women, or... No, I haven't read about this, but the thing is, there is no degradation in them becoming a woman from a man. Rather, they have attained very high ranks. They have made great progress. As it is said for Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, that in the next cycles (`karrat`), he will become Mawlatuna Zaynab. So, there are various such cases, through which their good deeds are fulfilled.

My second question is that we have learned about the believers, and then about the disobedient ones who were completely evil, `azdad`, or lesser `azdad`. They had stubbornness, of course. But what about a common Muslim who takes the name of Allah and the Prophet, prays... what will be their case?

Their... their... the common Muslims, even if they take the name of Allah and the Prophet, pray, perform Hajj, but if they have not taken the noble covenant (`'ahd-e-sharif`), what happens is that their soul becomes widespread in their body. And because of this spreading, they feel the punishments. And their evil conception, meaning the conception they hold of not accepting Sayyidna 'Ali as the successor or deputy of Rasulullah, and their love for others, all this is their evil conception. So that... that evil conception separates from them, and it wanders. Yes. But their condition is not like that of other infidels and polytheists. There is a difference between the condition of infidels and polytheists and the condition of Muslims. And among them, those who are noble Muslims, who have lived a pure and clean life, they are called `sulaha` (righteous) and `'afifa` (chaste). So these chaste people are given the opportunity to enter through the noble covenant. Meaning, they come and enter the lowest ranks of the believers and become part of the faith. And it happens very quickly. Their case is settled very quickly. From `sulaha` and `'afifa`, they very quickly enter the fold of faith.

When we were in Madinah al-Munawwarah for `hijrat`, at `jannat al-qiyari`. There, we used to enter `jannat al-qiyari` very easily. So Sayyidna sahib came, Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan. And he also came and sat in the `qiyari`. So we were sitting in your vicinity. Me and Asad bhai. So then, a person who was perhaps ahead of us, in the row in front, he asked us, "Who is this Mawlana, who has such a luminous quality? And when he arrived, everyone stood up. And we also saw, what a luminous quality he has." Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan. So we said, "Yes, he is one of our elders. And we study with him." I mentioned it in this way. Anyway, after that happened, after a little while, Sayyidna sahib asked us, `hadha min al-`afif` (this one is from the chaste). This person who was talking to you, he was from the `'afif`. Meaning, he was such a person that soon he would enter the fold of faith. From `sulaha` to `'afif`. Now, the `Da'i` makes these indications. He knows all these things. So there are various such states, `sulaha`, `'afifa`.

Hazrat Qaid Johar Mukhlesin had said in Kuwait, the one who had given the land... Yes, yes. About Hasan Molen. Yes, Hasan Molen. He said `sulaha` and `'afif` for him. Yes.

*Balligh Allahumma thawab al-Fatihah, hadiyyatan wasilatan thaniyyatan zakiyyatan mardiyyatan, ila ruh Sayyidina Hibatillah bin Musa al-Shirazi, qaddasa Allahu ruhahu al-munawwarah al-zahira, wa da'afa 'alayna min barakatihi wa 'anatihi wa ta'yidatihi, wa taqabbal minna ya Allah hadha al-fadah wal-istifadah bi-ahsan ma yakun, bi-haqq Sayyidina al-Mursalin wa al-Qur'an al-'azim wa Ahl Baytihi al-Tayyibin al-Tahirin, salawatullahi wa salamuhu 'alayhim ajma'in, al-Fatihah*. [Recitation of Fatiha].

...[Du'a for the Da'i al-Mutlaq]...

The name of the scribe is not here. Who the scribe is, we don't know. Otherwise, we would recite a `Fatihah` for the scribe too, usually. That the scribe has put in the effort to write this. But the scribe's name is not here. Because out of humility, some... some scribes are such that out of humility they don't write their names.

No, the `khutbah` which is there... that `khutbah` is different. The script is different.

No, it's not written. It's written at the end. At the very end. The `khutbah`... It is written, isn't it?

No, not here. At the very end. At the very end... `Abd al-Husayn`... It is there. `ahqar al-'ibad`. At the very end. At the end...

`bi-yad al-ahqar al-'ibad 'Abd al-Husayn, ghafara Allahu lahu` (by the hand of the most insignificant of servants, 'Abd al-Husayn, may Allah forgive him). It's during the time of Hazrat Zali sahib. Hazrat Zali sahib's time. In the court of our revered master Sayyid 'Ali `ala al-fadl sahib`. Meaning, the time was of Hazrat Zali sahib muqaddas, Nuruddin Malik's time. And Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn sahib's. But the one who ordered the writing was Hazrat Fazl sahib muqaddas. This `Abd al-Husayn`, it's not clear who he is. But... he should have written his father's name. But he hasn't written it.

*Balligh Allahumma thawab al-Fatihah, hadiyyatan wasilatan, ila ruh 'Abd al-Husayn... taqabbala Allahu khidmatahu bi-haqq Sayyidina wa Mawlana wa 'ala aali Sayyidina Muhammad wa aalihi wa barik wa sallim. Al-Fatihah*. [Recitation of Fatiha].

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 9 -->

In the *hadith* it says that the contemplation a human being does, if his thought is correct, then his thought is greater than 50 years of his worship. If he is contemplating on the Divine Sciences, on the verses of the Holy Quran, and his thought, the direction of his thought, is absolutely correct, then his, that is a great worship. Because intellectual worship, the intellectual form, is greater than the practical form. And knowledge has great importance, that any Prophet, Allah Ta'ala did not make him ignorant. Meaning, knowledge became a cause for their making. Be it any Prophet, or any *da'i*, or whoever are the *hudud-e-'aliyah*, the thing of honor and distinction within them is knowledge. so they were incapable of perceiving it. And they are incapable of perceiving it. so he negated divinity from it. Thus, they negated divinity from it. From it, meaning the First Intellect itself negated divinity from its own self. and from the members of its own kind. and from the members of its own kind. That this divinity, which is the quality of bringing from non-existence to existence, it is neither in it nor in the members of its kind. and he bore witness of that for its Originator. And he gave testimony that this attribute, the attribute of divinity, belongs to its Originator. So through this act, it deserved to be named 'First'. And because of this act, because it adopted firstness, did it not, in this thought, therefore it was given the name First. and Precedent. First Intellect, that is. and Precedently. the First Precedent. In this way. And it is what is named the First Intellect and the First Originated. `Aql-e-Awwal` (First Intellect) it was called, and `Mubda'-e-Awwal` (First Originated). Meaning, the Originator, *Subhanahu*, is that Being who originated. And this is the originated. And among the originated things, it became the `Mubda'-ul-Awwal` (the First Originated). Meaning, it is the first person who kept a clean and pure thought. and by the First Originated, it became. and by the Pen. And it became the Pen. Yes. Then, indeed, there flowed to it... from its Originator, exalted and high is He... Yes. In the Holy Quran, an oath has been taken by Allah Ta'ala of the Pen: `Nun wa-l-qalam wa ma yasturun` (Nun. By the pen and what they inscribe). First, before the Pen, `Nun` is mentioned. Meaning, the Pen, which is the `Aql-e-Awwal` (First Intellect), the substance it received from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`... it was given precedence. And then the `Aql-e-Awwal` was mentioned by Allah Ta'ala. Then, indeed, there flowed to it from its Originator, exalted and high is He... which was specified for it to the exclusion of the rest of the members of its kind. and such a substance, such a help, which became specified for it, leaving aside the other members of its kind. Meaning, it specifically received help. Yes. as a recompense for it... as a recompense for it. And this reward was given to it because of its thought and its firstness, this reward was given to it. for its professing of oneness (*tawhid*), its glorification (*tasbih*), and its acknowledgment of divinity. that the *tawhid* that was issued from it... the *tawhid* it professed of the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`, and its glorification, and it acknowledged divinity. Just as... `Shahidallahu annahu la ilaha illa hu` (Allah witnesses that there is no deity except Him). So it acknowledged the Spirit... so because of that, the honor it attained, so that substance was made to flow upon it from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`. for the originated. for the originated. So through that matter, it became aware of the knowledge of what has been and what will be. and because of which, because of this substance, it... it became aware of what has been and what will be. Meaning, it obtained this knowledge. Because of that substance which it received from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`. You mean the previous... the previous one? The previous one, so what, what... The previous one, which previous one? Previous, meaning its... this... its... its... We are not talking about the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`. The knowledge it obtained from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`... the attainment of that knowledge, its... its... the substance that it is getting, of what nature it is, it came to know that. `ma kana` from `wa-ma sa-yakun`. So... the knowledge that was to come, whatever was to happen, it had knowledge of that? Yes. So why didn't it... At that time, we who are stuck, having come into this... so why didn't it stop at that time? No, that would have... The `jabr` (compulsion) would have... That was a matter of `ikhtiyar` (free will). `jabr` would have happened, no? If it had stopped it, then `jabr` would have happened. Therefore, there was `ikhtiyar`. The three things that were given... But my saying this means that what was going to happen, it knew that this was going to happen. Yes, absolutely. That all this will happen, this mistake will be made and... so... the world will be created. Let alone the matter of the `Aql-e-Awwal`, for the `Ashir` (the Tenth) it has been said, `Zalika Taqdir-ul-'Aziz-il-'Alim` (That is the decree of the Exalted in Might, the All-Knowing). In Surah Yasin. This matter was in its decree, of the `Aziz-ul-'Alim` (the Mighty, the All-Knowing). That this... this group that has denied and has not acknowledged... so what he said, `Ihbitu minha jami'an` (Descend from it, all of you), you all will have to fall. This that is... the `Alam-e-Latafat` (World of Subtlety) is, you will have to go into the `Alam-e-Kathafat` (World of Density), and after being purified there, here you will have to ascend through the intellect. So this that... so its knowledge, that this group that is, which... has acquired physicality, so in purifying this physicality, how many `mawr` will it take, and in a `mawr` how many `qawr` will there be, and in a `qawr` how many `dawr` will there be, and in a `dawr` how many `sitr` and `kashf` will there be, it obtained the knowledge of all those things. This is a matter of the `Ashir` (the Tenth). So this is much higher than the `Ashir`. It made no mistake at all. And it... directly... received it from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`. No intermediary was there either. Its quality is very high. One thing is that... in the `arwah` (spirits) there was, there was also a negativity present in them? Yes, meaning... Positive is there... good, it is good... No, the three things... But there was negativity in them too. No, it wasn't negativity. The three, three things that were given to them... `Hayat` (life)... meaning `hayat` means in the most excellent state. In the most excellent state they were created, that there was no flaw of any kind. Rather, in the most excellent state, they were created. After that... `ilm` (knowledge) was given. That knowledge of everything was given to them. Of evil and of good. And power was given, that how they should use it. Their own... their own quality... power was given that how they should use this knowledge, their own life. Its complete... complete freedom was given by Allah Ta'ala. Because of that same freedom, their ranks were established. They moved forward, ranking happened, they became the pillars of the *da'wah*, because of this very thing. So these `arwah` that fell, they did not have this knowledge? They had it... that we will fall and this will be our state here. No, it... it... because the knowledge... the knowledge of all... Yes, that too was there... that too was there. Meaning, they were denying, denying the `Ashir` (the Tenth), it... they also had its knowledge, that if we deny, then... look in our own world, we are aware of all things, that we will get the reward for our good deeds in paradise, or our ranks will be high, or we will go to hell because of our bad deeds. Despite that, despite knowing, how many people do bad deeds? In our own life, look, no? That... we all know this. The... the evil... even among believers, you see, many believers who are... who adopt the path of waywardness. Don't they know? Don't they listen to sermons and advice? Don't they listen to gatherings? Despite that, waywardness is created in them. But that is because we have come into density, that's why all this... We have come into density, that's why all these things have come upon us. There it was a completely transparent matter. No, it was a transparent matter, but they had free will, no? Because of this free will, their ranks were established. Because look, among creations, who... who has free will? Only human beings have free will. And why do they have it? Because they were given physicality. There too, when they got physicality, when length, width, and depth came, and physicality came, at that time they had the choice of how to do it. And within that same choice, they also showed remorse, and they came into confirmation, and they also persisted. Yes. A small, small point arises here. When in the world of materiality, here a being of an Imam who is, he is the most noble for us. In the world of materiality. And we say that the being of the Imam is infallible, he cannot make a mistake at all, there is no possibility. So when the being of the Imam cannot make a mistake, its possibility is not there, then that is the World of Spirits. That is the World of Spirits. It is much higher. So there, there, the possibility of a mistake or not making a mistake, no question arises at all. No, but a mistake was made, no? They did not accept the word... they did not accept the word of the `Ashir` (the Tenth), despite his explaining. The delusion of equality that happened, that too is outside the realm of possibility, isn't it? No, the delusion of equality happened, so this... this that is... things were apparent on it. It... from it... just as it has been said, it had a delusion of equality, so a mistake occurred from it. It did not do it intentionally. Intentionally, its delusion of equality did not happen. And it did not persist in it. The `Munba'ith-e-Thani` (the Second Emitted one) did not persist in it. That whatever I did, I did better, it was not like that. It... it... its... when it realized that its rank was falling and that luminosity was not being created which is in my remaining intellects, then in the end, it... the ninth intellect's last person... from the last, last, last intellect, from the ninth, ninth circle's last intellect, it asked that what is the matter? I do not have this quality that is in you people. So then it said that this... you... the delusion of equality that you had, with your superior, from the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal` (the First Emitted one), so because of that this quality came in you. Yes. So therefore, it... when it acknowledged... its own mistake, it acknowledged it... and so it interceded for it. It interceded... with its higher... with the intellect before it. To the extent that its intercession, moving forward and forward, reached the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`. And the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal` forgave it. Yes. This has been said in the Holy Quran. It has been mentioned. Yes, sir, the `Aql-e-Awwal`'s, which is... the knowledge of `ma kana wa sa-yakun`, and the knowledge of the other intellects is... in that, just... in that, what is the difference? A clear difference. Of both? There is a very clear difference. The `Aql-e-Awwal`'s... the knowledge of `ma kana wa sa-yakun` and that of the remaining intellects, there is a very big difference in them. In appearance it is called the knowledge of `ma kana wa sa-yakun`, but each intellect's knowledge of `ma kana wa sa-yakun` is different. And it was distinguished by its honor, by its honor, and by the majesty and the greatness over the entire world of origination. and it became distinguished with that honor which it received because of that substance. And majesty and greatness were attained over the entire world of origination. On the world of origination, which... what is the world of origination? It is the intellects. Meaning, there is no such world in which Allah Ta'ala created the originated things. The world of origination, they are the intellects. Yes. Just as it is generally also that where is the spiritual world? It is conceived that it is upwards. The spiritual world is not upwards, rather it is all around us. All around us is the spiritual world. It is not that it is above us. and by that, it deserved... and by that, it deserved that the name of divinity should fall upon it. and it became deserving through that... In the world, they considered it above the throne, didn't they? It is not so. The spiritual world, what is it? the `Ashir`... above the `Ashir`... the `Ashir`... including the `Ashir`, above the `Ashir`, whatever there is, is the spiritual world. The spiritual world is that where there is the world of intellects. This other one who says that this `hayula` that is, which fell, it is equal to a dot in the World of Spirits. Yes. It is a dot. That World of Spirits is spread everywhere. And that dot is in the middle that is there. It is `hayula`. Yes. and it is that we are present in it. Yes, yes. And by that, it deserved... and it became deserving... the first... that the name of divinity should fall upon it, that the name of Allah should come upon it. for two reasons. for two reasons. One of them, one of them is that it became lost in love and bewildered in the perception of its Originator. that it became bewildered and perplexed, meaning stupefied, in perceiving its Originator. Meaning, what is the Originator like, what is it, since when is it, how is it, and why did it do it, in this matter, from perceiving it, it... it became bewildered within it. and it became incapable... and then after being bewildered, what happened? It became incapable of that thing. The incapacity to perceive Him is itself perception. The incapacity to perceive Him is itself perception. The great Amir al-Mu'minin says. The incapacity to perceive Him is itself perception. that to become incapable... Allah's... of attaining Allah, to become incapable of attaining is itself perception. Yes. In the Holy Quran also, that is, two aspects have been mentioned. `jahidu`... `jahidu`... One is `jihad`... `jihad` `fi-llahi`, `wa jahidu`... `jihad` is of two types, from the perspective of the Holy Quran. What word has been used for it? One is `jihad`... `jihad` is, and `wa jahidu fi-llahi` (and strive in the way of Allah), in Allah... do `jihad` within Allah. So what is `jihad` with Allah? It is the gnosis of the *hudud* of the *da'wah*. That is doing `jihad` within Allah. So for this reason the name of Allah came upon it. And the second reason, the second reason is that the entire world of origination (`ibda'`) was lost in love for it and was bewildered in its majesty and its greatness. that the entire `alam-e-ibda'` (world of origination), the world of intellects, was itself bewildered about it and stupefied at its majesty and greatness. with which it was specified. that which it was specified with, meaning the substance and the help from Allah that it was receiving... They were bewildered about the `Aql-e-Awwal` (First Intellect). Yes, about the `Aql-e-Awwal`, they were bewildered, the intellects of the `alam-e-ibda'` (world of origination). by what was of its action. that what did it do that this virtue was attained by it from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`. And it is also named by divinity. not for its bewilderment and its longing... for the perception of the majesty of its Originator... to perceive the majesty of its Originator... and its incapacity, which returns from that. that its incapacity, it was making it turn back from its perception. So all that we have mentioned, the name of divinity fell upon it. and whatever we have mentioned, it is manifest upon it, the name of divinity. The name of Allah comes upon it. And it is eternal in its ultimate end, not eternal in its beginning. It is eternal in its ultimate end. and not eternal in its beginning. Now this is a thing to understand, that what does it mean? It is eternal in its ultimate end, not eternal in its beginning. Allah knows best what its meaning is. Then, when there appeared to him... when there appeared to it, for this great `hadd`, two forms from those forms, so he looked with his gaze, and he negated divinity from their two beings and from the members of their two kinds. And... for us, in the physical world, Rasulullah is at the station of the `Aql-e-Awwal`. Rasulullah... And therefore, just as from the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`... inspiration came upon the `Aql-e-Awwal` and substance came, in the same way, revelation came upon Rasulullah from Allah's side. Yes. And then from that revelation, the perfections you achieved... and then the perfections that Amir al-Mu'minin himself had, you could not attain those perfections directly. For every perfection, for every miracle, for every state, the intermediary was Rasulullah. Rasulullah was the intermediary for you in everything. The... Did revelation not come to other prophets? Yes, it did, it came to them... But not to Mawla 'Ali? No, it did not come to him. And Mawla 'Ali also, in every deed, he explained it through your intermediary. To the people whom he explained, it was, "I received it from Rasul." Yes, absolutely. And what love there must have been, Rasul to you and you to Rasul. Meaning, never any opposition is seen. And you also see what a perfection it is that whatever Rasulullah said for Janab 'Ali, that 'Ali is like this, 'Ali is like that, he is truthful, he is trustworthy, he pays debts, and he is brave. So, the Prophet did say this, but Janab 'Ali has to fulfill that word. And he has to uphold the weight of the Prophet's word. This was the real thing. And he showed it by doing it. That what the Prophet... what was said for you, that `Wa amma bi-ni'mati rabbika fa-haddith` (But as for the favor of your Lord, report it). That you should narrate the favor of your Lord. Meaning, your Lord is Janab Abu Talib, his favor is Janab 'Ali. Mention him. Therefore, Janab Nabi 'alayhissalam, for him, he began to narrate thousands, lakhs of hadiths. People were astonished. Yes. So, in that, Janab 'Ali fulfilled all those things. Whatever the Prophet had said, he proved it. So that the weight of the Prophet's word would not fall. So what a perfection this is. His... the perfections were latent in him, weren't they? Yes, the perfections were latent in him, in their own place. Yes, definitely. He was infallible from there itself, so no mistake could have... Yes, it could not have happened. And then his `muhtajib` (veiled one) was also, he had a great status, no? Because of his `muhtajib`, this quality was created in the veil. And this was the seventh manifestation of the Era of Concealment. Due to the seventh manifestation, the quality is also very high. Yes. And then this also happened, that all... you became the `miqat` (appointed time/place) of the first and the last. All the first ones who had passed. And then not just of this era, but of the previous era. Because this `Qa'im` who is coming, seven eras of manifestation and seven eras of concealment of yours are going to be gathered. So that gathering, seven eras of concealment and seven eras of manifestation's gathering, and this is the last concealment. Meaning, before this, seven manifestations have passed and six concealments have passed. And this is the seventh concealment. So in this concealment, the stations that are there, meaning the pure Imams, the stations that are there, the collection of all of them was with Janab 'Ali. And those stations... the word `maqam` (station) for whom does it come? It comes for three people. Imam, and `Wasi` (Successor), and Prophet. These three things, the station for... So as many stations as there are, in them the Imams came to you. The `Quwwam` and Imams gathered with you. And the Prophets, all of them, gathered with Janab Nabi. You became the leader of the `mustawdi'in` (the entrusted ones). For which it was said that the `Qa'im` of the family of Ishaq was a prophet, and the `Qa'im` of the family of Isma'il was Janab 'Ali. Yes. And you can imagine that of seven manifestations and seven concealments, including this concealment, from Janab Hunaif to Janab 'Ashir, and from Janab Hasan to the future `al-Qa'im`, that entire gathering was gathered with you. So what is the status and greatness of yours? And just as a `Bab-ul-Abwab` (Gate of Gates) gathers, so you became the `Bab-ul-Abwab` for the `Qa'im`, in that rank you stood.
