And that gathering, you will hand it over. Yes. You mean, he even gathered the `Qa'im`s of the seven manifestations and six concealments? Yes, he gathered the `Quwwam` (Pillars) too. What is meant is that the `Qa'im`s who... were partial. But he is also a `Qa'im`. You gathered the `Quwwam` too. Then, when there appeared to him, when there appeared to it, for this great `hadd`, two forms. in those two forms. For this great `hadd`, meaning for the `Aql-e-Awwal` (First Intellect), there was contemplation that how this quality came to be in the `Aql-e-Awwal`. from those forms. From those sources. so he looked with his gaze. So both of them looked like his gaze. Just as he had thought, in the same way, they thought. Yes. and divinity was negated from their two beings. And they negated divinity from their own being. Meaning, they acknowledged this fact that the power to bring from non-existence to existence is not in us. so this was a clean and pure thought. and from the members of their two kinds. and from the members of their two kinds, they also negated it, that our companions also do not have this attribute, that they can originate (`ibda'`). Yes. and they acknowledged for their two Originators. And both of them acknowledged, the two rising intellects acknowledged for their Originator that this attribute is in their Originator. and they both bore witness for him. and both of them bore witness for him, meaning for the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`. by that, they professed his oneness and glorified him. and they glorified him, that is, they performed his glorification. and they acknowledged the Precedent over them both. And their own Precedent was also acknowledged, meaning the `Aql-e-Awwal`. that upon them... he has become superior to them. with virtue and honor. and with honor. that virtue and the honor of precedence have been attained by him. So by their action, those two were named Emitted ones. Therefore, these two were called `munba'ith` (emitted ones). rising ones. from the movement of the `Aql-e-Awwal`, emission was created in them. The forms that arose, were they individual? There was no other... no group with them. No, there was no group included. just as the `Aql-e-Awwal`'s also... the form is, it is one... Yes. Although, in the `munba'ithayn` (the two emitted ones), later additions were made. Yes. But the `Aql-e-Awwal`'s form, it remained a single form. Later also, meaning, no gathering was collected in it. The ascending intellect from below did not gather with it. This is why it has been said in the Holy Quran in Surah Ikhlas: `Qul huwa-Llahu ahad` (Say, He, Allah, is One). He is unique. Generally, `wahid` is translated as one, that Allah is one, but the correct translation is that he is unique. Yes. those two Emitted ones. because they both... because they both were emitted, following the First, in its action and in its deed. But one of them was precedent over the other. But this was the thing, that among them one was ahead of the other. Precedent. towards that glorification and that oneness. in that glorification that they were doing, and the oneness they were professing, and the sanctification they were doing, whose? Of the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`. In that, one was ahead. So he deserved by his precedence... that the `Aql-e-Awwal`, the Precedent... for him, a gate and a veil. Therefore, he became deserving, he who had gone ahead in glorification, sanctification, and oneness from the other, that the `Aql-e-Awwal`, he should appoint him as his gate and veil. so that he may address through him those who are below him and aid him with the substance. and he should help him with his substance, which had flowed to it from its Originator. that which he had received from the Originator, that substance. by which he was honored over the second emitted one. that because of which he became honored over the `Munba'ith-e-Thani` (the second emitted one). and all the members of his kind. and he knew by that what was and what will be. And this `Munba'ith-e-Awwal` (the first emitted one)... The `Aql-e-Awwal`'s knowledge of what was and what will be is different, and this `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`'s knowledge of what was and what will be is different. And it is what is named the Universal Soul. And it was named `Nafs-e-Kulliyyah` (Universal Soul). and the First Emission. and the First Emission it was called. and the Tablet. and it was also called `lawh` (tablet). Meaning, the pen is for the `Aql-e-Awwal`, and the tablet on which Allah... the `Aql-e-Awwal` poured its matter, that was the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`. So that is the pen, and this is the tablet. And the `Munba'ith-e-Thani` did not acknowledge the virtue of precedence of the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`. of the `Awwal`. that he is precedent. And he deluded himself that he was equal to him. And he deluded himself and understood that both of them are equal, equal. so that became a mistake. a mistake that he acquired. and he was delayed from his rank. and his rank fell. and it was a fall from his station. and he fell down. Then the `Aql-e-Awwal` called the entire world of origination (`ibda'`) to the oneness of their Originator and His glorification. And the `Aql-e-Awwal` gave the call, it gave a call to all the world of origination towards the oneness of their Originator and His glorification. This is how it is. The `Aql-e-Awwal` did not give the call. It never gave the call. Because if the `Aql-e-Awwal` gave the call, then because of its call, intellects would gather in it. The `Aql-e-Awwal` never gave the call. That is why it is said, so his call for them... the calling of the `Aql-e-Awwal` for them, meaning for its own kind, was through the First Emitted one. through the First Emitted one. Meaning, the `Aql-e-Awwal` is giving the call, but it is not giving the call by itself. Through the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`, it gave the call. Because it did not initiate the call. The initiation of the call happened from the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`. Therefore, all the intellects that have come into the world of intellects, their progress will be, and from the physical world, the intellects that will become, Imam, `Qa'im`, `jussa` becoming intellects, which will move forward, the return of all of them will be to the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`. Not to the `Aql-e-Awwal`. So two, two circles will remain. One, the `Aql-e-Awwal`, and one, the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`. All will merge into it, into the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`. They will merge into the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`. To the extent that it has been said that Shaytan also will merge into the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`, one day. And this is what in the Holy Quran, in Surah Fatiha, the last word is `al-dallin` (those who have gone astray). In `al-dallin`, the dot of the `nun` in `dallin`, it points towards Shaytan. that it will come into the circle. It will come into the circle. After misleading the entire community, it will ultimately repent. and will come into the circle. Meaning, in the last `mawr`. In the last `mawr`. It will gradually come into the Imam's `haykal`, etc., just as... just as it will be. But it will repent. In the end. It will be the last one to ascend, but... In the Imam's `haykal`... Yes, so... it will be alone. No, not alone. The others who will be with it... But this is that in the end, it will repent. Sir, at this station, what is the meaning of `da'wah` (call)? Is it only to acknowledge the `Mubdi' Ta'ala` and... that is it, right? Meaning, the meaning of `da'wah` is... Yes, meaning to acknowledge the `Mubdi' Ta'ala` and to acknowledge the `Aql-e-Awwal`. Yes. And the `Munba'ith-e-Awwal`, to acknowledge that this order has been established. It called towards that. Yes. Because, that is, those who denied the `da'wah`, from their denial, it becomes known what kind of `da'wah` it was. Because the deniers, what did they do? They said, "We neither accept you, nor those before you." So they denied the `da'wah` of the `Ashir` (the Tenth). But this happened, that those who fell, they did not deny the `Mubdi' Ta'ala`. That is why, that is why, in a disbeliever also, in a polytheist also, that particle of oneness exists. Because of which the hope is formed that he will come towards faith. Rasulullah also tried, that you, he tried that as soon as possible, they should come into the circle of faith. What is the Divine Power saying? That you... you cannot make anyone a believer. You cannot bring them into the circle of faith, until Allah wills. So Rasulullah also cannot guide anyone, until Allah wills. This has been made clear in the Holy Quran. whom He had made for him a gate and a veil. and He answered him from those forms which were preceded by the originated. The difficult topics that are there, the difficult topic is how the celestial spheres, attributes, and so on were formed from the `hayula`. That topic is a bit intricate. Is Shaytan identified as just one? Yes, the one who opposed the `Ashir` (the Tenth). He was one. He was one. Yes. And that was the spiritual Iblis. And those who followed him, all of them are in this world, they are behind him. Yes, they are behind him. And his agents were established. Just as among the followers of the `Ashir`, there were the remorseful ones, and among the remorseful ones there were Imams, `Quwwam`, and their followers, in the same way, of that Shaytan, that Shaytan, his... his agents were formed... his followers, in them, that quality was created. O Allah, make this reading of the *Fatiha* reach the soul of our master and our Mawla, `al-Mu'ayyad Fi-d-dinillah` Ibn Musa al-Shirazi, may Allah sanctify his noble and illumined soul. And make its blessings, ranks, and confirmations apparent to us. And accept from us this reading and this benefiting in the best way that can be. By the right of the master of the messengers and the successor of our master, Amir al-Mu'minin, and the Great Quran and the pure and purified Ahl al-Bayt, may the blessings and peace of Allah be upon them all. Al-Fatiha. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. By the grace of Sayyidna al-Mawla al-Musun al-Atlaq in the island of the Yemenite `da'wah`. For the honor of the noble, learned, and great Prophet, al-Haj Ashraf son of Husamuddin Ghulam Husain son of Sayyidna al-Mutlaq al-Muqaddas, and his father Sayyidna 'Abd-ul-'Ali son of Haybatullah son of Muhammad Husain al-Muqaddas `quddisa sirruhu`. May Allah elevate his station and may `al-Mawla Ta'ala` protect him and guard him and grant him success in fulfilling the desires of the `mumlakah` of his `shuyukh`. May `al-Mawla Ta'ala` make him triumphant over the people of his era. From the evil of the eclipses, and the misfortunes, and the divisions, and the calamities of the events and the passing of days and months. May He protect him and aid him and his affair. And may He cause the flag of the `da'wah` to wave, and protect him in the divine, spiritual protection. May `al-Mawla Ta'ala` lengthen his life and elevate his station and ranks, and may He make us and you among those who attain his `da'wat` and his service, and grant us and you the gnosis of his worth. By the right of the master of the messengers, Sayyidna, our Mawla, Amir al-Mu'minin, and the pure Ahl al-Bayt, may the blessings and peace of Allah be upon them all. Al-Fatiha. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim.

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 10 -->

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*, and blessings of Allah upon His Messenger, Sayyidina Muhammad, and upon the family of Muhammad, and may He grant peace.

Then, the First Intellect (`'aql al-awwal`) called the entire World of Origination (`'alam al-ibda'`) to the Oneness (`tawhid`) of their Originator (`mubdi'`) and to His glorification (`tasbih`). Then, the First Intellect (`'aql al-awwal`) gave an invitation (`da'wat`) to the entire World of Origination (`'alam al-ibda'`) towards the Oneness (`tawhid`) of their Originator (`mubdi'`) and towards their glorification (`tasbih`). And his invitation to them was through the First Emanated Being (`munba'ith al-awwal`). And his invitation (`du'a`) was through the First Emanated Being (`munba'ith al-awwal`). He did not invite them directly. `Du'a` means `da'wah` (invitation).

And he had made him a gate (`bab`) and a veil (`hijab`) for himself. While he had already made him his gate (`bab`) and veil (`hijab`). So seven intellects (`saba' 'uqul`) from those originated forms (`suwar al-mubda'a`) responded to him. And when he made the call (`da'wat`), then they said "Labbayk" (Here I am at your service), they accepted the invitation, from those new forms, which were originated (`mubda'at`), seven intellects (`'uqul`) from among them. Each one of them after the second. And this happened one after the other. They kept happening one after the other.

And under each intellect from them, from those originated forms (`suwar al-mubda'a`), is a world whose number cannot be counted. And with every intellect is such a vast multitude from those originated forms (`suwarat-e-mubda'at`) that its number cannot be known or encompassed.

So that intellect is like an Imam for them. And that intellect, under which all these forms were, that intellect became an Imam for them. And the chief (`ra'is`). And they became its chiefs (`ra'is`). And the model (`qudwah`). And their, ah, their, the one whose example is followed. Due to its precedence over them. Because its precedence has been established over them. Just as in Surah Waqi'ah it is said: `al-sabiqun al-sabiqun, ula'ika al-muqarrabun` (The forerunners, the forerunners, those are the ones brought near). That those who were to precede have preceded, and they are the ones brought near [to God]. So precedence (`sabaqiyat`) is a great thing. Yes.

Just as it is said that in the beginning, the people who brought faith (`iman`), then those who brought faith later cannot attain their spiritual state, even though they may perform much more worship (`ibadat`), those who come later, and perform many more good deeds, they will do good deeds, but they cannot attain their spiritual state, which belongs to the early people of faith and the early community of believers (`mu'minin`).

And they are like followers (`atba'`) to him. One is `ittiba'`, `ittiba'` means to follow. And `atba'` means followers. They were his followers, for that intellect. And those who follow (`muqtadin`). And they were those who followed the example. With what, uh, with him. Of his, his. So the ranks (`maratib`) of the World of Origination (`'alam al-ibda'`) became nine. So, in this way, the ranks (`maratib`) of the World of Origination (`'alam al-ibda'`) became nine. The First Intellect (`'aql al-awwal`), the First Emanation (`anbiya' al-awwal`), and the seven responding intellects (`saba' 'uqul al-mujiba`) to the call (`da'wah`). That first, there was the First Intellect, then the First Emanation after it, and then there were seven intellects after that. And then this impression continued forever. Just as there was Rasulullah, then Mawla-e-Ka'inat, and then seven A'imma Tahirin. In this way.

Just as when we perform `tawaf`, uhm, then there are seven rounds (`ashwat`). And the `tawaf` of seven rounds (`ashwat`) is not accepted until two `rak'ats` of prayer are offered at Maqam Ibrahim. So offering two `rak'ats` of prayer at Maqam Ibrahim is superior to those seven rounds (`ashwat`), seven `rak'ats`, seven `tawafs`. So those two `rak'ats` that are offered at Maqam Ibrahim are for the First Intellect and the First Emanated Being. And the seven `rak'ats`, in one sense, the seven, seven `tawafs`, they are for the seven intellects. In this way.

Are you not talking about the `'Ashir` (the tenth) in this? The `'Ashir` is the one who is performing the `tawaf`. The one who is striving (`sa'i`), the one who is making an effort, he is the `'Ashir`. Their efforts are already completed. The First Intellect, the First Emanated Being, their ranks are already established. And the seven intellects, their ranks are established. But the one who is striving, in the `da'wah`, who is he? He is the First Intellect... no, he is the `'Ashir`. Who, in the spiritual world, made great efforts so that he... established the delusion of equality (`wahm-e-musawat`), and brought that group to the truth. And he explained a lot, to the point that he said that even I made a mistake, and I admitted my mistake. You too, admit your mistake. But they, the people did not, and the `mubda'at` did not, they did not admit their own mistake.

Then, when the Second Emanated Being (`munba'ith al-thani`) fell from his rank because of his delusion (`tawahhum`)… and so, and while, then when the Second Emanated Being, when his rank fell, due to his delusion of equality (`wahm-e-musawat`). So the intellects (`'uqul`) preceded him. There is no `waw` there, you should remove the `waw`. The seven intellects (`'uqul al-saba'`) preceded him. So the seven intellects (`'uqul`), they preceded him. Over him. By their response (`ijabat`). Because of their response, because of their accepting the invitation. And every `masbuq` (one who is preceded) acknowledged the merit of his `sabiq` (predecessor). And every person who came after acknowledged his predecessor. Acknowledgment is very important. The acknowledgment of the `masbuq` for the `sabiq` is an extremely necessary thing. Yes.

Just as, uhm, at the place of Sayyidna Da'ud bin 'Ajabshah, uhm, uhm, she used to study, who? Uhm... A Bibi used to study, what was her name... Yes. She was the wife of Jabir bin Fahad. Her name was probably Zaynab. Jabir bin Fahad, meaning the elder brother of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahad, Jabir bin Fahad. His wife was there, who used to study with Sayyidna Da'ud bin 'Ajabshah. And she was a very learned and virtuous woman. And at that time, she was studying `Simt al-Haqa'iq` with Sayyidna Da'ud bin 'Ajabshah, which in those days was considered a very high-level book of the `mubtadi'at` (beginners), which is in poetry, written by Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hanzalah. So when Sayyidna Da'ud bin 'Ajabshah intended to come to India, when you intended to come from Yemen, then this Bibi Zaynab requested, "Mawla, how will my studies continue now? The chain of my learning will break with your departure." So he said, "Study with your husband."

She was very surprised. That… So then… so then she requested that, "Mawla, you know that Jabir doesn't know how to read or write. So how will I study with him?" So he said, "You keep reading, and he will keep explaining to you. Because," he said, "`zannuhu afzalu min yaqiniki`" (his mere thought is better than your certainty). His merely having a thought about something is greater than your certainty. So this was the spiritual state. That is, because he has preceded you, his merely guessing something is greater than your certainty. Yes.

And the `munba'ith al-thani` connected with the last (`al-tasi'a`) of those intellects. And then in the end, what happened was that the `munba'ith al-thani`, who was the last, the very last intellect among those intellects, and it was the ninth (`tasi'a`). And it was the ninth intellect, which in that sequence, in the sequence of the seven intellects, it was the ninth intellect. So from the last individual of that last intellect, because the last, the last intellect which was, the ninth intellect, so within it too, innumerable intellects are present, under it. So from among them, the very last intellect, in the sphere of the ninth intellect, the last individual that was there, with him he connected (`bilgha`), and after connecting, he asked, `mustakhbiran lahu` (seeking information from him), and he wanted to know his own condition (`an halatihi`), "Why has my state become like this, that this spirituality (`nuraniyat`) is not being created in me? And what is the reason that he has fallen from his rank (`martaba`)?" So he informed him that the thing that caused you to fall from your rank was the delusion of equality (`al-musawat`) with your predecessor (`sabiqihi`). That he knew, he informed him, he made him aware that the thing that made you fall from your rank is the delusion of equality (`wahm-e-musawat`) with your predecessor. That is, that was the `tawahhum` (delusion). That is, the delusion of equality is the `wahm` which you entertained with your predecessor. That is, you considered the `munba'ith al-awwal` as your companion, and you thought that he and I are equal. Whereas no person, no individual can be equal. If Rasulullah also said this, that `al-Hasan wa al-Husayn imamani qama aw qa'ada wa abuhuma khayrun minhuma` (Al-Hasan and al-Husayn are two Imams, whether they rise or sit, and their father is better than them both), then despite this, despite calling Hasan and Husayn the rightful Imams, this is a noble pronouncement (`nass-e-sharif`) of Rasulullah, but the ranks of Hasan and Husayn are different. Yes.

So he who is ahead, has progressed, Husayn has progressed, but in terms of precedence (`sabaqiyat`), Hasan is there. Yes. `Sabaqiyat` is with Hasan. And the rank of `awwaliyat` (firstness) is a very great rank. The rank of `awwaliyat`, `awwaliyat` itself has a rank. The station of `awwaliyat` is a great station. Just as there were 124,000 prophets (`anbiya`), but the honor (`sharafat`) of `awwaliyat` that Adam received, no other prophet could receive. Just as Imam Hasan ibn 'Ali, `salawatullah 'alayh`, the `awwaliyat` that he received among the A'imma Tahirin, that honor was achieved, that, no one else received it. Although among the later A'imma, many were higher, `sabi'in` were higher than you, but the honor of `awwaliyat` is in its own place. There is Sayyidna Dhu'ayb ibn Musa, the honor of `awwaliyat` that he received in the cycle of the `ta'im`, no one else could receive it. Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, who is the first in the cycle of the `qaim-i-dawr`, the honor of `awwaliyat` that he received, which, along with other honors, the honor of `awwaliyat` that was achieved, no one else could achieve it. So this honor of `awwaliyat` is a great thing.

And he who is first (`awwal`) is also the eighth (`athamin`). He who is first is also the eighth. Because after seven, the eighth one is the first of its circle. So the first person of any circle, he, that person, in the subsequent circle, is the eighth.

So he interceded for him with the one above him. So he interceded for him, for the one who was above him. And the one above him interceded for him with the one above him. And intercession was made for him by the one who was above him. Towards the one who was above him. In this way, the chain of intercession continued. Until it ended. And intercession is a great thing. Rasulullah said, didn't he, that "I have my, every, every single one was given an opportunity, and, but I kept my prayer (`du'a`) remaining for intercession (`shafa'at`), for the intercession of the `ummah` on the Day of Qiyamah. For whomever I wish, I will be an intercessor for him." `Wa'l-mustajabah`. Yes, yes. `Du'a-e-mustajabah`.

Until... And it is for Janabe Fatimah, too, that on the Day of Qiyamah, you will pick your lovers in such a way as a bird picks grain. In this way, you will choose your lovers.

...until the intercession (`shafa'at`) reached the Second Intellect (`al-'aql al-thani`). Until the Second Intellect (`'aql-e-thani`). To him that intercession reached. The intercession that was being made for him, it reached. Which is the First Emanated Being (`munba'ith al-awwal`). Who is the Second Intellect? He is, in fact, the First Emanated Being. That is, after the First Intellect, the second intellect that came into being, was in fact the First Emanated Being. So he knew that the Second Emanated Being (`munba'ith al-thani`) had regretted (`nadima`) what had preceded from him. So he came to know this matter, that the `munba'ith al-thani`, who was there, he had become remorseful over that matter which had happened from him. And that he did not do it intentionally (`ta'ammad`). And he did not do it intentionally. That is, the mistake that was made by him, of the delusion of equality, was not done knowingly. But it happened without intention (`ta'ammud`). And he did not insist upon it (`asarra 'alayhi`). And neither did he insist upon it. He did not show stubbornness. So he accepted his repentance. So what did he do? The `munba'ith al-awwal` accepted his repentance. From his slip (`zallatihi`). From his slip, he accepted the repentance. And He forgave his mistake (`khati'atuhu`). And He forgave his mistake. And He extended to him from the eternal substance (`madda al-azaliyya`). And made it flow, the eternal substance that was there, that divine grace was made to flow towards him. Which was connected to him from his predecessor (`sabiqihi`). That substance which he had received from his predecessor. That is, from the First Intellect, the spiritual state that he had received, that substance was also started to be given to him, but not the whole of it, but a part (`juz`) of it. Whatever he had received from the First Intellect, a part of it was given to him. With which he had aided all of those intellects. That is, that substance with which he had served all the intellects. When they responded (`ijabatiha`). When they had accepted his call. And he added to it. So, because of it, that darkness (`zulmat`) was removed from him. So, because of which, that darkness which had happened from that corrupt delusion (`wahm al-fasid`), that darkness was removed. From the Second Emanated Being (`munba'ith al-thani`). From the Second Emanated Being.

And it became. And it is not mentioned here, but that darkness, what happened to it? Where did that darkness go? It went to the one who was the spiritual Iblis. It attached to him. Because of which, what happened? That which was the spiritual Iblis, this one became the spiritual Adam. That is, the `'Ashir` is also called the spiritual Adam. The darkness of the spiritual Adam, where did it go? That darkness came out and who became its magnet? It was that spiritual Iblis. Because of which, his devilishness increased even more. And when that... this darkness was received by him, he turned from Iblis to Shaytan. Yes. He became Shaytan from Iblis. And who is Shaytan called? The one who has become distant from the mercy of Allah. The one who has become distant from the mercy of Allah. He is called Shaytan.

Just as the `atba'` were created, so he… `quwwa-e-nis` [unclear] will not do it. That is, it will not be finished. It is for eternity. Yes, absolutely. In that sense, the `wahm` (delusion), the `tasawwur` (imagination), that will also not be finished. Just as you are saying, their `wahm` went there, to Iblis. So `wahm` is also an imperishable (`la-fani`) thing. No, that `wahm` is not an entity (`zat`). It is not an entity. It is a state. His darkness. That darkness now, with the spiritual Iblis, it merged, because of which he became Shaytan. And the power of Shaytan increased, the evil power increased. Yes. It was a thought, so to speak. A thought, as it were. It was darkness.

And he became... And he knew by that substance (`madda`) what was and what will be. And then he came to know, because of that substance which he received, which he got from the `munba'ith al-awwal`, so what was and what will be, he became the master and knower of that entire state. And he was established in the tenth rank (`martaba al-'ashira`). The tenth rank was established. And he became, after having been the second in distinction (`imtiyaz`), while he was the second in distinction, the `munba'ith al-thani` he was, no? The second in distinction, and the third in number (`'adad`). And he was the third in number. Yes. That is, the First Intellect, the `munba'ith al-awwal`, and he was the third in number. And he became the tenth (`'ashir`) in rank (`rutba`). But when the rank was established, he became the tenth.

And he obtained, through that substance (`madda`) and all the preceding intellects (`'uqul al-sabiqa`), the second perfection (`al-kamal al-thani`). And that substance which he obtained, and all the intellects which had preceded him, he obtained the second perfection (`al-kamal al-thani`). The second perfection, and the second existence (`wujud al-thani`), by which they became eternal (`ta'azzalu`) and were protected (`'usimu`) and were made safe from transmutation (`istihalah`). Yes. And that is, their second existence came into being. That is, what was the first existence? The first existence was when the `mubda'at` were created, it was in a uniform state. That was the first existence. Giving knowledge, life, and power. And this was the second existence, because of which they attained eternality, and they attained infallibility (`'ismat`), and they became safe, they came into security from transmutation, that is, from spoiling, and from annihilation (`fana'`), from annihilation. And there remained from those originated forms (`suwar al-ibda'iyya`) a world whose number cannot be counted. And from that originated form, there was a world whose number cannot be counted or encompassed, who delayed in their response (`ta'akhkharu 'an al-ijabah`). That they delayed in responding. Yes. To the call (`da'wah`).

Just as you said to recite the `kalam-e-sharif` (noble word) slowly, in the same way, Imam al-Zaman has also written a `du'a` for us, in which we ask for forgiveness for how many mistakes we make. And now we, like in `Thamb Masha'ikh`, we recite that `du'a`. How fast we recite it. We cannot even... what we are reciting, we just have to finish it in a certain time.

Yes, in this it is said that you should recite a short `du'a`, but recite it with understanding or fulfill its right.

How many times we recite Alhamdulillah very much, how much we... Yes, yes.

That is, the number and abundance of `du'a` is not the point, but reciting it correctly and understanding it and taking its effect within oneself, what `du'a` we are making to Allah. That is an important matter.

And the `du'a` of `du'as`, Surah Fatihah, how important it is. Many people recite it very fast and its tone is also not correct. Once it happened that a person's hand had become crooked in a battle, in a jihad. So he came before Amir al-Mu'minin and said, "Mawla, my hand has become crooked. Please pray that it becomes straight." So you mumbled something, your lips moved, you passed your hand over it, and his hand became well. So he was very grateful. Then he went a little further and it occurred to him to ask what `du'a` Mawla had recited. So he took a few steps forward and then came back and said, "What `du'a` did you recite that my hand became better?" So he said, "I recited Surah Fatihah." So he was very surprised, thinking that you must have recited a very grand `du'a`. As soon as this doubt (`zann`) entered his mind, his hand returned to its previous crooked state. Yes. Just as with 'Umar ibn al-Khattab, it happened that `inna ba'd al-zann ithmun` (indeed, some suspicion is a sin), that when he was doing `tawaf` of the Baitullah, and he had said, "This is a stone, `la yadurru wa la yanfa'u wa la yadurru` (it neither benefits nor harms, nor does it harm)." That it can neither benefit nor cause harm. Amir al-Mu'minin was present there. So he said that "this stone benefits and it harms." This is the stone that can both benefit and cause harm. So it is said that because of that, two evil forms attached themselves to him. Because of which his stubbornness increased. So these kinds of things happen.

Just as we recite in `kalam-e-sharif`, where to stop and where not to stop, the meaning also changes. Yes, the meaning changes. Now, I, a Surah, Surah… Surah… is it… `Nashrah`. Surah Nashrah, which is `Alam nashrah laka sadrak`. So in that, I heard Sayyidna Muqaddas Sayyidna 'Abdullah bin Muhammad reciting, and he recited `fa inna ma'al-'usri yusran` in the first instance. And I saw in other `kalam-e-sharif` that `la` is written there. You have indicated it yourself. In the second one, he recited `yusra`. So, we make so many such mistakes, and then when we look in the `kalam-e-sharif`, `la` is in many places. And I have heard the reciters (`qari`) too, they sometimes stop, sometimes they don't. We don't get the correct... and then when we recite the `du'a` for finishing the `kalam-e-sharif`, then on that it is that I made this mistake and that mistake...

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. To Allah, `musbah al-fatiha` (the lamp of the Fatihah) to the soul of Sayyidna Mawlana al-Mu'ayyad fi'l-Din Allah bin Musa al-Shirazi, may Allah sanctify his soul and his noble spirit. And benefit us with his blessings, his moments, and his support. And accept from us, O Allah, the benefit and the use, in the best way that can be, by the right of our masters, the family of Muhammad, and may He bless and grant peace. Al-Fatihah. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim...*

[A long formal `du'a` is recited by another person.]

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 11 -->

Sir, there are a couple of pages missing from this, which I have handwritten. Eleventh. I will... eleventh. If two or three words are not understandable, you can ask me. I have handwritten it. Yes. This... Muharram Muslim Sāmi has put *i'rāb* [vowel markings] on the *rātib*, so it becomes easy for the reader. And... I shall see it, Insha'Allah. This is magnificent. It's magnificent. Yes, yes. What page are we on? Eleventh page. *As-salamu alaykum*. *Wa 'alaykum al-salam*. *As-salamu alaykum*, Pir Sharif, how are you? By the grace of Allah, yes. The voice is not coming. The voice is not coming. [feedback noise] Yes. I have it. [phone ringing] I'll have to put it on hold. [feedback noise] *A'udhu billahi min al-shaytan al-rajim* (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). *Bismillah al-rahman al-rahim* (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). *Wa bihi nasta'in wa sallallahu 'ala rasulihi sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala ali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallam* (And in Him we seek help, and may the blessings of Allah be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon the family of Muhammad, and may He grant peace). The discussion was that, one is *kamāl al-awwal* (the first perfection). That is, *Mabda' Ta'ālā* (the Originator, the Exalted), in equal states and without any deficiency, meaning every intellect (*'aql*) was created in the same state, in one single state, and its origination is called *ibdā'*. Its creation. And the creations that came later, that is called *takhlīq* (creation), as *'Āshir* (the Tenth Intellect) did. So, *ibdā'* happened only once. And the *mubda'āt* (originated beings), they were created in one state. They were created by being given knowledge, life, and power. And they were created in an equal manner. And that is called *al-kamāl al-awwal* (the first perfection). That is *kamāl al-awwal*. But what is greater than *kamāl al-awwal*? It is *kamāl al-thānī* (the second perfection). Although *kamāl al-awwal* is a gift from *Mabda' Ta'ālā*. Meaning, who is the giver (*mu'tī*) who bestowed *kamāl al-awwal*? It is *Mabda' Ta'ālā*. And the *mu'tī* of *kamāl al-thānī* is not *Mabda' Ta'ālā*. The *mu'tī* of *kamāl al-thānī*, a quality was born from their own essences (*dhawāt*) through which they came to *kamāl al-thānī*. Yes. But *kamāl al-thānī* is greater than *kamāl al-awwal*. Yes. Just as, for instance, when a believer-child is born, he is in the state of *kamāl al-awwal*. When he takes the *'ahd sharīf* (the sacred covenant), he attains *kamāl al-thānī*. So, in *kamāl al-thānī*, his own growth and his potential are at work. That he, having prepared himself, attained *kamāl al-thānī*. The giver is Allah Himself, the giver is the *Mabda'* Himself, but in it, the effort of the giver's own being is involved. And it is also called *wujūd al-thānī* (the second existence). *Kamāl al-thānī*. *alladhī bihi ta'azzalū wa 'uṣimū wa aminū min al-istiḥālah* (that by which they became eternal, were protected, and were safe from transformation). And due to this existence, *kamāl al-thānī*, and due to this *wujūd al-thānī*, they attained eternity (*'azaliyyah*), those essences. And *'ismah*, complete *'ismah* (infallibility/protection) was granted to them, meaning they cannot make a mistake. Yes, as it is in the Quran that *yaf'alūna mā yu'marūn* (they do what they are commanded). For the angels, that *yaf'alūna mā yu'marūn wa lā ya'ṣūn* (they do what they are commanded and they do not disobey). That whatever command is given to them, they carry it out. And they do not commit sin. So the quality of not committing sin and listening to the command with obedience, that quality is in the angels. Yes. And they remained safe. *Wa aminū*, they were saved from *istiḥālah*, that is, from transformation. *Wa-l-fanā'*, and from annihilation. *wa kāna qad baqā min tilka al-ṣuwar al-ibdā'iyyah*. And how did this state come about? Due to their obedience. The obedience they showed to the First Cause (*muba'ith al-awwal*), and they affirmed its true word, so this state was born in them. *wa kāna qad baqā min tilka al-ṣuwar al-ibdā'iyyah 'ālamun lā yuḥṣā 'adaduh*. And a very large number of *ṣuwar al-ibdā'iyyah* (originated forms) remained, who, the group that was with them, the group that was with these people, who are the *ṣuwarāt ibdā'iyyah*, that was a world of theirs that remained there, whose number cannot be counted. *ta'akhkharū 'an al-ijābah li-l-da'wah*. And they delayed in responding to the call. In accepting it, they delayed the call. Yes, the group of 'Ashir. Yes, whoever they were, the group of 'Ashir or all the intellects that were there, the intellects that were there, they delayed, and in accepting, they were late. *ma'a tilka al-'uqūl al-sab'ah*. And they were with those seven intellects, but they denied... they did not accept and they delayed. *wa takhallafū muqtadīna fī al-takhalluf bi-l-'Āshir*. And they fell behind, following 'Āshir in falling behind. *fa-lammā taradda al-māddah li-l-'Āshir*. When the substance, the help, and divine help was received by 'Āshir, *kamā qulnā bi-shafā'at tilka al-'uqūl*, as we have said, by the intercession of those intellects, as 'Āshir sought refuge with the ninth intellect, and the ninth intellect, its word was carried forward, until it reached *Mabda' al-Awwal*. So this matter that happened, *wa hiya al-kalimāt*, so they were called *kalimāt* (words). Who? These intellects. These intellects through which 'Āshir's repentance was accepted. The intellects that were above 'Āshir, from the ninth intellect up to *inbi'āth al-awwal*, all the intellects that were there, they were called *kalimāt*. *allatī talaqqāhā Ādam min rabbihi*. (which Adam received from his Lord). That these are the words that Adam met with from his Lord. Because he is the *Ādam al-rūḥānī* (spiritual Adam). Because you, you are the spiritual Adam. Who? 'Āshir. 'Āshir became the spiritual Adam. *fa-ṣaḥḥa fīhi mā qāla Allāhu Ta'ālā: fa-talaqqā Ādam min rabbihi kalimātin*. (Thus it was true of him what Allah the Exalted said: "Then Adam received from his Lord [certain] words"). As Allah Himself says, it is a verse of Surah Baqarah, that Adam met with, from his Lord's side, with the *Mabda' al-Awwal*, with words. The words that were received by them. *fa-tāba 'alayhi*. And Allah the Exalted, meaning *Mabda' al-Awwal*, forgave him. *innahu huwa al-tawwāb al-raḥīm*. (Indeed, it is He who is the Accepter of repentance, the Merciful). That He, among those who repent, is very accepting of repentance, and is merciful. *wa qīla ḥīna'idhin lahu: aqbil 'alā al-muqtadīn*. (And it was said to him then: "Turn to those who followed you"). It is said that at that time, it was said to him, that is, to 'Āshir, "Turn towards the followers. Come forward. The followers, your followers who were following you, who were following you, turn towards them." *bi-ka fī al-takhalluf*. The ones who fell behind. *fad'uhum*. So you give them the call. *wa khalliṣhum 'ammā waqa'ū fīhi*. And grant them salvation from what they have fallen into. *fa-inna man kasara 'aẓman fa-'alayhi jabruh*. (For whoever breaks a bone, upon him is its mending). Because whoever has broken a bone, it is incumbent upon him to mend it too. Yes. *fa-'aṭafa*. This sentence has become like a proverb. It became so famous that *man kasara 'aẓman fa-'alayhi jabruh*. (whoever breaks a bone, upon him is its mending). Whoever has broken a bone, it is incumbent upon him that he mend it. *fa-'aṭafa 'alā ulā'ika al-mutakhallifīn*. (So he showed compassion to those who had fallen behind). So, he showed great kindness to them, those who had fallen behind. In order to explain, he showed great kindness and love in giving them the call. Yes. *wa qāla lahum*. And all these qualities were born in the Messenger of Allah. What you, from the very beginning, gave the call to the Muslim ummah, to your opponents, to the disbelievers, to the polytheists, nowhere did you show your own force or anything. To the extent that in the Holy Quran, it has come in your honor that *law kunta faẓẓan ghalīẓ al-qalbi lanfaḍḍū min ḥawlik*. (If you had been severe and harsh-hearted, they would have dispersed from around you). If you were severe, *law kunta faẓẓan ghalīẓ al-qalbi*, if you were hard of heart and severe in nature, then *lanfaḍḍū min ḥawlik*, then this butterfly-like crowd that you see around you would not have existed. Yes. So, 'Āshir turned his attention towards them. *wa qāla lahum: innā qad żalalnā... wa-akhṭa'nā*. (and said to them: "Indeed, we have erred... and we have sinned"). And he said to all of them that I have made a mistake. A mistake has been made by me. And I have erred *fī... tarkinā i'tirāf bi-faḍl al-sābiq*. (in... our leaving of the recognition of the merit of the predecessor). because we did not acknowledge the merit of our predecessor, the one who was before us. Meaning, *'ālam al-muba'ith al-awwal*. *wa qad tubtu 'ammā ṣalafa 'annī*. (And I have repented for what has passed from me). And I have repented. For what has come from me. As far as I am concerned. *fa-tūbū antum, tas'adū wa tafūzū*. (so you too repent, you will be fortunate and successful). So therefore, you too repent, you will become righteous and you will be successful. As the Messenger of Allah said: *Qūlū lā ilāha illā Allāh, tufliḥū*. (Say there is no god but Allah, and you will be successful). *fa-qālū bi-ajma'ihim*. So, all of them, completely united and in one voice, said: *lā faḍla lahum 'alaynā wa-lā laka*. (They have no merit over us, nor do you). He said that upon all of them, we have no merit. Meaning, they are not greater than us. And you are also not greater than us. Yes. *li-annā... kullunā ibdā' al-Mabda'*. (Because... we are all the origination of the Originator). They denied everyone. Meaning, they also denied the merit of the first intellects and the merit of *Mabda' al-Awwal*, and the merit of 'Āshir himself, all of them denied it. Because we are all the origination of the Originator. And what reason did they present? That we are all the creations, the originated beings, of the Originator, *Mabda' Ta'ālā*. He had created us. Therefore, we are all equal. Meaning, what did they do? They looked at the state of *kamāl al-awwal*. The progress that had been made in *kamāl al-thānī*, the state that had been created, they did not look at that. They looked at the state of *kamāl al-awwal*, just as it is said that in the community of believers, there are *du'āt*, *ma'dhūnīn*, and *ḥudūd* (ranks). So if a person says that this person who has been given this merit, he used to play with me in childhood. So how did he get this merit? So what did he do? He looked at his initial state. But the progress that the person has made and the high station he has achieved, he is not looking at that. He is looking at his state of *kamāl al-awwal*, not at the state of *kamāl al-thānī*. So these have entered into *kamāl al-thānī*. These... *Mabda' al-Awwal*, *'aql al-awwal*, *Mabda' al-Awwal*, and these seven intellects and all the *marūsīn* and *ru'asā'*, all of them had attained that state, *kamāl al-thānī*. But they are presenting the argument that the Originator created us equally. *abda'anā sawā'an sawā'*. (He originated us equally, equally). He had created us, originated us, in one state. *fa-lammā ālaw dhālika, aẓlamat dhawātuhum*. (When they insisted on that, their essences became dark). So their essences became dark. Yes. *ba'da ināratihā*. After they were luminous. While their essences were luminous inside. *fa-lammā naẓarū ilā mā aṣābahum min al-ẓulmah*. When they looked, they pondered, that the darkness that had come upon them, that their luminosity had suddenly changed into darkness, *ankarūhā*, so they did not like that thing. That darkness was not liked by them. They denied it, that how did this happen? *wa-istaḥshaw minhā*. And they became estranged from it. This was an unpleasant thing for them. This coming of darkness was an unpleasant thing for them. Therefore, they became estranged. *wa-ilta'ama ba'ḍuhum ilā ba'ḍ*. And in that state, they became entangled with each other. Yes. *wa hum al-mukannā 'anhum al-hayūlā al-ūlā*. (And they are what is metaphorically called the First Matter). A clump was formed, that is, the intellects became completely intertwined with each other to get out of this state, so they were called *hayūlā al-ūlā*. *fa-taḥarrakū... ḥarakatan... ūlā*. (So they moved... a first... movement). And they made a movement. A first movement. Yes. They made a first movement from that. *yurīdūna bihā al-talāfī mimmā waqa'ū*. So they wanted that there should be rectification. That is, the thing they had fallen into, that darkness had come, that that darkness should leave them. Therefore, they made a movement. And from the thing they had fallen into... it should be removed from them. So because of that movement, what happened? *wa-khawfihi... fa-ḥadatha min ḥarakatihim tilka fī dhawātihim al-ṭūl al-awwal*. So what happened? That because of that movement they made, *al-ṭūl al-awwal* (the first length) appeared in their essences. Length appeared. Yes. *fa-ankarūhā*. Another problem arose after the darkness. After the darkness, length appeared. So they denied this thing and disliked this thing. *wa-istaḥshaw minhu*. And they became estranged from it, from this new state. It was greater than what had passed. This state was greater than the first state for them. *wa-taḥarrakū ḥarakah thāniyah*. So they made a second movement, to remove this. *fa-ḥadatha minhā al-'arḍ al-awwal*. So from it, *al-'arḍ al-awwal* (the first width) was produced. Meaning, width was produced in them. *fa-ankara... ankarū dhālika ayḍan*. So they disliked and denied that too. *wa-taḥarrakū ḥarakatan thālithatan*. So they made a third movement. *wa ḥaṣala minhā al-'umq al-awwal*. And from it, *al-'umq al-awwal* (the first depth) was obtained. That is, depth was created. So first, *ṭūl al-awwal* happened, then *'arḍ al-awwal* happened, and then *'umq al-awwal* happened. *fa-ṣārū jisman wāḥidan*. What does that mean? *ṭūl al-awwal*, 'awwal' means there will be a second too. The second has been mentioned, that it happened in the world. Meaning, in their... their... this is talking about the spiritual world. But the *ṭūl al-thānī* (second length) and *'umq al-thānī* (second depth) that happened, that these essences, these falling essences, that state kept coming to them later on. Its mention has been made in some book. In *Tarātib al-Sab'ah*, a book, perhaps it is mentioned in it. And the third state they came into, from that, *'umq al-awwal* was produced. *fa-ṣārū jisman wāḥidan*. And so they all became one body. *muntazijan ba'ḍuhum bi-ba'ḍ*. And they became intermingled with each other. *fa-kāna inki'ās al-ḥarakāt min ḥikmat al-'Āshir*. (So the reversal of the movements was from the wisdom of 'Āshir). And all these movements that were happening, they were happening from the wisdom of 'Āshir. 'Āshir wanted that it should happen like this for them, due to their denial. *al-mutawallī li-tadbirihim*. Because he was the one responsible for their management. It had been made incumbent upon him to manage this group. Therefore, he did this for the management of this group. *li-annahu 'alima bi-mā ẓahara lahu minhum min al-'iṣyān*. Because he had come to know that a complete sin had been committed by them. *innahum lā khalāṣa lahum*. That these people, their salvation is not possible in the world of purity (*'ālam al-ṣafā'*). Because that world is free from opposition and disobedience. It is a pure and clean world, that world. *fa-ḥarrakahum... ḥattā ṣārū jisman kulliyan*. So he moved them, all of them, until they became a corporeal whole. *fa-ja'ala minhum lahum dāran wa-ālāt*. So from them, he made for them an abode and instruments. So from them, he made an abode, meaning a house, place, and instruments were made. The place was made first, and time was made later. So a place was made, and instruments were made. The instruments are the different things made in the place. *li-yastakhliṣahum... wa-da'watan yuqīmuhā li-l-najāt*. (...so that he may save them... and a call he establishes for salvation). And he gave the call so that he could arrange for their salvation. *man yaqbal minhā awwalan fa-awwalā*. Whoever among them accepts that call, he was the first. *kamā nubayyinu ba'da dhālika Inshā' Allāhu Ta'ālā*. We, in the near future, will explain all those states, Insha' Allah Ta'ala. *wa dhālika annahum kānū fī 'iṣyānihim li-l-'Āshir mutafāwitīn fī al-ḍamā'ir*. And they were all, in their sins against 'Āshir, varied in their inner intentions. And they were all, in their sins, the sin of disobedience they committed against 'Āshir, they were varied in their inner intentions. Meaning, it was not one type of sin. Varied, meaning different, in their intentions, in their inner intentions. So each one's state was different. Someone's was more, someone's was less. Someone's... all the group that was there, the disobedient group, their states of disobedience were different. *fa-minhum al-nādim al-mustaghfir*. And among them were also those who were repentant and sought forgiveness. They were repentant. That their... inside them this state was born that they thought, "Now this state has come, the corporeal state has come," but they were intensely regretful that why did we do this? And they were seeking forgiveness. *wa minhum al-shākk al-mutaḥayyir*. And among them were those who were in doubt that whether we should go this way or that way. This action of ours is right or wrong? And they were perplexed. *wa minhum al-muṣirr al-mustakbir*. And among them were those who were insistent. That whatever we did, we did well. And there were arrogant ones too. This is the group from which all the mountains and everything were formed. Yes. So they will be more severe people. The insistent ones. Yes, the insistent ones are very severe. So the things that were made, the place that he made first, so all of them were made from these people. From these. What? In this there are three groups. Now in the other books we have, the books of our *a'illah*, like Sayyidna Isma'il ibn Hibatullah al-Makrami, who is also the *Dā'ī al-Shumukh*, and who wrote the rare book in the time of his own father, you can imagine. Sayyidna Hibatullah sahib is present, at that time you started the book, which is famous by the name of *al-Kawākib al-Durriyyah*. And it is a magnificent book that is worthy of being written in golden letters. That... he explained in detail that the group that was repentant, it was divided into 15 parts. And the ones who were doubters and perplexed, they came into 20 parts. 20 types of them were formed. And the insistent and arrogant, they came in 35 parts. So, 15 plus 20 plus 35, this came to 70 parts. These 70 sections were formed of that *hayūlā*. Yes. And then up to the point that you have mentioned the 10 sections that were formed, who they were. That has been mentioned. The doubters and perplexed, in which the 20 sections were formed, each one has been mentioned in detail. And the 35 sections that were formed, each one has been mentioned in it by you in detail. So it is a great, a great explanation. This was almost memorized by Waliyy al-Hind Adnan sahib. The explanation. When he used to explain, he would explain the whole 70 in order. Yes, because I have read the book with him. Yes. When the whole became one corporeal body... and it was informed... And among these are the various *tasbīḥāt*, 70, 70 times, just as in the Surah... in our... isn't it? The *da'zah* that is recited daily. In the *da'zah*... and it lasts for the whole week, doesn't it? Yes. In the *Amīn al-Wamīn* it is. What has been taught... in the *da'zah*. No, it is a separate thing, and that is 70 verses. Or then in the prayers of *tatawwu'*, in our... *maghfirat al-dhunūb*, what do we read at the end? *Yā Karīmu yā Raḥīmu yā 'Aẓīm, yā ghiyāth al-mustaghīthīn wa-rajā' al-āmālīn*. Yes. That is also recited 70 times. And who is that *ghiyāth al-mustaghīthīn* (succor of those who seek help)? It is *'Āshir al-Mudabbir*. In a sense. *Yā Karīmu yā Raḥīmu yā 'Aẓīm*. He is being praised. The group, the repentant group, is praising him. *Yā Karīmu yā Raḥīmu yā 'Aẓīm, yā ghiyāth al-mustaghīthīn, aghithnī*. (O Generous, O Merciful, O Great, O succor of those who seek help, help me!). Help me. Yes. And that prayer is recited 70 times. Yes. And... this group that was there, the group that was repentant, it was a noble group, extremely noble they were. So they, what did they do? The second virtue of theirs was this, that while falling... falling was for everyone. Some were such that they stayed there, meaning they did not enter the spiritual world, but there, the world of life, below the spiritual world, the world of life, they stopped there. And did not come into the physical world. But they fell from there. They fell from the spiritual world. So what did they do? They did not blame 'Āshir. They did not blame 'Āshir that whatever happened to us, the loss we suffered, the state we came into, it was because of you. If you had not sinned, if you had not disobeyed the *Mabda' al-Awwal*, then we would not have come into this state. We followed you and came into this state. They did not do that. Rather, they praised and glorified him as they descended from there. *wa-ākhiru da'wāhum an al-ḥamdu lillāhi rabb al-'ālamīn*. (and the end of their supplication is, "Praise be to Allah, Lord of the worlds"). Their last prayer was that all praise is for Allah, who is the Lord of the worlds. So that Lord of the worlds is who? *'Āshir al-Mudabbir*. That he is the Lord of the worlds. So praising and glorifying him, they descended from there. Therefore, their return (*raj'at*) happened very quickly. Yes. The Prophet's ummah was said to be... will be divided into 73 sects. Yes, yes. So those 73... Yes. That... that 73... 70 plus three... meaning 70, from which one, one... one was taken. From the insistent ones, one, who is the most severe. And then from the doubters, who is the most severe. And from the repentant, who is the most pure and clean. Yes, that one was taken. So 70 sects have been divided in this way. 73 sects... The tasbih we do, for the sunnah, one is the tasbihat we do after the fard prayer. The tasbihat after the fard prayer also have great importance. We say Allahu Akbar and Alhamdulillah and Subhanallah 33 times, and at the end we say La ilaha illa Allah. It has various interpretations. As I said before, it is upon the *nāmiyah* (vegetative), *ḥissiyyah* (sensory), *nāṭiqah* (rational) faculties, and the last La ilaha illa Allah is upon the *ṣūrat al-sharīf* (noble form). Yes. Because from their combination, our one *ṣūrat al-sharīf* was formed. Or then, that is upon the *nāsūt al-ṭabī'ī* (natural human form) of the Imam, the *nāsūt al-dhillī* (shadow human form) of the Imam, and the *nāsūt al-khāṣṣ* (special human form), and the Imam's *ṣūrah sharīf*. So this... these are the various interpretations of the tasbihat. Similarly, the tasbih that is for the *sunnat al-fajr* (the sunnah prayer of dawn), it is much greater than these tasbihat. Therefore, it is said that the tasbih of the *sunnat al-fajr*, if someone does not have enough time to do the tasbih, for example, the Imam has stood up, then at least by saying *astaghfirullāh*, he should complete that tasbih. What is that tasbih? It is the tasbih of that group, the *nādimīn al-mustaghfirīn* (the repentant, the seekers of forgiveness). The tasbih they did while falling: *Subḥāna rabbī al-'aẓīm wa-bi-ḥamdihi, astaghfirullāh al-'aẓīm wa-atūbu ilayh*. (Glory be to my Lord, the Great, and by His praise; I seek forgiveness from Allah, the Great, and I turn to Him in repentance). And they are seeking forgiveness from Him. The *munājāt* that is in the *istiftāḥ*, at the end it is that... *yā rabbī, ẓalamtu nafsī, fa-ghfir lī... fa-innahu lā yaghfiru al-dhanb al-'aẓīm illā anta yā 'Aliyyu yā 'Aẓīm*. (O my Lord, I have wronged my soul, so forgive me... for no one forgives the great sin except You, O High, O Great one). Before that, what is it? That *munājāt* is from Imam Husayn ibn 'Ali, peace be upon him. The *istiftāḥ* in the *munājāt* that we recite is from Imam Husayn ibn 'Ali, peace be upon him. It is a separate *munājāt*. Allah the Exalted is also saying, and the one supplicating is also saying, is that it? No, there are two parts to the *munājāt*. Yes, yes. At the end, there is. *Yā rabbī, ẓalamtu nafsī*. Oh Lord, I have wronged my soul. *fa-ghfir lī*. So forgive me my sins. *fa-innahu lā yaghfiru al-dhanb al-'aẓīm*. For my great sins, no one can forgive except You, *illā anta yā 'Aliyyu yā 'Aẓīm*. So only You can forgive, that You are the High, the Great. *bi-raḥmatika yā arḥam al-rāḥimīn*. Yes. So, the one who says this, it is in the narration, Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq, peace be upon him, says that the Lord, the Exalted, asks the angels, "What are my servants saying in their prostrations?" In their supplications. "What are they saying?" So the angels say that they are saying this, that You are their Lord, and they have committed great sins, they have wronged their own souls, so no one can forgive their sins except You are the Lord, the High, the Great. So the Lord, the Exalted, says that I make you all witnesses and say that I have forgiven them. And I have pardoned them. Yes. So, this thing is very dear to Allah, that one repents to Him, turns to Him, and seeks forgiveness. As it is said: *Lā taqnaṭū min raḥmat Allāh*. (Do not despair of the mercy of Allah). Do not be hopeless about the mercy of Allah. *Inna Allāh yaghfiru al-dhunūb jamī'an*. (Indeed, Allah forgives all sins). That Allah the Exalted will forgive all sins, He has the power and the authority. But it is also said that *illā al-shirk* (except for polytheism). Except for *shirk*. He will not forgive *shirk*, he will not pardon it. He will forgive all sins, *illā al-shirk*. He will not forgive *shirk*. Because *shirk*... what is *shirk*? *al-ẓulm al-'aẓīm* (the great wrongdoing). *Shirk* has been called *ẓulm al-'aẓīm* in the Holy Quran. Yes. And here, what are they saying? Is this *munājāt* a different *munājāt*? We... Allah is... are talking about 'Āshir. No, it has different levels. The way the state of the *munājāt* is. Sometimes the reference is towards 'Aliyy al-'Aẓīm. As it is said here, *Yā 'Aliyyu yā 'Aẓīm*. Yes. And what is said here? *illā anta*. So by *illā anta* is also meant that it can be the Imam of the time. Yes. Or it is said that no Imam will forgive, meaning, will not make a decision about heaven and hell. All the Imams who have passed, they will not do it. To the extent that the *Qā'ims* too, who, in the past times, the seven *Qā'ims* of the cycles of manifestation have passed, and the six *satr* (concealment) have passed, their *Qā'ims* too will not make this decision, who will be given hell and who will be given paradise. *Illā... 'Aliyy al-'Aẓīm*. Who? The one who is the *'ayn al-ākhirah* (the final 'ayn). The *'ayn al-ākhirah*, not the *'ayn al-ūlā*. The *'ayn al-ākhirah*. And who is meant by *'ayn al-ākhirah*? The *Qā'im 'alā dhikrihi al-salām*. Because he is the *jāmi'* (accumulator/embodiment) of 400,000 years. He has the *jāmi'iyyat* (comprehensiveness) of 400,000 years with him. The *jāmi'iyyat* of seven *satr* and seven *kashf* (manifestation) is with you. Therefore, you have so much power and ability that... the power to forgive. And then... and this is also there... that is the *qaṣīm al-nār wa-l-jannah* (divider of the Fire and the Garden). That is partially also, and Mawlana 'Ali is also called this, in a partial sense. The partial role. We recite in the verses of the Quran, "*aw ẓalama nafsahu thumma yastaghfirullāh...*" (or wronged his soul, then sought forgiveness from Allah...). And then we read further that, "If the Messenger, if you are in their hearts, then we will forgive them." Yes, yes. So, that is, the intercession of the Messenger of Allah is key to that. And while you are in them. Meaning, it is about the time of the Messenger, and after the Messenger's passing, if you are present in their hearts. If you are present in their hearts, then Allah the Exalted will forgive them. If you are present in their hearts... as you... we read. Yes. Yes. He will forgive. The thing I was telling you, that about the Qā'im. And what was I saying? That *qaṣīm al-nār wa-l-jannah* is also your quality. That whomever you wish, you will admit to paradise; whomever you wish... because your *walāyat* (authority), which is present in the Holy Quran, that when people will proceed towards paradise, when their judgment will be passed, they will be stopped. They will be stopped and a question will be asked of them. And that question... it is a verse of the Holy Quran, I don't remember the verse, so they will be questioned. And what will that question be? It will be the question of the *walāyat* of 'Ali. And upon this, most Sunnis and... and all Shias, of course, but Sunni gentlemen too, the great scholars of the Sunnis are also agreed on this, that the question that has been mentioned is about the *walāyat* of 'Ali. Yes. Sir, how will we envision this? A person passes away, then in the presence of the *Qā'im al-Muntazar*... at the time of the *'ayn al-ākhirah*, the settlement will happen. So thousands of years will pass. So where will that soul remain? Which soul? The one who has passed away. And its purifications are happening. The *Qā'ims* are coming and going. But it is not being put anywhere, meaning, its settlement has not happened yet. No, but that, as far as the believer is concerned, the forms of the believer also merge with each other. Its mention has also come. Meaning, the forms of past cycles, of the believer, they also unite with the forms that come later. This also happens. So things move forward in this way. Now look, this... in the various supplications of the Prophets, among them, Janab Yunus the prophet, who... whom the fish had swallowed, who is called *ṣāḥib al-ḥūt* (the companion of the fish), so what did he say? That... *lā ilāha illā anta, subḥānaka innī kuntu min al-ẓālimīn*. (There is no deity except You; exalted are You. Indeed, I have been of the wrongdoers). So he said three things. *Lā ilāha illā anta*. Meaning, the oneness of the Lord of the worlds. And... *lā ilāha illā anta, subḥānaka*. And he did *tasbīḥ* (glorification). *subḥānaka*. *innī kuntu min al-ẓālimīn*. And he sought forgiveness. He sought forgiveness. So, the various styles that were there in the Holy Quran, or of *lā ilāha illā*, among them there is *lā ilāha illā huwa*, *lā ilāha illā Allāh* is there, *lā ilāha illā alladhī* is there, *lā ilāha illā anā* is there, but yes, but the style that you used was *lā ilāha illā anta*. And *lā ilāha illā anta* has come only once in the Holy Quran. And its status is greater than all. The status of *lā ilāha illā anta* is greater than all, in all the *lā ilāha illā Allāh*. So you used that. And that is towards the *'ayn al-'aẓīmah* (the great 'ayn). The reference is towards the *'ayn al-'aẓīmah*, that you took recourse with the *'ayn al-'aẓīmah*. And if he had not done that *tasbīḥ*, Janab Yunus, then until the Day of Resurrection (*yawm yub'athūn*), he would have remained in the belly of that same fish. So, you did that *tasbīḥ* for 40 days. Yes. The 40's... in the 'Amwaz al-Fadl al-A'ma', the *tasbīḥ* is there, it's 40, isn't it? So the mention of 40, 40 that comes in various *tasbīḥāt*, so... its reasons are also this, that in the count of 40, a power is also created. As it is said that those who reach 40 years of age, a power of maturity is born. The Messenger of Allah also did not receive prophethood until he was 40. Prophethood was with him from the first day, but when he was made a messenger, his age was 40 years. Yes. And the *ḥāḍirī salāt*, in the prostration of which we... 40... 40 times we recite. So its various reasons, of the 40, are because of this. And it has also been said that if 40 people gather and pray for someone, their prayer is accepted. Yes. Meaning, for someone who has died, for example, if 40 people gather and are reciting Yasin or Quran, then their prayers, for whom they are being done, are accepted. Therefore, often, among us, when these are recited, *sadaqah* are recited, or on special occasions, when we gather and read, so in the count of 40, this blessing is kept. Yes. To the extent that the person who was the 40th person who accepted Islam, who was he? 'Umar ibn al-Khattāb. Yes. So, the Messenger of Allah said that now Islam has gained strength. When I read this narration, I was astonished. And when I looked, it was present in the books of our da'wah. Yes. So you said... there is a place where it is said that Islam gained strength. And some other words were also used for it. So then I... I asked my elder, Qutb al-Din sahib, that this is a strange and wondrous thing, that he later became an enemy of the Ahl al-Bayt and so on, but the Messenger of Allah said such a thing for him. So he said, look, you build a house, your own bungalow, and your friends ask you, "Hey, have all the things... has your bungalow been built?" So you say, "Yes, the whole bungalow has been built, but the bathroom is left." So the same thing happened, that the final things that are there, that are left, so when they are completed, then it is said, "Our house is built." So the same state happened here too, that when he, because of his primordial intentions, he entered into Islam, so the Messenger said this thing for him.

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 12 -->

Peace be with you.
Peace be with you.
Assalamu alaykum, Ya Shaykh. I present my respects at your feet.
Wa alaykum as-salam.
Assalamu alaykum, sir.
Wa alaykum as-salam.
`A'udhu billahi min al-shaytan al-rajim` (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). `Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim` (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). `wa bihi nasta'in` (and in Him we seek help). `wa sallallahu 'ala rasulihi sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala ali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim` (And may Allah's blessings be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon the progeny of Muhammad, and may He grant them blessings and peace). Which chapter is this from?
`fa lamma hawat al-kathafa saqitatan, wa qad kana hadathat tilka al-harakat al-awwala min al-mutaharrik, awtad al-falak` (So when the density fell, falling, and that first motion had already occurred from the Mover, [creating] the pegs of the sphere).
That when the density increased, while it was falling... when density came into them, into the *hayula* (prime matter), `wa qad kana hadathat tilka al-harakat al-awwala` (and that first motion had already occurred). And the motions that had been initiated in them, the first motion that was in them, from which the first length was created, so from that first motion, what happened? That which the *mutaharrik* (the Mover), meaning the *'Ashir* (the Tenth Intellect), the first motion that he had given them, because all these are from the plannings of the *'Ashir*. Whatever motion they performed was happening through the plannings of the *'Ashir*. So, that motion... the *mutaharrik*, meaning the *'Ashir*, the motion that he gave to that *hayula*, the first motion, from that were created `awtad al-falak` (*awtad al-falak*). *Awtad al-falak* means, the pegs of the spheres. The spheres are like a tent, so its pegs, which are non-visible, they were created. `wa hiya al-tali', wa huwa al-burj alladhi yakunu fi ufuq al-mashriq` (And it is the ascendant, and it is the constellation which is on the eastern horizon). And it is the *tali'* (ascendant), the first one. And these four... which are the *awtad al-falak*? There are four. So the first is the *tali'*, which is such a *burj* (constellation) that it is on the eastern horizon. On the horizon of the east. `wa al-gharib wa huwa al-burj alladhi yakunu fi ufuq al-maghrib` (And the descendant, and it is the constellation which is on the western horizon). And after that is the *gharib* (descendant), which is on the horizon of the west. And `wa al-'ashir` (and the tenth). The third is the *'ashir*. `wa huwa al-burj alladhi yakunu fi wast al-sama'` (and it is the constellation which is in the middle of the sky). And the third is the *'ashir*, which is in the middle of the sky. `wa al-rabi', wa huwa al-burj alladhi yakunu fawqa al-ard` (And the fourth, and it is the constellation which is above the earth). And that fourth one is a *burj* such that it is `fawqa al-ard` (above the earth).
[Side talk] No, it is not `fawqa al-ard`. It is `fi wast al-ard` (in the middle of the earth). And the first one that was... `yakunu fi fawqa al-ard` (it is above the earth).
`wa 'ashir huwa al-burj alladhi yakunu fi fawqa al-ard` (and the tenth is the constellation which is above the earth). And the `wast al-sama'` (middle of the sky). And the `wa al-rabi' wa huwa al-burj alladhi yakunu fi wast taht al-ard` (and the fourth is the constellation which is in the middle under the earth).
[Phone call ringing]
[Faint voice from phone call] Is it a physical *burj*?
Yes. No, no, they are non-visible. Non-visible, meaning they are not seen by the eyes. While the spheres can be seen, these are not seen.
And this should be `fi wast al-sama'` (in the middle of the sky). And this fourth one, the *burj* is `alladhi yakunu fi wast al-sama'` (which is in the middle of the sky), which is under the earth.
`fa lamma fadat tilka al-kathafa tajadhabat-ha tilka al-awtad` (So when that density overflowed, those pegs pulled it). When the density spread, those *awtad* began to attract it. Now, what is the meaning of all these things? Well. From all directions. `wa qutba al-falak min jihatay al-shimal wa al-junub` (and the two poles of the sphere from the directions of north and south). And the poles of the *falak* are from the directions of north and south. `wa waqafat fi wast al-falak` (and it stood in the middle of the sphere). And they stopped, they stood in the middle of the *falak*. `wa sara al-falak muhitan biha fi jami' jihatiha` (and the sphere became encompassing it from all its directions). And the *falak*, the sky, is *muhit* (encompassing), meaning it is spread out, having taken them from all directions. `wa al-ard wa ma 'alayha` (and the earth and what is upon it). And taking the earth as well, it is spread out. `wa abadan fi wast ka-ma muh al-bayda al-asfar al-muhit bihi al-bayad` (and forever in the middle, like the yolk of the yellow egg which is encompassed by the white). Just as there is an egg, whose yolk is its core, and the white encompasses it. `wa al-qishr min jami' jihatiha` (and the shell from all its directions). And its shell is from all directions. `thumma infataqa al-jaww wa turattibat al-aflak` (Then the atmosphere was split open and the spheres were arranged). Then, the atmosphere split, and the spheres were arranged. And they were nine spheres. `wa kullu falak min ha fi zimn al-ladhi ba'dah` (and every sphere from them is within the one after it). And every *falak* is within the next *falak*... the lower *falak* is within its scope. The lower *falak* is within its scope. An example for this is given in another book, that it is like an onion. One layer is under another layer. So in this way, the spheres are layered one on top of another.
Like a flower.
No, but flowers have their own individuality. The case of flowers is not like this. The flower... under one intellect there are countless flowers. But that second intellect is not under the higher intellect. They have their own individualities. Here it is such that there is a globe of the spheres, which the encompassing sphere (*falak-e-muhit*) has surrounded. Under the *falak-e-muhit* are all these spheres, and the spheres are such that the higher *falak* encompasses the lower *falak*.
And these are nine spheres. In total, there are nine spheres. `al-falak al-muhit` (the encompassing sphere).
The `falak al-muhit`, `falak al-buruj` (the sphere of the constellations), and these seven spheres. Up to the moon. From Zuhal (Saturn) to the moon, there are seven spheres.
And the `falak al-muhit`, `muhitun bi al-jami'` (encompasses all). And the *falak-e-muhit* encompasses all the spheres. `yuharriku jami' al-aflak fi kulli yawm wa layla harakatan kulliyatan ilahiyatan bi quwwatin mudabbira` (It moves all the spheres every day and night with a total, divine motion, by a governing power). And by the power of the *mudabbir* (governor/manager), that *falak-e-muhit* moves, day and night, it makes one total motion, which is its divine motion.
`bi quwwat al-mudabbir` (by the power of the governor). Yes. Meaning, day and night, in a day and night, it completes one motion. The *falak-e-muhit*, in a day and night, makes one total motion. `wa ma-bda'uha min al-mashriq ila al-maghrib` (and its beginning is from the east to the west). And its matter is spread from the east to the west. `wa turattibat aflak al-sab'a sayyarat kullu kawkab minha fi falakih` (and the seven spheres were arranged, the planet of each one of them orbiting in its sphere). And the seven spheres, from Zuhal to the moon, every *falak* has another *falak* under it. `wa sarat jami' al-nujum fi al-falak al-thamin` (and all the stars came to be in the eighth sphere). And all the stars, that we see or do not see, all of them are in the eighth sphere. That is, `al-musamma falak al-buruj` (named the sphere of the constellations). It is called the *falak-e-buruj*. In the *falak-e-buruj* are all the stars. An example for this is: just as all the believers, when they pass away, where do they ultimately gather? They gather at the *Bab al-Abwab*, which is the *falak-e-buruj* of the era. Meaning, they are not with the Imam. Rather, their final destination is at the *Bab al-Abwab*. And they gather at the *Bab al-Abwab*. In the same way, in the spiritual realm, the gathering of all the intellects will be at the *Mubda'-e-Awwal*. In the physical realm, the gathering of all the luminous forms will be at the *Bab al-Abwab*. And in the same way, all the stars that are visible or not visible, the gathering of all of them is happening in the *falak-e-buruj*. And the *falak-e-buruj* is the eighth sphere.
`maqsumatun...`
The *falak-e-muhit* is empty.
The motion that has been mentioned for the *falak-e-muhit*, that it completes one cycle in a day and night. But the *falak-e-muhit* is the highest. All the spheres below it have different durations of day and night. The day and night we have in the world, the duration of 24 hours. The duration of day and night for every sphere is different. So which duration is being talked about here? Which day and night?
Well, that is not clarified here. Because with its motion, all the lower spheres are also moving.
No, it says here, that it completes one motion in a day and night. So what day and night should we imagine? The day and night in the world is 24 hours. But the duration of day and night for every sphere is different.
Here, the motion of the *falak-e-muhit* is with respect to us, it's our day and night.
[Faint voice from phone call] My final thought is... the *muhit* is moving, due to which all the spheres are moving in their own days and nights. The measure of the world's day and night is different, the moon's day and night is different.
Yes.
The measure of Jupiter is different. But each one's day and night is different. They are all moving due to it, from the *muhit* itself.
Yes.
The day and night is the distance from the sun, isn't it? The distance is different, so the length and breadth of the day and night is different.
Yes, yes.
So the point is that the *falak-e-muhit* is moving, in a day and night, meaning in 24 hours, it completes one motion. Meaning, from where it starts, it comes back there and its motion is complete. Yes. And it is certainly true that the spheres below it, their timings will be different. It's not clarified here. We have to understand it for ourselves.
Is it that the spheres are around the earth, or around the sun?
No, the earth is the center point. All the spheres are around it.
But what science says is different. It says they are around the sun.
The center is the Ka'ba, so the spheres also revolve around it.
But science...
Science has changed its view three times on this. First, it used to say this, that everything revolves around the earth. Then they changed it, then they changed it back. They've changed it three times.
And this, `maqsumatun ithna 'ashar qisman` (divided into twelve sections). And the *falak-e-buruj* is also divided into twelve sections. `kullu qism minha burjun` (each section of it is a constellation).
And it is also that every section that it is divided into has 30, 30 houses. The houses of every *burj*. Meaning, the partitioned *buruj*. The small *buruj* that are made, inside one *falak-e-buruj*, the partitioned *buruj* are there, so they have 30 houses. In this way, 12 times 30 is 36, 360 degrees it becomes.
Now when our *fudala'* (excellent ones) ascend, then from one beginning to the end, to go through 360, they have to traverse this entire path. For their purification.
In going and in coming. In this way, what happens? 720 years pass. And the temperament of the initiated one, in ascending and descending... meaning, from the grave, for the *fudlat* (impurities) to ascend, and then from the spheres to come to the earth, becoming a human... when they become human, they will come to earth, won't they? So that... that 720 years and 280 years. 280 years in ascending and descending, and 720 years in this way, that from the first *burj* to the last *burj*, the 360 degrees that are there of the *buruj*, they traverse them. And then, they come back to the first *burj*. So this going and coming of theirs happens in 720 years. And so that their purification happens. After being purified, they become translucent, and then they are brought [back].
What is this purification of?
It is of the *qasathat* (dross/impurities). Our calculations, our impurities.
And we have *qasathat* in this world as well... So what kind of *qasathat* is that over there? Because these are the dealings with people in the world, that is the *qisath*.
Yes.
So over there...
Meaning, what kind of purification?
No, it's not detailed, but our life is within the circle of *qisas* (retribution). Just as `wa lakum fi al-qisasi hayatun ya uli al-albab` (And for you in retribution there is life, O you of understanding). That in your *qisas* is your life. We think that *qisas* is happening in our life, but in reality, our life is running within *qisas*. The circle of *qisas* is very large, and our life is a small line that is moving forward. So what are the matters? Because the *qisasat* are spread over many cycles. It's not a matter of one cycle. Whatever the past matters may be, what they may or may not be.
