`wa sara falak al-tasi' khaliyan la shay'a fihi min al-buruj` (and the ninth sphere became empty, with nothing in it of the constellations). And the ninth *falak*, which is called the *falak-e-muhit*, is empty. It has no *buruj* or *kawakib* (planets). It is empty. Just as with the Imam, none of our forms gather. Whatever form of ours gathers, it is through the *Bab al-Abwab*, and then the Imam, taking it, gives it to his *farzand* (son/successor). So with the Imam, not even a single one of our forms is gathering. Whether it's a high *hadd* or a low *hadd*, all of them are at the *Bab al-Abwab*, which is the *fasl al-khitab* (decisive speech/final judgment). Yes. So they gather there. And just as in the spiritual realm, with the *'Aql-e-Awwal* (First Intellect), no form gathers, no intellect came there. It is empty of that state. Yes.
And this *Mubda'-e-Awwal*, the forms with him... two *du'at* have given two explanations. One said they are 28. 28 means there is a grouping of 28 there. At the *Mubda'-e-Awwal*. At the *Mubda'-e-Awwal*, the *'uqul* that are there... when this... these divisions of *'uqul* were happening, at that time, the collection it had was of 28 *'uqul*. And that is in correspondence with the *kalima* `La ilaha illa Allah`. And someone said, one *da'i* said that there were 12. So I had asked in the *majlis* (assembly), "Why is there this difference, between 12 and 28?" Because one *da'i* says 28, another *da'i* says 12. So they said both are correct. I asked how. They said, one *da'i*... perhaps Sayyidna Hasan bin Nuh... he said 12. And Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman said 28. So what Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman is seeing, he is seeing the *'uqul* in their totality, that they are 28. And Sayyidna Hasan bin Nuh, he... what he is seeing is that the count of the letters of the *kalima* `La ilaha illa Allah` is 12, so he is seeing it from that perspective. So there is a difference in their perspectives, but both are correct.
The Imam takes the *suwarat*...
But the *Mubda'-e-Awwal* does not take them.
The plans are of the *'Aql-e-Awwal*. The decision he is making is the *'Aql-e-Awwal*'s, even though the forms are gathering at the *Mubda'-e-Awwal*. Yes.
And when the Imam gives that entire collection of forms to his *farzand*, the *farzand* until then is an Imam, made of *riya'* (subtle matter). And the life he lives, what he eats, breathes, drinks, all of it is *riya'*. Yes. And after that, when he receives this collection, that collection is the *imamat*. So when the Imam, when the *imamat* joins him, then from potential he becomes actual. The child. Yes. And the Imam, upon whom be peace, passes away. And with his passing, the *Bab al-Abwab* also passes away and becomes the *qalb* (heart) of the Imam's *farzand*. Just as Mawlana Abu Talib, upon whom be peace, three days after his passing, Janab Khadija also passed away. Yes. For which the Prophet said that this year is my year of grief. You have... you... he called that year the year of grief, that two great personalities passed away, who had helped him and who had taken care of him throughout their lives. Why? They were the Imam, and she was his *hujjat*. Yes.
Because Mawlatuna Khadija is the *Bab al-Abwab* of whom? Of Bibi Fatimah al-Zahra. Yes.
`wa sara falak al-tasi' khaliyan` (And the ninth sphere became empty). And the ninth sphere is empty, `li-latafatihi wa sharafih` (due to its subtlety and its nobility). Because of its subtlety, because it is made from the highest part of the *nadamat* (repentance) of the repentant ones. `faslun. fa lamma taharraka al-falak al-harakat al-ula` (Chapter. So when the sphere moved the first motion...). So when the *falak* made the first motion, `ramat al-kawakib bi-ashi'atiha nahw al-ard` (the planets threw their rays towards the earth). So, there is one *kawkab* (planet) and one *falak* (sphere). Within the *falak* is the *kawkab*. *Falak* is the sky. And *kawkab* is, for instance, Zuhal. Zuhal has a *falak*, a sky, an orbit. And within it is Zuhal. Similarly, every *falak* has a orbit. So it says here that when the *falak*, when it casts its rays, and it cast them towards the earth, so `fa kana qad baqiya 'ala wajhiha shay'un min jins al-falak` (so there remained on its face something of the nature of the sphere). So after casting the rays, what happened? On the earth, some things of the nature of the *falak* remained. `fa jadhabat-ha al-kawakib ila dhawatiha` (so the planets attracted them to their essences). And those... when the rays were cast, the *kawakib* attracted them to their essences, meaning they returned again. The spheres cast the rays, and they hit the earth and then returned to them. `wa sayyarat lahu al-asdaf musharraqa` (and they made shells/casings for it, which were illuminated). And in this way, upon the spheres, which were non-visible, *asdaf* (shells/casings) were placed upon them. Meaning, a covering was placed on them. Yes. And the spheres are made from the most subtle parts of the *nadimin*. But when their rays fell on the earth and the *sakhra* (rock/dense matter), and from there vapors rose and became their *asdaf*, what happened to the *asdaf*? A negativity (*nahusat*) was created in them. As for example, Zuhal. The planet of Zuhal is very pure and clean. It is *tayyib* (good). But because of its *sadaf* (shell), negativity was created in it. So he is explaining the same thing. That `wa hiya allati yudrikuha al-basar min al-nujum` (and it is they which the sight perceives of the stars). And in this way, the eyes can see those stars. `fa-hiya kanat wahmiyatan ka-l-aflak la taraha al-absar` (for they were imaginary/notional like the spheres, the sights did not see them). Whereas in their original state, they were notional. Just like the spheres, the eyes could not see them. `fa ja'ala al-mudabbir ta'ala tilka al-asdaf bi-hikmatihi li-inarat 'alam al-zulmani al-jismani` (So the Governor, may He be exalted, made those shells with His wisdom to illuminate the dark, physical world). Then the *Mudabbir Ta'ala* (Governing Being, Most High) made those *asdaf* through this planning, that they became visible and illuminated the world below. `wa li-anna qudrat al-hawas al-bashar` (And because the power of human senses...). Now a different topic is starting. I had some notes on this, on the *mabda'-ma'ad*, which Mawla 'Abbas Saheb had written. So I thought I would read them to you so that the topic, which is the topic of the spheres, which is a bit complex, would become clear. So you write that, at the time of the *da'wat* of the *'Ashir*, three groups were formed. Right? When the *da'wat* of the *'Ashir* happened, three groups were formed. And this xerox has become so bad that the letters are smudged. And when the *'Ashir* gave the *da'wat*, due to their disobedience, three states befell them. Meaning, despite the guidance of the *'Ashir*, when they disobeyed, saying, `la nasma'u wa la nuti'` (we will not hear and we will not obey), "we neither listen to your words nor obey you. We don't accept you, nor those who came before you, except for the *Mubda' Ta'ala* (the Originated Being, Most High). The *Mubda' Ta'ala* originated us, we accept him, and `sawa'an bi-sawa'in` (equal with equal) was created. You are also equal, we are also equal, the one above is also equal." So in that, no... all are in the state of the first perfection (*kamal-e-awwal*). So they saw the state of the first perfection and acknowledged it. But the second perfection (*kamal-e-thani*) which the *'uqul* and the *Mubda'-e-Awwal* had attained, they did not acknowledge that, due to which this state was created in them. And some among them were repentant (*nadim*) and were seeking forgiveness. Some among them were doubters (*shakk*) and were bewildered (*mutahayyir*). And some were stubborn (*hadd-dharam*), proud and arrogant. In this way, the three categories of *nadim*, *shakk*, and *musirr* (insistent/stubborn) or *mustakbir* (arrogant) were formed. Yes. This state continued. So it is said that this *isbagh al-wudu'* (performing ablution thoroughly), what is it? It is to perform ablution three times. So it was asked, can a person do it more than three times? Meaning, while performing ablution, can he gargle four, five, six times instead of three, or wash his face ten times while reciting the supplications? So they said, `la fadila lahu` (there is no virtue in it for him). There is no virtue in it for him. There is no virtue established in doing it after the third time.
Is it the same for *ruku'* and *sujud*?
No... `aqallu fihi` (the minimum in it is). At least three times should be done. Yes. But for that it is not said that one shouldn't do more than three. Yes, if a person is praying on his own, individually. Because if he is leading the prayer, then he must be mindful not to prolong it too much. Meaning, not to do the *tasbihat* ten times, or eight or seven times, because then the followers will have to wait, and they will become weary. So it is even said that you should not recite long *surahs* if the followers behind you are weak. Because such incidents have happened. The fathers of Sayyidna Muhammad al-Jamali and Sayyidna Ahmad al-Jamali, who were their fathers, what was his name? 'Ali. 'Ali bin 'Abdullah. And he was a great *mansub*. Like the... just as today Sayyid Mansub Sharf is there, who is the greatest *mansub*... *mansub* of Najran, the *mansub* of Najran becomes the greatest of all the ranks. Sayyid Ghulam Saheb appointed him. Yes. So, that Sayyidna 'Ali bin 'Abdullah al-Makrami, he used to lead such long prayers, he would recite such long *surahs* that it is said people would faint. [laughter] It's so long that they would fall down, fainting. So it reminds me, in the same way, we went to a village called Khiyara. Khiyara is a village in Sindh. To the... to the mosque there. There was a mulla there, what was his name, I don't remember. So we had gone there. So, Hazrat Abdullah Saheb was present. So after the Fajr prayer, there was an open courtyard, we were sitting there. And it was the cold season. So, Hazrat Abdullah Saheb said, "Is everything alright?" So the mulla said, "I have a big complaint against my community. I have a very big complaint against my community." So Hazrat asked, "What is the matter?" He said, "Mawlana, you see, I... `As-salamu 'alaykum wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuh, As-salamu 'alaykum wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuh`. After doing this, I see that all the people get up and leave. No one remains." "So for whom should I recite the *ratib* (post-prayer litany)? For whom should I recite the *ratib*?" So Hazrat also said, "Look, you people shouldn't do that. You should sit, the *ratib* has great importance, it is also a very good supplication of ours." So when the mulla recites, you should sit. So one of the men said, "Mawlana, you don't know the real story." He said, "This... the *surahs* he recites, you should understand, he recites Surah Yasin, Surah... if he starts Surah al-Baqarah, what will be our condition?" [laughter] "So, we cannot bear it." So then Hazrat explained to him, "You should be a bit considerate, recite medium-length *surahs*, don't recite such long *surahs*." And he was a very good scholar, that mulla. Keep in mind. I don't remember his name now. Shamsuddin... no, it wasn't Shamsuddin.
Wahid Bakhsh.
Yes, Wahid Bakhsh. Mulla Wahid Bakhsh. Mulla Wahid Bakhsh was his name. And he was a very loving person, a friend of knowledge. And the lessons of *Bab al-Muqaddas*, he used to remember them so well, and the things... he used to ask questions. He was a very simple-natured person.
So anyway. These all, from the realm of purity and light, due to density, fell down. Meaning, this *hayula*, which was divided into three parts, fell down. Because density had been created in it. Because density brings one down, and subtlety takes one up. Spiritual progress is attained through this.
Then the arrangement of the physical world. The wise governing *'Ashir*, from those who were the most pure and clean, who were the group of the *nadimin* (repentant ones), from them he arranged the seven spheres and the planets. Yes. This makes it very clear. These seven spheres and the planets were arranged. From the middle-level pure souls, that is... he... what did he do? The four mothers, meaning fire, air, water, and earth, were made. Yes. Meaning, these... these were made from whom? From the *shakkin* (doubters) and *mutahayyirin* (bewildered). And from the most dense, meaning those who were the *musirrin* (stubborn) and *mustakbirin* (arrogant), from them he made the *sakhra* (rock/foundation). Meaning, the core of the earth. The central part. Which is the center of the earth. The lowest part in the earth. This was the justice and wisdom of the *'Ashir*, which was in accordance with the intentions and consciences of these souls. Just as were their intentions, and just as were their consciences, in that way a place was appointed for them in the spheres, in the mothers, and on the earth.
Then what happened? The *falak-e-muhit* (encompassing sphere), this entire realm... and what is in the realm... whatever is in the realm, and the realm itself, it has encompassed it. The *falak-e-muhit*. That's why it is called the *falak-e-muhit*, meaning it has encompassed the realm, it has surrounded it. Just as the Imam encompasses all...
[Phone call ringing]
The *falak-e-muhit*... It is also called the gate of the spiritual world. Meaning, the door to enter the spiritual world. Because after this, after this is the world of life. The world of life. Meaning, when the *'Ashir* repented, then with his repentance, a large group repented. A large group repented with the repentance of the *'Ashir*. After that, what happened? The last person of that group, by the command of the *'Ashir*, gave the *da'wat* (call). Those who repented, that group was called `Ta'ibu bi-Tawbatihi` (those who repented with his repentance). They repented with his repentance. And then that last person of that group, when he gave the *da'wat* by the command of the *'Ashir*, another group was prepared, which was called `Labbu Nida'ahu` (those who answered his call). They said "Labbayk" (Here I am at your service) to his call. But the matter with all of them, the `Ta'ibu bi-Tawbatihi` and the `Labbu Nida'ahu`, what happened with them was that the `Ta'ibu bi-Tawbatihi` went with the *'Ashir*. They went with the *'Ashir*'s group. And they ascended into the spiritual world. They became intellects and ascended. With their group. But the `Ta'ibu bi-Tawbatihi`... they... in the spiritual world, they did not ascend with the *'Ashir*. Rather, for them, another world was created, which is called the world of life. And there is the *tamur* (repository), and there is the spiritual Ka'ba. And that spiritual Ka'ba is connected to the physical Ka'ba. Yes. So, this... this state happened. So after the *falak-e-muhit* is not the beginning of the spiritual world, but in between comes the world of life. Life comes.
Did the *qalam* then come down?
The *qalam*... yes, they came among the *nadimin*.
So they had to come.
Yes, they had to come. But among them too, among them too, what happened was that the first... the first... which Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah has written in *Kawakib*, that perhaps the first category, `lam tatasawwam` (they did not take form). Meaning, they did not come into the physical world. Meaning, physicality did not come to them. And they remained there. The first group that was there. After that, later on, from the groups, the spheres and so on were made. Yes.
This world of life, will we also call it the world of intention (*'alam-e-niyyat*)?
No, the *'alam-e-niyyat* is up there. The *'alam-e-niyyat* is... our... when our... when the *Mubda'* originated, at that time, what our intentions were. And our intention that was there, after the origination, we remained in a passive state. Then the *'uqul* affirmed the oneness of the Lord of the worlds, but we did not join them. We remained in a passive state. Until the *'Ashir*'s illusory equality came, and we also fell into that illusory equality. And thus, no intellect was such that it neither became one of the *'uqul* that entered the circle, nor joined the *'Ashir*'s illusory equality, but remained in the middle. It was not so. Only two things happened: either they went with the *'Ashir*, or they became intellects and ascended. Yes. So in this matter, what our intention was, with the *'Ashir*, our dealing that happened, in that, there were various intentions, not just one intention. All the consciences were different. Therefore, the state that befell us, we became *nadimin*, *shakk*, *musirrin*, in that, grouping happened. So our world of intention was established right there. Yes.
So, it is called the gate of the spiritual world. It is also called the world of sanctity (*'alam al-quds*), the gate of the world of sanctity. This *muhit*. Yes. Or there are other names for it too. Yes, it is called that.
The *tamur* is also that?
No, the *tamur* is different. The *tamur* is above. It is above the *falak-e-muhit*. It is in the world of life. The *tamur* is in the world of life. In which the forms of the *mohtajibin* (veiled ones) are present. Who are the *mohtajibin* of the Imams, their original essences are where? They are in the *tamur*. Yes. Who did not enter the spiritual world.
The Quranic verse, `wa kulli shay'in khalaqna zawjayn` (and of all things We created two mates/pairs). Where did this foundation of "two" come from?
I would have to look that up, I don't recall.
The Prophet's *mi'raj* (ascension) also, the Prophet's *mi'raj* was not to the spiritual world, nor to the world of life. Where did you go up to? To the *falak-e-muhit*. The *falak-e-muhit*. Your *mi'raj* was up to the *falak-e-muhit*. After that, you returned. Because once, if one goes beyond the *falak-e-muhit*, then one cannot return either. To enter there, one must become an intellect. Yes.
Then what? The *falak-e-buruj*. In this, the stars are seen. All the stars, which are countless. It has been divided into 12 parts, and each part has been given a name. And there are 12 names. The first name is Hamal (Aries). Its first name is Hamal. Then al-Thawr (Taurus). Al-Jawza' (Gemini). Al-Sartan (Cancer). These are four. Then Asad (Leo). This is the fifth. Al-Sunbula (Virgo), six. Then Mizan (Libra). Then al-'Aqrab (Scorpio), eight. Then... al-Qaws (Sagittarius), al-Jady (Capricorn), al-Dalw (Aquarius), al-Hut (Pisces). These are the 12 parts of the *falak-e-muhit*.
These are the Zodiac signs.
Yes, Zodiac signs.
I don't remember their English names. So these 12 parts were made. Then, below the *falak-e-muhit*, there are the orbits of the seven skies. Below the *falak-e-buruj*. Below the *falak-e-buruj*, there are the orbits of the seven skies. In each orbit, a planet moves. It is by this name that the *falak* is known. For instance, Zuhal. So it is called the *falak* of Zuhal. And it is the planet of Zuhal. The planet of Zuhal is in the *falak* of Zuhal. The first *falak* is of Zuhal. The second is of Mushtari (Jupiter). The third is of Mirrikh (Mars). And the fourth is of Shams (the Sun). And the fifth is of Zuhra (Venus). The sixth is of 'Utarid (Mercury). And the seventh is Qamar (the Moon). From the revolution of all these, the sphere of ether (*kurra-e-asir*), the sphere of air (*kurra-e-nasim*) came into existence. These all, the revolutions they made, so the *kurra-e-asir*, *kurra-e-nasim*, in which we breathe, that came into existence. And fire, water, air, and earth, these all came into being. Which are called the mothers. The governing being of the world assigned each planet to the governance of the physical world. Every planet governs the physical world. First was the governance of Zuhal. Then the helper of Zuhal was Mushtari. Then it was Mirrikh. Then Shams. Meaning, the governance of Zuhal is ongoing, but these became its helpers. And then it was Zuhra, then 'Utarid. Then in the seventh period, meaning in the seventh thousand-year period, under the governance of Qamar, the first human came into existence. When the governance of Zuhal is on, but in the governance of Zuhal, when Qamar became its companion in governance, then in that governance, in the seventh thousand-year period, that human came into existence.
Now these densities were falling. From this, four *awtad al-falak*... four directions were also established. Yes. Directions were later established. That is, *tali'*, *gharib*, *'ashir*, and *rabi'*, meaning East, West, North, and South. These four directions also came into being. And two poles, the North Pole and the South Pole. The density started falling. So the four... [text is cut off] and the pull of the two poles held it in the very middle. The sky encompassed it from all sides, like the white and shell of an egg encompass the yolk. These six directions are called *sitta jihat*. This... the four directions, up and down... a material world is encompassed in this. It is explained in this way, that right, left, front, back, and up and down. These all directions were established.
In the first thousand years, the governance was of Zuhal. And in it, vapors, rains, occurred frequently, and the entire earth became a vast ocean. In this, the substances for things were created that resembled the temperament of Zuhal. Iron, copper, Abyssinians, and farmers, laborers, and hardworking laborers, etc., these were created. In this way, it moved forward. So in the first thousand years, the governance of Zuhal continued. And then in the second thousand years, the governance of Zuhal continued, but Mushtari became its helper. In this way, their effects were established.

<!-- AUG:mabda-maad-gh-2022 lecture 13 -->

The last line of the seventeenth page, look. Assalamu 'alaykum. Wa 'alaykum as-salam.

A'udhu billahi minash shaytanir rajim (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). Wa bihi nasta'in (And in Him we seek help). Wa sallallahu 'ala rasulihi Sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala aali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim (And may Allah's blessings and peace be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon the family of Muhammad, and may He bless and grant peace).

Fa ja'ala al-mudabbir Ta'ala tilka al-azdad (Then the Planner, the Exalted, made those opposites). Then the Planner, the Exalted, that is, the Ultimate Planner, made opposites for those *kawakib* (celestial bodies).

And I have told you, you know, how the opposites were formed. The rays of the *kawakib* fell on the earth, fell on the rock, and then they rose from there, and they went towards the *kawakib*. Due to this, the opposites were formed.

Bi-hikmatihi (By His wisdom). This was His wisdom. Li-inarat al-'alam al-zulmani al-jismani (To illuminate the dark, physical world). So that in that dark and physical world, which was the earth, light would be produced. Yes.

Wa li-annaha tudriku hawas al-bashar (And so that the senses of man may perceive it). So that it, the senses of man... *tadriku*, *tadriku hawas al-bashar*... so that the senses of man can perceive it, this world of *kawakib* and so on. That, and of that time. Fa-tahaqqaqa `indahum qudrat al-mudabbir wa sahat wujud al-'alam al-falaki (So it was confirmed with them, the power of the Planner and the soundness of the existence of the celestial world). Therefore, this matter proves and confirms for them how great the power of the Planner is, and the existence of the celestial world and the soundness of it... it is indicating that matter.

Wa lamma taharraka al-falak... ma yalihi min al-jaww al-muntafa... alladhi fi zimn al-falak al-qamar (And when the celestial sphere moved... what is below it of the inflated atmosphere... which is within the sphere of the moon.) And when the sphere (*falak*), meaning the *falak-e-muhit* (encompassing sphere), moved, then whatever things were below it, in them... that... the atmosphere, and that which... in them, that space in which sluggishness and lifelessness had been created, such that they were under the sphere of the moon, that air and space... fi nihayat al-hararah (with extreme heat), in it, extreme heat was produced. Due to that movement of the *falak-e-muhit*. Wal-yubs (and dryness), and dryness was produced. Wa tilka tabi'at al-nar (and that is the nature of fire). And that was the nature of fire. Fa summiya al-asir (So it was named Ether). And that nature of fire, whose nature was fire, was called *asir* (ether). Wa huwa awwal al-umahat al-arba'ah (And it is the first of the four mothers).

Every sphere has its own *kawakib*. Every sphere. Only the *falak-e-muhit* and *buruj* do not have *kawakib*. Yes.

That *falak-e-muhit* is *ghair-mar'i* (unseen/invisible).

When it comes to its movement... yes. So the *kawakib* moves in its own sphere. That movement happens. If there is only a sphere, how does it move? No, that sphere, meaning the *falak-e-muhit*, its movement is a total/universal movement, under which are all the spheres (*aflak*) and *kawakib*. So its... its... the movement of the *falak-e-muhit* that is happening, due to that, the *kawakib* and *aflak* below are moving.

Mawla, the movement is being done by the *kawakib* in its own sphere.

Yes, it is moving in its own sphere. In its own sphere. The *kawakib*. The sphere (*falak*) is not moving. The sphere is not moving. The *kawakib* are moving. Yes. No, the sphere is not moving... It could be that the *aflak* also move. I don't know that for sure. But...

The *aflak* are like an orbit.

Yes, but movement is produced in them.

They move, then the ones inside move. The outer one also moves.

The *kawakib* move in their...

No, no. The *aflak* also move. The *falak-e-muhit* also moves. It's not stationary.

No, no. It's not stationary. The movement of the *falak-e-muhit* is a total/universal movement in 24... in day and night. That movement has been called the Divine movement. Because it is *ghair-mar'i* (unseen). And all the *aflak* and *kawakib* are under it... within its circle. Therefore, due to its movement, movement is produced in the spheres below. Its movement is one movement in 24 hours. Yes. And the *falak-e-muhit* itself is *ghair-mar'i*, meaning it is not visible. All the other *aflak* are visible to the eyes. And in the *falak-e-buruj* (sphere of the zodiac), all the stars are present. Yes, the *falak-e-buruj* is comprehensive... it has the collection of all the stars. Yes. And our *fuzalat* (spiritual essences/remains), after our demise, we are divided into three things. One is the *surat-e-sharif* (noble form), which in the blink of an eye, as soon as one passes away, goes to one's *hadd-e-'ali* (higher rank/minister). One state is this. Meaning, the highest thing went to the lowest place. Meaning, in a terrestrial sense. Your *hadd-e-'ali* is not in the heavens, but will be somewhere on earth. Or, will have... will have passed away. But despite having passed away, it is still with some *hadd*.

So *hudud-e-'ali* (higher ranks) are of two types. One is the one whose *hadd-e-'ali* has already passed away. And the second *hadd-e-'ali* is the one who has not passed away. And what is a *hadd-e-'ali*? It's not some very great saint. He is just nineteen-to-twenty different from you. The difference between our *hadd-e-'ali* and us is only a nineteen-to-twenty difference. So that *hadd-e-'ali* could be a person from our own family, a person from our neighborhood, a person from our island, or a person from another island. These... these... these are the things... It's possible that we have met him in life or we keep meeting him, or we have never met him. These are all possibilities.

So this... in short, the point is that his *nafs*... the *surat-e-sharif*... remains in the lowest part, that is, on the earth.

And the middle thing, which is called *riyat* (winds/essences), which are released after three days, those *riyat* go to the *aflak* (celestial spheres). Their main station is in the *falak-e-qamar* (sphere of the moon), and they also go to Jupiter and the Sun, and they are refined and processed, and then in a cleansed state, they gather again in the moon, and then there is a process of how they are to come to the earth again. But the lowest thing of ours, that is, our body, which was the lowest of all these things, but its... its... the purification that is happening, its return (*raj'at*), is to the highest place. That is, in the *falak-e-buruj* (sphere of the zodiac). In the *falak-e-buruj*, those 360... years, its... in a year... its... for one... from the first *burj* (zodiac sign) to the last *burj*, its... in 360 years, it has to go there.

Thirty... they are divided into 30 degrees, and it has to go like that, and then there is a return as well. And... in ascending and descending... in ascending and descending... 700... how many... 280 years... 720... yes, 720 plus 280 years. So in this way, after 1000 years, it comes back again. But when it comes back again, it is minus its *namiya* (vegetative), *namiya-hissiya* (sensitive), and *namiya-natiqa* (rational). And some who are *hudud-e-'aliya* (higher ranks), their higher parts also ascend in their first cycle, so they are also reduced. Yes. But it remains largely the same person. In this way, its purification continues.

So, it means that in one *kaur* (cycle), our... 360 times, our *fuzlah* (spiritual essence)... our own *fuzlah* repeats 360 times. It repeats 360 times. Therefore, Sayyidna `Ali साहब` has written in one place that in our own body, in a believer's body, there are 360 *nufus* (souls/selves). And every *nafs* makes progress in every cycle. Meaning that in 360 parts, that part is progressing which is more subtle, and it progresses by becoming a *nafs*. The progress it has to make from the body is by becoming a *nafs*. Yes.

So its... the one who will be the *hadd-e-'ali*, if he has passed away, will there be a pause or will he go to his own *hadd-e-'ali*?

If the *hadd-e-'ali* has passed away, then his *surat* (form) merges with the *surat* of that *hadd-e-'ali*. It becomes united with it. It merges. But if the *hadd-e-'ali* has not passed away, then the *surat* of the one who is passing away remains in the vicinity (*jiwar*) of the *surat* of his *hadd-e-'ali*. Because it is possible that his *hadd-e-'ali* might go astray. If he goes astray, then this one will be at a loss. The one ascending will be at a loss. Therefore, it remains in the vicinity (*jiwar*) of his *hadd-e-'ali*, in the neighborhood. Yes. Until he passes away. Until his *hadd-e-'ali* passes away, his end is good (*khatima-bil-khair*), then after that his *surat* ascends and merges with him.

If he went astray, the *hadd-e-'ali*, then what happens to this one?

His *surat* is removed. He is given another *hadd-e-'ali*. Then the *hadd-e-'ali* who goes astray, for them too the situation is such that... one thing is that if he is looked at... with investigation, he had also done good deeds. The person who went astray, he had also done good deeds in his life, and was connected to the *da'wat* (mission), had served. Then he went astray. So two states were created for him. His good, luminous *surat* (*surat-e-nayyirah*) was separated... from his bad *surat*. Just as Da'ud ibn Qutb's *surat-e-sharifah*... the noble *surat*... was given to Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fu'ad. It came to Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fu'ad. Yes. Just as Abu Bakr's *surat-e-nayyirah*, which was from the services he rendered... the service to the Prophet, or the service to the noble *da'wat*... his *surat-e-nayyirah* was separated, and it was given to his own son, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Abi Bakr. Yes.

This is a separate matter...

Yes...

Say it.

Alright. So, in this way, divine affairs keep happening. Yes.

Your device is switching power.

Mawla, this going to the *hadd-e-'ali*... is it necessary that he be in the same era, or is it possible that he exists in some other era?

In another era, meaning?

Is it possible that... the era in which we are living, he is not in that era? Or the era that has passed...

No, no, his *hadd-e-'ali* cannot be from another Imam's *hudud* (ranks), whose cycle (*haikal*) is over. For example, two Imams before, their cycle is over. For his *hadd-e-'ali* to be from among their *hudud* is not possible. Not possible. Rather, this Imam's... the current Imam... in his era, the *hudud-e-daniya* and *'aliya* (lower and higher ranks) that exist, his *hadd-e-'ali* will be from among them. Who... until now... have reached the *majma'-e-bab al-abwab* (assembly of the gate of gates) or have not reached. It's possible that his *hadd-e-'ali* has reached the *majma'-e-bab al-abwab*. This is possible. Or the *hadd-e-'ali* is lined up... and is in the process of promotion, so his *hadd-e-'ali* is among them. But once the *majma'* (assembly) that gathers at the *bab al-abwab*... when it is completed... and the Imam `alayhis salam` (peace be upon him) gives that *majma'* to his son... which is in fact the Imamate... he hands over the Imamate to his son, and that child, the son of the Imam, goes from potential to actual... then after that, his *hadd-e-'ali*... does not go into it. No, he cannot go into it.

But if, suppose, his *hadd-e-'ali* was in it... in that *majma'*, then he... his *adna* (lower one) will go into his...

He will be connected with his.

Whose? I didn't understand.

His *hadd-e-'ali* has already ascended. But now he will go and join the one who is present in the world, who is *adna* to him?

No, if his *hadd-e-'ali* has gone into that *majma'*, then this one has also ascended with him.

No, his demise was late.

Whose?

The one who has to go, his *hadd-e-'ali* went before him, and the one who... his demise was late...

Then he won't be his *hadd-e-'ali*. He won't be. His *hadd-e-'ali* will be someone else. Someone else. If he passed away and joined the *majma'*...

If his effort was such that his *hadd-e-'ali* is there...

His *hadd-e-'ali* has been promoted. He has gone into the *majma'-e-'aliyam* (higher assembly). So this one... his *hadd-e-'ali* can be replaced. He can be replaced. Absolutely. His *hadd-e-'ali* will be like him, who... it changed. His *hadd-e-'ali* changed. Because there is no restriction on promotion. Promotion cannot be stopped... that due to the lower one, the higher one should be held back. This cannot happen.

Mawla, these... the demises of this time will increase, right? Because they have to join the *majma'* of the *Qa'im*...

Yes, during the *daur-e-kashf* (era of unveiling), it is said that in the *daur-e-kashf*, the *raj'at* (returns) will happen... they have to come every thousand years. But the people of the *daur-e-qa'im* (era of the Qa'im)... meaning of the final era, like us... their return will be first. Their return... due to the *daur-e-qa'im*, their return in the *daur-e-kashf* will be first. And then after that... it will happen from the back.

So that thousand-year... will remain?

No, the thousand-year rule won't apply. Their... their... their return will be first.

Mawlana... the believers who have a *hadd-e-'ali*... do they have only one *hadd-e-'ali*?

One *hadd-e-'ali*.

Will only one believer come to a *hadd-e-'ali*, or...

No, no. A *hadd-e-'ali* can have a congregation (*majma'*).

The believer will have a congregation.

Yes, a congregation.

Yes. For example, its example is that the Messenger of Allah said that when a believer prays *namaz* (prayer), two rows are praying behind him. That is, he has a congregation of his *hadd-e-'ali* which is *ghair-mar'i* (unseen), not visible. It is in this way.

And it has also been said that when a questioner asks a question, in reality, he is not asking the question, but he has become a medium, and the congregation he has, in reality, one of the *surat*s (forms) from among them, to fulfill its desire for knowledge, for its satisfaction, is asking the question on his tongue. Yes.

It is also possible that his *hadd-e-'ali* is asking.

Yes, this is also possible. This is also possible. But it also happens that someone from the congregation asks through his... and that congregation is called *hasanat* (good deeds). In the Holy Qur'an, what is called *hasanat*... *Innal-hasanati yudhhibnas-sayyi'at* (Indeed, good deeds do away with misdeeds). That good deeds eliminate evil deeds. So what are good deeds? In reality, in the higher realities, they are the *hasanat* that are within the circle of the believer... they are his *hudud-e-daniya* (lower ranks). A believer's *hudud-e-daniya* are his *hasanat*. And those *hudud-e-daniya* don't sit idle. They are also engaged in *tasbih* (glorification) and *taqdis* (sanctification)... they are also concerned about their own promotion. And the desire for knowledge, that never decreases. The chapter of action... the matter of action, the chapter of action is over. Because action is related to the body. But the desire for knowledge, the pursuit of knowledge, that remains. So, when the pursuit of knowledge remains, that process continues. And the *hasanat*, which are his good deeds... his... the *hadd-e-'ali* he has, and the *hudud-e-daniya* are those. So it is said that *innal-hasanati yudhhibnas-sayyi'at* (Indeed, good deeds do away with misdeeds)... that good deeds eliminate evil deeds. So what are the evil deeds? The evil deeds are... the doubts and misgivings (*shukuk wa shubhat*) in his *hadd-e-'ali*. The doubts and misgivings... in understanding the divine matters, the Holy Qur'an, the sayings of the Prophet, the sayings of the *awliya Allah*... that the Prophet said this... in one place Allah says *Rabb al-mashriqayn wa Rabb al-maghribayn* (Lord of the two Easts and the two Wests). In another place He says *Rabb al-mashariq wal-magharib* (Lord of the Easts and the Wests). What is this matter? So until he asks the *Ahl al-Bayt* (People of the House) or asks their *du'at* (missionaries)... or their *hudud*, he cannot attain this knowledge (*ma'rifat*). So these doubts and misgivings are created. So those... his... those *hasanat* complete this, and these good deeds... they eliminate his evil deeds, meaning the doubts and misgivings. But for a believer to have doubts and misgivings is also necessary. Because the plant of certainty (*yaqin*) always grows in the soil of doubt (*shakk*). The plant of certainty always grows in the soil of doubt. Just as Ibrahim `alayhis salam` (peace be upon him), when he... from Allah's command... when he asked his... made his request, "How do you bring the dead to life?"... bring the dead back to life... so he was told the method that you do this, that... four birds... yes. Take four birds and place them on four different places... and then... Allah's command... they will all come flying to you. So this... so... when you asked, Allah says, *'A-fa-lam tu'min?'* (Do you not believe?). "Did you not believe? O Ibrahim?" So what did you say? *'Li-yatma'inna qalbi.'* (So that my heart may be at rest). "I am asking for the satisfaction of my heart." So the satisfaction of the heart is a great thing. It is certainty (*yaqin*). The satisfaction of the heart... this was faith (*iman*). This is *iman*, and that is *yaqin*. And *yaqin* is greater than *iman*. *Al-yaqinu fawqa al-iman* (Certainty is above faith). In the same way, on the soil of doubt, always that... the plant of certainty grows. That... when we... for example, a person does *wudu* (ablution), enters the mosque. Then he has a thought that I... did I do the *wudu* properly or not? And in between, did my *wudu* break? So what is he doing? He is doubting himself. He is doubting. So through this, he achieves certainty that no, such a thing did not happen. Or while we are praying, a thought comes to us that have I prayed three *rak'at* or two *rak'at*? So he is doubting himself. Therefore, only after doubt does he attain certainty.

I was once in *hijr* (emigration) and I was in *i'tikaf* (seclusion in a mosque). So at night, Sayyidna `Abdullah ibn Muhammad, although he would not be in *i'tikaf*, he would stay up almost the whole night. So he would say that you people have made it your custom to sit in *i'tikaf* for a whole month, but the Messenger of Allah, in his life, only sat in *i'tikaf* for ten days. Meaning, he never sat for a whole month in *i'tikaf*. So he would say this jokingly. He did not forbid it, but he would say that look, ten days was the Prophet's habit, that in one month... the first decade of the blessed month he would sit, then the next year the second decade, the third year the third decade, in this way. So you should also do it this way. And the minimum *i'tikaf* is for three days and three nights. So I was once praying... I was next to you. So I... I was making up my missed prayers. So I... after praying some prayers, to continue the sequence, I prayed... so after praying two *rak'at*, in the third *rak'at* prayer, I sat down. So then... after finishing the prayer, he saw me that... he said, "You prayed three *rak'at*, Mawlana?" So it was in his mind that I should be praying four *rak'at*. "You prayed three *rak'at*, Mawlana?" So I said, "Yes, Mawlana, I made up the *Maghrib* prayer." He said, "Oh, okay, okay." So he observed with such detail. He would point out... he was a great jurist, a great scholar. He was a very affectionate person.

Mawla... that...

Mawla... the matter of your... of Janab Ibrahim... in it, there is one point. That you said that the birds were placed on four mountains. So they did not come flying, they came walking (*sa'yan*). So I don't understand what the wisdom is in that. The birds should have come flying, but it is said that they came walking. Yes, yes.

So the *hadd-e-'ali* who is appointed... he is appointed after the `ahd-e-sharif` (noble covenant)? Yes. Absolutely. That is... before that... no, it's not possible before that. Before the `ahd-e-sharif`. If it doesn't happen, then he cannot have a *hadd-e-'ali*. That is, our `ahd`... our `ahd-e-sharif` is the spiritual birth (*wiladat-e-batini*). One is the physical birth (*wiladat-e-zahiri*) when we come from the mother's womb, that is the physical birth. But the spiritual birth that is happening, it is through the `ahd-e-sharif`.

Then we can be a medium to become a *hadd* for others?

Recording in progress.

No, no, recording...

Recording is off. No, no, it's recording. The recording is on. Recording has been put on.

Yes.

I'm recording. Don't worry.

Yes. So, no, suppose one has taken the `ahd-e-sharif`, then he will become a medium to become a *hadd* for others? Yes. So it's not possible before that. No, the `ahd`... so before the `ahd-e-sharif`, he cannot have a *zimmah* (responsibility). Because he doesn't have that magnet to attract the iron. So that... that is not possible. Yes.

Fi zimn falak qamar (within the sphere of the moon). Fi zimn falak qamar fi nihayat al-hararah wal-yubs (within the sphere of the moon, with extreme heat and dryness). So in the sphere of the moon, which had extreme heat and dryness, and that was called the nature of fire, fa summiya al-asir (so it was named Ether), and it was called *asir*. Wa huwa awwal al-umahat al-arba'ah (And it is the first of the four mothers). And it is the first of the four mothers. Wa huwa markaz al-nar (And it is the center of fire). And it is the center of fire. Wa ma'danuha (and its mine). And its mine. Illa annaha fihi ghair mar'iyyatin `inda al-absar (Except that it is not visible to the eyes in it). That is, it is not visible to the eyes. Wa law zahara daw'uha la-mani` al-absar `an idrak `alam al-aflak (And if its light were to appear, it would prevent the eyes from perceiving the world of the celestial spheres). And if its light were to become manifest, then the eyes would not be able to perceive the world of the celestial spheres. Wa kana ihtijab daw'uha hikmat min al-bari al-mudabbir Ta'ala (And the concealment of its light was a wisdom from the Creator, the Planner, the Exalted). And the concealment of its light is a wisdom from the Creator, the Planner, the Exalted. Li-zalika (For that). Thumma qasura ma yalihi min al-asir (Then what followed it from the ether fell short)... in the same way, in the spheres below, these conditions were created... that... their space became extremely hot and moist, because they were very far from the movement of the sphere. So moderation was created in them, and it was called *hawa* (air). And that was the second of the mothers from the four mothers. Then after that... it became cold and moist, and a flowing nature was created in it. It was the center of *ma'* (water). And it was the third of the mothers. Then after that came the earth... in extreme cold and dryness, in intense cold and dryness. So it became the fourth of the mothers. Thumma arad al-mudabbir an yaj'al al-ard maqarren li-zuhur ma yuzhiruhu min al-mawalid allati hiya al-ma'dan wal-nabat wal-hayawan wal-insan (Then the Planner willed to make the earth a place for the appearance of what He would manifest from the creations, which are the mineral, the vegetable, the animal, and the human). And then the Planner willed to make the earth a place... a station... for the appearance of what He would manifest from the creations. What are the creations? They are called the three creations (*mawalid-e-thalatha*): mineral, vegetable, and animal. And in animal, human is also included, the rational animal (*hayawan-e-natiq*). So they are three creations: mineral, vegetable, animal, and human. That is, that He may manifest them, create them. Wa kanat ma yaliha min al-jaww fi nihayat al-afrat (And what was below it of the atmosphere was in extreme excess). And the condition that was created in the atmosphere, it was in extreme cold and dryness. So the sphere moved it, fa-ramat al-kawakib bi-ash'i'atiha (so the celestial bodies threw with their rays). So the sphere was given motion by him, fa-ramat al-kawakib bi-ash'i'atiha (so the celestial bodies threw with their rays). Due to which, that movement, the *kawakib* threw their rays towards the earth. Wa qad sarat sakhrah saldah li-shiddati bardaha (And it had become a hard, solid rock due to the intensity of its coldness). And that became a rock, solid, due to the intensity of its coldness and its dryness. Fa-lam tajid al-ash'i`ah fiha (And the rays did not find a place in it). And the rays which... could not enter it. Into that rock. Into that condition. Fiha. Wa-nfarat `an salabatiha (And they recoiled from its hardness). Because they could not find a place to enter it. Fa-raj`at mun`akisah (So they returned, reflected). So they returned, reflected. Fa-sakhanat wajh al-ard (And they heated the face of the earth). And from their... due to the coming of the rays, the surface of the earth was heated. Wa ma yaliha min al-hawa (And the air below it). And the air... wa sayyarathu mu`tadilah (And made it moderate). And in it... due to the rays... the coldness which was there, extreme coldness, and dryness... moderation was created in it, and heat and moisture came, and dryness... then after that, it was called *nasim* (breeze). Wa huwa... markaz al-ma' (And it is the center of water). And in fact, it is the center of water, alladhin `anhu yu'tazilu kama sa-nadhkuruhu fi-ma ba'd (from which it is balanced, as we will mention later). We will mention it later. Wa kan in`ikas al-ash'i`ah (And the reflection of the rays was...). And the rays which reflected, they went to the limit where the air was heated. Fa-bakhura dhalika al-hawa alladhi lam tablughhu ash'i`at al-kawakib raj`atan (So that air from which the rays of the celestial bodies did not reach in their return was vaporized). So those rays, they did return, but they reached the first distinction, fa-baqiyat baridan yabisan (so it remained cold and dry). So it became... wa huwa a'la markaz al-asir wa yusamma kurrat al-zamharir (and it is the highest center of the ether, and it is called the sphere of intense cold). So it remained frozen right there, and that is the highest part of the sphere of *nasim*, and it is also called *zamharir* (intense cold). Now all these conditions have been created.

And...

Mawla, the opposites (*azdad*) of the celestial bodies were formed due to their rays, right? But the sun's *sadaf* (shell/opposite) was not formed.

No, the sun's was also formed.

No, because its rays were not interrupted, it was mentioned that its rays which...

...so *sadaf* was formed, its light was reduced.

No, no. In the first instance, when the rays of the *kawakib* came to the earth, on the rock, and they rose from the rock...

The *sadaf* was formed, so its light was reduced.

No, the light of the *kawkab* was reduced... no, the light was not reduced. It was that they were *ghair-mar'i* (unseen) before, they were not visible. Now, because of the *azdad*, they became visible. So the *kawakib* in their essence are very noble, because they are made from the highest parts of the *nadimin*. But the *kawakib*... because the *azdad* of the *kawakib* that were formed, they were formed from the mixture of the rock. Therefore, in the *azdad*, that quality of inauspiciousness was created, due to the mixture of the rock. So, which is why... it is said that this planet has this effect, the effects that are on children, that this planet has this effect, that effect. So it happens because of this. But the sun... the sun's *kawakib*... the sun's *azdad* were also formed in the same way. All... all... all of them... the *azdad* of all of them were formed in this way.

Let me just read this a little. The notes of Mawla `Abbas साहब`...

Mawla, I have a question.

Yes, yes.

Here, *ma huwa al-ma'* (what is water) or *markaz al-ma'* (center of water)... *markaz al-nar* (center of fire)... meaning...

It is not water, it is the *markaz al-ma'*.

Yes.

*Markaz al-ma'*.

Yes. Meaning, it's not water, it's its center.

It's the center. It's not water. Meaning, if we go up there, water is not present there. It's the *markaz al-ma'*. Yes.

Alright, I will read it out to you. It says here that when these densities were falling, from them the four pillars of the sphere, the four directions, were also established: *tali`*, *gharib*, *`ashir*, *rabi`*, that is, the directions of east, west, north, and south. And the two poles... we have read this. So...

Okay. *Budud-e-bashari* (human beginnings). In the first thousand years, under the governance of Saturn (*Zuhal*), there was much fog, vapors, and continuous, heavy rain, and the entire earth became a watery ocean. In this... in these... in these things, the essences (*khama'ir*) were prepared, which were similar to the nature of Saturn, for example, iron, copper, Abyssinians, farmers, and people engaged in menial labor, etc. In the second thousand years, when Jupiter (*Mushtari*) became Saturn's helper, its heat dried up most of the moisture, and every praiseworthy, good thing was separated from the blameworthy, bad thing. And the essences of such things and people were prepared that were in harmony with the noble nature of Jupiter. Although Saturn's governance was ongoing, Jupiter's partnership... due to its noble nature, the essences of religious, pious people were prepared. The first vegetation grew in this era, and crawling creatures, ants, insects, chameleons, were created in this very era. And in the third thousand years, Mars (*Mirrikh*) became Saturn's helper. These are both inauspicious planets. They dried up most of the moisture with their heat. Many frozen mountains crumbled, and they became plains. And... essences were prepared for commanders, soldiers, brave people, and minerals that are in accord with their nature. In the fourth thousand years, the Sun (*Shams*) became its helper. So darkness and moisture were removed from the face of the earth, and rain became moderate. And the essences for kings and great personalities were prepared. And things of its nature, for example, ruby, gold, and animals of its nature, and there was some moderation in the atmosphere. Then in the fifth thousand years, Venus (*Zuhra*) became Saturn's helper, in which the essences for Arabs, women, sportspeople, and singers were prepared. Sweet water springs appeared, and rain became moderate and... in their governance... fruits and fragrant flowers grew. And birds appeared. And moderate animals that are beneficial were created. This was the preliminary stage (*tashkhayma*) for the creation of man.

Then in the sixth thousand years, Mercury (*`Atarid*) became Saturn's helper. So rain and... natures became moderate, and the essences for scribes, ministers, and accountants were prepared. And moderate vegetation also grew well, and halal animals... at the end of its governance... at the beginning of the Moon's (*Qamar*) governance, the human person appeared from the earth. So our creation, the creation of humans, happened in the era of the Moon. Meaning, it is the era of Saturn... the governance is Saturn's, but the Moon's... the partial governance of the Moon and its partnership with Saturn, in that, the creation of humans happened. About which the Exalted Lord said in this way: `Wa-llahu anbatakum min al-ardi nabata` (And Allah has caused you to grow from the earth as a growth). And the creation that happened, it was from the earth. Because creations have been of two kinds. One creation is that which happened from the earth, and the second creation is that which is from our parents. In the seventh thousand years, the Moon was Saturn's helper. At the beginning of the era, the Planner of the world gave motion, so the essences of the highest grade from minerals, vegetables, and animals, in the form of clouds, rained down on the earth in some specific pits. They were not very deep, and the water that collected in these pits, vapors rose from them, which were higher and more noble than the first vapors. They were opposite the sperm of the male. In these pits... and those pits were in the shape of a woman's womb. Meaning, the ones that were in the father. This... we will read this, Insha'Allah. The Arabic... when we read it, its explanation will be better.

Mawla, the matter of the moon and sun... our month, the... Hijri month... it is based on the moon. Yes. And the moon is influenced by the sun. So the question is that seven planets are revolving around the earth. The moon is revolving around the sun. Therefore, our months which are made... are based on this. Yes, absolutely. So this becomes a separate issue, that the moon... is not revolving around the earth. It revolves around the earth with the sun. Yes, yes.

And the two... the two births... one birth is the birth from the pits, which is higher. And the birth which is from the mother's womb is lower. And this is what is called, that *innal-ba'tha haqqun* (Indeed, the resurrection is true). The resurrection is true. So the resurrection is of two kinds. Our... the believer's resurrection and the resurrection of the *nuqata'* (speakers) and *awliya Allah* (friends of God), there is a difference. The believer's resurrection is that after death in a state of faith, we will be raised in a state of faith. This is our resurrection. But the Prophet's resurrection is what... as it has come in the Holy Qur'an, that... or what we read in the... `Allahumma aqimhu... wa ab'athhu maqaman mahmudan alladhi wa'adtahu` (O Allah, establish him... and resurrect him to a praised station, which You have promised him). "The station about which you, O Allah, had promised the Prophet, resurrect him in that praised station." So what is the praised station? The Prophet's praised station is that he will appear from *Quds* (the holy realm). This is your praised station. In which there is a line. The one in *Quds*... the first to enter *Quds* was Salman al-Farsi. After that, you are. Yes.

Mawla, the *qutb* is born in every cycle. At the beginning of the cycle. At the beginning of the cycle. No, at the beginning. In a cycle... one *qutb*... at the beginning of the cycle. The *qutb* of this cycle was born in Sarandib. And the very first cycle, the *qutb* of the era of the *du'at*, was born in the Kaaba, and he is the one where Ali `alayhis salam` was born. In the place of...

Mawla, this... the Holy Qur'an says that *hadid* (iron) came from the sky. So what is the matter for *hadid*?

Yes, Surah Hadid is also there. `wa anzalna al-hadid fihi ba'sun shadid` (and We sent down iron, wherein is great might). And on Tuesday, reciting this verse has great virtue. On Tuesday.

Mawla, but...

Yes, that is true. That *hadid* came from the sky. We have sent down *hadid*. From the sky.

In that node... in that node too, there is a head and a tail (*ra's wa dhanab*). It has a tail and it has a head. So the tail is more severe than the head. And of the Imams... and their *hujjat*s... and the *awsiya*... their opposites... their evil concepts went to the tail. Yes. It was in this way.

About which the Exalted Lord said in this way: `Wa-llahu anbatakum min al-ardi nabata` (And Allah has caused you to grow from the earth as a growth). Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). Al-Fatiha. Hadiya-tun wasilah ila Sayyidina bi-Sayyid al-Anbiya wal-Mursalin. Ila ruhi Sayyidina Himmatullah Musa bin Shirazi, Sayyid al-Walid, qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara zarihahu wa `ada `alayna min barakatihi bi-haqqi Sayyidina Muhammad wa aalihi ajma`in wa sallim. Wa... taqabbal Allahu minna wa minkum bi-fadlih bi-ahsan ma yakunu bi-haqqi Sayyidina Muhammad wa aalihi ajma`in. Al-Fatiha. Bismillah... Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim... Sirat...

`Ala hadrat sayyidina wa mawlana al-Imam al-Tayyib Abi al-Qasim Amir al-Mu'minin, salawat Allahi `alayhi, wa `ala abaa'ihi al-tahirin, wa `ala abnaa'ihi al-akramin. Wa `ala hadrat sayyidina al-da`i al-mutlaq al-mujalla al-afdal al-mufaddal, sayyidina al-Hayy al-Muqaddas, sayyidina `Ali bin al-Sharif, wa walidihi al-Qutb... al-`Alam al-`Alim, al-Hajj al-Sharif... `ala hadrat sayyidina al-Qutb... `ala hadrat sayyidina al-Da`i al-`ajall al-mufaddal al-mukarram... `ala hadrat sayyidina wa mawlana al-Walid al-Hajj al-Sharif, `Usama Din. Mawlana Ghulam Husayn, Sayyid al-Walid al-Muqaddas. Walid al-Rasul, `abd al-Haqq, `abd Allah... wa Sayyid al-Walid al-Muqaddas... wa tawwala Allahu `umrahu wa baraka fi `umrihi. `Ata Allahu baqa'ahu... wa `ada `alayna... wa... wa tawwala Allahu baqa'ahu. `Ata Allahu Ta`ala yahfazuhu wa yahmihi wa yu`tihi wa yukhfihi wa yashfihi min sharr jami` al-amrad wal-asqam wa sharr jami` al-mawt... wa jami` al-shurur wal-a`da' wal-aafat... wa sharr al-nuhusat wal-khusufat wal-tathlithat wal-tasdisat wal-tarbi`at al-nazirah... wa... wa `afa `anhum wa ghafara lahum... ya`fu `anhum wa yaghfiru lahum... wa yu'ayyiduhu... wa yu`izzuhu wa yansuruhu wa yahmihi... wa yahfizh fi al-himayat al-rabbaniyyah al-shahaniyyah. Wa atala Allahu fi hayatihi... wa rafa` maqamahu wa darajatihi, wa yashrukna iyyakum fi salih da`awatih... bi-haqqi jaddihi Sayyidina Muhammad wa aalihi wa itratihi al-tahirin, salawat Allahi `alayhim ajma`in. Bi-ismika Allahumma. Wa bi-du`aika... wa fi `afiyatik... Al-Fatiha. Bismillah...
