[Mumbling] [Unclear] a bit. Absolutely not. As-salamu alaykum. Good. Good. As-salamu alaykum. Wa alaykum as-salam. Rashid Saheb, what page are we on? As-salamu alaykum. Wa alaykum as-salam. As-salamu alaykum. Wa alaykum as-salam. From page 18 of the book... As-salamu alaykum, Mawlana. A'udhu billahi minash-shaytanir-rajim (I seek refuge in God from the accursed Satan). Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim (In the name of God, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). Allahumma salli 'ala Muhammadin wa 'ala ali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim (O God, bless Muhammad and the family of Muhammad, and grant peace). Fasl (Chapter). *Thumma inna al-mudabbir ta'ala sarrafa tadbir al-'alam ila Zuhal* (Then indeed the Administrator, exalted is He, delegated the administration of the world to Saturn). Then this, that the administrator of the world... the administrator has... the Administrator, exalted is He, has delegated the administration of the world towards Zuhal (Saturn). Meaning, who is the administrator of the world? *'Ashir al-Mudabbir* (The Tenth Administrator). *'Ashir al-Mudabbir* delegated the administration of the world towards Zuhal. Meaning, towards Zuhal... the administration, whatever it may be, will be of Zuhal. *Bi-maddatihi* (with its substance/matter), from its own substance, from its own affirmations (*ta'yidat*). *Subhanahu... Subhanahu...* Allah, the great one, it is His essence. *Wa tahrikuhu lil-falak* (And its movement for the celestial sphere), and its movement is for the celestial sphere, *fa-yudabbiru al-'alama alfa sana* (so it administers the world for a thousand years), so that it... that world... will administer it for a thousand years. Meaning, who? Zuhal. *Fa-imtazajat al-taba'i' al-madhkura ba'duha bi-ba'd* (Then the aforementioned natures intermingled, some with others). So, the natures that have been mentioned before this, that the various natures were... they became intermingled with each other. *Fa-hadathat al-bukharat wa al-dhababat wa al-amtar* (Then occurred the vapors, the thick clouds, and the rains). Therefore, from that... from their intermingling with each other... of the natures... vapors were produced, and the dense clouds that exist, inside which water collects, so the dark clouds... they were produced. *Wa al-amtar*, and rains occurred, *mutawatira*, continuous rains kept falling. *Ghayra nafi'ah* (not beneficial). It was a rain that was not beneficial. Some... some rains are such that they give benefit. And the rains that fall are not beneficial. In several places, the Holy Quran has mentioned that it is called *matar al-su'* (the evil rain). Meaning, an evil rain. Yes, *matarun su'*... *matar al-su'*. Meaning, they are rains in which impurities (*khaba'ith*) descend, which... from the various... corrupted temperaments, from them, impurities descend, so that is *matar al-su'*. And the rains that are good, in them the... the luminous essences (*fudulat-e-nayyirah*) that were... the purified ones... in the celestial sphere of the zodiacs (*falak-e-buruj*), that have been purified, they descend onto the earth through the rain. They ascend through the rain and descend through the rain... They ascend through vapors and descend through the rain. Who are they? They are the ones who... the essences from our graves... the body that is buried in the grave, its purification occurs in the zodiacs. And the zodiac is divided into 12 zodiacs. Each zodiac has 30 degrees, 30 parts. So, from here, first, the journey of our essence starts from the first degree of the first zodiac, until it reaches the last degree of the last zodiac, so 300... 20 years... 360 years... 360 years pass. Then, until then, then it turns back from there and comes to the first degree again, so 360 years. In this way, its journey is of 720 years. This is what Sayyidna Mu'ayyad says, in the *Tahajjud* prayer, the *du'a* you have for *Tahajjud*, it is of Sayyidna Mu'ayyad. It is of Sayyidna Mu'ayyad al-Shirazi. The book of which we are reading. So, *la tuksiranna ya Rabbi fi hadha al-safarana* (O my Lord, do not prolong our journey in this). O... O Lord, in this world, do not prolong our journey. So, that is... all those... it points towards all those administrations, as to how the matters are. We... meaning, as soon as possible, our matter should be concluded from this world, and we, from here... from the physical world to the psychic world... look, there are many worlds. From the physical world... our physical world that is, from it we have to go to the psychic world. Our spiritual progress is in the psychic world. Meaning, we have to become a *nafs* (soul). Our body has to become a *nafs*. Meaning, *namiyah* (vegetative), *hissiyah* (sensory), *natiqah* (rational). And it has to reach the station of the *surah* (form). That *surah*... that *surah* has to become... what does it have to become? It has to reach the station of the *'aql* (intellect). And when will it reach the station of the intellect? *Yawman ma* (on some day). That journey is very long. *Yawman ma* it will come. But it... when it enters the *haykal* (form/temple) of the Imam, then it is like a fixed deposit. Now, wherever the Imam makes progress, our *surat-e-nayyirah* (luminous form) will make progress with him. So, this is a great station, that the Imam... um... uh... his... he... his *haykal*... *surah*... does not travel alone, but rather travels with his *haykal*, with his forms. Whatever their spiritual journey may be. Mawlana, the way you mentioned the rain (*barsat*), the clouds are very high up... Their level is very high. In the world... the celestial bodies that are there, it's much higher than them. Yes, yes. So then this... uh... the descent (*nuzul*) that happens after our purification, in what form does that happen? It... It wouldn't happen in the form of vapors (*bukharat*)... because the vapors are very low, the clouds are very low... from the surface of the earth. So this... this... مزاج... it is said, *mizaj-e-muntazij*. Ascending and descending, that is called *mizaj-e-muntazij*. So *mizaj-e-muntazij* happens in this way. Ascending is through vapors and this... descending is through the rainwater. So the vapors, after purification, they come in the form of vapor. They would come in the form of vapor, and through the clouds, through the rain, they descend. Yes. And secondly, this... of the *mu'min* and of the *da'i*... their turn... the *da'i*'s turn... happens quickly. Isn't that so? Yes. So that *da'i*'s turn... we... in the era in which we came, our *da'i* today is... The turn... look, the turn... for the turn... for... up to... even for the unbelievers, for the infidels too, this is set, that whether he is a believer or an unbeliever or a hypocrite, for everyone this matter is set, that they will return after 1000 years. Their affirmations (*ta'yidat*) etc., their purification... The purification happens. After purification, they become... the station of affirmations, they benefit others. [Unclear]. Their life span is a thousand years. A thousand years. And those... they were angels in potentiality... in life... they become angels in actuality. Meaning, they now have this... these powers come to them by the command of Allah that they can make great spiritual decisions. Page 18... 18... yes. So... And look, these... these *kuffar* (unbelievers) that are there, their... these times of Ganpati, or such... their... Thursday... uh... gatherings that happen or the worship matters... at that time rains descend. Those rains are *matar al-su'*. They are evil rains. The Holy Quran has mentioned it in three places. And... these rains... there are times for them when... Sayyidna 'Ali says that out of five times, one time is when prayers are accepted. That is *'inda nuzul al-ghayth*, meaning at the time of rainfall. Because that is the time of rain when our essences are descending and ascending. So our prayers are accepted. Because of those essences... because in a purified state... the state in which they went was very raw. But when they returned after being purified, they came back extremely pure and clean and transparent. So... therefore, through their... blessings, all these prayers of ours are accepted. The Mawla-e-Ka'inat... in one place... one time, a Jew... a person had come to you... and he said that Moses was like this, Jesus was like this... all... he mentioned all the people, the prophets, that they had this power. So you... all the companions were sitting, everyone was silent. But Amir al-Mu'minin, peace be upon him, answered him. And he said that you... you say that Moses was like this, our Prophet was like this. You have... in his praise, the... what you have mentioned... it is a sermon of the assembly, it is... in a magnificent way you have mentioned it... that, well... so in it, one mention is this, that... uh... people came to you, to the Messenger of God. You were in Madinah al-Munawwarah... the Master of the Universe... and they said, "O Messenger of God... uh... the rains have completely stopped, and our fields have dried up, and we are very troubled. Please pray." So you prayed. And from the acceptance of the prayer, this happened, that continuous rain began to fall. It rained to such an extent... on one Friday you had prayed. So from that Friday until the next Friday, rains kept falling. So much so that so much rain fell that the same people... came back on Friday. And they said, "O Messenger of God, our fields have filled with water, and we don't have a place to walk. So much water has collected. Please pray." So you said that *khuliqa al-insanu min `ajal* (Man was created of haste). You recited the verse of the Holy Quran. That... that man was created from haste. His nature has haste. So you then prayed, and... the rains stopped. A similar incident occurred in the time of Sayyidna Ghulam Husain Saheb Muqaddas. So... actually what had happened was that... Sayyidna... Ali bin Qutbuddin, who was a great elder of ours, and is buried in Baroda under a tree. Uh... and I remember that Sayyidna Husain bin Hasan, may God sanctify his soul, how many times he asked, "Where is 'Ali bin Qutbuddin?" So I said that, "Mawlana, he is in India, he is buried in Baroda." So he asked, "Is there anyone among his descendants?" I said, "No." "Do you know he wrote a book named *Bahrat al-'Adillah*?" So I said, "I haven't read it, but I have heard that he wrote it." So he said, "You should read that book, it is 'halwa' (a sweet dish)," he said. "It is halwa, it is so... so... it is so enjoyable to read." So what is in it? It is the first book... in the time of Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah, you had migrated, and the conditions of that time, you wrote in the form of *haqa'iq* (esoteric realities). Which is the first book... I mean, *haqa'iq*... a biography is a biography, but you wrote the biography in *haqa'iq*. And it is a book of great stature. So... in it... he writes of an incident that... we were gathered with Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hiba, and it was the rainy season. And... we were sitting and reading, probably, or there was some gathering, an academic gathering. So you made a mention that, "Look, in the time of the Messenger of God, it happened like this, that these signs of rain appeared. The companions were with you. So... you... the companions said, 'O Messenger of God, what will happen if the rains are continuous?'" So you... uh... you said, "*hawalayna wa la 'alayna*" (around us and not on us). You gestured like this, you gestured with your finger. Looking towards the sky. And after that, what happened was that... the rains were falling in the surroundings, and where you were, there was no rain there. So, Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah Saheb mentioned this in his gathering. So... so the people saw that rain was falling in the surroundings, and that place of yours is safe. So this... Sayyidna Ghulam Husain Saheb Muqaddas, when... uhm... where had they gone for a trip? Gandipet. Gandipet. They had gone to Gandipet for a trip, Osman Sagar... so... there, a... all the... *Hudud* and *da'wat* members... the community of believers was there. So they were sitting and discussing academic matters. So the signs of rain began to appear. So... you did the same thing, that... you said that, "Look, Sayyidna 'Ali bin Qutbuddin, when he had migrated in the time of Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah, such an incident had happened, and you had said so." So you also gestured and showed it. And the people saw that... this... rain is falling in the surroundings, and that... you... where you are sitting and the people are sitting, not a single drop of rain fell there. So... this incident... I... once... on the occasion of the migration, when I came to Jeddah, I happened to narrate this incident to... Mulla Khurshid Hasan Saheb Muqaddas. So... Ahmed Khurshid Saheb. So... so he said that, "Ghulam Husain bhai, I was present at that time. I was present in that gathering. That in that gathering, I was present. That I... I was present, and I bear witness that this incident is true." At that time, I was present in that gathering, he said. So, these are the very things... like you were saying, that... the progress of the *du'at*... I mean... their essences progress in 30 years, but they remain... their ancient effects, and the ancient effects become material and affirmation for others. And their own form that is, it always... keeps its gaze towards its remaining essence. That... that... just as Sayyidna Mu'ayyad's account also is, that when you had... the four of your students who were there... Lamak bin Malik... Malik bin Lamak... and Raslan and Yahya bin... Lamak... something like that, four of your students who became great *du'at*. So... they were with you at the end, they were present when you passed away. It was your final state. So... all these students were present with you. So tears were flowing from your eyes. So... they said, "Mawlana, who knows death better and more excellently than you? But there are tears in your eyes?" "Tears in your eyes?" So... he said that, "Look, I... on my... death... am not crying because of its... condition, but rather I am... saying that... *mata yalhaku al-baytu... mata yalhaku al-baytu...* when will the house join... what... when will the house be joined... what... my house... when will... when will it be joined... uhm... uhm... what is that sentence... uhm... a specific sentence, and we... when we were reading with Sayyidna Husain bin Isma'il, this sentence came up. So he said, "What is the meaning of this?" So I said that this is its meaning, and this is Sayyidna Mu'ayyad's sentence. So he was very happy. So that sentence, I don't recall it at the moment... uhm... So it is that my... yes, *mata yalhaku al-baytu ila rabbihi* (When will the house join its Lord?). *Mata yalhaku al-baytu ila rabbihi*. That when my... my house... will be joined with its Lord? So who is the Lord? That is the noble form. It is the noble form. And the house, in which house my form used to reside, that was my body. So... my... my... I have progressed by becoming a form. But my... my body, which served me, with which I performed prayers, performed Hajj, and all... performed good deeds, that poor thing is lying in the grave. So that... my house... when will it be joined with its Lord? This is why my tears are flowing. When I thought of this matter. A story about the rains was also from Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman Saheb. Yes, he was there too. Hasan Khan sent Abdul Malik. Yes, yes, he sent him. And you had said this too, that... if Hasan Khan went alone, it would rain. You had great affection for Sayyidna Hasan Khan. You would always say, "He is my deputy." "He is my deputy." You would always say "deputy"... you would use the word. And you see how strange things happen, that Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman performed the *nass* (appointment of successor) only on Sayyidna Ibrahim. He performed *nass* on only one *da'i*. His own brother was also there... and Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fayed was also there. And then Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad bin Fayed performed *nass* on his son, who was Isma'il. But Isma'il passed away. Therefore, you performed *nass* on your grandson, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il. So Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il then performed *nass* on you, on Sayyidna Hasan Khan. But Sayyidna Hasan Khan passed away during the time of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il. Therefore, you became *ghayr munfarid* (not solitary). You had a long life, Sayyidna Hasan. And an incident about you is also like this, about Sayyidna Hasan... Hasan Khan... that... you were 12 years old, and... uh... Sayyidna 'Ali Saheb... wrote to Sayyidna Ja'far in a letter... that... "Mawlana, a boy has come to us whose age is 12. And he is a Dawoodi boy. And he wants us... to come into our faith, into the Sulaymani path. So we took his... severe test. Just as we have... you gave the example that just as... raw gold is put into the fire. So that it becomes... pure gold. We took such a severe test of his." So you gave its reply. Sayyidna Ja'far, from there... you were in Taybah, Sayyidna Ja'far. So you said that, "We... *al-mu'minu yanzuru bi-nurillah* (The believer sees with the light of God). A believer sees with the light of God, and the luminous power that Allah Ta'ala has given us, with that we are seeing that there is a great station in this boy. *Yakunu lahu sha'nun min sha'n* (He will have a station of great importance). His station will be great. And... he will serve the *da'wah* greatly." And this and that... you praised him greatly. And this boy is 12 years old. And then after that you... came into the *da'wah* and then... you... great... you wrote 70 books... in *haqa'iq*. Your descendants... [Unclear] [Unclear] [Unclear] those people are there. Mulla Khazam Saheb. And Sayyidna Ibrahim Ali Saheb's children are these, right? Hasan 'Ali and others are there, right, Sayyidna Ali Saheb's? *Wa sarat al-ardu bahran muwwajan* (And the earth became a surging sea). And the earth became a sea with tossing waves. *Wa takhammara fi dhalika al-alf ma yushakiluhu tab'u Zuhal min al-hadid wa al-hijarah wa al-nuhas* (And in that millennium fermented that which resembles the nature of Saturn, of iron, stone, and copper). And in that one thousand years, which is the complete administration of Zuhal, in the complete administration, the first one thousand years, in it, ferments were formed, just as the nature of Zuhal was, according to its nature, iron... iron was made. And stone... stones were formed. And copper... copper was made. In copper... the ferments of copper were formed... meaning, leavens were created. *Wa sarat al-ardu maghmurah bil-miyah* (And the earth became submerged in water). And the whole earth was filled with water. *Wa mayyaza kullu jinsin min al-ashya'i ila jinsihi* (And every species of things was distinguished into its species). The distinction between good and bad was made. *Wa khammarat khama'iru al-Sudan wa al-zarra'ah wa ahl al-shaqa wa al-naqd* (And the leavens of the Sudanese, the farmers, the wretched, and the severe fermented). And the leavens that were formed were of the Sudanese... who... who do farming, those people, and the wretched ones, meaning the unfortunate ones, and who are of a harsh nature... tyrannical people, theirs. And... then indeed Jupiter accompanied Zuhal in the administration for a thousand years. And after that, the one that... the celestial sphere that is, the celestial sphere of Jupiter is... between Zuhal and Mars, Jupiter comes. So Jupiter... assisted... became its companion, of Zuhal, in the administration. For one year... for one thousand years. In the second thousand years... I'm talking about the Sudanese people, why was it... from the beginning it was... [unclear] I don't know, it was like that. Their ferments were formed within Zuhal. And the Jupiter that is, it is... *Sa'd-e-Akbar* (the greater good fortune). Meaning, *sa'dayn* is said for... Jupiter and Venus. So two... two planets are very good. One is Jupiter and the second is Venus. Yes. And... uh... Jupiter that is... so it is said that... *al-Mushtari bayna al-nahsayn* (Jupiter is between the two inauspicious ones). Meaning, the grave of the Messenger of God is between two people. Abu Bakr and 'Umar. Yes. Mawlana, the graves they show in the map, that is at your feet, isn't it? It is... yes... it's not at the feet... We cannot pray with them, right? It's not at the feet, but it is set apart. Set apart. Yes. And of both... one is behind the other. Abu Bakr, and behind him is 'Umar. Yes. That is what they say. That is what is made above. What is below, who knows... The original is below. Below, in the cellar. Above, it is just... of the Messenger of God. They show that. And from above, one is not allowed to see it. Yes, yes. They don't let you see from inside. They don't even let you go near the lattice. They don't let you go near the lattice. There are sentries there. The spot of the *ras* (head), that is the... the *ras* of the *kiari* (garden) is the highest because the Prophet's chamber is right there. Your noble chamber is adjacent to it. In the heavenly *kiari*. The spot of the *ras* is the highest spot. Yes. Because... the heart of Madinah al-Munawwarah is the Prophet's Mosque. And the heart of the Prophet's Mosque is that spot of the *ras*. The *mihrab* of the Prophet is also not... no, the *mihrab* of the Prophet is also not... it also does not come in that. It does not come in the *kiari*. It does not come in the *kiari*. One *mihrab* comes in the *kiari*. One *mihrab*... yes, one *mihrab* comes, that's right. One *mihrab* comes. Where... the pillar... the one that is called the spot of the Prophet. So the 14 *rak'at* prayer that we pray, in that the *mihrab* comes. Two *rak'at* we pray at the *ras*. Then two *rak'at*... at the spot of repentance (*maqam al-Tawbah*), at the pillar of repentance. And then two *rak'at* are at the pillar of *Hannanah*. Six have been done. And then... after that... at two *rak'at* at the spot of... at the pillar of 'Ali we pray. Yes, on which the pillar of 'A'isha is. It says 'A'isha. Then, eight *rak'at* have been done. After eight *rak'at*, that is... at the spot of the Prophet. These are 10 *rak'at*. And then... in the *kiari* somewhere... anywhere, two *rak'at*. These are 12. And then at the spot of Gabriel. So these are 14. But in the 14, the eight are more meritorious. Which are prayed at the pillars. And they indicate the bearers of the Throne. Yes. That *ras*... at the *ras*... at the spot of... the pillar of repentance, at the pillar of 'Ali, and... at the pillar of *Hannanah*. These four... at these four spots where two *rak'at* each are prayed, they are more meritorious than the remaining... *rak'at*. Yes. And then the Messenger of God says that in my mosque there is such a spot that if people knew its virtue, they would draw lots to go there. Yes. And which spot is that? That is the spot of repentance. The Messenger's grave which is below... Imam Husayn etc's graves... the surface of the earth was above at that time... below... how was it then? Was the ground lower? No, the ground was not lower... I think that... by digging... it was probably made lower. Perhaps. It was dug that much? Look at Imam Husayn's, how far below it is. We went there, didn't we? We had to go down. Yes, we had to go down. And we also went down at Abbas's. I didn't understand the matter then. Yes, yes. That it's below. Meaning, it's almost this much... Yes. When the second time... the second year came, so Jupiter when... became a companion of Zuhal... in the administration... for a thousand years, so its heat... dried up most of the moistures. So due to its heat, most of the moistures... dried up. He's re-reading it, it seems. [Unclear] *Wa mayyaza ma yushakilu...* all the natures. [Unclear] Then, in the third year, in the third thousand years, then Mars... became the companion of Zuhal... in the administration. For one year... for one thousand years. Third... third thousand years. *Wa huma al-nahsan* (And they are the two inauspicious ones). So both are... inauspicious planets. Zuhal and Mars. So because of Mars and because of this administration of Zuhal, its heat and its dryness, most of the moistures... dried up. *Wa fattatat al-jibalu al-mun'aqidah* (And the solid mountains were crumbled). And... so much so that... the big mountains... broke... and... in the millennium of Zuhal... they were turned... into sand. And the small animals that are malicious... appeared at that time. Like rats... and... cats. And things like that. And from the minerals... and from the beasts and the animals and the venomous creatures, and the leavens of the powerful, the elites, and the brave people were formed. And the... good people and the brave people, their... leavens were formed. The beginning of creation... in the mention of the creation of crawling creatures, rats and cats are mentioned. Yes. So, this dinosaur etc. that science talks about... So there is no mention of that with us... no. No. It must be fiction. Because they speak of it as a fact. Then after that, the Sun accompanied... Zuhal, in the administration... for one thousand years, in the fourth. In the fourth thousand years, the Sun was with it... with Zuhal. So it removed that darkness. Whatever darkness had fallen, it was dispelled. The clouds... and whatever clouds had gathered... on the earth, all of them were removed. And the rains... fell with moderation. And leavens were formed in it... which are... of kings and of great people... leavens were formed. From the noble things, like the ruby and gold... so much so that in minerals, ruby and gold were also... appeared in this. And the animals which are compatible with that, the good... noble animals were created. And in the atmosphere, moderation was created. Then Zuhra (Venus) accompanied... Zuhal. Then Zuhra was with it. For one thousand years... yes. Zuhra is... then with Zuhal, for one thousand years, it came. Its... became a companion by being with it. Fifth... in the fifth year. *Wa khammarat...* and in it, the... who write... who write books, they... and the vizier, and who do accounting, were created. In the fifth... the leavens of the Arabs were formed, and of women, and of people of leisure, and... those who sing and play instruments, those people, and of the... sweet water springs were formed. So rains fell moderately. And in its administration, trees... trees that have fruits, and... good... fragrant... fruits... appeared. And birds appeared in the air. And they... spread. And animals of moderate nature and beneficial ones were created. All this is a prelude to the creation of the human person. And all these things are becoming a prelude... that for the coming of the human person, a prelude is being prepared for 6000 years. Then indeed 'Utarid (Mercury) accompanied... Zuhal. Then 'Utarid... is... the companion of Zuhal... in the administration, for one thousand years... in the seventh... in the sixth year. So... the rains... increased... and with moderation. And the natures... with refinement. And... the plants increased in the land... in its one thousand years. The leavens of... the Arabs were formed, and of women, and of people of leisure... yes, that's it. And from the noble things, like the ruby and gold... here it is written... in it, the... of kings and of great people... the leavens were... were spread... I mean, fermented. In the fifth, the leavens of the Arabs were formed, and of their women and people of leisure... and... those who sing and play instruments, those people, and... the sweet water springs were formed. Then it was... at the end of its administration... when its final administration came... and the beginning of the administration of the Moon is... and... this is the final administration of 'Utarid, and the administration of the Moon has begun. So *zuhur al-shakhs al-bashari* (the appearance of the human person)... so the human person appeared. In the seventh thousand years, the human appeared. *Nabatan min al-ardi* (as a plant from the earth). And how did that happen? As a plant... from the earth. As Allah Ta'ala says, *wa-Allahu anbatakum min al-ardi nabata* (And God has caused you to grow from the earth as a growth). As... Allah Ta'ala says that Allah Ta'ala has created you... from the earth... by making you grow like a plant. Yes, this is a verse of the Holy Quran. So it means there are two kinds of our creation. One is from the earth, and one is from the spiritual parents. So the creation from the earth is more excellent and higher. And the creation from the spiritual parents is lower than that in comparison. Our chapter is finished, Insha'Allah we will do the second chapter tomorrow. May God accept it from us and make it reach. Fatihah. To the soul of Sayyidna al-Mu'ayyad... Shirazi... and may He increase his blessings... and his support... and that he accepts from us the benefit and giving benefit in the best way possible... by the sanctity of our master... and the Ahl al-Bayt... Fatihah. Bismillahirrahmanirrahim. Al-hamdu lillahi Rabb al-'alamin. Ar-Rahman ar-Rahim. Maliki yawmid-din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdinas-sirat al-mustaqim. Sirat alladhina an'amta 'alayihm, ghayril-maghdubi 'alayihm wa lad-dallin. And to the lord of the two seas, Sayyidna... al-Mutlaq in the two islands of India and Sindh... and for the elevation of the station... of our master... and for our master... Ghulam Husain... son of Sayyidna... may God sanctify his soul... may Allah elevate his station in the highest of ranks and protect him and grant him health... from all illnesses... and the evils of the enviers and the enemies... and by the sanctity of our master... the Imam of the time... and his holy Ahl al-Bayt... Fatihah. Bismillahirrahmanirrahim. Al-hamdu lillahi Rabb al-'alamin. Ar-Rahman ar-Rahim. Maliki yawmid-din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdinas-sirat al-mustaqim. Sirat alladhina an'amta 'alayihm, ghayril-maghdubi 'alayihm wa lad-dallin.

I was saying that one of our Sindhi brothers asked me what *hadd 'ali* is, I didn't understand. And what are its... what are its affairs? So, for everyone's sake, I am stating that *hadd* is called a limit, or a measurement. For example, the *hudud* (boundaries) of this masjid are from that wall to this wall, up to the *mihrab*, and from this wall to that wall. So, that is called a *hadd*. It's gone again. Behind... Why... Why... Why... It keeps starting. Software keeps starting. No, just the clean one... why did it... [unclear] Okay, why... turn this off... I haven't turned on anything. Therefore, the fixed measurement, that is called a *hadd*. Similarly, it has come in the noble Quran that: `la ta'tadu hudud Allah... man yata'adda hudud Allahi faqad zalama nafsahu` (do not transgress the limits of Allah... whoever transgresses the limits of Allah has indeed wronged himself). Meaning, do not transgress the limits of Allah, and whoever transgresses the limits of Allah, he has wronged his own soul. So... it's gone again. One minute, there's a problem. Can you all hear my voice? Can you hear my voice? Yes. Okay. Just one minute. Now if it happens again, what to do... Okay. Now, the mention that has been made here of *hadd 'ali* is that when a *momin* (believer) passes away, then immediately after death, in that very instant, by the arrangements of Allah, that is, the Imam of the Time, his *surah sharif* (noble form) which has been formed at the time of death... Until now, before death, his *nami'ah* (vegetative soul), *hissiyah* (sensory soul), and *natiqah* (rational soul) were separate. But as soon as death occurs, they become one, and that is called the *surah sharifah*. So that *surah sharifah* goes to its *hadd 'ali*. The purpose of *hadd 'ali* is that which is superior to it in *martabah* (rank). And that is a difference of nineteen-twenty, meaning, it's not that a *momin*'s *hadd 'ali* becomes a *da'i*, or that he becomes a *hujjat* of the time. It doesn't happen like that. Rather, it is of the same level as his own rank. And his form goes to him. If that *hadd 'ali* is alive, then his form remains in *mujawarat* (proximity) with his own *surah*, with his *natiqah*. Meaning, it stays in his neighborhood. And if the *hadd 'ali* has passed away, and this dying person is reaching him, then his form undergoes *muwazajat* (joining/merging) with his form. Why? Because if his *hadd 'ali* goes astray, if a defect arises in him, then it should not happen that the *surah* of the *hadd dani* who has reached him also becomes defective. Therefore, this point has been taken into consideration. And this *hadd 'ali* is an important person in our lives because our spiritual progressions are tied to him. And to the extent that our *Tahiyyat al-Masjid* (prayer for greeting the mosque), its *mamsulat*... the various *mamsulat* are... among them... Yes. Yes. Yes, there are various *mamsulat*; among them, one *mamsul* is our *hadd 'ali*, and its *hadd 'ali*'s *hadd 'ali*. So these are our *mamsulat* of the *Tahiyyat al-Masjid*. Because what is the *Tahiyyat al-Masjid*? It is a gift for our mosque. So, it is as if when we come, we pray the *Tahiyyat al-Masjid*. And within the *Tahiyyat al-Masjid*, the necessity has been established that first, one recites the descending *surahs*. Meaning, one recites *Nas*... sorry, *Falaq*, and then afterwards one recites *Nas*. So, this practice of reciting the descending *surahs*, we do it only in our obligatory prayers, or in the *sunnah* of *fajr*. And if we do recite it, it is only in *Tahiyyat al-Masjid*. So *Tahiyyat al-Masjid* has its own special status. Yes. Does it happen that when one goes to the *hadd 'ali*, he is alive. So if he is about to do some bad deed, he stops him... The lower one... No, the one who has gone to him... Meaning, the *hadd 'ali* stops him. The *hadd dani* stops him... I mean... Stops him from doing a wrong deed... He doesn't know, right? The *hadd 'ali*... He doesn't know, but can he help him in some way to stop him from doing a wrong deed? But the decision hasn't been made yet, right? This *hadd dani* hasn't died yet, has he? He has. He has. He has. After it has happened... He went to his *hadd 'ali*. The *hadd 'ali* is alive. Yes. And suppose he is doing some wrong deed... Who is doing the wrong deed? The *hadd 'ali*. The *hadd 'ali*. If the *hadd 'ali* does it... Does he stop him? Do such things happen that he can stop him? That has not been said, but it is so that if his inclination is more towards the world, the *hadd 'ali*'s, then what is the meaning of the *hadd dani*? If the *hadd dani* is fond of knowledge, then he wants to always remain inhabited in the world of knowledge. Therefore, if he is entangled in the world, then this one incites him that you should inhabit your world of knowledge and deliver the benefit to me. In this way. So he does that. But that he should do wrong deeds or evils, and this one stops him from that, that has not come in my reading. It is definitely so that if he has a disinclination towards knowledge, then this one incites him and gives him the inclination to pay attention towards knowledge. Yes. Malik, similarly, if he is lazy in the acts of Shari'ah, does this inspire him? That too has not come in my reading. That too has not come in my reading. It is so much that regarding knowledge, it has come in my reading that the *hadd dani* affects his heart in such a way that he inclines towards knowledge. So much has come in my reading. So this *hadd 'ali*, when it reaches him, his journey of faith begins. And it is necessary for this person who is arriving to be a *mustajib-e-da'wat* (one who has responded to the call). Meaning, until he takes the *‘ahd sharif* (sacred covenant), his *surah sharif* is not formed. And if the *surah sharif* itself is not formed, then his *hadd 'ali* cannot be appointed, no matter how much worship he performs. Therefore, we... our `‘ahd sharif` which we take, it is the greatest act in our lives, our greatest worship. Meaning, our acts of worship, whether they be obligatory prayers, or supererogatory prayers, or Hajj, or Zakat, or fasting, whatever things there are, they are lesser in comparison to the `‘ahd sharif`. The `‘ahd sharif` that we take repeatedly, we renew it, we do it for this reason so that we are reminded again and again, because man is a forgetful animal. And in the noble Quran too, it is: `wa-dhakkir... fa-inna al-dhikra tanfa'u al-mu'minin` (and remind, for indeed, the reminder benefits the believers). Meaning, you keep reminding them, because reminding benefits the believers. Therefore, based on this principle, we arrange for the `‘ahd sharif` every year on Yawm al-Ghadir. Otherwise, the `‘ahd sharif` that is taken once, with that he receives... he... he... with that very `‘ahd sharif`, he gets a *nuqta-e-nur* (point of light), and from where does he get it? He gets it from... his own `hadd` which is in the `‘alam-e-ruhani` (spiritual world), his `inabat` (turning in repentance) and `tawbah` (repentance) that was there, from his `inabat` and `tawbah`, he gets it from his own `hadd`. That is why it has been said in the noble Quran: `la tansa nasibaka min al-dunya` (do not forget your portion of this world). Do not forget to take your portion from the world. Meaning, it is not that my own property and other things have been indicated by Allah Ta'ala, but it has been said that: `la tansa nasibaka min al-dunya` (do not forget your portion of this world). Do not forget to take your portion from the world. Meaning, do not die without the `‘ahd sharif`. Take the `‘ahd sharif` in some way so that your `surah sharif` is formed. And one who does not take the `‘ahd sharif`, his `surah sharif` is not formed at all. Yes. I remember that such cases have also occurred where a person did not take the `‘ahd sharif` his entire life. And but when his last breath was leaving, his relatives strongly urged him that this is the time, so a *buzurg* (elder) of the `da'wat` had gone to meet him, so he said that at this time... So, it was as if in the last moment, when about ten minutes were left, that `mansub` (a rank), that `buzurg` of the `da'wat`, told him to take the name of the Imam of the Time. Take this name and send blessings upon him, this is your `‘ahd sharif`. So, in that way, his `‘ahd sharif` was completed. Yes. Meaning, at the very least, a complete, formal `‘ahd sharif` did not happen, but Imam al-zaman, who is our `markaz-e-da'wat` (center of the call) and is `khalifat Allah fi al-ard` (vicegerent of Allah on earth), Allah's vicegerent on earth, and is infallible, so at least his... we... if we follow him, if we obey him, and we profess love for him, then this is a tremendously great thing for us. Yes. The `ahl-e-istighar`, the children who pass away without... Yes. Does their *surah* form? No, it is not formed, but they have a `majma'` (assembly), a `majma'` is prepared which is... `majma'-e-sighar` (assembly of the young). So that `majma'-e-sighar` attains `taraqqi` (progress). Because they did not get the chance. If they had gotten the chance, if life had been established, then those children who pass away at a young age, their `‘ahd sharif` could not happen. Their age did not permit it, for that reason. Otherwise, if it had permitted, they would certainly have taken the `‘ahd sharif`. And this very `majma'-e-sighar` was first given to Bibi Fatimah al-Zahra. Yes. And to her... and after that, the `majma'` came to Mawlana Baqir. Yes. Yes. And it is also called `majma'-e-mufritin`. And those who are `ahl-e-istighar`, who are especially the children of the `A'immah`, and who pass away in childhood, they also have a `majma'` which is called `majma'-e-mufritin`. Bibi Fatimah also took responsibility for that. Yes. So in this way, he goes to his `hadd 'ali` after death. In this way, his journey begins from the higher `hudud`, until he reaches the `Bab al-Abwab`. And the `haykal` (structure/assembly) that is prepared at the `Bab al-Abwab`, when it is completed, the Imam of the time acquires it and gives it to his `farzand` (heir). And this `farzand` was an Imam `bi al-quwwah` (in potentiality). This `farzand` was an Imam, and by receiving this `majma'`, he attained the `imamat`. So when `imamat` is received from the Imam, that `farzand` goes from `bi al-quwwah` to `bi al-fi'l` (in actuality). Meaning, he becomes a fully active Imam. Therefore, after that, the Imam passes away. Yes. This distance is... thousands of `hadd 'ali`s will be there for one person. No, it's not like that. No, as there is progress, one reaches... So, the `hadd 'ali` will keep changing. No, they do change. I mean, one went from one `hadd 'ali` to another. He went from that `hadd 'ali` to another. So he keeps taking him along. He takes him along. Yes. Now this... at the Imam's, the Imam who gives to his `farzand`, its duration has also been stated, that it happens in four days. The Imam, in four days, hands over his `majma'`, which is gathered at the `Bab al-Abwab`, to his `farzand`, or he hands it over in four years. He hands it over in four days or four years. Yes. And a rare incident was that only one, Mawlana... Mawlana `Abd al-Muttalib`, received this `majma'` before birth. Yes. Therefore, when you were born, Mawlana `Abd al-Muttalib`, your mother said: `wulidtu shaybah` (I gave birth to an old man). Because his hair was white. So the indication is that that `majma'` which was received from Mawlana Hashim, it was received by you at the time of your birth. It was necessary to receive it because Mawlana Hashim had passed away. Yes. This `majma'` that exists, the spiritual `majma'` that exists, of `nasut` and `lahut`, is it `mumathil` (similar/parallel) to the `nasut`? Yes, it is absolutely `mumathil`. Meaning, if someone attains a rank in `nasut`... so in comparison to that, his `surah sharif` takes its place in `lahut`. It's not that in `nasut` he takes a lower rank and in `lahut` a higher rank, it doesn't happen like that. They run completely parallel. And thus in the beginning of `bashar` (mankind)... And that `al-mudabbir al-a'la harraka al-falak` (the higher governor moved the celestial sphere). That is, until now, the genesis of animals and minerals had been prepared and had also appeared. But until now, man has not been born. So in this seventh thousand-year period, which is the `dawr` of the moon, the beginning of `bashar` is happening, and `al-mudabbir al-a'la` has given motion to the sphere. `Wa sa'idat al-bukharat al-hadithah min safl al-ma'adin wa al-nabat wa al-hayawan` (And the newly formed vapors ascended from the dregs of the minerals, plants, and animals). And the vapors ascended, which had come about, from the essences of minerals, plants, and animals. Those minerals which were ground and worn, and the plants which were rotten and from which vapors... ascended, and the animals which died their own death. The one who dies its own death, that is an animal whose death is bad. Meaning, until the knife of `Allahu Akbar` is not passed over it, its death is not `halal`. Meaning, we cannot eat a dead animal. We cannot take a dead animal as food. Unless the knife of `Allahu Akbar` is passed over it while it is alive. While it is in a living state, `Allahu Akbar` is said. But this situation is a special situation, that in the 6000 years, the animals that have died, they have all died a `halal` death. Their death is `halal`. Yes. Just as its example is the pigeons of the Ka`bah and the pigeons of Madinah Munawwarah. They die, they cannot be hunted, they cannot be slaughtered. But when they die their own death, they die a `halal` death. Yes. Because they are formed from the `fudlat` (remains/essences) of the `hudud`... of the `mukasirin`'s `fudlat`. A `mukasir` is a person in the `da`wah` who teaches and performs `kasr` (breaking), meaning he breaks the beliefs of his disciples, his students, and establishing his own `hujjat` (proof), he establishes the correct belief in them. So that `mukasir` has great importance. The `mukasir` is a person just as the entire weight of our body falls on our feet, so the feet have such importance. The entire load of our body is carried by the feet. Similarly, in the `da`wah`, who is carrying the entire load of the `da`wah`, from the Imam of the Time down to the lowest rank? The `mukasir` is carrying it. Because that person is teaching, giving instruction, and is preparing the `surah sharif`. So he has great importance in it. And therefore, when the `mukasirin` pass away, Allah Ta'ala gives them this reward, that from their `fudlat`, someday `jawahir` (jewels) are formed, and `yawaqit` (rubies) are formed from their `fudlat`, from their bones. And the second thing that happens is that from their bones, three things are formed. One is that salt is formed, which is used before our meals. As the Messenger of Allah, peace be upon him, said: `al-milh qabl al-ta'am wa ba'd al-ta'am... yaqta'u sab'ina da'an, awwaluha al-junun wa al-juzam` (Salt before and after food... cuts off seventy diseases, the first of which are madness and leprosy). Meaning, tasting salt before and after eating, it keeps seventy diseases away, and the least of those diseases is madness and leprosy. So how did this situation come about? That the hard work he did his entire life, because of that, God has placed this blessing in his `fudlat`, that he is benefiting people even after his death. And what is the second situation that is happening? That from his `fudlat`, those pigeons of the Ka`bah are being formed. Yes. And this is the third situation, `jawahir` are formed from it. The mention in the noble Quran that Allah Ta'ala created the world in six days, does that point to 6000 years? It could be. But there, what is it? `Khalaqa al-samawati wa al-ard fi sittati ayyam` (He created the heavens and the earth in six days). So, the heavens and the earth have been mentioned. And in one place it has also been said that `khalaqa al-samawati wa al-ard fi yawmayn` (He created the heavens and the earth in two days). So I will have to look at its interpretations, what its meaning is. Yes. The noble Quran has such... in many places, such contradictions appear, externally. Until we adhere to the cloak of the Ahl al-Bayt completely and to their `du'at`, we can never be on the right path or have the right thinking. Yes. It is very necessary that... we get the correct understanding. Once Mawlana Imam Husayn was sitting in a gathering, and he said, "Have you read this verse of the noble Quran: `fas'alu ahl al-dhikr in kuntum la ta'lamun` (ask the people of remembrance if you do not know)?" "Ask the people of remembrance if you do not know." So he said, "We, the Ahl al-Bayt, are the `ahl al-dhikr`." Yes, "We, the Ahl al-Bayt, are the `ahl al-dhikr`." And Allah Ta'ala has divided the world into two parts: `ahl al-dhikr` and `la ta'lamun` (those who do not know). "You come in the category of `la ta'lamun`." You... you people of the world... you do not know. So for you who do not know, for the people of the world, it has been made obligatory upon you to ask the `ahl al-dhikr`. But it has not been made obligatory upon us, the `ahl al-dhikr`, to give you the answer. Yes. He said this. `ghuyum` (`ghuyum` means clouds). `ghuyum`. So those vapors ascended, and because of the ascent of the vapors, after ascending, they became `ghuyum`. `ghuyum` means those clouds in which water is filled. `Thumma inhallat 'ala al-ard` (Then it poured down upon the earth). Then those clouds rained upon the earth. `amtaran` (`amtaran safiyatan`). `amtaran safiyatan`. And they rained as rain, those vapors that had ascended from minerals, plants, and animals, so in those 6000 years, they were gathered. So a rain, a clear and pure rain, fell. `mu'tadilah` (moderate). It was a moderate rain. `fa-ukhiddat fi al-ard khudadan` (And it made furrows in the earth). And because of this rain, furrows were created in the earth. Yes. `ghayr 'amiqatin` (not deep). They were not very deep. `wa-qarra safwah tilka al-ma' fi 'umqiha` (And the purest part of that water settled in its depth). And the purest part of that water, which came through the rain, it settled in its depth, in the depth of those furrows. `Thumma sa'ida bukharan ashraf wa altaf wa asfa wa a'la min al-awwal` (Then ascended a vapor more noble, more subtle, purer, and higher than the first). And then, water ascended... from these furrows, through vapors, and that was more noble and subtle and purer and higher than the first. Meaning, the rain which had fallen from the vapors of minerals, plants, and animals, this water, which was rising from the furrows, was more excellent than that. Vapors were rising. `fa-inhalla mataran nazira maniy al-rajul` (And it poured down as a rain similar to the semen of a man). And then after that, that water descended from the sky. And its example was that it was like a man's semen. Yes. `fa-waqa'a fi tilka al-magharat wa al-khudad allati hiya shabihatun bi-arham al-nisa'` (And it fell into those caves and furrows which are similar to the wombs of women). And that water fell into those furrows, which were like the wombs of women. Yes. The example of the water is that of the semen of a man, and the furrows, their example is that of the wombs of women. `fa-maza ja al-ma' al-qa'im fiha al-mushakil li-ma' al-mar'ah` (And the water standing in it, which was similar to the water of a woman, mixed). And the water mixed... the water that was there, it mixed, and it was similar to the water of a woman. `fa-sara shay'an wahidan` (And it became one thing). And that water, by mixing together, became one. `Thumma askhanathu hararat al-ard` (Then the heat of the earth warmed it). Then the heat of the earth warmed that water. `fa-sa'ada hariban min al-harr` (And it rose, fleeing from the heat). And that water rose, fleeing from that heat. `fa-laqiyahu min kharij al-khudad bard al-nasim` (And the coolness of the breeze met it from outside the furrows). When it rose, the breeze, the `nasim`, which is the breeze that blows in the morning, is called `nasim`. The `nasim`, due to that breeze, the water that was outside the furrows, the breeze that was there, because of that, the water settled again. `fa-habata minhu hariban` (So it descended from it, fleeing). Then it settled down. `Thumma yanzilu taratan wa yas'adu taratan` (Then it descends at times and ascends at times). Until it would settle down and then rise up, that water. Yes. `wa-yanqusu... wa yan'aqidu` (And it decreases... and it congeals). And in rising and falling, in this way, a deficiency would occur in it, in the water. It would become even more... condensed. `wa yan'aqidu` (And it congeals). And a firmness would arise in it. `wa-yatawakkalu fi maratib al-khalqah muddata tis'ati ashhurin` (And it takes form through the stages of creation for a period of nine months). And this formation continued in this way through the nine stages of creation for a period of nine months. `bi-tadbir al-mudabbir` (by the arrangement of the governor). So all these things are happening by the arrangement of the governor. `wa-ta'thir al-quwwa al-kawakib` (and the influence of the power of the celestial bodies). And the celestial bodies that were there, the nine celestial bodies and spheres, their influences were also established on it. `fi hila an-kamulat al-muddah` (until the period was completed). `thumma fataha 'aynayhi` (then he opened his eyes). Until that period, which was the nine-month period, was completed. Then he opened his eyes. Meaning, humanity arose in him. `wa hawassuhu` (and his senses). His senses took motion. `wa-istanshaqa al-nasim` (and he inhaled the breeze). And he took a breath from the `nasim`. That child. `fa-tamaddada taratan wa-qa'ada taratan` (so he stretched out at times and sat at times). Until he would lie down, get up and sit, and sometimes he would get up and sometimes he would sit. `wa-ja'ala yatamarra'u bi-badanihi fi baqiyyah dhalika al-ma'` (and he began to roll his body in the remainder of that water). And until he began to roll around with his body in the remaining water. In the water that was remaining in that pit, he began to roll around in it. From which his creation took place. `wa-yastadhibuhu li-masammi badanihi` (and he would absorb it through the pores of his body). And he... from the pores of his body, that water which was remaining in that pit, he would absorb it into his body through his pores. `wa-qad sara duhhanan` (and it had become oil). Whereas that water was no longer water, but had taken the form of oil. `Thumma talaba al-ghidha' min fihi` (Then he sought food from his mouth). And until he sought the nourishment of food from his mouth. `wa-qad kana awwalan yaghtadhi min surratihi` (whereas before, he was nourished from his navel). Before this, before taking food, he used to take nourishment from his navel, from his umbilicus. `min safl tilka al-duhn` (from the dregs of that oil). That water which had become oil, from its essence, he used to take nourishment from his umbilicus. `fa-ja'ala yamtassu isba'ahu al-ibham` (so he began to suck his thumb). Until he began to suck his thumb. `fa-ajara khaliquhu lahu Ta'ala fihi labanan khalis-an` (so his Creator, may He be exalted, caused pure milk to flow in it for him). Until his Creator, who is great and exalted, caused pure milk to flow from his thumb. For that child. `fa-ja'ala yaghtadhi bihi` (so he began to be nourished by it). Until he began to take nourishment from that milk which was flowing from his thumb. That is why you see in children, when they are born, they have this habit of sucking their thumb, which is an ancient habit that has been established. Yes. `wa-yanamu taratan wa-yaq'udu taratan` (and he would sleep at times and sit at times). And he would sleep sometimes, and sometimes he would sit up. `an-kamula lahu sanah` (until a year was completed for him). Until a year passed. Meaning, he has not come out of the pit yet. Yes. Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il had asked me this question, that the `jussa-e-ibda'iyyah` and the people with him, how long did they stay in the pit? You had asked this question. So that is a period of one year. I couldn't answer. I had said nine months. I said no, not nine months, the child was born in nine months. But how much time did it take for him to come out of the pit? So that is a period of one year. A year and nine months combined... combined, combined. In total, for one year he remained in the pit. `thumma qama` (then he stood up). Then he stood up. `wa-huwa yawma'idhin fi kibr al-jussah ka-mithli ibn arba' sinin` (and he, on that day, in the size of his body, was like a four-year-old child). And he, in terms of his body, was like a four-year-old child at that time. Meaning, Allah had made him in such a way that he was not a small child, but it seemed as if he was a four-year-old child. `wa-dhalika li-kibar al-awwalayn alladhina huma al-sama' wa al-ard` (and that was due to the greatness of the first two, who are the heaven and the earth). And this thing happened because of the greatness of his parents, who are the heavens and the earth. Because of this. And look, this child, who is the `jussa-e-ibda'iyyah` or his companions are... or the Imams of the time are, so they get sick or poison affects them, or whatever happens, so it happens for this reason that the celestial bodies affect them. The celestial bodies affect their bodies, and those bodies are affected. Because their bodies are made of borrowed water. Their bodies are not their own. They are made from the `riya` (subtle bodies), and the `riya` are of the believers and the `hudud` of the `da'wat`. So their own personal thing is what? Look, the Imam has four things. One is his `nasut` (physical body). And his `lahut` (spiritual counterpart). And... and the Imam's `nafs` (soul). A third thing. And the fourth is his `surah` (form). So the `nasut`... that is why the four `rak'at` prayer that we pray, the obligatory one, in it, two `rak'at` have been separated. What is the reason for that? That the two `rak'at`, the first two `rak'at` are higher. They allude to the Imam's `nafs` and `lahut`... sorry, to the Imam's `nafs` and `surah sharif`. And the remaining two `rak'at`, they... on his `nasut` and `lahut`. So this `nasut` and `lahut` are lesser than the Imam's `nafs` and `surah`. And the `nasut` is made from the `riya` of the believers and the `hudud` of the `da'wat`. And the `lahut` is made from the `surat` of the believers and the `hudud` of the `da'wat`. So both these things are borrowed. They are not the Imam's own. But it is the Imam's nobility and greatness that he takes these things. Because the deceased have loved him. The deceased have loved him and professed his love. Their whole life, they took his name, they remembered him always in their prayers. So all these things, the Imam has an awareness of them. Therefore, a system was established from the side of God that you take them. Yes. You acquire them. Now, the Imam's `nafs` that exists, that `nafs` is `nafs-e-hissiyah` (sensory soul). The Imam's `nafs-e-hissiyah` is so exalted that it becomes the bearer of the `nawasit` and `lawahiq` of the entire era. It becomes the bearer of all the `lahut` and `nasut` of the era. It becomes the bearer, the one who carries it. It has so much power in it that it can carry the `nasut` and `lahut` of the entire era. And his `surah sharif` is very exalted, that it is... Similarly, the one who was the Amir al-Mu'minin, Amir al-Mu'minin, `Ali ibn Abi Talib`, may blessings be upon him, his `nafs-e-hissiyah` was such that it had the power to carry all the `nawasit`, `lawahiq`, and `majma'` of all the `A'immah`. Yes. This quality was in him, that all... meaning from Janab Hunays to Abu al-Qayyim, the `hayakil` (structures) that there are, he carried the `hayakil`. Who carried them? Amir al-Mu'minin's `nafs-e-hissiyah` did. Yes. The Imam is after all infallible. So... for this reason... The Imam is infallible. Hence, you... the Imam, Mawlana 'Ali was so brave, I mean, his physical condition was... No, that's not the reason for it, but this has been mentioned, just as when the gate of Khaybar was uprooted, you said that `qala'tu baba Khaybar... la bi-quwwatin jismaniyyatin bal bi-quwwatin ruhaniyyah` (I uprooted the gate of Khaybar not with physical strength but with spiritual strength). Yes, this he said. So, by `quwwat-e-ruhaniyyah`, it means `quwwat-e-rahmaniyyah` (divine strength). He said `rahmaniyyah`. So that `rahmaniyyah`... sorry, `rabbaniyyah` (lordly strength), he said `rabbaniyyah` or `rahmaniyyah`. So that, in fact, is an allusion to your `muhtajib` (veiled one). That the strength which the `muhtajib` granted you, because of that, you established this state. Yes. Yes. This happened. What are you saying? The Imam is infallible. Yes. Now, he is fulfilling our `qisasat` (retributions). Yes. So if any difficulty comes, it doesn't happen to him, it happens to the `riya` and the... for the believers... Yes, yes. He is far removed from all these things. Yes. The Imam's original essence is that which you... Yes. As-salamu 'alaykum. Wa 'alaykum al-salam wa rahmatullah. How are you? The `nafs` and `surah` which are, they are his own. The rest is borrowed. Yes. That `nafs`, `surah`, normally for a human, it is formed after death. So for the Imam, it is already... It is already prepared. Similarly, the `du'at` that there are, the `tawassul` that we take, in many `du'as`, the `tawassul` is from the `surat` of the `du'at`. Or that we say that I am taking `tawassul` from your radiant `surah`, whereas the `da'i`'s `surah` has not been formed yet. But we have faith that someday the `da'i`'s `surah` will be formed. Therefore, we take `tawassul` from him. Yes. When Mawlana 'Ali said when the blow was struck, `fuztu bi-Rabb al-Ka'bah` (I have succeeded, by the Lord of the Ka'bah), so was that on the Ka'bah, I mean, on you, or was that the matter of `qisasat`? The `qisasat`. In the name of Allah... Maulana 'Ali's essence was from Mawlana Hunays up to the Qayyim, the `surat`s... the bearer of the `lahut` and `nasut`. It is mentioned in one place that Mawlana 'Ali was the bearer of the `surat` of 10,000 years. So the 3,000 years of Fatrat are also added to that. Yes, they are added. For example, now we have the concept of 400,000 `surat`s with the Amir Sahab. 400,000, meaning the `dawr-e-kashf`, seven have passed, and the `dawr-e-satr`, six have passed. The `jam'iyat` of the `A'immah` of all of them is also with you. Yes. Because this is your seventh manifestation. Yes. And you are the `khalifah` of the Qayyim. Yes. You are the `khalifah`, and the Qayyim is the `khalifah` of the Ashir. The Qayyim is... you are `‘ayn-e-awwalah` (the first eye/essence), so the Qayyim is `‘ayn-e-akhirah` (the last eye/essence). Yes. It's finished. Hello. Yes. You join. Hello. Yes, I'm dialing. This `khuddah` in which males and females were both... at the same time? No, not at the same time. First, the males were created, and after that, from the remaining water, the females were created. So how many such `khuddah` were there at the time of Sarandib, meaning, were several `khuddah` created? Yes, several `khuddah` were created. And this... specifically, the talk is about the `khuddah` of the `jussa-e-ibda'iyyah`, otherwise `khuddah` were created in the islands too. `khuddah` of animals were also created. Yes. So it's not necessary that it's only in Sarandib, it can be... Yes, it can be, and he has mentioned it, right, that in the 12 islands, there were 12 islands, and `khuddah` were created in those 12 islands. These who were there, the `khuddah`, were all created transparent, meaning these others who were there, they didn't come out of any other `khuddah`? Or were their `khuddah` also formed? We'll have to see that explanation, but it did happen that evil `khuddah` were also created. The evil ones, who became enemies and adversaries, the enmity started from there. They were also created from `khuddah`. Like the animals were. They probably wouldn't have any guidance then. Yes. It's possible. There's a city in Sarandib called... when a volcano erupted, its name is Nur Eliya. They say it was the تجلی (`tajalli` - manifestation) of Mawlana 'Ali. Its name is also Nur Eliya. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Allahumma salli 'ala Fatihah, al-Hadiyyah, wa al-Wasilah, wa al-Manzilah, wa al-Raf'ah, wa al-Waliyyah, ila ruhi sayyidina wa mawlana Rahmatullah bin Musa al-Shirazi, qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara zarihahu wa 'ala zarihi wa sa'iri ruhaniyyin, barakatihim wa hadayatihim wa ta'idatihim. Wa taqabbal minna ya Allah al-Fatihah bi-istijabah, bi-ahsan ma yakun, bi-haqqi sayyidina Muhammad wa ali Muhammad wa barik wa sallim. Al-Fatihah. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Alhamdulillahi Rabbil 'alamin... Wa bi-niyyati Sayyidi wa Mawlaya al-Mansub al-Mutlaq fi al-jaziratayn al-Hindiyyah wa al-Sindiyyah, li-sharaf zu al-fakhm al-nabih wa al-'ilm al-'alim, al-Haj al-Sharafi Usamuddin Mawlana Ghulam Husayn bin Sayyidi al-Nasir al-Muqaddas walidihi al-Nasrullah bin Mulla Rahmatullah bin al-Mansub al-Muqaddas Fathullah. Atala Allahu baqa'ahu wa 'ala Allahu ta'ala... wa yahfuzuhu wa yahmihi wa yu'ayyiduhu wa yakfihi... min sharri jami' al-amrad wa al-asqam wa shiddat al-awja' wa al-alam, sharri al-khala'iq wa al-layali wa al-ayyam, sharri al-nufusat wa al-khusufat wa al-tasdidat wa al-taskhisat wa al-tarbiyat wa al-munazarat, wa yu'ayyiduhu bi-aydihi wa nasrihi wa yahfuzuhu wa yahfaz amrahu... bi-himayati al-Bab wa al-Shah Thaniyyah... wa al-tani' wa al-barakah al-hayat... wa yarfa' alihim 'ala al-darajat, wa yashrikna iyyakum fi salih da'watihi wa rizqina iyyakum mardatihi... bi-jahi sayyid al-mursalin wa sayyid al-awliya' al-muminin... wa Ahl al-Baytihi al-tahirin al-tayyibin, salawatullahi 'alayhim ajma'in. Al-Fatihah. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Alhamdulillahi Rabbil 'alamin...
